# Session Changelog

Older completed-session entries extracted from `docs/status/NEXT_TASKS.md` on 2026-05-24.
Current handoff lives in `docs/status/CURRENT.md`; detailed session history is archived here. Newest entries at the top.

Current local delta (2026-06-22): **Elle/Lilith source-shape completion.** The chicken-blood skull bridge now spawns persistent owner-following Elle; she safely catches up to her owner, searches loaded chunks for a source-shaped large lava pool, locks onto it, warns at the molten threshold, and transforms into Lilith. Lilith now has source-style intro invulnerability/healing, capped damage, hostile regen/weakness/fire pressure/spell attacks, friendly transition, and rewards: empty Glass Goblet -> Lilith blood, Garlic cure, Poppy level 6->7, and enchantable item/book blessing. `GlassGobletItem.withLilithBlood()` and Lilith blood hybrid refusal close the goblet path. `ElleLavaPoolTest` covers the pool detector; `compileJava processResources` and the focused test are green. In-game QA remains to verify the lead-to-lava feel.

### Local delta history rotated from NEXT_TASKS.md (2026-06-03 .. 2026-06-21)

Current local delta (2026-06-21): **Visual ritual recipe pages in the Witches' Primer (ring diagrams + coven faces).** Circle Magic rite pages now show the ring setup as a diagram instead of one generic panel + text. The custom `StationRecipePage` (model/page/renderer) gained two optional features: `rings` (a list of `{color,size}` — color white=ritual / purple=otherwhere / red=infernal, size small/medium/large) drawn as concentric dashed-glyph circles (dot count = the real glyph count 16/28/40, gold centre, largest first) and `coven_faces` (an int, drawn as that many witch faces blitted from the `covenwitch1-5` skins' face u8,v8 + hat u40,v8 region). Offerings stay as live item slots (hover tooltips); the caption summarises ring footprint(s) / chalk / altar power (or "no altar power"). A new `ritualCard(power, coven, rings, offerings…)` builder in `WitchesPrimerBookProvider` (with `ringWhite/ringOtherwhere/ringInfernal` + `ringFootprint`/`chalkName` helpers) replaced the old `ritualRecipe`; all 14 rite entries in the Circle Magic & Rites category now use it. Per-rite data (size/chalk/offerings/power/coven) taken from each rite's port-accurate prose and cross-checked against the source `WitcheryRecipes.java` rite table (`Circle(r,o,i)` glyph counts → ring color+size; `SacrificePower` → power; terrain/recovery rites like Broken Earth / Moving Earth / Nature's Power / Earth's Wrath / Prior Incarnation confirmed to take no altar power; Beastial Call coven 3, Icy Expansion coven 2). `compileJava processResources` + `runData` green; generated JSON spot-checked (beastial_call = purple large ring + 3 coven faces; icy_expansion = white small + 2 faces). **Needs in-game tuning pass** (not yet launched): the ring centre/radii, coven-face row, and offering-row Y are first-pass constants in `StationRecipePageRenderer` and will want visual nudging. Earth's Wrath uses a small infernal ring (source `RiteRaiseVolcano` `Circle(0,0,16)`) — the prose's "large" refers to the volcano's scale, not the circle.

Current local delta (2026-06-20): **Vampire armor — custom textures + 3D model (was rendering as leather/iron).** Root cause: every `VAMPIRE_*` item used vanilla `ArmorMaterials.LEATHER`/`IRON`, whose equipment asset is `minecraft:leather`/`iron`. **Stage A:** added three `EquipmentAsset`s + `equipment/vampire_{masculine,feminine,metallic}.json` + textures (ported from Witchery Resurrected into `textures/entity/equipment/humanoid{,_leggings}/`), three vampire `ArmorMaterial`s (clone LEATHER/IRON stats, custom `assetId`), and rewired all 18 items to their variant material by name (coat/trousers/top-hat = masculine; veil/corset/skirt/heels = feminine; chain/chestplate/helmet = metallic). **Stage B:** new `client/model/VampireArmorModel` (`HumanoidModel`, three variant meshes — top-hat+caped coat / veiled dress / collared chestplate, 128x64) registered as three layer defs in `WitcheryClient.LAYER_DEFS`; `VampireArmorClientExtensions` (one per variant) returns the baked model via `getHumanoidArmorModel`, and NeoForge's default `getGenericArmorModel` copies per-slot visibility + pose so decorative cubes parented to head/body/arms appear with the right slot (legs use plain cubes so leggings/boots render through their textures). Registered in `registerClientExtensions`. `compileJava processResources` + full `test` green. **Per-slot scoping fix:** 1.21.11 has no `setPartVisibility` and vanilla scopes slots via separate baked models, so the single model bled onto every slot; `VampireArmorClientExtensions` now overrides `getGenericArmorModel` to copy pose from the original then hide all parts and re-enable only the worn slot's parts (HEAD→head+hat, CHEST→body+arms, LEGS/FEET→legs), reading the real slot from the stack's `Equippable`. **Head pass (confirmed in-game):** each variant *replaces* the head part with its headwear geometry (top-hat brim+crown / veil band+veil+feather+mini-hat / full helmet+collar+spikes) and re-adds an empty `hat` child — required because `HumanoidModel` resolves `head.getChild("hat")`, and it also drops the standard 8×8×8 head box that was rendering as a bucket over the face. **Chest pass:** body/arm geometry rewritten cube-for-cube from the Resurrected models — masculine collar/scarf+knot/cloak + cuffed short sleeves (the long coat-tails are the LEGS/trouser piece, not the body — putting them on the body wrapped them to the feet where the body sheet's boot UVs read as phantom shoes and the tops z-fought the waist); feminine bodice + flared front/back skirt with layered side panels + full sleeves; metallic front/back chest plates + cloak + right pauldron + both gauntlets with spikes. The metallic main plates and their trim ridges abut at y=5; a uniform 0.5 cube inflation grew them into a 1-unit overlapping band with coplanar faces (z-fighting on the chain coats/chestplate), fixed by inflating those plates in x/z only (`CubeDeformation(0.5,0,0.5)`) so they stay proud of the torso but flush at the seam. The 1.12 source's arm-reparenting/±1 pivot hacks are not copied; decorations are translated into the standard 1.21 part-local space (pose copy overwrites arm pivots anyway). **Slim arms:** masculine/feminine ship distinct 3px-arm models (`createLayer(variant, slim)`, slim layer `"slim"`) + `_slim` textures; vanilla armor is slim-agnostic so `WitcheryClientEvents` stashes the player's skin model (`PlayerModelType.SLIM`) in a `ThreadLocal` over `RenderPlayerEvent.Pre/Post`, and the extension picks the slim model in `getGenericArmorModel` + redirects the HUMANOID texture to `_slim` via `getArmorTexture`; metallic reuses its single model (matches source). **Legs/feet pass:** each leg part is now an empty standard part with two slot-scoped child groups built by `addLegSlot` — `"legs"` (trousers / leg-armor, shown for LEGS via the HUMANOID_LEGGINGS `_legs` sheet) and `"boots"` (footwear, shown for FEET via the HUMANOID body sheet); `applySlotVisibility` enables only one group per worn slot so the leggings and boots pieces never render each other's geometry. Geometry per variant: masculine trousers/tall boots (full leg box, recessed `0.25` under the chest coat-tails so they don't z-fight, boots proud `0.75`); feminine ankle + heeled shoe (heel + toe — the chest skirt covers the legs); metallic leg-armor plate + armored boot (sole + shin plate + sleeve, sleeve inflated `0.75` proud of the `0.5` leg plate to avoid coplanar z-fight on the overlapping lower leg). The source's combined-model dual-texture trick (one model, `_legs` vs body sheet) is mapped onto 1.21's separate LEGS/FEET slots by splitting the geometry and using the proud-outer-piece rule learned on the chest/gauntlets. Hunter clothing shares the same root cause and can reuse this infra later.

Current local delta (2026-06-20): **Sun Grenade thrown projectile now renders.** `SunGrenadeEntity` was registered with `NoopRenderer` and supplied no item, so the thrown grenade was invisible in flight (same class of bug as the earlier thrown-brew fix). It now `implements ItemSupplier` (`getItem()` returns the Sun Grenade, or the Duplication Grenade in duplication mode, via a synced `DATA_DUPLICATION` boolean) and is rendered with `ThrownItemRenderer`. Both item models exist, so the bottle/grenade shows arcing through the air. `compileJava` + full `test` green; in-game QA: throw a Sun Grenade and a Duplication Grenade and confirm each shows its own item mid-flight.

Current local delta (2026-06-20): **Witchery crop soil — Herbalism Primer parity (farmland-only).** Follow-up that supersedes the earlier "keep grass" call: the in-game **Herbalism Primer** (the canonical herbalism guide) lists Belladonna/Mandrake/Snowbell on *tilled earth / farmland*, Wolfsbane/Wormwood on *tilled farmland*, and Garlic on *farmland* — i.e. **farmland only** — with Water Artichoke on a **still water source block, not farmland**. Although the 1.7.10 `BlockWitchCrop.func_149854_a` code also accepts grass/dirt, the primer is treated as the authority, so every normal crop's `mayPlaceOn` is now `state.is(FARMLAND)` only (Mindrake matched to its Mandrake sibling though the primer doesn't list it; Artichoke already water-source-only, unchanged). This reverses the prior `farmland | grass_block | dirt` normalization. `compileJava` green.

Current local delta (2026-06-20): **Rowan Key now works (source-checked).** Source (1.7.10-0.24.1 `BlockWitchDoor`/`ItemGeneral.placeDoor`): the Rowan door is **key-item-driven** — placing one drops a bound key, and the key opens the door. The port's `RowanDoorKeyItem` had **no `useOn`**, so opening relied entirely on the door's empty-hand `useWithoutItem`; that works for a plain right-click but does nothing when you **sneak-right-click** while holding the key (the block interaction is skipped), contradicting the key's tooltip. Fix: extracted the door's lock-checked toggle into `RowanDoorBlock.toggleWithKeyCheck(...)` (used by `useWithoutItem`) and gave both `RowanDoorKeyItem` and `RowanKeyringItem` a `useOn` that routes there — so right-clicking the bound door with the key/keyring opens it whether or not you sneak. (UUID-per-door binding kept; non-sneaking behavior unchanged since `useWithoutItem` still wins there.) `compileJava` + full `test` green; in-game QA: place a Rowan door, then open it with the key both normally and while sneaking.

Current local delta (2026-06-20): **Books close on the X key.** New `WitcheryClientEvents.onBookScreenKeyPressed` (`ScreenEvent.KeyPressed.Pre`) closes an open guidebook with X (mirrors Escape) — covers the native Modonomicon books (Witches' Primer etc., detected by the `com.klikli_dev.modonomicon.client.gui.book.` screen package) and the vanilla read-only `BookViewScreen` (Immortal's Observations). Skipped when a text field is focused (e.g. a Modonomicon search box) so the key still types. `compileJava` green.

Current local delta (2026-06-20): **Altar no longer dumps power to chat.** `AltarBlock.useWithoutItem` printed an "Altar power: x / y (recharge…, range…, valid…)" chat line on every empty-hand right-click — a debug leak. Removed it (the empty-hand click now passes through). Altar power inspection stays where it belongs: the **Debug Inspector** item (`DebugInspectorItem.useOn` already prints the full altar power/structure/range/enhancement breakdown) and the `/witchery` command. `compileJava` green.

Current local delta (2026-06-20): **Spirit World cotton — source parity + nightmare art.** Source check (1.7.10-0.24.1 `WitcheryWorldGenerator.generateDreamworld`, `BlockCotton`, `WorldProviderDreamWorld`): Wispy Cotton seeds via a `WorldGenFlowers` patch (64 tries) in **~75%** of dream chunks, then *spreads* only next to Flowing Spirit; harvesting it **during a nightmare** drops Disturbed Cotton; there is **no** disturbed-cotton worldgen (it's item-only). The port had three divergences, now fixed: (1) `placed_feature/wispy_cotton` used `rarity_filter chance:4` = ~25% of chunks (≈3× too rare) — rarity_filter can't express 75% (1/N only), so it now places in every eligible chunk and matches source *expected density* via `tries 6 → 48` (~0.75×64); (2) removed the invented Disturbed Cotton worldgen (`configured/placed_feature/disturbed_cotton.json` deleted, biome ref + `/witchery verify` check removed) — matches source; (3) **Disturbed Cotton is now justified as the nightmare *appearance* of Wispy Cotton**: a dynamic `BlockStateModel` wrapper (`NightmareCottonModel`, installed via `ModelEvent.ModifyBakingResult`) renders the wispy block with the disturbed cross texture when `SpiritWorldSpecialEffects.isNightmareLike()`, with a chunk re-mesh on nightmare transitions (`WitcheryClientEvents`). The Disturbed Cotton block/item stay (item-only harvest drop + the spinning conversion recipe). Spread + nightmare-drop logic in `CottonBlock` already matched source. `compileJava processResources` + full `test` green; in-game QA pending (confirm cotton density and the nightmare texture flip). Removed the only worst-case spike in the move logic: `VanishingHutEvents.findValidAnchor` no longer force-generates candidate chunks during the in-world ring search. It gained a `mayForceLoad` flag — the Overworld hop passes `false` and only inspects **already-loaded** chunks (`level.hasChunk`), skipping unloaded ones (there's near-always loaded ground by the player who just visited; if not, the move just waits a cycle), while the rare cross-dimension crossing and the QA/setup placement pass `true` because that destination is intended and is a single small area. Ring attempts trimmed 24 → 12. Idle cost was already negligible (BE ticks only when loaded, gated every 10 ticks; the `LevelTickEvent` queue short-circuits on empty). `compileJava` + full `test` green.

Current local delta (2026-06-20): **Vanishing Witch Hut — slice 2 (natural worldgen).** The hut now generates in the wild, not just by command. New `VanishingHutStructure` (code-built, mirrors `StrawManStructure`: dry/flat 5x5 footprint validation in forests/swamps) + `VanishingHutPiece` whose `postProcess` clears footprint clutter and builds the hut + buried anchor + resident through the shared `VanishingHut.build`/`spawnResident` (generalized to `ServerLevelAccessor` so the same logic serves worldgen and the QA command), then records it in `VanishingHutData` so goto finds it. Registered as `witchery:vanishing_hut` (`WitcheryStructures.VANISHING_HUT_TYPE`/`_PIECE`) with `worldgen/structure/vanishing_hut.json`, `structure_set/vanishing_huts.json` (random_spread spacing 44 / separation 20), and biome tag `has_vanishing_hut` (forests + swamps, concrete swamp entries to avoid the missing-tag world-create crash). A generated hut behaves exactly like a command-placed one (relocates unobserved, same witch carried, held by salt). `NbtStructureTemplateTest` pins the structure/set/biome-tag wiring. `compileJava processResources` + full `test` green; in-game QA pending.

Current local delta (2026-06-20): **Witches' Salt acquisition + white tint.** Salt is now obtainable two ways: a Witches' Oven recipe (`recipe/oven/kelp_to_salt.json` — kelp → 2 Witches' Salt; a furnace-type recipe can't return a bucket, so we render "sea salt" from kelp rather than consuming a water bucket) and a Coven Witch trade (1 emerald → 4 Witches' Salt, added to `CovenWitchEntity.updateTrades`). The salt line reuses the vanilla redstone-dust models, so a block colour handler in `WitcheryClient.registerBlockColors` now tints `SALT_LINE` white (0xFFFFFF) so the trace reads as salt, not redstone. `compileJava processResources` + full `test` green; in-game QA pending. (Cauldron boil-dry / rock-salt worldgen remain optional later sources.)

Current local delta (2026-06-20): **Vanishing Witch Hut — in-game QA fixes (slice 1b).** Acted on first playtest feedback. (1) **Resident stays inside** — `CovenWitchEntity` gained an optional `hutHome` tether (mirrors the Crazy Cat Lady): when set she pulls back to the hearth whenever she drifts >~1.4 blocks, so she no longer paths out the door. Only vanishing-hut residents get a `hutHome`; normal/village/recruited witches are unaffected. (2) **Same witch moves with the hut** — the anchor BE now tracks its resident UUID; on relocation `VanishingHutEvents.moveResident` teleports the *existing* witch to the new hut (same-dimension `teleportTo`, or the 8-arg cross-dimension `teleportTo` which preserves UUID), only spawning a fresh witch if she's truly gone. `VanishingHut.build` no longer spawns its own witch (that was the duplicate cause). (3) **No more dirt cap over the wood floor** — `vanish` stopped refilling the floor footprint with coarse dirt; it now only removes the hut's own palette blocks (original terrain underneath is left intact), restores the single buried-anchor cell from an untouched neighbour ground block (no hole), and scatters debris (loose planks, cobwebs) **on** the ground surface. (4) **Salt-line counterplay** — new `SaltLineBlock` (item **Witches' Salt**, `witchery:witches_salt`) reusing the **redstone-wire connection code** (NESW `RedstoneSide` properties, climbs/links into a connected ground trace) but powerless; a salt line laid across the hut's doorway threshold (`VanishingHut.isHeldBySalt`) holds the hut so it won't relocate (force-move QA still bypasses). Salt renders via the vanilla redstone-dust models; obtain with `/give @s witchery:witches_salt` for now (no recipe yet). `compileJava processResources` + full `test` green; in-game QA pending.

Current local delta (2026-06-20): **Vanishing Witch Hut — slice 1 (relocation loop).** New folklore structure that teleports when unobserved (the approved `vanishing_witch_hut`). A hidden, unbreakable `VanishingHutAnchorBlock` + BlockEntity is buried under the floor as the hut's brain: each tick it scans the interior for occupants (priming any player who steps in), runs a look-cone observation test (`VanishingHut.isWatched`, reusing the Creaking dot-product math), and when nobody is inside, a primed player has left, nobody is watching, and the cooldown (1 min) has elapsed (`VanishingHutHelper.shouldRelocate`), it enqueues a relocation. `VanishingHutEvents` (budgeted `LevelTickEvent.Post` queue) then picks a destination — usually a nearby Overworld ring spot, ~25% a crossing into the Spirit World at the mirrored 1:1 coords (`VanishingHutHelper.pickCrossDimension`) — validates a level/dry/open 5x5 footprint, rebuilds the hut + resident Coven Witch there, and demolishes the old one leaving **ash/debris** (scorched coarse-dirt floor, loose planks, cobwebs) + a WHITE_ASH/SMOKE/WITCH particle burst + the witch's "We may not meet again." line. Only the hut's own palette blocks are cleared (player edits survive); it never moves while a player is inside; new `VanishingHutData` (`witchery_vanishing_huts`) tracks the live hut by stable id so it survives relocations. QA: `/witchery setup vanishing_hut`, `/witchery goto vanishing_hut`, `/witchery hut move` (force, bypass gate), `/witchery hut watch` (state readout). Pure helpers locked by `VanishingHutHelperTest` (relocation gate, look cone, ~25% cross-dim). `compileJava processResources` + full `test` green. **Next slices:** natural forest/swamp worldgen (NBT structure like Witch Well), then counterplay (red thread / iron nail / salt line to hold it once). In-game QA pending (needs client launch).

Current local delta (2026-06-20): **Village walls now spawn on ~40% of villages (60% unwalled), config-tunable.** Automatic wall generation was unconditional — every detected village got walls. Added a deterministic per-village roll: `VillageWallGenerator.shouldGenerateWalls(worldSeed, minX, minZ, spawnPercent)`, seeded from the world seed XOR the village bounding-box min corner (vanilla-style coordinate multipliers, so swapped/symmetric coords don't collide). The percent is exposed as `worldgen.villageWallSpawnPercent` in `WitcheryConfig` (0-100, default = `VillageWallGenerator.DEFAULT_WALL_SPAWN_PERCENT` = 40; 0 = never, 100 = every village). Gated in `VillageWallEvents.queueVillage` right after the `isProcessed` check, reading the config each evaluation; a failed roll returns without queuing and is **not** marked processed, so the stable seed re-derives the same "no" on every chunk-load re-eval and the village stays open to a manual `/witchery walls` force-build (separate code path, unaffected). Existing walled villages stay walled. New `VillageWallGeneratorTest` cases pin determinism, 0/100% bounds, and the ~40% distribution. `compileJava processResources` + the wall test green.

Current local delta (2026-06-19): **WorldEdit added to dev runs (designer tooling).** Dev `runClient` / `RUN_MINECRAFT.bat` now load [WorldEdit](https://www.curseforge.com/minecraft/mc-mods/worldedit) for in-game world-building. Wired into `build.gradle` as a dev-only `localRuntime` dependency via a new CurseMaven repo (`curse.maven:worldedit-225608:7855515` = WorldEdit 7.4.2, the CurseForge MC 1.21.11 / NeoForge build), `transitive=false`. It chains into `runtimeClasspath` (existing `localRuntime` wiring) so the client picks it up with no run-config change, and is **not** on the compile classpath or in the shipped mod jar. Verified: `compileJava processResources` green; `dependencies` shows WorldEdit on `runtimeClasspath` and absent from `compileClasspath`. In-game load is pending the user's explicit go-ahead to launch the client. Tooling only — no FEATURE_MATRIX / BUGS / PORTING_NOTES rows.

Current local delta (2026-06-19): **Ritual full-offering dispatch slice 9 - Total Eclipse + Fertility.** Added full dropped-offering dispatch for ordinary/portable Total Eclipse and ordinary/portable Fertility, so support items can sit closer to the center than the old representative trigger without misrouting the rite. Total Eclipse now matches Stone Axe + Quicklime or Iron Axe + Quicklime + Charged Attuned Stone before falling back to the old axe trigger path; portable Eclipse still returns a discharged Attuned Stone after the sacrifices drain. Fertility now matches Bone Meal + Hint of Rebirth + Diamond Vapour + Quicklime + Gypsum + Mutandis, while the portable no-power branch matches Bone Meal + Hint of Rebirth + Diamond Vapour + Quicklime + Gypsum + Mutandis Extremis + Charged Attuned Stone and keeps the port's existing portable-first Bone Meal behavior. The representative routes remain as fallback, and focused dispatch tests pin support-order independence, missing-support rejection, chalk-family rejection, and portable Fertility overlap order.

Current local delta (2026-06-19): **Shade of Leonard — doc sync (README / Book of Shadows / in-game Primer) + melee-only parity fix.** Reviewing the player-facing docs surfaced a real parity bug: the Book of Shadows correctly says Leonard is "melee only — projectiles bounce" (source gates on the `"player"` damage type = direct melee), but the port's `hurtServer` allowed any `source.getEntity() instanceof Player`, so player **arrows** could hurt him. Fixed to `source.is(DamageTypes.PLAYER_ATTACK)` — now only a player's own melee blow lands, matching source. Doc updates: the **in-game Primer** (`WitchesPrimerBookProvider` → `runData`) Shade of Leonard entry had the *old wrong* summon recipe (Refined Evil + Mandrake Root) — corrected to the source ingredients (Nether Wart + Tear of the Goddess + Diamond Vapour + Diamond + Nether Star, Witches' Hat trigger) and its behaviour prose enriched (spell-casting, Mortal Coil clears on his death, below-half soul waves, giant near death) in the old-witch diary voice. **Book_of_Shadows.html** Leonard section + bestiary rows fixed: HP 500→600, soul threshold "~1/3 HP"→below half, summon recipe/availability (no longer gated behind the unbuilt capacity-12 brew system), drops (enchanted book + Demon Heart + Urn), and added his spell-casting/giant/field-control rows. **README** mob highlight now calls out the Shade of Leonard as a full parity boss. `compileJava runData` + full `test` green.

Current local delta (2026-06-19): **Shade of Leonard — cauldron summon recipe corrected to source.** The Witch's-Hat cauldron summon (`WitchCauldronBlock.trySummonLeonard`) required invented offerings (Refined Evil + Mandrake Root). Corrected to the source `BrewActionRitualSummonMob` recipe (1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` / Resurrected `summonleonard.json`): **Nether Wart + Tear of the Goddess + Diamond Vapour + Diamond + Nether Star**, Witches' Hat trigger, 10000 altar power. The summon now also calls `setInvulnerableStart()` explicitly (source) so the Shade coalesces at quarter health behind its invulnerable phase, and adds the source explosion particles alongside the Wither-spawn sound; persistence was already set. Support-message lang updated to the correct ingredients. Port divergence (documented): it remains a direct cauldron interaction with dropped offerings, not a fully composed ritual-brew through the generic `triggersRitual()` pipeline (that broader brew-ritual lane is still unbuilt). `compileJava processResources` + full `test` green. QA: `/witchery setup stations` gives a heated cauldron (Diamond Vapour is already in its supply chest); bring a Witches' Hat + the other four.

Current local delta (2026-06-19): **Shade of Leonard — Phase 5: authentic sounds + spell FX. Parity pass complete.** Imported the authentic source 1.7.10 Leonard sounds (`mob/leonard/say1-5`, `hit`, `death`) into `sounds/entity/leonard/`, registered `entity.leonard.say` (5-variant random) / `entity.leonard.hit` / `entity.leonard.death` sound events + `sounds.json`, and pointed `getAmbientSound`/`getHurtSound`/`getDeathSound` at them (Wither placeholders gone). Added source-coloured note-harp hex bursts — green Death Coil (0x009900), dark-red Sinking (0x990000), blue Insanity (0x000099), teal Overheating (0x009999) — plus a witch-spark flourish + ghast-cue when he looses a spell. `compileJava processResources` + full `test` green. **With this the Shade of Leonard parity pass (Phases 1-5) is complete:** combat spine, hex kit + 5 effects, Lost Soul waves, demonic model + authentic 64×64 texture + full-bright render, and authentic sounds/FX. **Remaining:** in-client visual QA of the model (geometry/UV unverifiable headless) and the cauldron brew-summon wiring (dynamic action lane). Current local delta (2026-06-19): **Shade of Leonard — Phase 4: demonic model + authentic texture.** Replaced the generic-humanoid stub model with a faithful port of source `ModelLeonard` (1.7.10-0.24.1): the horned goat-shade with snout, beard, two ears, three horns (left/middle/right), a neck-driven head-look, a gown skirt nested under each leg, and the source attack-timer arm raise. Swapped the placeholder 64×32 `leonard.png` for the authentic 64×64 source art (`witchery-1.7.10-0.24.1/.../entities/leonard.png`). `LeonardRenderer` now uses a `LeonardRenderState` carrying the attack timer and renders **full-bright** (source `getBrightness → 1.0` shade-glow), dropping the invented gray-translucent tint/hover. `compileJava processResources` green. **Remaining FX (Phase 5):** custom `mob.leonard.say/hit/death` sound events + ogg assets (still using Wither placeholders), and spell-cast/hex particle polish.

Current local delta (2026-06-19): **Shade of Leonard — mechanics parity (Phases 1-3 of a planned 6).** Playtesters reported Leonard was a placeholder (a 500 HP melee monster that slapped Wither II and spawned 6 souls once). Rewrote `LeonardEntity` to the source `EntityLeonard` (1.7.10-0.24.1) combat spine. **Phase 1 (combat core):** source attributes (600 HP / 0.35 speed / 50 follow / 0-armor / `fireImmune()` / never despawns); **ranged Mystic Branch spell-casting** via `RangedAttackGoal` + `RangedAttackMob` reusing the existing `SpellEffectEntity` — weighted Ignianima 14 / Expelliarmus 2 / Flipendo 2 / Impedimenta 2 / Confundus 1, 50% per-window fire chance, ghast-shoot cue; source melee restored (7+rand(15), +0.4 knock-up, iron-golem cue — the invented Wither II is gone); **damage rules:** immune to all non-player damage, player hits capped at 12 (≥25% HP) then 4 / 1-when-giant, plus the 150-tick invulnerable summon phase (heal-in + Wither-spawn cue) and +1 HP/s regen. **Phase 2 (hex kit + 5 new effects):** registered `MORTAL_COIL` (Death Coil — kills on expiry), `SINKING`, `INSANITY`, `OVERHEATING`, `RESIZING` MobEffects; the 40-block Death Coil AOE hex, the random buff-strip / Sinking IV / Insanity IV / Overheating IV roster, the <25% giant phase (via the `minecraft:generic.scale` attribute), and the **on-death coil cleanup** so winners don't die. **Phase 3 (souls + field control):** repeating Lost Soul waves below 50% HP (4+rand(2), 60-90s, immune-while-alive) and the radius-4 anti-brew sphere that burns away `brewgas`/`brewliquid`. Drops now include the enchanted book + Demon Heart + Urn. QA timesaver added: `/witchery spawn leonard_weak` (spawns him at 280/600 HP so soul waves fire immediately and a few hits cascade to the giant phase and death). **Divergences (documented):** Insanity is approximated (Nausea + eerie cues — no illusion mobs yet); Resizing is a marker effect (full player tier system deferred); incurability isn't milk-gated (matches the port's other incurable effects). `compileJava processResources` + full `test` green. **Deferred FX (Phases 4-5):** the demonic horned-goat model/64×64 texture (port source `ModelLeonard`, reuse `leonard.png`), full-bright render, custom `mob.leonard.say/hit/death` sounds, and spell-cast particle polish.

Current local delta (2026-06-19): **Cursed Witch Well placement polish.** The cave well vertical anchor now puts the adaptive surface at local Y=12, one block below the visible well opening, instead of burying the ruin about three blocks under terrain. The visible surface patch is an oval skirt that samples nearby terrain so beaches stay sandy, swamps stay muddy, and forests stay grassy; under the surface, the full footprint is repaired to stop sandy terrain collapsing into voids. The cave clear mask keeps the upper well shaft to the actual 3x3 column, expands to a 5x5 lower transition room four blocks below the surface, and never clears the surface layer; after template blocks are restored it removes only terrain-like backfill from the shaft, connector, lower chamber, and upper ruin void. Focused NBT regression test pins the cave anchor, oval skirt, 3x3 shaft, 5x5 lower room, preserved surface, and side-chamber void.

Current local delta (2026-06-19): **NBT-backed Witch Well first slice.** Added `witchery:witch_well`, generating in forest/swamp tags with regular `well.nbt` 90% and `well_cave.nbt` 10% when the cave site fits. The downloaded NBTs are cleaned before packaging: captured dirt/grass/snow/stone/ore terrain, structure blocks, air, leaves, and designer/player NBT are stripped; runtime placement builds an adaptive surface patch and clears only the cave room/shaft instead of pasting a terrain cube. The well loot table supplies herbs, bottles, ash/quicklime, graveyard dust, and rare spectral/refined drops; `/witchery goto witch_well` is available for any well, and `/witchery goto cursed_well` filters to the cave variant for QA. Witch wells now write to persisted `witchery_structures` saved data when generated, and loaded start chunks backfill older wells so goto can use remembered coordinates before attempting any scan. Loaded-only misses no longer apply the goto cooldown, and `/witchery setup cursed_well` deliberately places, indexes, and opens a safe path to a nearby cave well for QA worlds that have not naturally found one yet.

Commit audit (2026-06-14): checked current local work on top of the 2026-06-13 audit through `bbc25752`. The June 10-14 commits/work are represented across this file, `FEATURE_MATRIX.md`, `BUGS.md`, `PORTING_NOTES.md`, and `FX_TECHNIQUES.md`: Creaking familiar/ritual trigger fixes, staged sacrifice consumption, Sky's Wrath sword focuses, Wolfsbane/Redstone Soup visual fixes, Glamour Bleed + mandrake/fibre texture corrections, Primer chapter reorder/open behavior, station/rite recipe card baking, Spectral Coven, placed Modonomicon live pages/navigation/rendering, custom brew fluid polish, altar book focus bonus, triggered cursed blocks, Apothecary hardening/jobsite/trades, Ender Dew creative access, Crystal Ball/Waystone scrying, and Dog Tongue ritualized custom-brew cauldron release.

Current local delta (2026-06-19): **Spirit World atmosphere — source research + nightmare-colour fix.** Documented the source (1.7.10) dream/spirit look in `docs/reference/SPIRIT_WORLD_ATMOSPHERE.md` (pink/green/red fog by nightmare level, horizon 0, no stars/clouds, fixed day/night angle, overworld terrain + wispy cotton, silent spirits, no music). Audited the port: `SpiritWorldSpecialEffects` + `WitcheryClientEvents` + the biome/dimension already reproduce most of it. **Bug fixed:** `SyncPlayerDataPayload` synced `inSpiritWorld` but not the dream/demonic flags; with `isNightmare()` = `!sw.isDream` (default false), the client always rendered the **green nightmare** sky for a normal **pink** dream. Added `spiritWorldDream` + `demonicNightmare` to the payload + client apply, and aligned the fog gate to the sky's dimension-only check. **QA commands:** added `/witchery nightmare demonic` (red) to go with `/witchery nightmare false` (pink dream) and `/witchery nightmare` (green) so all three atmospheres are testable; `/witchery dream` now marks a normal dream on entry (was a raw teleport that would have shown green after the sync fix); `/witchery status` reports the mode. compileJava + full test suite green.

Current local delta (2026-06-19): **Hobgoblin Hut goto hardening.** Local QA showed `/witchery goto hobgoblin_hut` could hard-freeze/crash with the last log line inside the synchronous vanilla structure locate. Overworld non-village structure gotos now queue the same ticked candidate-probe path used by village gotos, capped to the nearer safe radius, so Hobgoblin Hut / Coven Circle / Straw Man searches no longer monopolize the integrated server thread. Follow-up log QA showed the first safe run still lagged because it inherited village-style 5x5 arrival prep; non-village structure arrival prep is now just the destination chunk.

Current local delta (2026-06-19): **World-creation logging hardening.** Fresh-world logs showed no crash report, but did show thousands of `[VillageBuildings:Debug]` / `[VillageWalls:Debug]` info-level lines from village jigsaw/wall lookup hot paths during chunk generation. `witchery-common.toml` now defaults `debug=false`, and the village building/wall debug helpers write at logger debug level instead of normal info, so existing configs with `debug=true` no longer flood ordinary logs or stall world creation with synchronous log spam.

Current local delta (2026-06-19): **World-creation biome-tag fix.** Create World failed because `witchery:has_hobgoblin_hut` referenced missing vanilla tag `#minecraft:is_swamp` in 1.21.11. The Hobgoblin Hut tag now keeps `#minecraft:is_forest` and lists `minecraft:swamp` / `minecraft:mangrove_swamp` directly; the older swamp biome modifier was corrected the same way, and the NBT structure test pins the concrete swamp entries.

Current local delta (2026-06-18): **Nether structure goto landing hardening.** `/witchery goto warped_coven` now uses a Nether-scaled search origin when run from the Overworld, then resolves a real safe landing near the located structure instead of teleporting to raw locate `Y + 2`. The Nether landing resolver requires solid non-magma/non-campfire floor, open feet/head, four blocks of clearance, at least two open horizontal sides, and no nearby lava; if no safe foothold is found near the located hut, the command fails instead of placing the player underground or in a cramped pocket.

Current local delta (2026-06-18): **NBT-backed structure pipeline first slice.** Designer-authored NBT templates now drive the Hobgoblin Hut and new Warped Coven structure. Hobgoblin huts use `data/witchery/structure/hobgoblin_hut/hobgobhut.nbt`, generate only through `#witchery:has_hobgoblin_hut` (forests + swamps), reject water/river/ocean and steep footprints, use `terrain_adaptation: none`, clear local clutter without carving a terrain shelf, have no chest, and spawn a resident Hobgoblin. Warped Covens use `data/witchery/structure/warped_coven/warpedcoven.nbt`, generate in warped forests during `top_layer_modification`, require a stable Nether floor plus interior clearance, clear late vegetation/stems/leaves from the occupied volume, remove the designer-error snow/mirror, spawn a Coven Witch, and place a focused infernal loot chest. `/witchery goto warped_coven` locates it in the Nether; `/witchery goto nether` now finds a safe Nether landing and stores the player's prior position for `/witchery exit nether`. `NbtStructureTemplateTest` locks NBT size/cleanup and structure JSON tags.

Current local delta (2026-06-18): **Item-book creatures look forward.** `WitcheryEntityPageRenderer` now aligns the render state's head yaw to the body yaw (`yRot = bodyRot`) and levels pitch, so mobs like the Hobgoblin (which spawn with a turned head) look straight out at the reader instead of off to the side. Client-only; compiles.

Current local delta (2026-06-18): **Item-book creature vertical centring.** With the custom renderer's box fix the mobs framed correctly but sat a little low; the Hobgoblin (tall, `sized(0.6,1.8)` — its model sits low in its hitbox) was cropped at the feet. Raised each with a negative `offset` (negative = up, confirmed): owl −0.3, cat −0.2, toad −0.3, Witch Hunter −0.4, Hobgoblin −1.0. Compiles + regenerated; **push held pending QA** of the centring.

Current local delta (2026-06-18): **Item-book creature pages — custom renderer fixes the framing/clip (root cause).** The clipping/high placement was a Modonomicon bug: its `BookEntityPageRenderer` omits `getBookLeft()/getBookTop()` when building the entity scissor box, so the box sat off the frame (bottom at frame middle → lower body clipped); `scale`/`offset` only moved the mob within the bad box, so pushing it down just clipped it. Added `WitcheryEntityPageRenderer` (override-registered for `modonomicon:entity` via `enqueueWork`) that builds the box from the real frame position (`getBookLeft()+left+9`, `getBookTop()+top+7`, 106²) → mob centres in-frame, no clip. Static (`rotate=false`/180), page `offset` reset to 0, scales owl 0.7 / cat·toad 1.0 / hunter·hobgoblin 0.85. Compiles + runData + book tests green; **push held pending QA**.

Current local delta (2026-06-18): **Placed-book creature lighting fix.** The framed mob rendered lit-from-below with dark faces: the upright flip used `rotateZ(180)` (a pure rotation), but the page content pose already carries a -Y scale, so the net transform had an odd reflection count → inverted entity normals. Switched the flip to a Y-reflecting `scale(sx,-sy,sz)`, which pairs with the page's -Y for an even reflection count → correct normals and winding, so the mob is lit normally. Item-book creature pages confirmed good (static, facing reader). Client-only; compiles.

Current local delta (2026-06-18): **Placed-book Beartrap/Wolftrap depth-flattened.** Their crafting cards rendered the full 3D special model protruding from the page; the flatten detector skipped them because the trap's `base` icon model parents to `item/generated` (sprite art). Added `itemDefinitionUsesSpecialModel` so any `minecraft:special` block-item model (real 3D geometry) is flattened before the base sprite-art check — traps now lie flat like the station panels. Client-only; compiles.

Current local delta (2026-06-18): **Item-book creature pages render static (no spin).** Per tester feedback the item-opened book's entity pages now render a steady flat isometric portrait instead of Modonomicon's default turntable: `creature(...)` sets `rotate=false` + `default_rotation` = `BOOK_ENTITY_FACING_DEGREES` (180, face reader). Applies to all five living-entity pages (owl/cat/toad/witch hunter/hobgoblin); the Creature Traps entry already uses flat item recipe cards. **Pending QA:** facing angle (180; ~135/-45 for a 3/4 view, 0 if facing away) and the still-unconfirmed `offset` direction. Compiles; `runData` regenerated; book tests green.

Current local delta (2026-06-18): **Placed-book entity render + 20% larger book + entity-page tuning round 3.** (1) The placed Modonomicon block now renders the actual mob in the Creatures frame (was a spawn egg): `PlacedModonomiconPages.submitFramedEntity` drives `EntityRenderer.submit` into the page collector with the `CameraRenderState` threaded from the renderer, try/catch → egg fallback. Facing flipped 180→0 after QA (mobs faced inward). (2) Placed book enlarged 20% via a shared `PlacedModonomiconBlock.BOOK_BASIS_SCALE` (0.8→0.96) used by both the renderer and the hit/selection geometry, so it stays clickable. (3) Item-book entity tuning: owl/cat/toad/hunter `offset` +0.3 (push down from "too high" — direction unconfirmed), Hobgoblin scale →0.6 (clip fix). **Pending QA:** confirm block-book facing, item-book `offset` direction (flip sign if wrong), and readability/clickability at the larger size. Item-book has no horizontal control via Modonomicon — overriding its renderer is the only fix if "too left" persists. Compiles; book tests green.

Current local delta (2026-06-18): **Witches' Primer book QA round 2 (Path list + entity pages).** (1) **Path of the Craft** no longer renders as a run-together blob: new `WitcheryBooks.orderedListPage` emits CommonMark ordered-list markdown so Modonomicon draws one chapter per line (its `visit(ListItem)` finalizes a component per item). The entry is now an intro prose page + a clean 1–11 index page + a closing prose page. (2) **Entity Creatures pages** in the item-opened book were off-centre/clipped (owl oversized/high; Witch Hunter & Hobgoblin feet clipped); `creature(...)` now takes `scale`/`offset` and has first-pass `scale` (owl 0.7, Hunter/Hobgoblin 0.85), `offset` 0 pending QA. (3) **Still open:** the placed-block book draws a spawn egg instead of the mob — next step is to render the entity model through the existing page flatten path (the BESR-station flatten collector), egg kept as fallback. `compileJava processResources` + `runData` + book tests green.

Current local delta (2026-06-17): **Witches' Primer book text review.** Acted on an external review of the in-game Primer (Modonomicon). Root-caused the "missing space" run-ons (e.g. "Imprisonment.Cast") to Modonomicon's renderer concatenating CommonMark paragraphs with nothing between them (no `visit(Paragraph)` in `CoreComponentNodeRenderer`); fixed in `WitcheryBooks.ink` by collapsing each `\n\n` to a single space, which clears every reported run-on (Pg 94/151/170/172/180/184/187/214/218/255) plus unflagged ones across all native books. Content fixes: clay-jar "Place"→"Insert" (Pg 28), Witches' Oven recipe caption "Iron and netherbrick"→"Iron ingots and iron bars" (the recipe has no nether brick; Pg 29), Sky's Wrath "the better the steel"→"the better the blade" (Pg 168), Sanctity's "study Imprisonment" redirect reworded to a "do not confuse" note (Pg 172), and the Distillery intro's vague "condensed fear / leavings of dark workings" flavour line removed (Pg 121, "needs no altar" already correct). `compileJava processResources` + `runData` regenerated the book JSON; book tests green. **Still open (layout/rendering, need in-client QA):** the numbered category-outline reads with offset/inline numbers, and entity Creatures pages render the mob offset from its portrait frame **only in the item-opened book** (not the placed block).

Current local delta (2026-06-17): **Ritual transaction hardening - Total Eclipse + Fertility family.** Total Eclipse now uses `RitualTransaction.commitDeferred`, so Quicklime and the portable Charged Attuned Stone drain one-by-one before midnight is applied or the discharged Attuned Stone is spawned. Rite of Fertility and Portable Rite of Fertility now share the same delayed growth/cure body: every offering is staged first, powered Fertility peeks altar power before queuing sacrifices, and crop growth, zombie-villager Weakness, golden-apple drops, success feedback, and logging wait until the final sacrifice pop. Focused ritual tests and full `test --no-parallel --rerun-tasks` are green.

Current local delta (2026-06-16): **Ritual activation refactor slice 7 - Redstone Soup curse/weather full-offering dispatch.** Converted dropped full-offering dispatch for the source Redstone Soup cluster: Blight, Blindness, and Rain of Toads. Full-recipe matches now stage the exact matched dropped Charged Attuned Stone/Redstone Soup/Reek/support entities and return no-trigger-consume; the old representative-trigger routes remain as fallback. Current local delta (2026-06-16): **Ritual activation refactor slice 4 - Waystone full-offering dispatch.** Converted the dropped full-offering paths for small Ritual bind Waystone, bind Waystone copy, portable bind/copy, Blooded Waystone powered/no-power variants, and small Otherwhere Waystone transposition. Full-recipe matches now consume the exact matched dropped Waystone/support entities through staged sacrifice and return no-trigger-consume; old representative-trigger fallback routes remain, preserving existing Grassper behavior where it already existed. Current local delta (2026-06-16): **Ritual activation refactor slice 3 - stone + scrying full-offering dispatch.** The full-recipe activation table now supports companion-ring requirements, so Charge Stone can match the complete source dropped offering set (Attuned Stone + Glowstone Dust + Redstone + Wood Ash + Quicklime) only when the paired small/medium Ritual rings are present. Converted dropped full-offering dispatch for Charge Stone, Necromantic Stone, Spectral Stone, Crystal Ball, and Seer Stone, feeding the exact matched item entities into staged sacrifice consumption and returning no-trigger-consume. Current local delta (2026-06-16): **Ritual activation refactor slice 2 — reusable full-offering dispatch for protection circles/barriers + Banish.** The Storm/Sky's Wrath pre-scan is now part of an ordered `FullRecipeRitualDefinition` table that checks complete dropped-offering recipes before the representative trigger table and returns `SUCCESS_NO_CONSUME` so matched sacrifices are not double-consumed by the click handler. Converted the source protection-circle cluster in registration order: Sanctity/Protection (Feather + Redstone), Imprisonment (Slimeball + Redstone), small monster barrier (Obsidian + Redstone), medium player barrier (Obsidian + Glowstone Dust), and portable barrier (Obsidian + Charged Attuned Stone). Converted Banish Demon with portable Charged Stone + Blaze Powder + blank Waystone before the altar-powered Blaze Powder + blank Waystone recipe, preserving source order when extras are present. Current local delta (2026-06-16): **Ritual activation refactor slice 1 — Sky's Wrath full dropped-offering recipe match.** Storm/Sky's Wrath no longer depends solely on whichever representative item `RitualEvents` picked near the center. Before the normal trigger-definition table, small Ritual-circle activation now scans the dropped offerings near the center, uses `RitualRecipe.assign(...)` to choose distinct item entities in nearest-first order, and matches the source Storm recipes in registration order: Wooden Sword + Wood Ash (base), Stone Sword + Wood Ash (large), Iron Sword + Wood Ash + Charged Attuned Stone (portable). The chosen entities are fed into the existing staged sacrifice path, with optional Bound Waystone relocation preserved. Added focused `RitualRecipeMatchTest` coverage for support-first ordering, portable's required Charged Stone, and source-order precedence when multiple Storm sets are present. `compileJava processResources --no-parallel --rerun-tasks`, targeted ritual tests, and full `test --no-parallel --rerun-tasks` are green.

Current local delta (2026-06-16): **Rite timing + Crystal Ball GUI scale.** Staged sacrifice consumption now has a drained-queue callback, so deferred rites can wait until every dropped/Grassper sacrifice and the trigger item have popped one-by-one before the visible result starts. Active ritual ticks pause while staged sacrifices are pending, success feedback is held until the queue drains, and high-visibility instant rites now use `RitualTransaction.commitDeferred` for infusion grants, familiar/creaking binds, familiar/witch/imp/wither/demon summons, charge stone, Necro/Spectral/Seer/Crystal/Mystic output rites, Waystone bind/copy outputs, and entity transposition. The Crystal Ball item model also has an explicit larger GUI transform (`scale` 1.6, slight upward translation) so it reads as a bigger inventory icon. `compileJava processResources --no-parallel --rerun-tasks` + full `test --no-parallel --rerun-tasks` are green; manual QA should watch a multi-offering rite and confirm no output/summon/success cue appears until the final pop.

Current local delta (2026-06-16): **Village walls: outer corner stair orientation follow-up.** The diagonal rampart cap at outside village-wall corners now faces inward toward the adjacent straight north/south rampart stair and derives `OUTER_LEFT`/`OUTER_RIGHT` from that neighbor's facing, matching vanilla stair auto-shape logic. This replaces the previous east/west-outward quadrant mapping that could make the corner stair fail to line up with its neighboring rampart lips. `VillageWallGeneratorTest` expectations updated. `compileJava processResources --rerun-tasks` and the targeted wall test are green.

Current local delta (2026-06-16): **Village walls: entity blockers are relocated.** Wall columns no longer silently skip when a living entity intersects the target wall column. Before placement, the generator now searches nearby non-wall/non-gate cells for solid ground, clear non-liquid feet/head space, no entity overlap, and no collision for the blocker's bounding box; matching blockers are teleported there and the wall column is placed. If no safe nearby cell exists, that single column still skips rather than burying the entity. Forced `compileJava processResources` and the targeted wall test are green.

Current local delta (2026-06-15): **Village walls: square inner/outer corners + corner ramparts.** The wall perimeter's diagonal-corner bridge now fills the empty outside/notch-side shared square instead of converting the interior-side square to wall. Convex outer corners and concave inner notches should now read as hard square 90-degree turns rather than clipped chamfers, and the rampart layer now adds diagonal corner caps with stair `SHAPE` set to the matching outer angle instead of leaving straight/default stair caps. Added `VillageWallGeneratorTest` coverage for both corner shapes and all four corner-stair directions. Targeted test + `compileJava processResources` green.

Current local delta (2026-06-15): **Custom brews — deferred action roster finished.** Added the three remaining composed-brew effect actions to `Brews`: **Transpose** (Ender Pearl, 1000 power, lvl 4 — random safe-spot teleport on contact, skipped under Ender Inhibition; source `BrewActionTranspose` splash path), **Raising / summon undead** (Bone, 2000 power, lvl 4 — raises 1 + strength-scaled enslaved Zombie/Skeleton/Zombified-Piglin thralls, persistent + loot-suppressed via a new `witchery_no_drops` tag honoured in `WitcheryMobDropEvents`; source `BrewActionRaising`), and **Spread Affliction** (Creeper Head, 2000 power, lvl 8 — casts the target's curable debuffs onto nearby creatures then strips them; source `spreaddebuffs`). Pure helpers (`sourceSpreadDebuffsRadius/MaxBuffs`, `shouldSpreadDebuff`, `sourceRaiseDeadExtraCount`) locked by `BrewNatureActionTest`. The other two items I'd listed are already in the port via their canonical homes: **Raise Land tiers** (already strength-scaled), **Biome Change** (the Shifting Seasons rite / `BiomeNoteItem`), **Summon Leonard** (Witch Cauldron Witch-Hat summon). Build + full test green.

Current local delta (2026-06-15): **Coven QA-hardening: living-coven debug commands.** Added QA timesavers that skip the familiar gate + recruit quest: `/witchery coven recruit` (swears one Coven Witch to you, reusing a free loaded one within 12 blocks or spawning one), `/witchery coven fill [count]` (swears up to the cap / [count]), `/witchery coven clear` (empties the roster and frees every *loaded* sworn witch — it clears `covenOwner` level-wide so the tick reconcile can't re-add them; unloaded witches can't be reached but the roster clear stops them counting). New `CovenData.clear(owner)`. Build + full test green.

Current local delta (2026-06-15): **Coven system — Phase 3 close-out: fight quests + persisted roster.** Closed the two items deferred from the Phase 3 ally-AI pass. (1) **FightPet/FightZombie recruit quests** (source `FightPetQuest`/`FightZombiePetQuest`): the recruit roster is no longer fetch-only — a witch may instead conjure a buffed pet (100 HP, 5 atk Spider or Zombie, named after her, targeting you). Slaying it drops her **UUID-stamped remains** (a tagged Spider Eye / Rotten Flesh via `WitcheryMobDropEvents.onQuestPetDrops`), and returning that stamped item — and *only* that item, matched on the witch's UUID in `CUSTOM_DATA` — completes the quest (source `isQuestItemForEntity`: a plain item never satisfies a fight quest). Roster now FIGHT_SPIDER, FIGHT_ZOMBIE + 5 fetch quests; `questKind` persisted on the witch. (2) **Persisted player-roster coven cap** (source player `WITCCoven` NBT list): the 6-witch cap now reads a persisted `world/CovenData` SavedData (`Map<owner, List<witchUUID>>`, overworld-stored like `PredictionData`) instead of counting loaded entities, so it survives the witches being unloaded; recruit adds, dismiss/death removes, and a one-time tick reconcile heals any witch recruited before the roster existed. `covenSize` + the Seer Stone readout now read the roster. `CovenRecruitTest` extended (cap enforcement, removal, quest-kind ordinal). **Port divergence (documented):** witches stay live world entities (to keep the ally-AI), where source dematerialises them into player NBT and re-summons via rites — the roster is a parallel record, not the witch's storage. Build + full test green.

Current local delta (2026-06-15): **Coven system — Phase 3: ally combat AI.** Recruited witches now fight for their coven leader (the port's merchant witch previously had no combat AI). `CovenWitchEntity implements RangedAttackMob` and `performRangedAttack` throws a witch-style harmful splash potion (Slowness/Poison/Weakness/Blindness — a weaker roster than Baba Yaga's boss kit). New `CovenDefendTargetGoal` (a `TargetGoal`) picks a hostile target gated on `isRecruited()`: the witch's own attacker first, then whatever her **leader** is fighting (`getLastHurtMob`) or has been hurt by (`getLastHurtByMob`), all within range. A guard (`isHostileToCoven`) never targets the leader herself or a sister witch of the same coven. New `CovenAttackGoal` closes to ~8 blocks and lobs a potion every ~60 ticks while a target is in line of sight. Normal trading/recruit/dismiss flows unchanged. Build + full test green. **Phase 3 remaining (deferred):** the source FightPet/FightZombie recruit-quest variants (the current quest is fetch-only); coven cap still counts loaded witches rather than a persisted player roster.

Current local delta (2026-06-15): **Coven system — Phase 1: owned-coven recruitment.** Added the source "your coven" layer the port lacked (it only had open coven-counting for rites + a Seer Stone). `CovenWitchEntity` now carries a persisted `WITCCovenOwner` UUID: **sneak-interact** a free Coven Witch to recruit her into your coven (max 6, source `MAX_COVEN`), sneak-interact your own to dismiss her; recruited witches are persistent and never despawn. Normal right-click still trades. The cap counts your loaded recruited witches (`covenSize`); the recruit/dismiss/full/serves-another decision is a pure `recruitOutcome` (locked by `CovenRecruitTest`). The **Seer Stone** now reports your recruited coven size (own-coven support). Recruited witches still count for coven-gated rites via the existing `CovenMember` path (unchanged). Lang for joined/left/full/serves-another. Build + full test green.

Current local delta (2026-06-15): **Coven system — Phase 2: recruit quest gate.** Recruitment is no longer free: a sneak-interact now requires the player to have an **active familiar** (source gate) and to complete a **fetch quest** before the witch joins. First sneak offers a random quest from a roster (Demon Heart ×1, Bones ×30, Mandrake Root ×16, Graveyard Dust ×8, Quartz Sphere ×1); subsequent sneaks while holding the item deliver it (progress/complete); completion joins the coven. Dismiss / serves-another / full / no-familiar branches handled. Quest state (`WITCQuestFor`/`WITCQuestItem`/`WITCQuestLeft`) is persisted on the witch. The gate decision (`recruitOutcome`, 5-arg) and the fetch step (`questStep`) are pure + locked by `CovenRecruitTest`; lang for offer/progress/not-finished/not-interested. **Simplification vs source:** one-step quest (offer = accept), and the source FightPet/FightZombie quests are not ported. Build + full test green. **Phase 3 (deferred):** ally combat AI (recruited witches defend their owner / owner-hurt-target — the port's merchant witch has no combat AI) + the FightPet quest variants; cap still counts loaded witches, not a persisted player roster.

Current local delta (2026-06-15): **Wolf Altar statue model + apothecary worldgen freeze fix.** (1) **Apothecary freeze** (long-standing): `ApothecaryVillager.configure` queried the POI manager (`ensureJobSite`) *during structure generation*, force-loading the job-site POI section and deadlocking on the chunk being generated (log: "foundation finished" with no "resident phase finished"). Fixed — `configure` now only records the job site; binding happens later on a live level via `refreshIfApothecary` (join/tick). Apothecary was the only village piece touching POI during gen. (2) **Wolf Altar statue model** (was a placeholder stone cube): ported the source `ModelWolfAltar` (38-box wolf-god statue, `wolfaltar.png` 128×128) as `client/model/WolfAltarModel` + `client/renderer/WolfAltarRenderer` (a `BlockEntityRenderer` mirroring `StatueGoddessRenderer`, reproducing the source per-subtree scaling — wolfman ×0.7, each flanking wolf ×0.5, plinth ×1). `WolfAltarBlock` refactored to a `BaseEntityBlock` (FACING + INVISIBLE render shape + `WolfAltarBlockEntity`); registered the BE + model layer + renderer. (3) **Creative tab:** added Wolf Altar + Tongue of Dog. Build + full test green. **Visual QA pending** (statue geometry/UV is an unverifiable-without-client port); the inventory icon stays the placeholder cube (no special item renderer yet).

Current local delta (2026-06-15): **Lycanthropy questline — Phase 4: Wolftrap curse-spread + Book.** Completed the entity-backed Wolftrap (source `BlockBeartrap` silvered branch). `BeartrapBlockEntity.trySummonBaitWerewolf` (was a stub) now spawns a persistent `WolfmanEntity` 16-32 blocks from a baited trap (leashed sheep within 8 + adjacent Wolf Altar + full-moon night) and records its UUID; `tryCaptureWolf` makes that exact lured Wolfman **infectious** when it steps on the trap, so its next villager bite spreads the curse (via the existing `onInfectiousWolfmanDamage`). `BeartrapBlock.entityInside` now springs a silvered trap for either the lured Wolfman (capture → infectious) or a werewolf player; the unused `canTrap` helper was removed. **Book of Shadows** (→ Version 10 / Ninth Edition): Chapter 17 ladder corrected to the implemented questline (gold **ingots** not nuggets, 30 mutton, 10 Tongue of Dog, Horn of the Hunt, airborne-kill/howl-16/tame-6/pigmen-30/take-a-life; per-level abilities aligned to dig-bones/feast/paralysing+pack howls/armour-rend/L10 bite), Wolftrap callout rewritten for the live capture, cover changelog updated. Build + full test green. **With this the Lycanthropy roadmap (Phases 1-4) is complete.** **Deferred (visual/QA):** the four-legged wolf client model (form drives the wolfman render layer only), the hunter-armour wolf-protection gate, and a Witches' Primer entry (datagen).

Current local delta (2026-06-15): **Lycanthropy questline — Phase 3b: per-form combat abilities.** Ported the source `Shapeshift` per-level/form powers. **Howl** (`HowlPayload`) is now form-gated: a **wolfman (L7+)** howl paralyses nearby non-were/vampire creatures (`PARALYSED` III, 4+rand(level-6)s), a **wolf (L8+)** howl summons 2+rand(level-7) tamed wolves, and lower levels keep the port's frighten+2-wolf baseline. **L3 wolf** dig-for-bones (`onWolfDig`, `BlockDropsEvent` on dirt, ~60s cooldown via `lastBoneFind` game-time, 1/20 → 1-2 bones). **L4 wolf** kill-feed (`onWolfFeed`, restores 8 hunger on a fresh non-undead kill). **L9 wolfman** armour-rend (`onWolfmanRendArmor`, `LivingDamageEvent.Pre`: damages a random victim armour piece 25% durability, or tears off unbreakable armour). Build + full test green. **Phase 3 done bar the four-legged wolf client model** (form drives only the wolfman render layer for now) and the hunter-armour wolf-protection gate. **Phase 4 next:** entity-backed Wolftrap + Book of Shadows/Primer chapter.

Current local delta (2026-06-15): **Lycanthropy questline — Phase 3a: form selection + form-gating + L10 bite.** Added the wolf/wolfman form layer. `WerewolfShiftUtil` now has `inWolfForm`/`inWolfmanForm`/`inWolfShape`/`canControlTransform` (L2)/`isWolfmanAllowed` (L5)/`cycleForm`; `setTransformed` seeds the wolf shape on transform and clears it on revert. **Moon Charm:** voluntary transform now requires L2 (`canControlTransform`); **sneak**-use while transformed cycles WOLF↔WOLFMAN (L5+, source). The Phase-2 quest counters are now **form-gated** to source: L5/L9 require wolf-shape (animal), L8 requires wolfman, L6 howl + L7 tame require the strict wolf shape. **L10 infectious bite** (`onWerewolfPlayerBite`, source `Shapeshift.processWolfInfection`): a level-10 werewolf in wolf shape biting a target below ¼ health has a 1-in-4 chance to spread the curse — villagers → Were-Villagers, players → werewolves (vampires/existing werewolves immune; no hunter-armour gate yet). Build + full test green. **Phase 3b remaining:** the minor per-form combat abilities (L3 dig-for-bones, L4 kill-heals-hunger, L7 wolfman howl Paralysis, L8 wolf howl-summon refinement, L9 armour-rend) and the four-legged wolf client model. **Phase 4:** Wolftrap + Book/Primer.

Current local delta (2026-06-15): **Lycanthropy questline — Phase 2: quest counters + Horned Huntsman.** Wired the gameplay events that feed the Wolf-Altar counter quests (source `GenericEvents`/`Shapeshift.checkForHowling`), so the whole 1→10 ladder is now climbable. `WerewolfEvents.onWerewolfQuestKill` (LivingDeathEvent): **L4→5** defeating the Horned Huntsman completes the Horn quest; **L5** airborne hostile-mob kill, **L8** zombie-pigman kill, **L9** villager/player kill each tick the counter (all gated on quest STARTED + transformed). `onTameQuestWolf` (EntityInteract): **L7** taming wild adult wolves (1-in-3, source odds). `HowlPayload`: **L6** howling in distinct chunks at night records a chunk + counts (reusing the persisted `addHowledChunk` dedupe). Counters complete on the next altar visit (`WolfAltarBlock.counterQuest`), which levels up. **Phase 2 simplification:** the wolf-vs-wolfman form requirement is reduced to "transformed" until form selection lands in Phase 3 (L8 should require wolfman, L5/L9 wolf-animal form). Build + full test green. **Phase 3 next:** wolf/wolfman form selection (Moon Charm) + per-form combat abilities (dig-for-bones, kill-heals-hunger, howl Paralysis/wolf-summon, armour-rend, L10 infectious bite).

Current local delta (2026-06-15): **Lycanthropy questline — Phase 1: data model + Wolf Altar gates 1→4.** Started the full-parity werewolf roadmap (planned 4 phases). Replaced the flat `wolfMastery` 0-3 placeholder with a nested `WitcheryPlayerData.WerewolfState` (codec slot `werewolf_state`, swapping the old `wolf_mastery` field to stay under the 16-field DFU limit): source `werewolfLevel` 0-10, a `WolfForm` (NONE/WOLF/WOLFMAN), the Wolf-Altar `WolfQuestState` + counter, a persisted distinct **howled-chunk** set (for the L6→7 quest, reusing the vampire `visited_villages` pattern), and howl/bone-find timers. New accessors `getWerewolfLevel`/`increaseWerewolfLevel`/`getWolfForm`/`getWolfQuestState`/`addHowledChunk`…; `setWerewolf(true)` now seeds level 1 (source infection) and `getMaxBloodPower`'s hybrid check reads `level>=2`. Migrated call sites (`CreativeBatWolfTokenItem` cycles 0-10, `/witchery wolfmastery 0-10`, Moon Charm tick, debug/status readouts). **`WolfAltarBlock` rewritten** from a flat mastery boon into the source `BlockStatueWerewolf` questline state machine: L0 not-worthy, L1→2 (3 gold ingots → Moon Charm), L2→3 (30 mutton), L3→4 (10 Tongue of Dog), L4→5 Horn-of-the-Hunt quest start, and the L5→9 counter-quest scaffolding (begin/progress/complete) + spare-Moon-Charm re-craft; full `witchery.werewolf.level{2..10}{begin,progress,complete}`/`notworthy`/`mooncharmcrafted` lang added. Wolf-form buffs now scale Speed/Strength/Jump by level (simplified port of `Shapeshift.getStatBoost`; exact StatBoost arrays = Phase 3). Build + full test green incl. new `WerewolfStateTest`. **Phase 2 next:** the quest-counter increments (kill@5 / howl-16-chunks@6 / tame@7 / pigmen@8 / person@9) + Horned Huntsman defeat → L4→5 complete. **Divergences:** old `wolf_mastery` saves reset to level 0; altar uses gold **ingots** (source) not the port's old nuggets.

Current local delta (2026-06-14): **Vampire progression close-out: Resist Sun + levels 7→8 / 8→9 gates.** Brought the player-vampire sunlight + late-level quests to 1.7.10 parity (source `GenericEvents.onPlayerTick`/`EntityInteract`). **Resist Sun:** a level-5+ vampire with blood power now truly endures daylight, bleeding off 60 power per interval under Weakness IV + Slowness I + Mining Fatigue I (was Weakness + Hunger); a fledgling (<5) still loses all power, and a starved vampire (0 power, >20s in sun) now **dies outright** (source `instantDeath`) instead of taking light fire damage, with `setOnFire` raised 2s→5s. The flat `checkSunburn` damage tick is now skipped while Resist Sun is active so the ability is coherent across both paths (`resistsSun` helper). **Level 7→8:** stand in **3 distinct villages** (source village-chunk quest) — uses `ServerLevel.isVillage` with a transient per-session 64-block de-dupe cell (divergence: the distinct-village set is not persisted across relog; the count is). **Level 8→9:** bite a villager held in an **iron-bar cage** 5× while its blood sits in the 250-280 window — new `util/VampireCage` ports source `villagerIsInCage`/`isCaged` (≥15/16 ring bars over two layers + a solid 3×3 roof) behind a `BlockQuery` so the geometry is unit-tested (`VampireCageTest`). 9→10 (Glass Goblet) was already done. Build + full test green; world-dependent (manual QA).

Current local delta (2026-06-14): **Vampire close-out follow-ups: maxBloodPower hybrid, persisted village quest, cure rite.** Closed the three items left open above. (1) `WitcheryPlayerData.getMaxBloodPower` now halves the per-level scaling for an established werewolf-hybrid (`wolfMastery >= 2`, the port's analog of source `werewolfLevel >= 2`). (2) The level 7→8 visited-village set is now **persisted** in `VampireState` (codec field `visited_villages`) instead of a transient per-session map, with `addVisitedVillage`/`getVisitedVillageCount`/`clearVisitedVillages` accessors. (3) **Vampire cure rite** ported (`RiteRemoveVampirism`): a Medium Ritual ring with a filled player Taglock Kit + Breath of the Goddess + Silver Sword + Garlic + Diamond + Brew of Love, gated on a coven of 6 and an active cat curse-mastery familiar, drains 10000 altar power and (source odds) cures the bound player 3-in-4, else fizzles harmlessly. New `remove_vampirism` dispatch entry (roster 74→75), `findCureVampirismOfferings`/`CureVampirismOfferings`, lang keys. Build + full test green (roster lock + `VampireCageTest` updated); world-dependent (manual QA).

Current local delta (2026-06-14): **Creature-power close-out: reactive `onDamage` variants + in-field Infernal sacrifice.** Completed the deferred creature-power follow-ups. `InfusionEvents.onIncomingDamage` now ports every source `CreaturePower.onDamage` reaction (Infernal infusion holding the power): **Blaze/Ghast** extinguish a fire hit (3 energy → Fire Res 200t, skip if already lit; Pigman keeps its source 1200t variant), **Skeleton** (5 energy) and **Squid** (1 energy) cancel a drowning blow with Water Breathing + refilled air, and **Creeper** soaks a lightning strike — cancelling the damage and gaining 25 power charges instead (source's charged-creeper easter egg, via `DamageTypeTags.IS_LIGHTNING` rather than the old stack-trace hack). In-field sacrifice (source `InfusionInfernal.trySacrificeCreature`): sneak-left-click the Witch Hand on a mob **you have enslaved** to slay it and absorb (or recharge) its creature power — no altar needed; shares the altar-kill charge tables (`WitcheryPlayerEvents.creaturePowerFor`/`creaturePowerChargesFor`, now public). **Divergence:** the sacrifice itself is free (the preceding enslave already cost 5 energy; source's 1-charge cost is un-payable before you own a power). Build + full test green; world-dependent (manual QA). **With this the creature-power subsystem is complete bar a charge/selection UI (optional, deferred).**

Current local delta (2026-06-14): **Infusion completion — Phase 4: Infernal enslave AI (`PotionEnslaved`).** New `util/Enslaved` ports the source `PotionEnslaved` static helpers (`enslave`/`isEnslavedBy`/`isEnslaved`/`enslaverName`/`canBeEnslaved`), recording enslavement on persistent-data tags rather than a `MobEffect` so it interoperates with the existing `BrewOfLove`/`BrewOfRaising` tags (`WITCEnslaverName` name + port-only `witchery_enslaver_uuid`). New `events/EnslavedMobEvents` drives the AI in place of the source goal: a thrall never targets its enslaver (`onLivingChangeTarget`, source `onLivingSetAttackTarget`) and, when the enslaver is struck, nearby thralls round on the attacker (`onEnslaverHurt` via `LivingIncomingDamageEvent`, source `EntityAIEnslaverHurtByTarget`). `WitchHandItem.infernalCommandOrEnslave` now routes through `Enslaved` (replacing the ad-hoc `WITCHERY_INFUSION_ENSLAVED_OWNER` UUID-only tag) and gates on `canBeEnslaved` (excludes boss/golem/witch + the mod's demon/imp/ent natives). **Scope note:** the Phase-4 "Otherwhere `InfusedSpirit*Effect` family" turned out to be the Fetish conjuration system — already ported as `FetishBlockEntity` — so Phase 4 is reduced to the enslave AI. Build + full test green; world-dependent (manual QA). **Deferred follow-ups:** remaining reactive creature `onDamage` variants (Blaze/Ghast/Skeleton/Squid/Creeper), in-field Infernal sacrifice, charge/selection UI. **With this, the infusion completion roadmap (Phases 1-4) is done.**

Current local delta (2026-06-14): **Overworld earth-metal powers — Milestone 3 / done: shockwave.** New `events/EarthShockwaveEvents` — a transient per-server tick-task (ticked from `LevelTickEvent.Post`) that expands a ring outward each tick dealing 8 damage + radial knockback with a dust ring (source `InfusionOverworld.ShockwaveTask`). **Simplified divergence:** the literal block-moving ripple is replaced by the damage/knockback ring + particles (cosmetic + heavy). Wired into the Overworld release as the long open-ground fallback (non-sneak, channel ≥2s, cost 6×seconds, radius 2×seconds). **The deferred Overworld earth-metal set is now complete** — fling a metal target, disarm, magnetise+smelt, ore→ingot, raise-pillar, thrown rock, shockwave. Build + full test green; world-dependent (manual QA).

Current local delta (2026-06-14): **Overworld earth-metal powers — Milestone 2: thrown rock.** New `entity/EarthRockEntity` — a thrown-item projectile rendered as cobblestone (`ThrownItemRenderer`), 5 damage on impact + stone-break FX — registered in `WitcheryEntities` + `WitcheryClient`. Wired into the Overworld Witch-Hand release: aim at the side of a loose earthen block (`EarthItems.isThrowableRock`) with air in front and release → the block is torn loose and hurled along the look vector (cost 3). Build + full test green; world-dependent (manual QA). **Next:** Milestone 3 = shockwave (tick-task + expanding damage/knockback ring).

Current local delta (2026-06-14): **Overworld earth-metal powers — Milestone 1: EarthItems + metal powers.** New `util/EarthItems` (metal-gear set / ore→ingot map / throwable-rock set; modern divergence: explicit Block→ingot map, no OreDictionary). Overworld Witch-Hand powers now source-faithful (`InfusionOverworld`): left-click flings a metal-bearing target (sneak = high launch, cost 4; else cost 2); sneak-channel magnetises loose metal items + smelts ores in a 13×7×13 box to stone+ingot (cost 2 each, until energy runs out); release on a metal-wielding mob disarms it (cost 2); sneak-release on an ore smelts it to stone + 2 ingots (cost 2); the raise-pillar from Phase 3 is kept. Build + full test green; world-dependent (manual QA). **Next:** Milestone 2 = thrown-rock projectile (`EarthRockEntity`, mirror `ThrownCatEntity`); Milestone 3 = shockwave (tick-task + damage/knockback ring).

Current local delta (2026-06-14): **Infusion completion — Phase 3 (slice 5 / wrap): Overworld raise-pillar; Phase 3 core done.** Added the Overworld Witch-Hand release power (source `InfusionOverworld` block-top branch): aim at a block top and release to **raise a 6-block earth pillar up by 3**, lifting riders; needs solid ground below, costs 2 energy. With this, **all four infusions now have source active powers through the Witch Hand** — Light (barriers/cage/shield), Otherwhere (phase/teleport/recall/recall-drag), Overworld (fall-cushion + raise-pillar), Infernal (minion-command + creature-power activate) — plus creature-power passive hooks (Bat/Jump fall, Pigman fire-res). Phase 3 core complete; build + full test green. **Deferred (follow-up / Phase 4):** Overworld's `EarthItems`-registry powers (metal disarm/throw, ore-smelting, thrown-rock projectile, shockwave — needs a new registry + projectile entity), the remaining reactive creature `onDamage` variants (Blaze/Ghast/Skeleton/Squid/Creeper), the in-field Infernal sacrifice, and the full `PotionEnslaved` enslave + Otherwhere `InfusedSpirit*Effect` family (Phase 4).

Current local delta (2026-06-14): **Infusion completion — Phase 3 (slice 4): creature-power passive hooks.** Source `CreaturePower` onFalling/onDamage wired in `InfusionEvents` for an Infernal/beast-infused witch holding a power: **Bat** caps a fall at 5 blocks, **Jump** negates it (slime squelch), **Pigman** wards a fire hit by spending 3 energy for Fire Resistance (cancels the hit). Acquisition (altar-kill → power + charges) was already done in `WitcheryPlayerEvents`.
