Current local delta (2026-06-14): **Infusion completion — Phase 3 (slice 3): Overworld fall-cushion.** Ported source `InfusionOverworld.onFalling` as a `LivingFallEvent` hook in `InfusionEvents`: an Overworld witch landing hard (>3 blocks) on natural ground (grass/mycelium/podzol/gravel/sand/snow/dirt) spends infusion energy to negate the fall — 5 for a soft landing that kicks the sod loose, or 10 while sneaking for a strength-3 ground-slam explosion. Adds a shared `spendEnergy` helper (creative bypass + client sync). Build + full test green; world-dependent (manual QA). **Phase 3 remaining:** Overworld's earth-shaping active powers (thrown rock, raise column, petrify), Infernal minion/sacrifice refinement, and the creature-power `onActivate`/`onDamage`/`onFalling`/`activateCost` + charge accounting (the latter two also Phase 4 territory with `PotionEnslaved`).

Current local delta (2026-06-14): **Infusion completion — Phase 3 (slice 2): Otherwhere parity.** Source `InfusionOtherwhere` gaps closed on the Witch Hand: sneak-left-click now **drags self + the grasped creature to the stored recall point** (cost 4) instead of always air-hiking; all Otherwhere teleports (air-hike, recall, recall-drag, teleport-to-look) now respect **Ender Inhibition** — an inhibited caster fails, an inhibited target is left behind. Recall set/use, teleport-to-look, and air-hike were already first-pass faithful. Build + full test green; teleports are world-dependent (manual QA). Cross-dim recall-drag uses the modern `teleportTo(ServerLevel,…)` API. **Phase 3 remaining:** Overworld nature powers, Infernal minion/sacrifice refinement, and creature-power `onActivate`/`onDamage`/`onFalling`/`activateCost` + charge accounting.

Current local delta (2026-06-14): **Infusion completion — Phase 3 (slice 1): Light infusion barrier powers.** Reusing the existing Witch-Hand channel/energy framework, the Light placeholders are replaced with source `InfusionLight` behaviour: left-click a creature hoists it up 4 (if a clear column exists) and **cages it in light-barrier** (1×1, 3 tall, floored/roofed) for 5 energy; sneak + quick-release aiming at a surface raises a **3×3 barrier shield wall** for 3 energy; the hold-to-channel light-bend (Invisibility + drop aggro within 20) was already faithful. Barriers use `BarrierBlock.setBarrier` (200t) placed only on air/replaceable cells. Build + full test green; barrier placement is world-dependent (manual QA). **Phase 3 remaining:** Otherwhere phase/teleport/recall refinement, Overworld nature powers, Infernal minion/sacrifice (enslave proper is Phase 4), and the creature-power `onActivate`/`onDamage`/`onFalling`/`activateCost` + charge accounting.

Current local delta (2026-06-14): **Infusion completion — Phase 2: energy-bar HUD.** New client `InfusionEnergyHud` (port of `RenderInfusionEnergyBar`), drawn from `WitcheryClientEvents.onRenderGui` beside `SymbolHud`: a vertical current/max infusion-energy bar tinted per infusion (Light gold / Overworld green / Otherwhere amethyst / Infernal ember), a low-energy pulse toward white, the numeric value, and a second bar for the active creature-power charges (capped at the source 20). Shown only while holding a focus that spends energy (Witch Hand or Mystic Branch). Reads the client-synced `WitcheryPlayerData` (`getInfusionEnergy`/`getInfusionMaxEnergy`/`getCreaturePower*`). Build + full test green; the HUD is client-render so it is manual-QA (`/witchery infuse <type>` grants 200/200, hold the Witch Hand or Mystic Branch, watch the bar). **Next: Phase 3** — the four infusions' active powers through the Witch Hand + full creature-power activate/onDamage/onFalling hooks.

Current local delta (2026-06-14): **Infusion completion — Phase 1: identity passives + FX (start of phased roadmap).** Research finding: 1.7.10 player infusions confer **no passive buffs** (no `onUpdate` on the four infusion classes; only energy + Witch-Hand active powers + creature powers); the port's old passive buffs were invented. By decision they are kept but pared to **one small thematic identity effect each** (Light = Night Vision, Overworld = Regeneration only on natural ground, Otherwhere = Slow Falling, Infernal = Fire Resistance), documented as a deliberate divergence in `FEATURE_MATRIX`/`PORTING_NOTES`. `InfusionEvents` rewritten: per-infusion grant flash (`onInfusionGranted`, fired from `riteOfInfusion` + `/witchery infuse`) and an ambient aura particle on a 12-tick cadence. Build + full test suite green. **Phased roadmap for the rest (approved):** Phase 2 = infusion energy-bar HUD (`RenderInfusionEnergyBar` via `RenderGuiEvent.Post`, mirror `SymbolHud`); Phase 3 = the four infusions' active powers through the Witch Hand + full creature-power hooks; Phase 4 = Infernal `PotionEnslaved` + Otherwhere `InfusedSpirit*Effect` family; broad infusion FX pass woven throughout. Source refs: `infusion/Infusion.java`, `infusion/infusions/Infusion*`, `infusion/infusions/creature/CreaturePower*`, `infusion/infusions/spirit/InfusedSpirit*Effect`, `client/renderer/RenderInfusionEnergyBar.java`, `brewing/potions/PotionEnslaved`, `item/ItemWitchHand.java`.

Current local delta (2026-06-14): **Custom Brew Ingredient Reference in the Primer.** The native Modonomicon Witches' Primer now has a `Brews & Infusions -> Brew Reagents` entry generated from the live `Brews` registry, not a static duplicate table. It groups capacity, modifier, reach/target, dispersal, potion-effect, and action reagents on a custom icon-backed `witchery:item_reference` page, so each row shows the real reagent item stack beside its notes and works in both the normal Primer and placed block book. It covers the blocked-JEI discoverability path in-game. `Brews.ingredientReferences()` exposes the live registry metadata for book/datagen consumers, with `BrewLogicTest` and generated-book coverage checking key effect, gas, explicit-stir, ritual trigger, and item-reference page entries.

Current local delta (2026-06-14): **Dog Tongue ritualized custom-brew delivery parity.** Sneak-use `Tongue of Dog` on a heated, full Witch's Cauldron to release the live cauldron brew via the old `BrewActionRitual` path. It charges the source 250 altar-power trigger cost plus the brew cost, validates no-effect, drink-only, missing triggered-item, and blocked gas/liquid releases before any state is consumed, refunds altar power if final delivery fails late, empties the cauldron only on success, and supports instant block delivery at `3 + extent`, moving `brewgas`/`brewliquid` placement above the cauldron, and triggered dropped-item curses with cat-familiar charge doubling. Focused brew tests cover the trigger, cost, radius, range helper, item-curse charge math, and ritual delivery failure matrix. **Still open:** exact long-pulse choreography, target-modifier/entity-target variants, ritual backfire oddities, old spell-projectile routing, and wider dynamic action roster.

Current local delta (2026-06-13 late): **Full Disease brew + disease-block spread parity.** Rotten Flesh now restores the source `Brew of Disease`: level-4 capacity, 2000 altar power, three-minute `witchery:diseased`, normal strength modifiers, and source immunity gates for undead, demonic, werewolf, boss, golem, and named Witchery boss/minion targets. `Diseased` now applies the capped source attack-damage penalty, ticks on the old 40-tick phase, and has a 1-in-3 chance to leave a `witchery:disease` patch at the infected entity's feet when standing over sturdy support. `witchery:disease` is a no-drop, tinted, non-colliding source-style disease patch that self-decays on the old 1-in-10 removal chance and infects vulnerable living entities for 1-4 minutes on the old 1-in-3 contact chance. Locked by `BrewLogicTest`, `BrewActionMathTest`, and full `gradlew test`. **Still open:** exact ritual pulse/backfire/target-modifier parity, wider dynamic action roster, and visual/in-game QA edge cases.

Current local delta (2026-06-13 late): **Custom Brew Cure/Ritual cleanup pass.** Purified Milk now has the old source disease exception on its normal `Cure Debuffs` route: strength 2+ clears `witchery:diseased` even though that source effect is incurable, and strength 3+ scans a 9-block cube for Witchery disease blocks to remove. A minimal `witchery:diseased` marker effect is registered so this branch has a real source ID to target. Ritual `Cure Debuffs` backfire math is now pinned in reusable helpers: harmful effects are eligible, reapply chance is 1% below modified strength, 25% at strength, 75% above strength, and a backfire restores the effect one amplifier higher. Locked by `BrewLogicTest` and `BrewActionMathTest`; focused compile/test green. **Still open:** exact old ritualized pulse choreography wiring and any non-Diseased source `setIncurable()` cure hooks.

Current local delta (2026-06-13 late): **Source projectile transport parity for custom brews.** `ThrownBrewEntity` now follows the old `EntityBrew` item-throw constants for supported fixed and composed brews: eye-height minus 0.1, 0.16 block yaw backset, -20 pitch offset, 0.75 velocity, 1.0 inaccuracy, and 0.05 gravity. Hand throws, Brew Bag, Leonard's Urn, and Mystic Branch urn casts share the same launch helper. Composed projectiles now save/load fixed and composed payload state, liquid brews render as liquid bottles in flight, splash delivery receives exact block-hit context, and gas/liquid placement follows source `Coord(mop, impactPosition, true)` semantics: hit-face adjacent block, hit-entity block, or impact-position fallback. Locked by `BrewSplashMathTest`; focused compile/test green. **Remaining custom-brew backlog:** wider dynamic action roster, exact old ritualized pulse choreography, old spell-projectile route, and visual edge cases.

Current local delta (2026-06-13 late): **Gas Brew parity pass.** Source Iron Ore now restores the old `witchery:potion.gasmask` route as a composed-brew effect: level-2 capacity, 100 altar power, 90 seconds, and user-facing `Gas Mask` naming. Moving `brewgas` contact now checks the target for Gas Mask before applying gas potion effects; like 1.7.10 `BlockBrewGas`/`BrewPotionEffect`, it suppresses harmful potion effects only, while beneficial effects and non-potion actions still apply. Gas block entities now persist a per-brew max run cap, so Belladonna/Lapis/Hay lifetime tiers extend moving gas from the base 30 seconds through 60/150/300 seconds instead of every cloud sharing the old base cap; spread, 5-tick cadence, source 1-in-10 contact roll, and 0.25 power / 0.5 duration contact scaling remain unchanged. Gas particle polish now biases toward brew-coloured dust with a lighter Witch-particle accent. Locked by `BrewLogicTest` and `BrewActionMathTest`; focused compile/test green. **Remaining moving-brew backlog:** wider dynamic action roster, exact old ritualized pulse choreography, and visual edge cases.

Current local delta (2026-06-13 late): **Waystone + Player Compass parity finish.** Old `EntityItemWaystone` dropped-item behavior is restored for already-bound stones: a dropped Bound or Blooded Waystone on a small Otherwhere circle now consumes one stone, finds nearby living entities and loose item stacks in the old four-block radius, respects Ender Inhibition, and transposes them either to the stored location or to the live Blooded Waystone target. The source branch leaves the circle intact, unlike dropped blank auto-binding, which still clears chalk after conversion. Player Compass now uses the original 32 `playercompass` frames through a 1.21 range-dispatch item model property; player/right-click and filled player Taglock Kit binding remain intact, unbound compasses keep the current base sprite, and missing/unloaded client targets spin rather than pretending to point. Locked with `PlayerCompassItemTest`; `compileJava processResources test` green.

Current local delta (2026-06-13 evening): **Placed Modonomicon + scrying/Waystone + Apothecary parity rollup.** Today's commits moved the placed Primer block from "pretty book model" to a real live Modonomicon surface: entry-index pages, gating, per-entry page order, back-to-entries center button, text/button-only page-surface clicks, correct forward/back flip direction, hidden text during open/flip animation, attended-only level-5 glow, abandoned glow-off rest state, hidden debug hitbox, tighter text flow, and Primer-matching page item rendering. Crystal Ball scrying now covers Bound Waystones, Blooded Waystones, and filled Taglock Kits with source-style 1200-tick held use, altar-power cost, coordinate camera payloads, hidden `ScryingEyeEntity` location anchors, terrain-aware living-target third-person camera, hidden held item while scrying, and release/timeout/invalid cleanup. Waystone bind/copy/transposition outputs use the source IDs, Ender Dew is exposed in the creative tab, and Apothecary villagers now keep their cauldron jobsite, name/look, and full levelled trade list instead of collapsing into ordinary villagers after reload.

Current local delta (2026-06-20): **Cursed-block family - triggered dispersal's faithful block half (data-driven, no block replacement).** A triggered (Zombie Head) splash that lands on a **button, lever, pressure plate, wooden door, or ladder** records a curse on that block's position; on the next activation/contact the stored brew is dumped on whoever triggered it, and the record is dropped when the charges run out. Re-cursing the same brew stacks a charge (source `updateCurse`); a different brew resets. **Key decision:** unlike 1.7.10 (which swapped the block for a cursed twin - fine when there was one wood type), the port does **not** replace the block. The curse is data in a per-level `CursedBlockData` (`SavedData`, persisted), so the block keeps its exact wood/state (any vanilla or modded wood, stone, etc.), the appearance is automatically correct, "revert" is just forgetting the position, and there is zero registry/asset bloat. Activation is intercepted in `CursedBlockEvents`: buttons/levers/doors via `PlayerInteractEvent.RightClickBlock` (precise activator, doors normalised to the lower half), pressure plates via a `LevelTickEvent.Post` rising-edge POWERED poll, and ladders via contact-entry polling so stacked charges do not drain while someone stands still. Warning-only records use sibling `CursedBlockWarningData` and the same detectors for actionbar tells. Player-authored warning brews are now live too: speak a line near a heated cauldron, add `Writable Book` to bind recent words, add `Zombie Head`, bottle, and throw at a supported block or loose item. The stored text returns in purple actionbar text on trigger, with no block swap. `CursedBlocks.tryCurse`/`tryWarn` check the hit block **and** the block one step along the hit face, since pass-through/thin targets can make the bottle hit the block they're mounted on. The cursed cave well now warns `You feel uneasy.` on its top ladder and seeds the triggered poison curse on a lower ladder. Wired into `ThrownBrewEntity.onHit`'s TRIGGERED branch. QA timesaver: `/witchery curse` gives triggered splash brews. Build + `CursedBlockDataTest`/`CursedBlockWarningDataTest` + full suite green. **Untestable without a client launch - pending in-game QA:** hand-make a warning brew, throw at each target, confirm warning/curse applies on press/step/open/ladder entry and redstone still fires (the block is unchanged), re-curse stacks, the cave-well top ladder warns without poison, and the curse expires after its charges. **Remaining:** in-game QA for Dog Tongue ritual item-cursing and the thrown splash route across edge cases.

Current local delta (2026-06-12): **Placed Modonomicon block-book live pages + Apothecary crash hardening.** The placeable Primer/guide-book block now renders Modonomicon pages directly on the opened book mesh instead of opening a separate screen first: pages are resolved from the same generated book data, left/right page clicks turn pages, the book stays open for nearby players, and page item art now uses the same flat GUI `ItemStackRenderState` path as the normal Primer so logs, machines, stone blocks, and station ingredients match the in-book renderer instead of silhouettes. The Hexerei-style model/animation pass also leaves the closed book centered and flat on the support surface, with the right cover axis mapping documented for future edits. `/witchery goto apothecary` also received a defensive placement pass after another world-load crash report: village element placement catches/logs `Throwable`, and Apothecary shell/decor/foundation/chest/sign/resident phases now log enough breadcrumbs to isolate any remaining crash. Focused compile/test green; next QA is item-heavy block-book pages plus apothecary goto after a client restart.

Current local delta (2026-06-12): **Custom brew fluid polish + altar guide-book focus bonus.** The liquid brew QA issue is now closed: liquid brews no longer replace non-empty vanilla fluids, non-source cells decay like vanilla flowing liquid instead of becoming new full sources, shallow `distance` states render with lower tinted liquid models, and bottle/bucket/cauldron/placed-liquid colour comes from the same `BrewContents.color()`. Thrown custom splash brews now play the glass-break impact sound and use tinted flight/impact particles. Also added a small altar synergy: a placed guide book / Modonomicon block directly on the altar grants +1 recharge scale as a focus bonus, counted once and applied before Pentacle/Infinity multipliers; adding/removing the stored book invalidates the supporting altar immediately. Locked by `BrewFluidFlowTest` + `AltarPowerTest`; focused compile/test green.

Current local delta (2026-06-11): **All recipe cards switched to pre-baked images (definitive offset fix + rite cards use the raw uncropped Circle Magic art).** The live `StationRecipePage` render still mis-sized items because 1.21.11 `renderItem` ignores the pose scale (items always 16px) — and, decisively, the user reloads resources (F3+T) rather than recompiling, so a Java-side renderer fix never took effect for them. So every recipe card (oven/distillery/cauldron/kettle/rite) is now a **static `BookImagePageModel`** pointing at an image baked by `tools/recipe_image.py`: the real station GUI with item sprites composited into its slots at the correct scale, fit-centred in 256×256. Modonomicon scales the whole image uniformly → pixel-aligned at any page size, and it's **resource-only** (no renderer class needed, so an F3+T reload shows it correctly). Rite cards use a new `ritual(name, items)` baker that composites the trigger+offerings (2x) into the **raw, uncropped `GUI_Ritual_Circle_Magic.png`** the user supplied (copied to `tools/recipe_assets/ritual_circle_magic.png`) → `ritual_imprisonment.png`, `ritual_gathered_lives.png`. Book changes: dropped `ovenRecipe/distilleryRecipe/vesselRecipe/ritualRecipe` live builders for one `recipeImage(image,title,caption)` helper (+ `ritualRecipe(...)` now returns a `BookImagePageModel`); all 11 station call sites + both rite cards updated. Trade-off: recipe slots no longer have live hover tooltips, but every ingredient is already named in bold in the caption. `bg_circle.png`/`bg_ritual.png` removed; the live `StationRecipePage*`/`bg_oven|distillery|cauldron.png` machinery is now dead code (left in place, harmless). compile + runData green; pixel-verified oven + Gathered Lives images. **Re-QA in game** — an F3+T reload should now suffice to see correct cards.

Current local delta (2026-06-11): **Recipe-card item offset fixed + rite cards now use the original Circle Magic GUI art.** Root cause of the offset (confirmed by decompiling `GuiGraphics.renderItem` + a pixel sim): in 1.21.11 `renderItem` copies the 2D pose matrix into the `GuiItemRenderState` but the item model is **always drawn at 16px and only honours the pose translation, not its scale** — so the previous `StationRecipePageRenderer` height-downscale (panel→52px) left full-size 16px items overflowing the shrunken slots (most visible on the oven's tight bottom-row slots). Fix: render panels at **native 1:1** (16px items land exactly in 16px slots); only clamp by *width* as a last resort (`scale = min(1, PAGE_WIDTH/bgWidth)`), since all real panels are ≤112w/≤70h and fit. The height-bounded caption (`renderBookTextHolder(...,height)`) still auto-shrinks long captions into the room left below the panel, so the old caption-overflow stays fixed without scaling the panel. Rite cards: replaced the invented `bg_circle.png` with **`bg_ritual.png`** — the user's original `GUI_Ritual_Circle_Magic.png` (FTB "Circle Magic" GUI) downscaled 2x→1x (NEAREST) and cropped to the circle + top row of 3 slots (110×64), so 16px items align in its 16px slots; `ritualRecipe(...)` now lays the trigger+offerings at native slot coords `(47,7)(69,7)(90,7)`. Verified by pixel-sim: oven + Gathered Lives cards both pixel-perfect. compile + runData green; re-QA in game.

Current local delta (2026-06-11): **CRASH FIX — topic guide books NPE'd on right-click.** Right-clicking a topic guide (book_oven/book_herbology/…) crashed the client: `WitcheryBookOpener.openCategoryList` called `BookGuiManager.openCategory(category, …)` directly, but that path assumes the manager's `openBookParentScreen` is already set — it was null, so `BookEntryScreen.<init>` NPE'd (`this.parentScreen.getBook()`). Verified against the decompiled Modonomicon 1.121.0: only `openBook(BookAddress)` creates the `BookParentIndexScreen` and sets `openBookParentScreen` before delegating to `openCategory`. Fix: route `openCategoryList` through `openBook(BookAddress.defaultFor(category))` (the address carries book+category, no entry, so it lands on the chapter's index screen with its default/saved entry — same intended UX, no crash). The Primer path (`openResumingOrFirst` → `openBook(BookAddress.of(...))`) was already correct. compile green; re-QA the topic-book open in game.

Current local delta (2026-06-11): **NEW FEATURE — Spectral Coven ("Rite of the Gathered Lives").** Finding **5 of the 12 in-world story books** unlocks a new rite that conjures **5 translucent, footless witches that float in a slow ring around the altar** and **count as coven members** so a solo caster can meet coven-gated rites. Pieces: (1) `CovenMember` marker iface — implemented by `CovenWitchEntity` + new `SpectralWitchEntity`; `RitualExecutor.countNearbyCovenWitches` now counts `CovenMember` (was `CovenWitchEntity` only). (2) `SpectralWitchEntity` (`entity/`, MISC, noSave/noSummon, invulnerable, `noPhysics`): no AI — orbits its bound altar anchor (r2.4, ~9s/lap, vertical bob), faces inward, decays after `DEFAULT_LIFETIME` 6000t with a witch-particle poof. (3) `SpectralWitchRenderer` — vanilla `WitchModel` on the coven-witch skins, `RenderTypes.entityTranslucent` + ghostly `getModelTint` `0x99CCDDFF`, **walkAnimationPos/Speed zeroed in extractRenderState** (the "no feet"). (4) Tracking: each of the 12 loot books tagged with hidden `set_custom_data {witchery_story:N}` (1-12); `WitcheryAttachments.STORIES_FOUND` (int bitmask, copyOnDeath) + `GATHERED_LIVES_UNLOCKED` flag; `WitcheryPlayerEvents.tickStoryBookCollection` scans the inventory every 40t until unlocked, records distinct ids, and at 5 grants advancement `witchery:guide/gathered_lives` (impossible-trigger, code-granted) + sets the flag. (5) Rite `gathered_lives` — MEDIUM Otherwhere ring, trigger **Mellifluous Hunger** + offerings **Mandrake Root** + **Wormwood**, 2000 power; gated in-rite on the unlock flag, refuses to stack a 2nd coven; roster test 71→72 (+ match assertion). (6) Gated Primer entry `circle_magic/gathered_lives` ("The Spectral Coven", last in the Circle Magic & Rites chapter, sort 17 before the What-Comes-Next closer) — the short-story lore reward + a `ritualRecipe("9×9","Otherwhere","2000",0, hunger/mandrake/wormwood)` card, hidden behind the advancement (and the Circle Magic chapter gate). Note: a player can find 5 story books before unlocking Circle Magic, so the entry stays hidden until that chapter is also unlocked — the rite itself is still castable once known. (7) QA timesavers: `/witchery coven unlock` (sets flag + grants adv) and `/witchery coven summon` (spawns 5 around you). compile + runData + RitualDefinitionTest green. **Untestable without a client launch — pending in-game QA:** (a) the translucent render + ghostly tint look, frozen legs, and the orbit/bob motion; (b) the `set_custom_data {witchery_story:N}` SNBT form actually parses in the loot table and the inventory scan records ids (watch for the unlock toast at 5); (c) the gated Primer entry appears only after unlock with the recipe card laid out right; (d) summoned spectral witches actually satisfy a coven-gated rite (e.g. Beastial Call needs 3). Use the two debug commands to shortcut (a)/(d).

Current local delta (2026-06-11): **Guidebook open behaviour.** Was: topic books (book_oven, book_herbology, …) jumped to one hard-coded entry at page 0 every time, and the Primer opened the index/last-screen. Now: the **Primer resumes the last page read, or opens the first entry (`getting_started/first_steps`) page 1 on first use** — via `BookAddress.of(PRIMER, getting_started, first_steps, 0)`, whose three `ignoreSaved` flags are all false (confirmed in bytecode), so the saved category/entry/page wins when present and the addressed entry is only the first-time fallback. **Topic books open straight to their chapter's entries list** — new `WitcheryBookOpener.openCategoryList()` resolves the `BookCategory` via `BookDataManager.get().getBook(id).getCategory(id)` and calls `BookGuiManager.openCategory(category, defaultFor(category))`. Other books (Cauldron, Observations) keep `defaultFor` (resume/index). `WitchesPrimerItem.use()` branches on categoryId (topic → list) vs PRIMER (resume-or-first) vs else (default). compile green; GUI behaviour — pending in-game QA.

Once the format is approved, roll out cards to all Circle Magic rites (per-rite size/chalk/power/coven/inputs come from the `RITUAL_DEFINITIONS`/rite bodies + the local `wiki_scrape/ftbwiki/` rite pages).

Current local delta (2026-06-11): **Station-recipe book pages — fixed image overflow + caption overflow (was NOT fixed; QA'd from 5 screenshots).** `StationRecipePageRenderer` blitted the GUI panel at native size (60–70px tall) with the full caption below it on one non-paginating page; Modonomicon `PAGE_HEIGHT=155`, so title (y=24) + panel left only ~55–65px for the caption and long ones overflowed the page bottom (panels also sat tight against the page edge). Fix: (1) scale the whole panel composite — background + items — to a max drawn height of 52px via the 2D `Matrix3x2fStack` (`pose().pushMatrix()/scale()`; note 1.21.11 GUI pose is `Matrix3x2f`, not the old `PoseStack` — `translate(x,y)`/`scale(x,y)`, no z), mapping the mouse into pre-scale space so item hover/tooltips still register; (2) render the caption with the **height-bounded `renderBookTextHolder(g, holder, x, y, width, height)`** overload so it auto-shrinks to fit the remaining space (the "reduce font to fit more text" the user asked for, auto-tuned per caption); (3) trimmed the three longest captions (Odour, Whiff — which the chapter reorder had lengthened — and the distillery "Two In, Four Out"). compile + runData green. **Untestable without a client launch — pending in-game QA:** confirm panels fit with room for text, captions no longer overflow, and item tooltips appear at the cursor (the pose-scale mouse mapping is the riskiest part; if tooltips are offset, switch the items back to unscaled native render). Note: Modonomicon has no book-wide text-scale knob (only `withFont`); a denser whole-book font would need a custom/TTF font — open question for the user.

Current local delta (2026-06-11): **Primer chapter reorder — Collecting Fumes (oven) now precedes Herbology.** Herbology's Mutandis (its gate item) needs Wood Ash (oven byproduct, no non-oven recipe — verified) on its crafting path and Exhale of the Horned One (oven) on its cauldron path, but the oven chapter sat *after* Herbology — so the Primer taught Mutandis before the oven that makes its reagent (same class of gating bug as the 2026-06-05 oven↔infusions reorder, missed for Herbology). Fixed by swapping the two chapters: chain is now Getting Started → **Collecting Fumes → Herbology** → Brews & Infusions → Distilling → … Everything the oven chapter needs early is vanilla (oven = iron+netherbrick, Wood Ash = any log, Exhale = oak sapling, Breath = birch); Hawthorn (Odour) and Rowan (Whiff) saplings still come from Herbology, so those two fumes now carry a "waits for the next chapter" note. Changed: category `sort_number`s (collecting_fumes 2→1, herbology 1→2), the three `whatsNext` targets/captions (getting_started→oven, collecting_fumes→herbology, herbology→infusions), the intro chapter list, and the recipe-unlock advancement rewards (`guide/{primer,oven,herbology}.json`: primer→book_oven, oven→book_herbology, herbology→book_infusions). compile + runData green; generated book JSON + display order + advancement chain all verified consistent.

Current local delta (2026-06-10): **Redstone Soup icon re-centered.** Its art sat at rows 5-14 (low) and an earlier `display.gui.translation [0,4,0]` hack overcorrected it too high in the slot. Fixed properly: shifted the `ingredient.redstone_soup.png` art to be centered in the 16x16 (content now rows 3-12, cols 3-11, via alpha-thresholded whole-image offset so faint AA pixels don't skew it) and removed the gui-translation override from `models/item/redstone_soup.json` (plain `item/generated` now). Texture/model resource only.

Current local delta (2026-06-10): **Wolfsbane crop "green cross" fixed** — its 8 `wolfsbane_crop_stage*.json` models were missing `"render_type":"minecraft:cutout"` (every other cross-crop model has it) and carried a UTF-8 BOM, so the `block/cross` plant drew with the solid render type and the transparent background filled in opaque. Rewrote all 8 to the belladonna format (cutout, no BOM); textures + age wiring were already correct (authentic 1.7.10 art). Audited all cross-crop models — wolfsbane was the only offender. Also removed a stray, never-packaged `assets/minecraft/models/block/oak_fence_gate.json` (a no-op vanilla override at the repo root, outside any resource root). Block-resource only; in-game QA to confirm.

Current local delta (2026-06-10): **Sky's Wrath focus items corrected to swords** (was a mis-port). Source `RiteWeatherCallStorm` + Resurrected `sky_wrath`/`large_sky_wrath`/`portable_sky_wrath` JSON both use wooden/stone/iron sword (any damage) + Wood Ash — base 1000 power radius 0-3/8 bolts, large 2000 power 3-7/18, portable 0 power + Charged Attuned Stone, optional Bound Waystone to relocate. The 1.7.10 obfuscated ids decode as swords (`field_151040_l` = iron_sword, confirmed via goblin blacksmith). The port had Snowball/Water Bucket/Gunpowder; since every variant also requires Wood Ash, the user's water-bucket+wood-ash cast worked but a lone snowball did nothing. The project's `Book_of_Shadows.html` already listed the swords — only the code diverged. Fixed `storm_base/large/portable` triggers + `findStormWeatherFocus`, added swords to the small-ritual trigger preference, updated storm support lang + Primer entry; `runData` regen. Build + 134 tests green. Manual-QA: drop a Wooden Sword + Wood Ash on a small ritual circle → inner storm; Stone Sword + Wood Ash → outer; Iron Sword + Wood Ash + Charged Attuned Stone → portable.

Current local delta (2026-06-10): **Ritual sacrifices now consume one item at a time** (one per 20 ticks with an EXPLODE particle + pop sound), matching source `SacrificeItem.StepSacrificeItem` (one step per item, each acting only on `ticks % 20 == 0`), instead of every offering vanishing at once. New transient per-dimension stager `SacrificeConsumer` that the consumption choke points route through (`RitualExecutor.consumeOfferedItem`, `RitualOffering.consumeOne`, and the `RitualEvents` dropped trigger); ticked in `RitualEvents.onLevelTick`; flushed on server stop (`cancelAllWithRefund`). While pending, items are non-pickable and skipped by both offering finders + the trigger scan, so a rite can't re-detect its own un-popped offerings and double-fire. The rite effect still applies immediately (the port's rites are atomic, not source's step machine); only the removal is staggered. No refund dup (ongoing-rite refunds store a copy at queue time; the original still pops). Build + 134 tests green; the stagger is world-dependent (manual-QA). See PORTING_NOTES 2026-06-10. Manual-QA: drop a multi-item rite's offerings + trigger, activate, watch them pop one per second with the pickup-pop sound; confirm offerings still all get consumed and the effect fires.

Current local delta (2026-06-10): **Rite of Imprisonment now actually holds mobs** — fixed by cadence, after correcting a rite mis-identification. Source `setUnlocalizedName` chaining shows Imprisonment = `RiteProtectionCircleAttractive(4, 0.8F, 0)` (every-tick inward pull), NOT the Barrier cylinder (that's the separate `witchery.rite.barrier`); Repulsive = the port's Sanctity. (A first attempt wrongly ported Imprisonment as a barrier; reverted.) The port's `pullProtectionMob` (0.22 toward-centre pull, boundary-gated) was already faithful — the only bug was `pulseInterval=20`, so the pull fired once a second while mobs moved freely the other 19 ticks. Source's `RiteProtectionCircle` upkeep ticks `update()` every tick. Fixed: `pulseInterval=1` (radius 4, upkeep 0.8 to match source); dropped the non-source Slowness. Build + 134 tests green; containment is manual-QA. **Sanctity** (source `RiteProtectionCircleRepulsive(4, 0.8F, 0)` → `witchery.rite.protection`, the every-tick outward push) had the identical `pulseInterval=20` bug and was fixed the same way (→ 1) in the same pass; its `pushProtectionMob` already matched source `repulse()`. Lesson (PORTING_NOTES 2026-06-10): `RiteProtectionCircle` subclasses all run `update()` every tick — parity is about per-tick cadence.

Current local delta (2026-06-10): Hardened ritual trigger selection against an offering/trigger collision that misfired **Banish Demon** on a forgotten-Wither-Rose **Blight** setup. With no Wither Rose, `RitualEvents.selectIngredientSource` fell through to nearest-item-of-any-type and grabbed Blight's Charged Attuned Stone offering, which is the `banish_demon_charged` trigger on a small ritual ring → confusing "needs blaze powder + blank waystone" message. Fix: when a Blight circle is armed (medium-infernal ring + full offering set) but no Wither Rose is present, exclude Blight's own offering items (`isBlightOfferingItem`) from the nearest-item trigger fallback → no ingredient → `NO_MATCH` → clean `blight.trigger` hint. Correct Blight setup verified vs source `RiteBlight`/`Circle(0,0,28)`: one medium 11×11 ring entirely in Infernal chalk, golden centre, 3000 power, the 7 offerings (Charged Attuned Stone, Redstone Soup, Reek of Misfortune, fermented spider eye, poisonous potato, rotten flesh, diamond), plus a Wither Rose as trigger. Build + 134 tests green; selection logic is world-dependent (manual-QA). Generalizes the Creaking-Heart lesson (PORTING_NOTES 2026-06-10): items that serve as one rite's *offering* must not be auto-selectable as another rite's *trigger*.

Current local delta (2026-06-10): Restored source's **re-click toggle-cancel** for ongoing rites and clarified ritual concurrency. Source (`BlockCircle.TileEntityCircle`) toggles a circle OFF when you right-click it while a rite runs (`deactivate` → `ABORTED_REFUND`); concurrency is per-circle (separate circles run independently). The port already scoped active rites per-`center` (concurrency across circles worked), but *refused* re-activation ("already at its work") with no way to stop an ongoing rite short of breaking the chalk — so with a strong altar an upkeep rite (Imprisonment/Sanctity/Protection) never ended. Fix: `RitualExecutor.cancelActiveRitualsAt(level, center)` (refunds sacrifices like source `ABORTED_REFUND`), wired into `RitualEvents.onRightClickBlock` **gated on `NO_MATCH`** so an empty-hand re-click cancels the running rite (lang `witchery.ritual.cancelled`) while a valid new rite still runs. This lets an **instant** rite (e.g. Summon Imp — spawns immediately, no active-state) be cast on a concentric/second ring *while* Imprisonment keeps running; two *ongoing* rites on one centre are still refused (use separate circles). `already_active` lang updated to point at the toggle + second-circle option. Build + 134 tests green. See PORTING_NOTES 2026-06-10. **Confirmed for QA:** Imprisonment (small ring) + Summon Imp (large ring, instant) coexist — on separate circles, or concentric small+large at one centre; the imp summon does not disturb the running Imprisonment. Manual-QA: cast Imprisonment, verify it ticks; cast Summon Imp nearby and confirm both; empty-hand right-click the Imprisonment centre and confirm it ends + refunds.

Current local delta (2026-06-10): Clarified the chalk/ritual-activation rules in the Witches' Primer (Book of Shadows guide), Circle Magic chapter, after QA confusion: the **centre activation glyph must be Golden Chalk** (`CIRCLE`, the only block `RitualEvents` accepts on right-click — a white centre does nothing) and the **ring colour gates the rite variant** (white = ritual/common, Otherwhere = boundary rites, Infernal = infernal rites). Also corrected a factual error in the old "Stronger Chalks" prose, which claimed *golden chalk opens demanding rites* — golden is in fact the universal centre glyph for every rite, never a ring variant. Edited the `chalk` and `drawing_circles` entries in `WitchesPrimerBookProvider`, kept the old-witch-to-pupil voice ([[book-of-shadows-tone]]), and regenerated the book JSON via `runData` (`src/generated/resources/.../circle_magic/{chalk,drawing_circles}.json`). The Cauldron Book/Observations are brewing/lore-only, so the circle rule lives in the Primer. Also corrected the same inaccuracy in the standalone **`Book_of_Shadows.html`** reading copy (Ch 9 Golden Chalk + Ch 9/10 "Drawing a Circle"/"Drawing and Activating"), which had claimed "simpler rites work with regular chalk throughout" / golden only "for demanding rites" — now every rite needs the golden centre. (Note: a second stale copy exists at the repo-parent `C:\claude\witchery\Book_of_Shadows.html`; the canonical one is under `witchery-port` and was already out of sync — sync the parent if it's still used.) Fixed the stale `RitualCircleDetector` class javadoc, which wrongly said to "right-click the floor block at the center with the arthana" — activation is right-clicking the golden centre glyph (`RitualEvents.onRightClickBlock`), no arthana. compile + runData green. **Source-parity confirmed (not a divergence):** checked decompiled `witchery-1.7.10-0.24.1` — `BlockCircle` (golden; `getPickBlock` → `CHALK_GOLDEN`) is the only block with `onBlockActivated`/`activateBlock`, while `BlockCircleGlyph` (the ritual/otherwhere/infernal ring) has no activation handler, and `ItemChalk.drawGlyph` only places `Witchery.Blocks.CIRCLE` for golden chalk. So 1.7.10 also requires a golden centre for *every* rite; the HTML's old "simpler rites need no golden" wording was simply incorrect, and the port + the corrected books are faithful.

Current local delta (2026-06-10): Fixed the new **Creaking familiar** rite (`bind_creaking_familiar`, Rite of the Creakening) not activating in-game. Root cause was trigger selection, not the rite effect: `RitualEvents.selectIngredientSource` picks the nearest dropped item of any type as the rite ingredient on a MEDIUM ritual ring, and the Creaking Heart trigger is not one of the rite's own offerings (unlike `bind_familiar`, whose Tear/Odour triggers double as offerings), so a nearer offering was mis-selected and the rite fell through silently to "unknown ritual". Fix: `selectIngredientSource` now prefers a dropped Creaking Heart in the MEDIUM ritual-ring branch. Reusable rule recorded in PORTING_NOTES 2026-06-10 (a rite whose trigger isn't one of its own offerings must be preferred explicitly in `selectIngredientSource`). The rite binds a wild vanilla `Creaking` (not a `TamableAnimal`) via a parallel familiar implementation (`CreakingFamiliarData`/`Helper`/`Events`, `CREAKING_FAMILIAR` attachment, `FamiliarHelper` widened to `LivingEntity`); `/witchery familiar creaking`, familiar type id 5. Build + 134 tests green. **Open manual-QA:** in-game bind/follow/defend/sit/dismiss feel, the freeze-when-watched behavior, and resin-drop cadence; selection logic is world-dependent and not headless-testable. **Note:** off-roadmap Pale Garden hook (handoff lists Creaking/Pale Garden as carefully-scoped, not approved) — confirm scope before building further on it.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Snow Trail snow placement rules were tightened against `PotionSnowTrail`. Footprints now route through a single source gate: target block must be air, the source temperature must be below `1.6F`, and vanilla snow-layer survivability must pass. The temperature path now uses NeoForge-modified biome temperature plus a locally recreated vanilla-style `PerlinSimplexNoise` sample with the old seed `1234L`, old `x / 8, z / 8` sampling, and old `y > 64`, `noise * 4`, `/ 30` height-adjustment formula. Snow covering bursts from Snow Golems and Snowball block hits now expose the old `i = 1..7` vertical search limit and use the source support gate of opaque-cube or ice-material support, with modern snow survivability kept as a final safety check. Focused JVM tests cover the Snowball mapping, duration, strength radius, footprint offsets, temperature threshold/height adjustment, deterministic source noise scaling, footprint gate, search limit, support gate, and Snow Golem constants.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Coal / Extinguish, Stone / Dissipate, Dandelion / Grow Flowers, and Bone Meal / Fertilization are restored for the supported composed-brew action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps all four to effect level 1; Coal and Stone cost 0 altar power, Dandelion costs 200, and Bone Meal costs 250. The port maps them through level-1 capacity, uses explicit-stir registration so dropped Coal/Stone/Dandelion/Bone Meal remain available to current rite and cauldron-offering paths, restores Extinguish entity fire clearing plus Blaze damage and source Nether strength gate, tags composed/fixed gas clouds as Witchery brew-gas stand-ins so Dissipate can remove them, keeps the old powered non-Witchery gaseous-fluid branch blocked until those block types are ported, and restores the source filled-circle block-hit branches for fire removal, flower growth, and bonemeal-style fertilization. JVM brew tests cover mapping, capacity, explicit-stir protection, altar costs, Nether gate, damage/gas constants, old flower variant count, circle radius scaling, and fertilization application count.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Snow Trail and Chilled block-hit composed-brew branches are restored for the supported instant/splash path. Direct 1.7.10 Snowball calls `PotionSnowTrail.createSnowCovering` at radius `2 + 2 * strength`, and Icy Needle checks the impact position then runs `BlockActionReplaceSphere` over connected water-material blocks at the same radius. The port now passes an explicit block-hit signal from thrown composed brews, applies Snow Trail's source snow-covering burst only on real block hits, freezes connected vanilla water to Ice for Icy Needle / Chilled block hits, and keeps entity/gas delivery unchanged. JVM brew tests cover the shared source radius, water-only freeze gate, and source replace-sphere recursion boundary.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Slime Ball / Frog's Tongue Pull is restored for the normal supported composed-brew action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps `minecraft:slime_ball` to brew-name `witchery:brew.frogtongue`, 150 altar power, effect level 2, and old display name `Frog's Tongue`; the cauldron book presents the page as `Pull` and explains that unpowered Pull targets the impact location while powered Pull targets the thrower. The port maps modern `minecraft:slime_ball` through level-2 capacity, applies source horizontal motion `delta * 0.15`, fixed `0.4` upward motion, and one second of Jump Boost II to pulled living targets, skips Ender Dragon / Horned Huntsman / occupied target positions, and adds the source block-hit non-living pull branch for dropped items/projectiles within the instant radius. Existing fixed Brew of Frog's Tongue item behavior is unchanged. JVM brew tests cover mapping, capacity, altar cost, strength targeting gate, FakePlayer fallback, and source motion math.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Breath of the Goddess / Air Hike is restored for the normal supported composed-brew entity action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps old `itemBreathOfTheGoddess` (ingredient metadata 32) to brew-name `witchery:brew.airhike`, 750 altar power, effect level 2, and old display name `Air Hike`; the cauldron book says it throws hit creatures into the air. The port maps modern `witchery:breath_of_goddess` through level-2 capacity, clears fall distance, and applies the source vertical launch formula `0.6 + 0.2 * strength` while preserving existing Breath of the Goddess recipe/rite uses. The old source used a player velocity packet and direct non-player Y motion; the NeoForge port sets server-side delta movement for all living targets and marks the entity for sync. JVM brew tests cover mapping, capacity, altar cost, strength carry-through, and the motion formula.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Silver Deposits / Harm Werewolves is restored for the normal supported composed-brew entity action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps `itemSilverDust` (old display `Silver Deposits`) to brew-name `witchery:brew.harmwerewolves`, 1000 altar power, effect level 4, and the old display name `Harm Werewolves`; the cauldron book presents it as a level-4 brew and notes the ritual rain branch. The port maps modern `witchery:silver_deposits` through level-4 capacity, deals source `ceil((3 << cappedStrength) * splashPower)` damage to source-werewolf targets and `ceil((1 << cappedStrength) * splashPower)` to other living targets, keeps the ordinary strength cap at 1 unless Tropical Fish opens it to 3, and threads instant-splash falloff into action delivery. Source-werewolf matching is limited to represented 1.7.10 behavior: Wolfman entities and actively transformed werewolf players; unshifted werewolf players and Were Villagers do not take the strong branch because source calls `CreatureUtil.isWerewolf(target)` with `includeUnshifted=false`. Matching hits use a brew-scoped synthetic silver source so Wolfman's old 1.5x silver cap and the transformed-player silver bonus can observe non-werewolf-caster hits without making unrelated magic damage silver. JVM brew tests cover mapping, level-4 capacity, altar cost, strength ceiling carry-through, damage math, and transformed-player matching. The old ritual rain/thunder branch remains blocked until exact ritual rain/thunder pulse wiring exists.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Creeper Heart / Potion Longevity is restored for the normal supported composed-brew action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps Creeper Heart to brew-name `witchery:brew.durationboost`, 5000 altar power, effect level 6, and the old display name `Potion Longevity`; the old cauldron book presents the recipe as Nether Wart + Tear of the Goddess + Diamond Vapour + Creeper Heart. The port maps modern `witchery:creeper_heart` through level-6 capacity. On a non-Queasy target with active effects, it clears all current effects, reapplies non-instant effects with duration increased by `min(remainingTicks, ceil(3 minutes * splashPower))`, and then adds Queasy for `3 * max(1, 4 - strength)` minutes. On a target already affected by Queasy, it follows the old backfire branch: one-in-three Hunger for 2 minutes, otherwise Nausea for 5 minutes. JVM brew tests cover mapping, level-6 capacity, altar cost, strength carry-through, duration math, Queasy duration math, and backfire durations.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Skeleton Skull / Steal Buffs is restored for the normal supported composed-brew action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps `Items.skull` metadata 0 to brew-name `witchery:brew.stealbuffs`, 100 altar power, effect level 6, and the old display name `Steal Buffs`; the old cauldron book confirms the modern route as Nether Wart + Tear of the Goddess + Diamond Vapour + Skeleton Skull. The port maps modern `minecraft:skeleton_skull` through level-6 capacity, scans nearby living entities within `(strength + 1) * 8` blocks of the affected target, and steals up to `ceil((strength + 1) * 2 * splashPower)` active positive curable effects whose amplifier is at or below brew strength. Matching existing effects on the target are only replaced when the donor effect is at least as long and at least as strong, just like source. JVM brew tests cover mapping, level-6 capacity, altar cost, strength scaling, source radius/max-buff math, curable/positive gates, and the replacement rule.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Ice / Demonbane is restored for the normal supported composed-brew direct damage action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps `Blocks.ice` to brew-name `witchery:brew.harmdemons`, 500 altar power, effect level 5, and the old display name `Demonbane`. The port maps modern `minecraft:ice` through level-5 capacity, applies the source Demonbane damage table of `ceil((10 << cappedStrength) * splashPower)` to source demonic targets and `ceil((3 << cappedStrength) * splashPower)` to non-demonic targets, keeps the ordinary strength cap at 1 unless Tropical Fish disables it up to 3, and threads the existing instant-splash falloff into action delivery. Demonic matching is limited to the old source set represented in this port: Demon, Ghast, Blaze, Magma Cube, Leonard, Lord of Torment, Flame Imp, Lilith, and Wither; Hellhounds are deliberately not included because the 1.7.10 source did not list them. Leonard uses the source special weakness route: Demonbane only hurts him below 40% health, bypasses the current Lost Soul normal-damage gate like old `super.attackEntityFrom`, uses magic damage, and caps the hit at 15 because the port does not yet have the old large Resizing effect branch. JVM brew tests cover mapping, level-5 capacity, altar cost, strength ceiling carry-through, and Leonard's source weakness gate/cap.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Garlic / Weaken Vampires is restored for the normal supported composed-brew action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps `SEEDS_GARLIC` to brew-name `witchery:brew.weakenvampires`, 500 altar power, effect level 4, and the old display name `Weaken Vampires`. The port maps the modern crop/seed item `witchery:garlic` through level-4 capacity, carries strength, duration, and Gold Nugget ambient/no-particles modifiers into action effects, drains vampire-player Blood Power by `50 + 20 * strength` using the old `exact=false` semantics (any positive blood can be drained and clamps at zero; creative players are not drained), and applies source-style Weakness for 90 seconds modified by duration/splash scaling. Vampire matching is limited to the live port vampire entity plus vampire player attachment rather than inventing third-party class-name heuristics. JVM brew tests cover mapping, capacity, altar cost, modifier carry-through, blood-drain math, and Weakness duration scaling.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Pumpkin / Undeadbane and Blue Orchid / Insectbane are restored for the normal supported composed-brew direct damage action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps Pumpkin to `witchery:brew.harmundead`, Blue Orchid (red-flower metadata 1) to `witchery:brew.harminsects`, 200 altar power, and effect level 2; the old display names are `Undeadbane` and `Insectbane`. The port maps modern `minecraft:pumpkin` and `minecraft:blue_orchid` through level-2 capacity, applies the source damage table of `ceil((10 << cappedStrength) * splashPower)` to matching undead/arthropod targets and `ceil((3 << cappedStrength) * splashPower)` to nonmatching targets, keeps the normal strength cap at 1 unless Tropical Fish disables it up to 3, and threads the same instant-splash falloff into action delivery. Undead matching includes vanilla undead tags plus the port Vampire entity/player attachment; arthropods use modern `EntityTypeTags.ARTHROPOD`. JVM brew tests cover mapping, capacity, altar cost, strength ceiling, and damage math.

Current local delta (2026-06-06, updated 2026-06-13): Source Purified Milk / Cure Debuffs is restored for the normal supported composed-brew action route. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` maps Purified Milk to brew-name `witchery:brew.removedebuffs`, 200 altar power, and effect level 2; the old display name is `Cure Debuffs`. The port maps `witchery:purified_milk` through level-2 capacity as an explicit-stir ingredient so dropped Purified Milk still feeds current kettle recipes, and the action removes harmful curable active effects from the target when their amplifier is at or below the brew strength. The normal source disease exception is also restored: strength 2+ clears the registered `witchery:diseased` marker despite its source incurable role, and strength 3+ scans a source 9-block cube for Witchery disease blocks to remove. JVM brew tests cover ingredient mapping, capacity requirement, altar cost, strength scaling, explicit-stir protection, positive/negative/curable gates, the disease exception, and the cleanup radius. Dog Tongue ritualized cauldron release now covers supported delivery; exact ritual pulse/backfire/target-modifier parity remains future work, and the old ritual `Cure Debuffs` harmful-effect backfire probabilities are pinned in helpers for that wiring.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Reek of Misfortune / Cure Buffs is restored for the supported composed-brew action route. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` maps Reek of Misfortune to brew-name `witchery:brew.removebuffs`, 250 altar power, and effect level 2; the old display name is `Cure Buffs`. The port maps `witchery:reek_of_misfortune` through level-2 capacity as an explicit-stir ingredient so dropped Reek still feeds current kettle recipes, and the action removes positive curable active effects from the target when their amplifier is at or below the brew strength. JVM brew tests cover ingredient mapping, capacity requirement, altar cost, strength scaling, explicit-stir protection, and the positive/negative/curable source gates.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Grotesque / Fear is restored for the currently supported composed-brew entity-effect route. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` maps Infernal Blood to brew-name `witchery:brew.fear`, `Witchery.Potions.GROTESQUE`, 3 minutes, 500 altar power, and effect level 2; the old displayed effect name is `Grotesque`. The port maps `witchery:infernal_blood` through level-2 capacity, registers `witchery:grotesque`, and restores the source mob-only repulsion aura: every five game ticks on phase 3, mobs within 4 blocks are pushed away unless they are demons, bosses, or golems. JVM brew tests cover ingredient mapping, duration, capacity requirement, altar cost, strength modifier path, tick phase, range/exemption gates, and source push vector. The old demon-head render overlay remains renderer backlog, and the existing direct Brew of the Grotesque item behavior is unchanged.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Chilled / Cold is restored for the currently supported composed-brew entity-effect route. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` maps Icy Needle to brew-name `witchery:brew.cold`, `Witchery.Potions.CHILLED`, 3 minutes, 200 altar power, and effect level 2; the old displayed effect name is `Chilled`. The port maps modern `witchery:icy_needle` through level-2 capacity, registers `witchery:chilled`, applies the source operation-2 movement slow, reduces fire damage with the old source floor formula, and deals 1 magic damage to Blazes or level-III+ Chilled targets on the old `25 >> amplifier` tick cadence. JVM brew tests cover ingredient mapping, duration, capacity requirement, altar cost, strength modifier path, tick cadence, Blaze/high-strength cold damage gates, fire-damage reduction, and the source block-hit water-to-ice sphere.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Paralysis / Paralysed is restored for the currently supported composed-brew potion-effect route. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` maps Demon Heart to brew-name `witchery:brew.paralysis`, `Witchery.Potions.PARALYSED`, 10 seconds, 750 altar power, and effect level 4; the old display name is `Paralysed`. The port maps modern `witchery:demon_heart` through level-4 capacity and registers `witchery:paralysed`. The source movement lock is restored with a transient per-entity operation-2 movement-speed modifier of `-40.0 * (amplifier + 1)`, the old player gate (players freeze only at amplifier 2+), boss immunity, villager/Village Guard amplifier-5 exception, Creeper fuse reset, vertical pin to `-0.2`, and the high-amplifier hurt break (`amplifier >= 4` and damage at least 1). Swim Boost now checks the real Paralysed effect before applying the old player water boost, and high-amplifier player Paralysed now applies the source 90-second `Queasy` after-effect when it expires naturally. The vampire-directed villager AI branch remains documented until that dependent system exists rather than invented. JVM brew tests cover ingredient mapping, duration, capacity requirement, altar cost, strength modifier path, source movement constants, vertical-pin exception, hurt break gate, and Queasy expiry gates.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Sun Allergy / Undead's Curse is restored for the currently supported composed-brew potion-effect route. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` maps raw fish metadata 1 to brew-name `witchery:brew.allergysun`, `Witchery.Potions.SUN_ALLERGY`, 60 seconds, 1000 altar power, and effect level 6; the old lang display name is `Undead's Curse`. The port maps modern Salmon through level-6 capacity and registers `witchery:sun_allergy`. The source tick branch is restored server-side: every 20 game ticks during daytime, if brightness is above `0.5`, the entity is not in water, the sky is visible, and the random roll is below `brightness - 0.45`, players take 1 fire damage or 2 at amplifier 3+, non-player entities with any helmet avoid burning while damageable helmets take 0-1 durability, and other non-player entities burn for 8 seconds. JVM brew tests cover the ingredient, duration, capacity requirement, altar cost, strength modifier path, daylight predicate, random threshold, and player damage tiers.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Fortune / Luck is restored for the currently supported composed-brew block-drop route. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` maps Clay Ball to brew-name `witchery:potion.fortune`, `Witchery.Potions.FORTUNE`, 3 minutes, 1000 altar power, and effect level 6; the old lang display name is `Luck`. The source `PotionFortune` hook recomputes harvest drops only when the block has no tile entity, Silk Touch is not active, and the normal drop list is non-empty, adding `+1` effective Fortune or `+2` when the potion amplifier is above 2. The port registers `witchery:fortune`, maps modern Clay Ball through level-6 capacity, and bridges the hook through NeoForge `BlockDropsEvent` by replacing the already-captured drops with a recomputed `Block.getDrops` result using the boosted Fortune level. JVM brew tests cover the ingredient, duration, capacity requirement, altar cost, strength modifier path, and source drop-rule helpers.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Swim Boost is restored for the currently supported composed-brew player movement route. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` maps raw fish metadata 0 to brew-name `witchery:brew.swimming`, `Witchery.Potions.SWIMMING`, 3 minutes, 0 altar power, and effect level 1; the old lang name is `Swim Boost`. The port maps modern Cod through the level-1 capacity route, registers `witchery:swimming`, and adds the old player-side water movement branch: when the local player has Swim Boost, is in water, is not Paralysed, and holds forward, horizontal motion is multiplied by `1.15 + 0.03 * 3 = 1.24` on the client tick. JVM brew tests cover the ingredient, duration, altar cost, strength modifier path, player boost predicate, motion multiplier, and the source non-player tick phase helper. The original source also has a separate client-side water branch for non-player living entities that are both Swimming and Paralysed; that branch remains pending until there is a safe modern server/client delivery shape for non-player composed-brew movement.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Snow Trail is restored for the currently supported composed-brew entity-effect route. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` maps Snowball to brew-name `witchery:brew.snow`, `Witchery.Potions.SNOW_TRAIL`, 3 minutes, 0 altar power, and effect level 1; the same source action also has a block-hit snow-covering burst with radius `2 + 2 * strength`. The port now registers `witchery:snow_trail`, maps modern Snowball through the level-1 capacity route, applies the old 10-tick snow-footprint behavior, restores source-style Snow Golem overload, and runs the source block-hit snow-covering burst only on real splash block hits. JVM brew tests cover the ingredient, duration, altar cost, strength modifier path, source constants, and block-hit radius.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source effect-level capacity gating is restored for composed custom-brew potion effects and the existing Heart of Gold action route. Direct 1.7.10 `EffectLevelCounter` comparison showed capacity modifiers raise an available level pool and each potion/action effect consumes its source `EffectLevel` before it can stir or apply. The port now gives supported potion-effect ingredients their source levels (`Ender Dew` / `Wheat` / `Wolfsbane` level 1, common potion effects level 2, Wither / apple buffs / Ink Sac Blindness / Stout Belly / Demon Heart Paralysis level 4), refuses under-capacity stir attempts, and resolves under-capacity stored lists as inert/mundane. Source capacity still uses the old no-clamp increments, so Mandrake Root opens level 1, Nether Wart opens level 2, Nether Wart + Tear of the Goddess opens level 4, and higher capacity items retain their existing source thresholds. Capacity ingredients are now part of the normal source brew shape, so older same-day "brew X alone" QA snippets below should be read through this capacity route until rotated. JVM brew tests cover add-time refusal, inert under-capacity effects, used/remaining capacity, and the full updated potion/action brew roster.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Ink Sac Blindness is restored in the currently supported composed-brew potion-effect path. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` maps `Dye.INK_SAC` to brew-name `witchery:brew.blindness`, vanilla Blindness for 15 seconds, 1000 altar power, and source effect level 4. The port now maps modern `minecraft:ink_sac` to a 15-second Blindness brew effect at 1000 altar power with the normal source strength/duration modifier path. This does not change the existing fixed `brew_of_ink` / `ink_brew` direct item behavior or Ink Sac ritual uses. JVM brew tests cover the ingredient, duration, altar cost, and Glowstone strength scaling. Fresh QA now needs the restored capacity route: sneak-stir Nether Wart + Tear of the Goddess, then add Ink Sac and expect Blindness for 15 seconds; add Glowstone Dust before Ink Sac and expect Blindness II for 15 seconds. Broader old effect-level capacity gating for the supported potion/action route is now restored in the newer capacity-gating delta above; remaining custom-brew backlog is the wider action/container/liquid/targeting architecture.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Wolfsbane is restored as a composed-brew potion marker. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` maps Wolfsbane to `Witchery.Potions.WOLFSBANE` for 60 seconds at 0 altar power; the old potion class is plain `PotionBase`, with meaningful behavior coming from werewolf/wolfman checks that look for the marker. The port now registers `witchery:wolfsbane`, maps `witchery:wolfsbane_flower` to that 60-second effect, lets the existing player werewolf suppression path accept either the current inventory flower mechanic or the source potion marker, and replaces the Wolfman non-full-moon reversion placeholder check from Poison to Wolfsbane. JVM brew tests cover the ingredient, duration, altar cost, and effect registration. Fresh QA: brew Wolfsbane Flower and expect Wolfsbane for 60 seconds. A werewolf player with the effect should be forced/restored out of wolf form just like the existing carried flower behavior, without losing the carried-flower shortcut. A source-converted Wolfman carrying the effect should not revert to Were Villager until the effect expires and the normal 100-tick recheck runs.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Three source vanilla-effect brew ingredients are restored in composed custom brews. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` maps old Skeleton Skull metadata 1 to Wither for 15 seconds at 200 altar power, Golden Apple metadata 0 to Absorption for 30 seconds at 1000 altar power, and Golden Apple metadata 1 to Health Boost for 2 minutes at 1000 altar power. The port maps those old metadata routes to modern Wither Skeleton Skull, Golden Apple, and Enchanted Golden Apple respectively, using the normal source strength/duration modifier path. JVM brew tests cover all three ingredient mappings, durations, altar costs, and strength scaling. Fresh QA: brew a Wither Skeleton Skull and expect Wither for 15 seconds; brew a Golden Apple and expect Absorption for 30 seconds; brew an Enchanted Golden Apple and expect Health Boost for 2 minutes. Add Glowstone before the skull and expect Wither II for 15 seconds.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Moonshine / Feel No Pain and Stout Belly are restored in composed custom brews as real Witchery mob effects. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` maps Wheat to brew-name `witchery:brew.moonshine`, `Witchery.Potions.FEEL_NO_PAIN`, 90 seconds, 0 altar power, and effect level 1; the old `PotionFeelNoPain` converts source mob/player/living-caused damage against players into hunger loss while food is available, then adds the amplified random Hunger side effect on the old one-second tick phase. Foul Fume now maps to `witchery:stout_belly` for 90 seconds at 1000 altar power; the old Stout Belly tick removes Hunger only when the effect is amplified above level I. JVM brew tests cover Wheat/Foul Fume duration, altar cost, strength scaling, Moonshine food-loss math, Hunger roll timing/duration, and the Stout Belly amplifier gate. Fresh QA: brew Wheat and expect Moonshine for 90 seconds; with food, damage from a mob/player/living source should become food loss instead of heart loss. At Moonshine I, 4 damage costs 8 food; at Moonshine II, 4 damage costs 4 food; at Moonshine III, 4 damage costs 2 food, all capped by current food. With no food, damage should land normally. Brew Foul Fume and expect Stout Belly for 90 seconds at 1000 power; add Glowstone Dust first and Stout Belly II should clear Hunger after about one second. Non-Diseased source `setIncurable()` cure hooks remain future work.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Ender Inhibition is restored in composed custom brews as a real Witchery mob effect. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` maps Ender Dew to `Witchery.Potions.ENDER_INHIBITION` for 90 seconds at 200 altar power; the old `PotionEnderInhibition` cancels the Forge ender-teleport event while active, and its helper blocks checks whose required amplifier is at or below the active effect amplifier. The port now registers `witchery:ender_inhibition`, uses Ender Dew as the composed-brew ingredient, and cancels NeoForge ender-entity and ender-pearl teleports for affected living entities without touching normal command/mirror/village teleports. JVM brew tests cover Ender Dew duration, altar cost, effect registration, and the source amplifier gate. Fresh QA: brew Ender Dew and expect Ender Inhibition for 90 seconds; an affected Enderman/Shulker should fail its normal random teleport, and an affected player should not teleport from a thrown Ender Pearl until the effect expires.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Reflect Projectiles / Attract Projectiles are restored in composed custom brews as real Witchery mob effects. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` and Resurrected `reflectprojectiles.json` both map Cobweb to `Witchery.Potions.REFLECT_PROJECTILES` for 90 seconds at 250 altar power, inverted by Fermented Spider Eye into `Witchery.Potions.ATTRACT_PROJECTILES` for 45 seconds. The old Reflect tick scans projectiles within 2 blocks, skips projectiles owned by the protected entity, and multiplies X/Z motion by `-0.25 * (1 + amplifier)` while preserving the odd source Y-flip condition. The old Attract tick scans `(1 + amplifier) * 3` blocks, waits 1 tick for fast projectiles or 10 ticks for slow ones, then calls the projectile shoot vector toward the target. The port now registers `witchery:reflect_projectiles` and `witchery:attract_projectiles`, uses Cobweb as the composed-brew ingredient, and keeps that source motion math on the modern server-authoritative effect ticks. JVM brew tests cover Cobweb duration, inversion duration, altar costs, reflect radius/factor, attract radius, and fast/slow projectile delay. Fresh QA: brew Cobweb and expect Reflect Projectiles for 90 seconds; incoming arrows/throwables from someone else should reverse/slow when they pass within about 2 blocks, while the protected player's own projectiles should not be reflected. Add Fermented Spider Eye before Cobweb and expect Attract Projectiles for 45 seconds; nearby projectiles should begin homing toward the affected target within about 3 blocks, or 6 blocks with Glowstone Dust.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Poison Weapons is restored in composed custom brews as a real Witchery mob effect. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` and Resurrected `poisonweapons.json` both map Red Mushroom to `Witchery.Potions.POISON_WEAPONS` for 90 seconds at 200 altar power. The old `PotionPoisonWeapons` listens to all living hurt events but only applies when the damage type is source `mob` or `player`, then reads the attacker's Poison Weapons amplifier: level I applies Poison I for 5 seconds, level II applies Poison II for 5 seconds, level III applies Poison II for 15 seconds, and level IV+ applies Wither I for 20 seconds. The port now registers `witchery:poison_weapons`, uses Red Mushroom as the composed-brew ingredient, and applies the same table from the modern melee damage hook. JVM brew tests cover Red Mushroom duration, altar cost, strength scaling, and the source attack-effect table. Fresh QA: brew Red Mushroom and expect Poison Weapons for 90 seconds; while it is active, hit a living target in melee and expect that target to receive Poison I for 5 seconds. Add Glowstone Dust before Red Mushroom and expect melee hits to apply Poison II for 5 seconds. Projectile, magic, thorns, and brew damage should not trigger Poison Weapons.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Floating is restored in composed custom brews as a real Witchery mob effect. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` and Resurrected `floating.json` both map old Reeds / modern Sugar Cane to `Witchery.Potions.FLOATING` for 90 seconds at 250 altar power. The old `PotionFloating` ticks every tick, clears fall distance, scans 3 + amplifier blocks below the entity, sets vertical velocity to `0.25` when any non-air block is in that range, and otherwise drifts at `-0.05` one tick in five or `0.0` on the other ticks. The port now registers `witchery:floating`, uses Sugar Cane as the composed-brew ingredient, and applies the same hover constants through the modern server-authoritative effect tick. JVM brew tests cover Sugar Cane duration, altar cost, strength scaling, hover height, and vertical velocity constants. Fresh QA: brew Sugar Cane and expect Floating for 90 seconds; near the ground the player/mob should hover upward, while over empty air the effect should suppress fall distance and mostly hang level with occasional tiny downward drift. Add Glowstone Dust before Sugar Cane and the hover scan should reach one block farther down.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Repell Attacker is restored in composed custom brews as a real Witchery mob effect. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` maps ordinary Bramble (`Witchery.Blocks.BRAMBLE`, not Void Bramble) to `Witchery.Potions.REPELL_ATTACKER` for 90 seconds at 250 altar power. The old `PotionRepellAttacker` listens for non-projectile living hurt events on the protected entity, ignores self/projectile hits, checks the attacker is within 3 blocks, then shoves the attacker away with horizontal strength `1.0 + amplifier * 0.75` and vertical strength `0.5 + amplifier * 0.2`. The port now registers `witchery:repell_attacker`, uses Bramble as the composed-brew ingredient, adds a NeoForge damage hook with the same source pushback math, displays the source potion name `Repell`, and uses the source Wild Bramble art as the mob-effect icon. JVM brew tests cover Bramble duration, altar cost, strength scaling, source range, and shove scaling. Fresh QA: brew Bramble and expect Repell Attacker for 90 seconds; let a nearby melee mob hit the protected player and expect the attacker to be pushed away. Arrows or other projectile hits should not trigger the shove.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Feather Fall is restored in composed custom brews as a real Witchery mob effect. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` maps Feather to `Witchery.Potions.FEATHER_FALL` for 1 minute at 100 altar power; the old `PotionFeatherFall` tick clamps downward velocity to `-0.2` once fall distance reaches 5/4/3 blocks at strength 0/1/2+ and caps tracked fall distance at 6/5/4/3 blocks at strength 0/1/2/3+. The port now registers `witchery:feather_fall`, uses Feather as the composed-brew ingredient, removes the previous Phantom Membrane slow-falling placeholder from the Witchery brew registry, and reuses the Resurrected mob-effect icon. JVM brew tests cover Feather duration, altar cost, strength scaling, source thresholds, and the Phantom Membrane regression. Fresh QA: brew Feather and expect Feather Fall for 60 seconds; while falling, base strength should slow descent only after about 5 blocks and cap landing damage as if fall distance were at most 6 blocks. Add Glowstone Dust before Feather to make the clamp start at about 4 blocks with a 5-block fall-distance cap.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Jump is restored in composed custom brews. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` comparison and Resurrected `jump.json` both map the old Jump brew to Leather, not Rabbit Foot. The port now registers Leather as the Jump Boost ingredient for 3 minutes at 200 altar power with the normal source strength modifier path, and Rabbit Foot no longer counts as a Witchery custom-brew ingredient. JVM brew tests cover Leather duration, altar cost, strength scaling, and the Rabbit Foot regression. Fresh QA: brew Leather by itself and expect Jump Boost for 3 minutes; add Glowstone Dust before Leather and expect Jump Boost II. Rabbit Foot should not stir into a composed Witchery brew.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Blaze ingredient roles are corrected in composed custom brews. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` plus Resurrected JSON comparison shows Blaze Rod, not Blaze Powder, is the tier-2 strength modifier (`power_2.json`, 100 altar power), while Blaze Powder is the Damage Boost effect ingredient, inverted by Fermented Spider Eye into Weakness, for 200 altar power. The port now uses Blaze Rod for the capped strength tier between Glowstone Dust and Charged Attuned Stone, and explicit-stirred Blaze Powder now resolves to Strength for 3 minutes or Weakness for 90 seconds while dropped Blaze Powder remains available to the Infernal Chalk cauldron upgrade offering. JVM brew tests cover the role swap, Damage Boost/Weakness durations/costs, and Blaze Powder's explicit-stir guard. Fresh QA: brew Sugar with Glowstone Dust + Blaze Rod and expect Speed III; brew sneak-stirred Blaze Powder by itself and expect Strength for 3 minutes, or Fermented Spider Eye + sneak-stirred Blaze Powder for Weakness for 90 seconds. Dropping Blaze Powder into the cauldron should still be available to the Infernal Chalk offering path instead of auto-stirring.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source composed-brew effect modifiers now include the old Gold Nugget and clownfish/Tropical Fish routes for supported potion-effect brews. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` plus Resurrected JSON comparison maps Gold Nugget to `noParticles` for 50 altar power and fish metadata 2 to the secret strength-ceiling bypass for 200 altar power; fish metadata 3 remains the separate Water Breathing/Pufferfish ingredient. The port now lets explicitly stirred Gold Nugget mark the next resolved potion effect with the old ambient/no-particles flag and then reset, preserving dropped Gold Nuggets for the existing Golden Chalk cauldron offering path. Tropical Fish now disables per-effect strength ceilings for the resolver like source, so Healing/Harming can exceed their normal strength ceiling when enough source strength modifiers precede them, and that ceiling-bypass flag persists through effect resets because old `ModifiersEffect.reset()` did not clear it. Brick and Nether Brick target-skip modifiers are now live for supported potion/action delivery: Nether Brick suppresses block-target impact branches, Brick suppresses entity-target branches, both cost 50 altar power, and both persist through effect resets like source ModifiersEffect. JVM brew tests cover Gold Nugget power/explicit-stir/reset behavior, the Tropical Fish ceiling-bypass behavior, and the Brick/Nether Brick target flags. Fresh QA: sneak-stir Gold Nugget then Sugar and expect a normal Speed brew whose effect is flagged as ambient/no-particles internally; Gold Nugget should not auto-stir when dropped. Brew Tropical Fish + Glowstone Dust + Blaze Rod + Charged Attuned Stone + Glistering Melon and expect a stronger-than-normal Healing effect instead of the old Healing strength ceiling stopping at tier 1.

Current local delta (2026-06-06, superseded by 2026-06-12/13 moving-brew polish): Source instant/splash extent and lifetime impact modifiers are restored for composed custom brews. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` and Resurrected JSON comparison shows Wood Ash adds extent tier 1 for 50 altar power, Cocoa Beans add extent tier 2 for 100 power, and Wispy Cotton adds extent tier 3 for 150 power; Belladonna adds lifetime tier 1 for 50 power, Lapis Lazuli adds tier 2 for 100 power, and Hay Block adds tier 3 for 150 power. `DispersalInstant` uses radius `3 + extent`, keeping the same direct-hit full-power/half-duration and distance falloff math across that wider sphere. Source moving brew fluids convert lifetime to a decay denominator of `5 + lifetime^2 * 5`, yielding 5/10/25/50 for tiers 0/1/2/3, while extent maps to source fluid expansion `4 + extent` capped at 10. The new `brewgas`/`brewliquid` spine uses those helpers for first-pass spread/decay, and the legacy gas-cloud duration helper still preserves the earlier 600/1200/3000/6000-tick ratios for any compatibility path.
