JVM brew tests cover the extent tiers, lifetime tiers, altar costs, source decay denominator, source expansion cap, legacy cloud duration, and widened falloff math. Fresh QA: make a Sugar splash brew with Wood Ash and expect the falloff edge at about 4 blocks instead of 3; add Cocoa Beans and Wispy Cotton to reach about 5 and 6 blocks respectively. Make a Bat Wool gas brew with Belladonna, then with Belladonna + Lapis Lazuli, then with Belladonna + Lapis Lazuli + Hay Block, and expect progressively longer-lived moving gas blocks. Exact optimal liquid flow, the old adjacent block-action pulse, brew buckets, renderer/color polish, triggered cursed-block dispersal, Gas Mask protection, lifetime-scaled moving gas caps, normal Purified Milk disease cleanup, and Disease brew/block spread are complete; wider dynamic action roster, exact old ritualized pulse choreography, and visual edge cases remain moving-brew backlog.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source instant/splash delivery falloff is restored for composed custom-brew potion effects. Direct 1.7.10 `DispersalInstant` comparison shows splash brews use a 3-block radius sphere, direct entity hits apply full power but half duration, and nearby non-direct entities use `1.0 - distance / 3.0` power with half-duration scaling from that value. The port's composed splash bottles now query the same 3-block sphere instead of the earlier wide box and scale non-instant effect duration plus instant Healing/Harming power through the source factor. Gas/lingering clouds, liquid dispersal, triggered dispersal, and block-action radius behavior remain separate custom-brew backlog; source item-throw projectile travel is now restored for supported throws. JVM tests cover the source half-duration direct hit and 3-block falloff math. Fresh QA: make a Sugar splash brew and hit a mob directly; expect Speed for 90 seconds instead of the 3-minute drink duration. Splash another mob about 1.5 blocks from impact; expect about 45 seconds. At roughly 3 blocks from impact, expect no potion effect.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source vanilla/custom potion-effect ingredients are restored for the cleanly mapped composed-brew entries. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` comparison shows Spider Eye makes Poison for 45 seconds at 0 altar power and has no separate inverted effect; Sugar makes Speed for 3 minutes or inverted Slowness for 90 seconds at 100 power; Pufferfish makes Water Breathing for 3 minutes at 100 power; Magma Cream makes Fire Resistance for 3 minutes at 100 power; Golden Carrot makes Night Vision or inverted Invisibility for 3 minutes at 200 power; Ghast Tear makes Regeneration or inverted Poison for 45 seconds at 200 power; Blaze Powder makes Damage Boost/Strength for 3 minutes or inverted Weakness for 90 seconds at 200 power; Glistering Melon makes instant Healing or inverted instant Harming at 200 power with source strength ceiling 1; Leather makes Jump Boost for 3 minutes at 200 power; Sugar Cane makes source Floating for 90 seconds at 250 power; Feather makes source Feather Fall for 1 minute at 100 power; Bramble makes source Repell Attacker for 90 seconds at 250 power; Red Mushroom makes source Poison Weapons for 90 seconds at 200 power; Cobweb makes source Reflect Projectiles for 90 seconds or inverted Attract Projectiles for 45 seconds at 250 power; and Ender Dew makes source Ender Inhibition for 90 seconds at 200 power. The composed-brew engine now stores per-effect altar costs, separate inverted durations, the old default potion strength ceiling, and applies instant effects directly instead of treating source Healing/Harming as a ticking Health Boost stand-in. JVM brew tests cover source durations, instant entries, power costs, Golden Carrot -> Invisibility, Ghast Tear -> Poison, Blaze Powder -> Strength/Weakness, Glistering Melon -> Healing/Harming, Leather -> Jump Boost, Sugar Cane -> Floating, Feather -> Feather Fall, Bramble -> Repell Attacker, Red Mushroom -> Poison Weapons, Cobweb -> Reflect/Attract Projectiles, Ender Dew -> Ender Inhibition, and Spider Eye staying Poison when inverted. Gas Mask restored in this pass: Iron Ore now gives the old 90-second protection route, and moving gas suppresses harmful potion effects while keeping beneficial effects live. Fresh QA: brew Spider Eye and expect Poison for 45 seconds; add Redstone and expect 90 seconds; add Fermented Spider Eye before Spider Eye and still expect Poison, not Regeneration. Brew Sugar and expect Speed for 3 minutes; Fermented Spider Eye + Sugar should be Slowness for 90 seconds. Brew Leather and expect Jump Boost for 3 minutes. Brew Sugar Cane and expect Floating for 90 seconds. Brew Feather and expect source Feather Fall for 60 seconds, not vanilla Slow Falling. Brew Bramble and expect Repell Attacker for 90 seconds. Brew Red Mushroom and expect Poison Weapons for 90 seconds; melee hits from the affected attacker should poison the target, while non-melee damage should not. Brew Cobweb and expect Reflect Projectiles for 90 seconds; Fermented Spider Eye + Cobweb should become Attract Projectiles for 45 seconds. Brew Ender Dew and expect Ender Inhibition for 90 seconds; affected ender-entity and Ender Pearl teleports should be cancelled. Sneak-stir Blaze Powder and expect Strength for 3 minutes; Fermented Spider Eye + sneak-stirred Blaze Powder should be Weakness for 90 seconds. Fermented Spider Eye + Golden Carrot should be Invisibility for 3 minutes, Fermented Spider Eye + Ghast Tear should be Poison for 45 seconds, Glistering Melon should instantly heal like a Healing potion, and Fermented Spider Eye + Glistering Melon should instantly harm like a Harming potion.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source custom-brew drink-speed modifiers are restored for drinkable composed brews. Direct 1.7.10 comparison shows base brew drinking time is 32 ticks, Rowan Berries add `-8` ticks for 50 altar power, Exhale of the Horned One adds `-4` ticks for 0 power, Spanish Moss adds `-4` ticks for 50 power, and the final duration clamps to at least 2 ticks. The port now stores those ingredients in composed brews, applies the derived use duration to `brew_bottle`, shows a Fast/Very Fast/Slow/Very Slow tooltip line when duration differs from 32, and counts the source altar-power costs. Exhale is explicit-stir-only so ordinary right-click still triggers the existing Mutandis cauldron recipe; sneak-use it on the heated filled cauldron when it is meant as a drink-speed modifier. JVM brew tests cover 24-tick Rowan Berries, 16-tick Rowan + Exhale + Spanish Moss, power costs, and the Exhale explicit-stir guard. Fresh QA: bottle a simple drinkable brew with Rowan Berries and expect a 24-tick/1.2-second drink; add sneak-stirred Exhale plus Spanish Moss and expect 16 ticks/0.8 seconds plus a Fast tooltip; ordinary Exhale use with Mandrake Root + Egg offerings should still produce Mutandis instead of stirring.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source strength/duration/invert modifier tiers are restored for composed custom brews. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` comparison shows Glowstone Dust increases strength only up to tier 1 for 50 altar power, Blaze Rod can raise strength up to tier 2 for 100 power, Charged Attuned Stone can raise it up to tier 3 for 150 power, Redstone raises duration only up to tier 1 for 50 power, Obsidian can raise duration up to tier 2 for 100 power, Mindrake seed raises duration up to tier 3 for 150 power, and Fermented Spider Eye sets inversion for 25 power rather than toggling it back off. The port now caps repeated strength/duration modifiers the same way, adds the missing Blaze Rod / Charged Attuned Stone and Obsidian / Mindrake Bulb routes, and counts the old altar-power costs. JVM brew tests cover repeated cap behavior, higher-tier aliases, inversion-not-toggle, and source power costs. Fresh QA: brew Sugar with two Glowstone Dust and confirm only Speed II, brew Sugar with Glowstone Dust + Blaze Rod and confirm Speed III, brew Spider Eye with two Redstone and confirm only doubled duration, then add Obsidian and Mindrake Bulb for longer tiers; repeated Fermented Spider Eye should keep Spider Eye as Poison, while Fermented Spider Eye + Ghast Tear should become Poison through the source Ghast Tear inversion pair.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Empty generic composed throwable brew placeholders are now inert. The old custom-brew bottle path only produces filled brew bottles; the modern `splash_brew_bottle` / `lingering_brew_bottle` / `liquid_brew_bottle` creative IDs can exist without `BREW_CONTENTS`, and those empty stacks are refused by Brew Bag and Leonard's Urn storage. Direct right-click now follows the same rule: empty generic thrown brew stacks show a short "no prepared effect" message, play no throw sound, spawn no projectile, are not consumed, and can no longer fall through to the fixed Foulness projectile path. Filled composed splash/gas/liquid bottles keep the existing throw behavior. Fresh QA: give an empty `splash_brew_bottle`, `lingering_brew_bottle`, and `liquid_brew_bottle` from creative/commands and right-click them; expect no projectile, no bottle consumption, and no Foulness effect. Then throw a real bottled composed brew to confirm the filled path still works.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source wool colour modifiers restored for composed custom brews. Direct 1.7.10 comparison shows `BrewActionSetColor` registers every wool metadata variant using the old Witchery `Dye.rgb` table at 0 altar power, and Resurrected data mirrors this with `set_color` JSON entries. The composed-brew engine now accepts all 16 vanilla wool items as colour modifiers, uses the old RGB values, and applies the latest wool colour as the bottle tint while leaving the brew's effects/actions unchanged. This is only the colour/tint modifier path; the separate old Colorful potion/action family is still part of the remaining dynamic action roster. JVM brew tests cover red/blue wool RGB values and latest-colour replacement. Fresh QA: stir a visible composed brew with Red Wool before bottling and confirm the bottle tint shifts red; repeat with Red Wool then Blue Wool and confirm the tint becomes blue while the brew name/effects stay based on the actual effect/action ingredients.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Generic composed splash/gas/liquid brews now work with the existing Brew Bag and Leonard's Urn storage paths. The old 1.7.10 Brew Bag / Leonard's Urn slot classes accept Witchery brew bottles rather than only a fixed enum family; the port's first practical versions had only accepted `ThrownBrewItem` direct brews, leaving the new `splash_brew_bottle` / `lingering_brew_bottle` / `liquid_brew_bottle` composed containers outside the storage loop. A shared throwable-brew launcher now accepts either current fixed splash/gas brews or non-empty composed custom thrown brews, including Liquid Brew, preserves their `BREW_CONTENTS`, and creates the correct `ThrownBrewEntity` path when released. Brew Bag still consumes one stored brew and returns a Glass Bottle; Leonard's Urn direct release still keeps its stored focus, while Mystic Branch urn-slot casts keep the current consuming behavior. Empty generic custom-brew placeholder stacks are not accepted as stored projectiles. Fresh QA: bottle a composed Heart of Gold splash brew, a composed Bat Wool gas brew, and a composed Wormwood liquid brew, store each in a Brew Bag and Leonard's Urn from the other hand, verify the tooltips show the composed brew names, throw/release them, and confirm the animal action/effects come from the composed contents rather than falling back to a fixed Foulness brew.

Current local delta (2026-06-06, superseded by 2026-06-12/13 moving-brew polish and 2026-06-17 gas-contact polish): Moving custom-brew gas/liquid spine restored for composed-brew dispersal. Direct 1.7.10 `DispersalGas`, `DispersalLiquid`, `BlockBrewGas`, `BlockBrew`, and `TileEntityBrewFluid` comparison shows gas/liquid impacts place `witchery:brewgas` / `witchery:brewliquid`, store the brew payload plus color/duration/expansion/thrower, tick at 5 ticks, spread, and apply contact effects at 0.25 power and 0.5 duration on a 1-in-10 collision roll. The port now registers those source-ID blocks with a shared `BrewFluidBlockEntity`; Bat Wool composed thrown brews place moving gas, Wormwood-resolved brews bottle as `Liquid Brew` and place moving liquid, and source lifetime/extent helpers feed the first decay/expansion spine. Gas contact has since been hardened so `brewgas` scans living entities from its scheduled gas tick on a deterministic every-other-5-tick pulse instead of relying on empty-shape collision callbacks; liquid keeps the collision-contact path. JVM brew tests cover Artichoke splash, Bat Wool gas, Wormwood liquid selection/bottling, latest competing dispersal replacement, Wormwood no longer being an Invisibility ingredient, source fluid expansion/decay math, and deterministic gas contact cadence. Fresh QA: stir a simple visible effect brew with Bat Wool, bottle a `Lingering Brew`, throw it at replaceable ground/air space, and expect purple Witch particles spreading through invisible gas; standing in it should refresh the effect on the gas pulse. Stir the same visible effect with Wormwood, bottle a `Liquid Brew`, throw it at ground, and expect a shallow visible liquid block/spread that can apply the effect on contact. Since this note, optimal liquid flow, the 1% adjacent block-action pulse, brew buckets, liquid render/color polish, triggered cursed-block dispersal, normal Purified Milk disease cleanup, and Disease brew/block spread have landed; open follow-up is wider dynamic action roster, exact old ritualized pulse choreography, and visual edge cases.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Old custom-brew capacity disambiguation restored for the source dual-purpose trio. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` comparison shows Mandrake Root adds `EffectLevel(1)` at 0 altar power, Nether Wart adds `EffectLevel(2)` at 50 power, and Tear of the Goddess adds `EffectLevel(2)` at 100 power; these are now registered in the composed-brew engine. To preserve the port's existing source-offering cauldron recipes, dropped Mandrake Root / Nether Wart / Tear of the Goddess item entities are still **not** auto-stirred as brew ingredients. Instead, sneak-use one of those items on a heated, filled Witch's Cauldron to explicitly stir it as capacity; dropped items remain available for Mutandis, Pit Dirt, chalk upgrades, Drop of Luck, Mutating Sprig, and Summon Leonard. JVM brew tests now cover the old capacity math, Heart of Gold unlock through Nether Wart + Tear of the Goddess, and the explicit-only auto-stir guard. Fresh QA: heat/fill a Witch's Cauldron beside an altar, sneak-use Nether Wart then Tear of the Goddess, drop Heart of Gold, bottle with Glass Bottle, and expect `Brew of Animal Attraction` with 650 altar power spent; separately drop Mandrake Root / Nether Wart / Tear near the cauldron and confirm the old offering recipes still see them as offerings rather than consuming them into the composed brew.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Animal Attraction / Animal Repulsion dynamic brew route restored from source. Direct 1.7.10 comparison shows `itemHeartOfGold` registers `animalattraction`, inverted to `animalrepulsion`, with 500 altar power and `EffectLevel(4)`; Resurrected data confirms `golden_heart`, `power_cost: 500`, `capacity_required: 4`, and invertible behavior. The composed-brew engine now has source capacity modifiers for the non-conflicting current route (Diamond Vapour + Diamond, Nether Star, Pentacle/Koboldite Pentacle), Heart of Gold stirs only when the running capacity can pay level 4, and bottling spends the accumulated altar power before producing drink/splash/gas custom brew bottles. Drinking applies the old entity effect around the drinker: animals within `(strength + 1) * 16` blocks path toward the target for Attraction or pick the old random 6-11 block repulsion offset for Repulsion; tameables are tamed/healed/owned or untamed when not owned by the target, while Witchery familiars are skipped like old `Familiar.couldBeFamiliar`. Splash direct hits pass the struck living entity to the action, block hits fall back to nearby living targets, and gas applies the custom animal action immediately while potion-effect gas still uses the existing vanilla cloud. Fresh QA: brew Diamond Vapour + Diamond + Heart of Gold beside an 800-power altar and verify `Brew of Animal Attraction`, then add Fermented Spider Eye before Heart for `Brew of Animal Repulsion`; test drink/splash/gas on vanilla animals, Wolves/Cats, already-tamed non-owned tameables, and Witchery familiars.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Witches' Primer turned into a progression-unlock guidebook with live station-recipe pages. The native Modonomicon Primer is now **one growing book of 12 book-aligned categories** (Getting Started always open + 11 gated), 51 content entries plus per-section "What Comes Next" next-recipe entries. Players are **given the Primer on first join** (new `WitcheryAttachments.RECEIVED_PRIMER` boolean attachment, `copyOnDeath`, to dodge the `WitcheryPlayerData` 16-field codec cap) and can re-craft it cheaply (shapeless Book + black dye + feather). Crafting a topic book **unlocks its Primer category** (hidden until then — the book is INDEX display mode so its parent screen filters locked categories) and unlocks the **next** book's recipe through a 12-step advancement chain under `data/witchery/advancement/guide/` (`inventory_changed` criterion → `rewards.recipes`). Four new books were invented for previously book-less topics (`book_poppets`, `book_spirit_world`, `book_creatures`, `book_transformations`) with dedicated thematic 16x16 textures on the shared purple guide-book base. **Gating order fix:** the brew reagents (Odour of Purity / Breath of the Goddess / Whiff of Magic) are Witches' Oven products (sapling→fume), not cauldron brews, so Collecting Fumes was reordered before Brews & Infusions to keep the chain craftable in sequence (full chain re-audited: every reagent obtainable from strictly-earlier knowledge or vanilla; advancement chain validated intact + acyclic). **Recipe display:** custom Modonomicon page `witchery:station_recipe` (`book/page/StationRecipePage` + datagen `StationRecipePageModel` + client `StationRecipePageRenderer`) renders the station GUI background with **live ItemStacks** at slot coordinates, giving real **hover tooltips and stack counts** for oven, distillery, kettle, and cauldron recipes (replaced the earlier baked recipe PNGs; backgrounds emitted by `tools/recipe_image.py`). Debug: `/witchery guide unlock [all|<book>]` and `/witchery guide reset`. Compiles + `runData` + `build` all green. **Fresh QA (in-game):** fresh world → confirm the Primer is in inventory on join and opens to Getting Started only with all other categories hidden; craft Herbology (recipe present in the recipe book) and confirm its section appears and the next book's recipe unlocks; walk the chain Herbology → Collecting Fumes → Brews & Infusions → … → Transformations; open a recipe page (e.g. the oven's "What the Oven Makes" or a cauldron working) and **confirm the book loads at all** (the custom page is network-synced server→client, so a serialization mismatch would break the book) and that **hovering each item shows its tooltip** with correct counts (Mutandis ×6, Brew of Sleeping ×3); confirm `/witchery guide unlock all` reveals every category and `reset` re-hides them; confirm a lost Primer is re-craftable.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Custom Crazy Cat Lady witch-hut encounter added by request while preserving the source Witch Hut baseline for normal rolls. Direct 1.7.10 `ComponentVillageWitchHut` comparison confirms the original compact hut spawned one Coven Witch; the port now keeps that path for 19/20 generated Witch Huts and replaces the resident with one persistent Crazy Cat Lady on a 1-in-20 roll. The new hostile witch has a spawn egg and `/witchery spawn crazy_cat_lady`, uses a custom WitchModel skin, speaks action-bar witch/cat lines, uses vanilla witch ambient/hurt/death/throw/celebrate sounds, keeps one upright black cat familiar perched permanently on her hat, readies a larger visible familiar-model cat in her hands for the last 2 seconds of each 5-second throw cycle, and throws cats until seven throws are spent before retreating to her hut home with empty hands. Thrown cats now apply explicit projectile-style motion, gravity, drag, and collision expiry so they should arc toward the player instead of sitting at the witch's head/hat; they still play a high-pitched vanilla cat death cue on launch because no packaged legacy `Witch celebrate.ogg` or `Cat baby death.ogg` asset was found in the decompiled/source assets. On hit they deal 1 damage, apply short Blindness/Nausea, and apply the new `Scratched` effect for 5 minutes with -4 max health; the hit path also clamps/syncs health to that reduced maximum so the player heart bar should lose two heart containers. The same source-shaped Witch Hut is also registered as a rare wild forest surface structure using the existing biome/village material helper fallback so walls/doors/trim adapt outside villages too. Fresh QA: use the spawn egg and `/witchery spawn crazy_cat_lady`, confirm the permanent hat cat is upright and follows her head/hat, confirm her hands are empty before a larger cat appears shortly before each throw and empty again after the seventh, let seven thrown cats hit/miss while watching for real travel arcs, verify Scratched removes two heart containers for 5 minutes, and explore fresh forest chunks for rare wild Witch Huts plus 1-in-20 Crazy Cat Lady residents.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Villager/living-mob poppet binding tightened after live Voodoo Poppet QA. The old 1.7.10 path stores creature taglocks as persistent entity UUIDs and resolves them by scanning loaded worlds; the split modern item path already had creature-bound custom-data, but direct right-click binding could report success while leaving the interacted stack and held stack out of sync. Direct Poppet binding now replaces the held stack with a freshly bound one-count copy, Vampiric Poppet victim binding uses the same held-stack replacement, and Voodoo Poppet use falls back to resolving any loaded living entity UUID when no online player matches. Fresh QA: in survival, sneak-right-click a Villager with an unbound Voodoo Poppet and confirm the held item tooltip immediately says bound to that Villager; then use the bound poppet while the Villager is loaded and confirm throw, sneak+Bone Needle, lava, and underwater effects can hit the Villager like player-bound targets.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Keep ladders and Apothecary goto landing corrected after live QA. Direct `ComponentVillageKeep` comparison shows the tower shafts and both bridge spans all use old ladder meta `2`; in this fixed NORTH-oriented modern piece those spans need `LadderBlock.FACING=NORTH` so they attach to the generated south-side support instead of facing 180 degrees into air. `/witchery goto apothecary` also now resolves the transformed Apothecary front at the generated south face (`bb.maxZ()+1`) and prefers the nearest safe village road/path block near that door before falling back to doorway-height landing. Fresh QA: generate/goto a Keep and climb every tower/bridge ladder both ways, then run `/witchery goto apothecary` on an unseen and known village and confirm arrival is on the path in front of the Apothecary, not behind or on the roof.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Village Watch Tower upper ladder span extended after live traversal QA. The lower source shaft remains at local `(4,1..12,4)`, but the offset upper handoff at local `(3,*,5)` now runs from `y=10` through `y=15` instead of only `y=13..15`, giving the top span the three additional lower ladder blocks QA found missing. The upper handoff uses its own wall-supported facing toward the solid south wall, while the lower shaft keeps the previous support-facing correction. Fresh QA: generate/goto a new Watch Tower, climb from the lower shaft onto the offset upper span, reach the open top exit, and descend without any missing ladder blocks or popped segments.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Natural Rowan tree frequency halved by request. The wild Rowan placed feature now uses `minecraft:rarity_filter` chance `200` instead of `100`, so natural Rowan placement attempts are `1-in-200` everywhere the Rowan feature is injected. Sapling-grown Rowan trees, the configured tree shape, the `tree_clearance` guard, and the wild-only Belladonna decoration chance after a successful natural tree are unchanged. Fresh QA: explore newly generated plains/savanna/forest/taiga/snowy terrain and confirm Rowan trees are noticeably rarer while still appearing occasionally.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): `/witchery goto apothecary` now reuses the stable village goto arrival path for unseen villages. Live QA showed the building-only landing shortcut could still freeze when the target Apothecary was in an unvisited village, so building-targeted goto no longer marks that village as effectively processed. If the target village is fresh/unprocessed, the command full-loads the village footprint one chunk per tick, holds the temporary prep tickets, runs the same wall/gate prep as `/witchery goto village_*`, then resolves the final landing through the Apothecary doorway/floor anchor. Only already-processed villages keep the smaller known-village landing bubble. Fresh QA: from a world that has not seen the target village, run `/witchery goto apothecary`, confirm progress logs mirror village goto prep rather than landing-only prep, confirm no freeze, and confirm arrival lands near the Apothecary doorway/floor.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Gatehouse lantern brackets and Watch Tower ladder facing polished after live structure QA. Gatehouse lantern supports now explicitly connect toward the gatehouse face when generated from wall/fence-like bracket blocks, so the hanging lantern support ties into the tower wall instead of reading as an isolated post. Watch Towers keep the lower decompiled source ladder shaft at `(4,1..12,4)` and extend the offset upper handoff to `(3,10..15,5)` after fresh QA found the lower three handoff blocks missing; modern ladder facings are set per span so each run sticks onto its supporting wall in-game. Fresh QA: generate a new walled village and Watch Tower, confirm both outside gatehouse lantern brackets touch the wall, then climb the tower up and down without ladder segments floating, facing open air, or popping off after chunk updates.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): 1.21.11 Meadow villages are covered as plains-family villages. Local vanilla data confirms `BiomeTags.HAS_VILLAGE_PLAINS` includes `minecraft:meadow`, so Meadow villages use the existing `minecraft:village_plains` structure and `village/plains/houses` pool rather than a separate registered `village_meadow` structure. Witchery's injected buildings and automatic walls therefore already attach through the plains pool; the material helper now maps any future `village_meadow`/`village/meadow` identifier and explicit `minecraft:meadow` terrain fallback to the plains palette. `/witchery goto village_meadow` is now a player-facing safe-locate command that probes `minecraft:village_plains` candidates but only accepts Meadow-biome village centers. Fresh QA: use `/witchery goto village_meadow`, confirm the safe locate either reaches a Meadow village or fails cleanly, then verify Witchery buildings and walls use the plains cobble/oak plus stone-brick palette there.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Witchery village material selection now resolves the vanilla village structure family (`village_plains`, `village_savanna`, `village_desert`, `village_taiga`, `village_snowy`) from the enclosing `StructureStart` before falling back to terrain-biome sampling. This standardizes Apothecary, Bookshop, village Witch Hut, Watch Tower, Keep, and generated wall/gate palettes so a plains village cannot pick savanna terracotta/acacia just because a building piece samples nearby savanna terrain. Fresh QA: in newly generated plains and savanna villages, confirm all Witchery buildings and walls agree with the village type: plains stays cobble/oak plus stone-brick walls, savanna uses the requested terracotta/acacia building and wall palette, desert uses sandstone-family materials, and taiga/snowy use spruce-family building roofs.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Village Apothecary taiga roof palette and duplicate cap tightened after live QA found a taiga roof mismatch and two Apothecaries in one village. Direct 1.7.10 source confirms the old handler could request up to two Apothecaries in larger villages, but the modern port's village-building policy remains max one of each Witchery building per village start. The source-shaped Apothecary now uses the shared biome `roofSolid` material for its three solid roof bands, so taiga/snowy roofs read as spruce with the existing spruce stair rows instead of cobblestone bands. `WitcheryVillageElement` also picks a deterministic canonical planned origin per building from the `StructureStart`, so duplicate planned Apothecaries/bookshops/huts/towers/keeps in the same modern village start are skipped even after chunk replay or restart; `/witchery goto apothecary` uses the same canonical origin. Fresh QA: generate a new taiga village, confirm there is no more than one Apothecary, confirm its roof bands/stairs match the spruce roof palette used by nearby Witchery buildings, and confirm `/witchery goto apothecary` lands at the placed canonical building rather than an unplaced duplicate candidate.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Savanna village walls/gatehouses now use a terracotta-forward palette by request. The adaptive wall generator keeps ordinary savanna roads as Dirt Path and terrain support as Dirt, but the curtain wall bodies, wall-walk floors, gatehouse pillars, and gate arches now use Orange Terracotta; merlons, over-gate rails, lantern brackets, and rampart stair lips use Red Sandstone wall/stair pieces because vanilla has no terracotta wall/stair blocks. Fresh QA: generate or reset a savanna village wall and confirm the wall/gate mass reads orange terracotta, the shaped cap/lip pieces read red sandstone, paths still blend with village roads/terrain, and desert/badlands walls still use sandstone while plains/taiga/snowy walls stay stone-family.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): `/witchery setup stations` QA hut roof gable cleanup after screenshot QA. The latest screenshot showed the front/back Rowan plank gable infill overlapping the stair slope and creating blocky "teeth" at the roof ends. The command now keeps the bottom closed gable row but narrows the two higher infill rows so they occupy only the air under the stair roof, leaving the stair surfaces and Rowan-log ridge unobstructed. Fresh QA: rerun `/witchery setup stations`, inspect both gable ends from ground level and roof level, and confirm the roof reads as a clean pitched stair slope in all player facings without losing the closed front/back gables.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Village Watch Tower ladder reverted to the original two-run source layout while fixing the modern support direction. Old `ComponentVillageWatchTower` places ladders at local `(4,1..12,4)` and `(3,13..15,5)` with one metadata-derived facing; the port's previous straight-shaft workaround also ran the lower shaft through y=15, added a cobble support post, and rotated the upper segment separately. The Watch Tower now preserves the lower source shaft and extends the offset upper handoff downward to `y=10..15` for modern climbability after live QA found the three lower handoff blocks missing. Fresh QA: generate/goto a new Watch Tower, climb from the entrance up to the deck and back down, and confirm no ladder segment is rotated into air or pops off during chunk updates.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Village Watch Tower roof visual QA follow-up. Direct 1.7.10 `ComponentVillageWatchTower` comparison shows the peak uses the old wooden slab metadata `0`, i.e. a bottom-half slab; the port had used a top slab, making the cap read as a floating block above the stair peak. The Watch Tower now uses the biome bottom slab at local `4,22,4`. Modern stairs also do not infer L-corner shapes from the source's overlapping stair rows in this roof layout, so the two inner roof rings now explicitly set the eight corner stair blockstates to `OUTER_LEFT`/`OUTER_RIGHT` while preserving the source footprint, stair facings, materials, ladders, chest, guards, and roof rows. Fresh QA: generate/goto a new Watch Tower, confirm the roof peak cap sits on the stairs, and inspect the y=20/y=21 roof corners for real L-shaped stair joins rather than straight stair blocks crossing at the angles.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): `/witchery setup stations` QA hut roof stair facings flipped 180 degrees after in-game visual QA. The pitched Rowan roof geometry, ridge, gables, overhangs, station layout, boosted altar, chest, traders, and power garden are unchanged; only the stair `FACING` values for the left/right roof slopes are swapped. Fresh QA: run `/witchery setup stations` facing north/south/east/west and confirm every Rowan stair on both roof slopes now faces the intended way while the ridge/gables still line up cleanly.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): five-item plant/trinket QA batch continued after the Dream Catcher pass. Blood Roses now allow the tester-requested self-sample extraction path with a distinct Blood Rose message while preserving Boline-only item drops and leaving the older own-sample refusal on other stored-sample sources such as Leech Chest. Leaping Lily still uses the source non-refreshing Speed/Jump pulse, and its item now uses the vanilla water-surface placement route so it can be placed onto water like a lily pad. Critter Snares now clear caught state around sneak-release so released critters cannot immediately clone from a still-caught snare, and they can also catch/release vanilla Rabbits for the requested desert hare/bunny coverage; this is a deliberate port extension rather than original 1.7.10 critter parity, and `caught=5` currently reuses the empty snare model rather than adding rabbit-specific art. Alluring Skull QA is documented as source pathing-only: original `BlockAlluringSkull.allure` path-navigates undead toward the lit skull but does not clear or override their current attack target. Fresh QA: brush through a Blood Rose and collect your own sample with an empty Taglock Kit, place Leaping Lily on water, catch/release a Rabbit and confirm one release empties the snare without cloning, and test lit Alluring Skulls with unaggroed or line-broken undead rather than expecting them to drop an active survival-player target.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Dream Catcher host/weave behavior corrected against 1.7.10 source. `BlockDreamCatcher` is hidden from the old create tab and returns no block drops; the five `ItemGeneral.DreamWeave` subitems are the normal placeable objects, each placing the wall-hung host block and storing itself in the tile entity. The port now hides the standalone `dream_catcher` host item from the Witchery creative tab, keeps Dream Weavers as the player-facing placement items, rejects inserting a second weave into an occupied catcher, and returns the stored weave only through break/support loss like source. The placed model no longer bakes in a permanent net: the host renders frame/decorations only, while a new `weave=1..5` blockstate overlay uses the old 32x32 texture offsets for Fleet Foot, Iron Arm, Fasting, Nightmares, and Intensity. Fresh QA: place all five Dream Weavers on north/south/east/west walls, confirm each shows a distinct inner woven panel, try right-clicking an occupied catcher with another Dream Weaver and confirm it does not consume or swap, then break/support-drop each catcher and confirm only the stored Dream Weaver item returns.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): `/witchery setup stations` QA hut supplies and altar boosted. The double chest now uses all 54 slots: the redundant Charcoal stack was replaced and the empty space is filled with Glass Goblets, Arthana, Boline, Pentacle, Mystic Branch, all four Chalk variants, and Taglock Kits while keeping the early oven/distillery/spinning/herb/jar supplies. The altar now uses the real enhancement hooks across all six altar tops: Candelabra, filled Chalice, Skeleton Skull, and placed Arthana/Pentacle/Mystic Branch items. The cleared pad also gets a compact Witchery power garden with log/leaf posts, saplings, mature herbs, garlic, and a Blood Rose so `/witchery altar` has useful nearby source blocks immediately. Fresh QA: run `/witchery setup stations`, inspect the double chest, run `/witchery altar` at the altar, confirm valid structure plus boosted max/recharge/range/enhancement, and check the garden does not block door/window/machine access.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): `/witchery setup stations` QA hut visual quality pass. This is a command-built tester workspace, not the source village Witch Hut: the station coordinates, stocked chest, machines, traders, sunken floor, and 20x20 clearing behavior are preserved. The Rowan shell now has extra log framing and a pitched Rowan stair roof with front/back overhangs, gable infill, and a horizontal Rowan-log ridge so it reads more like a witch workshop instead of a flat-topped plank cube. Fresh QA: run `/witchery setup stations`, confirm the sloped roof/gables generate cleanly in all player facings, the door remains accessible, the interior stations/traders/chest are unchanged, Glowglobes still light the room, and the roof does not clip the oven/fume funnel or altar.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): `/witchery goto apothecary` freeze path retargeted after in-game QA. The previous building-only landing prep could still freeze for an unvisited target village. Building-targeted goto now follows the same full village arrival prep and wall/gate generation path as `/witchery goto village_*` whenever the target village is not already processed, while retaining the Apothecary doorway/floor landing resolver. Fresh QA: restart, run `/witchery goto apothecary` toward an unseen village, confirm it no longer freezes, lands at/near the Apothecary doorway/floor rather than roof, and still finds the same room/chest.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Garlic Garland art and targeting box widened after QA found the flat sprite stretched and the hitbox narrower than the block face. The placed wall model now uses a full 16x16 wall plane so the restored 32x32 garland texture keeps its square aspect instead of being squeezed into a short strip, and the interaction outline now covers the whole wall face width on each horizontal facing. Fresh QA: place on all four wall faces, confirm the art is no longer visibly stretched, the outline covers the full block face width and matches the sprite better, survival break/drop still works, and vampire pushback/break prevention still works.

Tester QA result (2026-06-05): latest ten-point QA pass confirms Garlic Garland survival break/drop, vampire pushback, and non-creative vampire break prevention; Apothecary chest contents; hidden `witchery:light` startup warning cleanup; Wickerman ignition/burn behavior; Horned Huntsman combat feel; and Rowan Door redstone behavior. Apothecary villager/trades were not tested. Apothecary goto froze before this local fix and needs retest. Garlic Garland art/hitbox were not acceptable before this local fix and need retest.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Garlic Garland placement-side mapping corrected after in-game QA found the wall-hung art rendered on the far side of its own air block. Direct `BlockGarlicGarland` / `RenderGarlicGarland` comparison showed the old metadata is derived from player yaw while the rendered garland sits on the back face for that metadata, so the modern block now stores the opposite horizontal player direction on placement. This preserves the already-restored wall-hung model, source-derived targeting outline, no-collision pass-through behavior, vampire counterplay, recipe, and toughness while making normal wall placement hang against the surface the player is facing/clicking instead of floating on the opposite face. Fresh QA: place Garlic Garlands on all four wall facings again, confirm the art and outline sit against the clicked wall surface, confirm pass-through still works, and retest survival removal/drop plus vampire contact/left-click counterplay.

Tester QA result (2026-06-05): Apothecary loot/build/room checks are confirmed acceptable in game. The `/witchery goto apothecary` command works and finds an Apothecary, but landed the tester on the Apothecary roof, so doorway/floor landing remains an open QA-command polish item. Rowan Door redstone retest is now confirmed OK after the source-backed lock fix. Garlic Garland pass-through/no-collision is confirmed, while the placement-side issue above was found and patched for retest.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): client startup logs audited after the 08:32 dev launch. There are no `ERROR`, `FATAL`, or exception entries in `run/logs/latest.log`; the Modonomicon refmap warning, vanilla command ambiguity warnings, missing dev data-pack `tests`, offline chat-session key warning, and one integrated-server tick catch-up warning are normal dev-environment noise. The only Witchery-side resource warning was `Missing model for variant: 'Block{witchery:light}'`; direct 1.7.10 `BlockLight` comparison confirms the old block was invisible, non-colliding, non-opaque, full-bright, hidden from the creative tab, and no-drop, so the modern hidden Sun Grenade helper now only gains the same `minecraft:block/air` blockstate pattern already used by invisible `blood_crucible`, leaving mechanics unchanged. Fresh QA: restart the client after rebuilding and confirm the `witchery:light` missing-model warning no longer appears during resource loading.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): `/witchery goto apothecary` added for village Apothecary QA. Witchery village buildings live inside vanilla village jigsaw starts rather than standalone structures, so the command reuses the existing ticked safe village-locate queue across all five vanilla village variants and filters candidates to starts containing `WitcheryVillageElement[apothecary]`. Follow-up QA showed the trimmed building-only prep path was still unstable on unseen villages, so building-targeted goto now shares the normal `village_*` arrival prep for fresh villages while keeping the Apothecary-specific final landing anchor, cooldowns, and custom village mechanics unchanged.

Tester QA result (2026-06-05): bloodied Wickerman ignition is confirmed. A valid bloody straw man / Wickerman lit successfully and spawned the Horned Huntsman, and follow-up combat QA says the Huntsman feels good in play. Keep exact Huntsman pose/scale/drops, arrow details, wolf behavior, Wickerman structure offset details, Demonic Blood compatibility, Wicker Bundle axis preservation, and Wicker Bundle break/blast feel open unless separately retested.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Apothecary chest loot tuned for gameplay while preserving story books. Direct comparison with 1.7.10 `ComponentVillageApothecary` showed the old floor-level chest rolled 2-5 source entries, but the requested port direction is no longer strict source loot for this table. The modern `witchery:chests/apothecary` now rolls 3-6 stacks from a focused apothecary starter cache: bottles, food, clay, small currency, Witchery herbs/components, rare utility pulls, and source-flavored curios like Water Diviner, Voodoo Poppet, Fire Protection Poppet, Skeleton Skull, and Diamond. The random enchanted-book roll was removed so written books in Apothecaries continue to come only from the custom `witchery:village_story_books` global modifier, which remains unchanged at its existing 18% chance. Fresh QA: open new Apothecary floor-level chests, confirm the base loot uses the tuned ingredient/utility set without random enchanted books, and remember that a Witchery story book may still appear as the separate preserved modifier roll.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Garlic Garland wall render and targeting polished. Direct comparison with 1.7.10 `BlockGarlicGarland` / `RenderGarlicGarland` showed the old block is not a crossed plant render: it is an invisible normal block drawn as a wall-hung garland at the upper wall face, with empty collision and a small source outline around the hanging model. The modern block model now uses a cutout wall plane in that source position instead of `minecraft:block/cross`, and the block exposes the same source-derived outline through the modern interaction shape so it can be targeted/removed while remaining pass-through. Follow-up QA confirmed pass-through works but found placement rendered on the opposite face of its own air block; the newer local delta above corrects that placement-side mapping and needs wall-facing retest.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Rowan Door redstone bypass fixed. Source `BlockWitchDoor.func_150014_a` refuses the redstone-open path for Rowan doors, but the modern port inherited vanilla `DoorBlock.neighborChanged`, letting neighbor power flip `OPEN=true` on a locked Rowan Door. Rowan Doors now place closed/unpowered even beside redstone, record neighbor power without opening, and preserve the source-style redstone-off close path for an already-open door. Fresh QA: place a locked Rowan Door with no matching key, try lever/button/pressure-plate power on both halves and nearby blocks, confirm it stays closed and still shows the locked message on use, then verify a matching key/keyring can open/close it and redstone power removal can close an already-open door without opening it.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Village Apothecary source furnishings corrected. A direct `ComponentVillageApothecary` comparison showed the port had misread old `Blocks.field_150383_bp` as a Crafting Table, but source places a full vanilla Cauldron at local `7,1,1`; source also uses flower-pot metadata `10`, the old potted dead bush, and skull metadata `9` for the standing skull rotation. Fresh Apothecaries now place a full Water Cauldron, a Potted Dead Bush, and a source-rotated Skeleton Skull while keeping the old sparse two-stair counter, torch, floor-level source chest coordinate, villager spawn/trades, shell, windows, biome palette, door/sign/step, and one-per-village injection unchanged. Fresh QA: generate or goto a new village Apothecary and confirm the cauldron/potted dead bush/skull rotation are visible, the chest at the source floor-level coordinate can be found/opened, and the named `Apothecary` villager still spawns with the source Emerald trades.

Tester QA result (2026-06-04/2026-06-05): wood-family checks `1`, `2`, `3`, and `5` from the short in-game QA list are confirmed OK. Rowan/Alder/Hawthorn Planks pass survival break and contiguous-row TNT behavior, Rowan/Alder/Hawthorn Logs pass axis/break/blast behavior, the restored `BlockWitchLog` Ent roll has been observed working, and Rowan Door manual lock/key/keyring/lower-half drop plus source toughness behavior passed. Follow-up QA found redstone could open a locked Rowan Door; the source-backed fix has now been retested and confirmed OK. Rowan Door facing/hinge feel remains a separate polish check unless explicitly retested.

Tester QA result (2026-06-04): short QA check `9` is confirmed. `witchery:infernal_blood` right-clicks plain Wicker Bundles and stains five nearby bundles into Bloodied Wicker Bundles. Follow-up QA confirms a bloodied Wickerman can be ignited and spawns the Horned Huntsman. Keep Demonic Blood compatibility, axis preservation if not directly observed, Wicker Bundle break/blast feel, Wickerman structure offsets, Horned Huntsman pose/drops, Infernal Blood drop source, bloodied-wicker crafting, Ruby Slippers, and Summon Imp acceptance open unless separately retested.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Witchery planks toughness parity restored. Original 1.7.10 `BlockWitchWood` is one metadata-backed rowan/alder/hawthorn planks block, calls `setHardness(2.0F)`, and has no explicit blast-resistance override, so old resistance is the implied `10.0F`. The split modern Rowan, Alder, and Hawthorn Planks registrations now use that source toughness pair without changing recipes, tags, self-drop loot, block models, fuel/flammability expectations, altar/Brew of Sprouting wood-family use, or Ice World block-family conversion behavior. Fresh QA: compare all three plank variants' survival break feel against the old `2.0F` target, try ordinary blast cleanup against the old `10.0F` resistance target, and retest plank crafting, tags, loot, and related wood-family conversions.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Broom damage lifecycle parity restored. Original 1.7.10 `EntityBroom.attackEntityFrom` flips forward direction, sets a 10-tick hit timer, adds `damage * 10` to `damageTaken`, decays that accumulated damage by 1 per tick, and only breaks/drops the broom once accumulated damage exceeds `40.0F` or a creative player attacks it. The port no longer ejects/drops/discards a placed broom on every tiny hit; small hits now leave the broom placed and accumulate/decay like source, while threshold/creative breaks still eject riders and return the matching normal/enchanted broom item with custom name and `BrushColor`. Fresh QA: punch a placed broom once in survival and confirm it remains, hit it repeatedly before decay and confirm it breaks around the old threshold, wait between hits to confirm decay, and confirm creative break still removes it without a drop.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Guidebook migration to Modonomicon started (Phase 1). The old Patchouli "Witches' Primer" never opened on 1.21.11 (no Patchouli build), so the guide is being reimplemented natively on **Modonomicon 1.121.0** (added via Cloudsmith maven, `implementation "com.klikli_dev:modonomicon-${minecraft_version}-neoforge:${modonomicon_version}" {transitive=false}`, MIT). New files: `book/WitchesPrimerBookProvider` (a `BookSubProvider` building a minimal `witches_primer` book — one `getting_started` category, one `first_steps` entry with a text + spotlight page), `datagen/WitcheryDataGenerators` (registers it on `GatherDataEvent.Client`), `client/WitchesPrimerScreenOpener` (client-only `BookGuiManager` open). `WitchesPrimerItem.use()` now opens the Modonomicon book client-side instead of the dead reflection-based Patchouli path; `modonomicon` is a required dep in `neoforge.mods.toml`. The `data {}` run block now uses `clientData()` (the old `data()` run type was removed in MDG/NeoForge 21.11; `GatherDataEvent` split into Client/Server). The legacy `assets/witchery/patchouli_books/witches_primer` JSON + `PatchouliCompat` are kept in parallel as the conversion source (deleted in a later phase). `compileJava`, `runData`, and `build` (incl. tests) all clean; book JSON generates under `data/witchery/modonomicon/books/witches_primer/`. Next: Phase 2 `$()`→Markdown converter + golden tests, then bulk-migrate the 8 categories / ~43 entries. **QA pending (needs a client launch):** hold the Witches' Primer and right-click to confirm the Modonomicon book opens to the First Steps entry. See `docs/reference/PORTING_NOTES.md` (2026-06-04 Modonomicon entry) for the full API/gotcha writeup.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Brazier source interaction parity restored for break drops and water-bottle resets. Original 1.7.10 `BlockBrazier.breakBlock` drops stored ingredients when the Brazier is not burning, but drops one `Wood Ash` when the Brazier is burning. The port already returned idle ingredients on player break; it now also drops `witchery:wood_ash` for burning player-breaks instead of silently consuming the active burn marker and ingredients. A direct `BlockBrazier.func_149727_a` comparison also showed plain water bottles reset non-empty Braziers and become Glass Bottles; the port now handles vanilla water-potion stacks before ingredient insertion instead of treating them as recipe inputs. `compileJava processResources` clean. Fresh QA: load a Brazier with a valid/ignited recipe, break it before completion, and confirm one Wood Ash drops; repeat with an idle loaded Brazier and confirm the three ingredients still drop. Then insert one ingredient, right-click with a plain Water Bottle, and confirm the loaded Brazier clears while the held stack becomes a Glass Bottle.

Current local delta (2026-06-03): Removed the non-source standalone Torment Chamber residue. Original 1.7.10 has Torment maze generation (`GenerateMaze`, `WorldChunkManagerTorment`, `WorldProviderTorment`) but no separate `torment_chamber` structure. The port no longer registers the dead structure/piece type, no longer exposes `/witchery goto torment_chamber`, and no longer ships the orphan structure JSON or chamber loot table; Torment QA remains focused on the fixed legacy maze, portal chamber, and Refilling Chest rooms.

Current local delta (2026-06-03): Blight prototype QA reconciled. Tester pass confirmed both the Rite of Blight and Brew of Blight embed the heart into the ground, the heart hitbox/light/drip behavior is good, Blight Fibre behaves well as carpet plus climbing vine, Blighted Soil blends with terrain, spread is local/stable, rite offerings are consumed, and the bottle art no longer bleeds outside the vanilla bottle. The duplicate visible Blight bottles were traced to canonical `brew_of_blight` plus compatibility alias `blight_brew`; the alias remains registered for saved/test stacks but is no longer exposed in the Witchery creative tab. Mystic Branch QA is now recorded as stable for no-cross symbols but still suspect for retraced/crossing paths; source treats those as ordinary stroke sequences, so the next fix should compare affected `EffectRegistry` stroke sets before changing recognition.

Current local delta (2026-06-03): Custom brew composition system implemented. The port had 3 hardcoded generic brew types (Foulness/Foaming/Poison) but no way to *compose* arbitrary brews; that core is now in. New `com.emoniph.witchery.brewing` package faithfully ports Resurrected's `BrewActionList`/`ModifiersEffect`: `Brews` (the ingredient→action registry + accumulate-modifiers-then-apply-effects engine), `BrewContents` (ordered ingredient list, derives effects/name/colour/dispersal/power, stored as a `BREW_CONTENTS` data component). Mechanic: a heated, water-filled **Witch's Cauldron** (now an `EntityBlock` with `WitchCauldronBlockEntity`) stirs in dropped brew ingredients one at a time (server tick); modifier ingredients accumulate (Glowstone = +strength, Redstone = +duration, Fermented Spider Eye = invert, Gunpowder = splash, Dragon's Breath = lingering gas), each effect ingredient then applies its potion at the accumulated strength/duration and resets — exactly like source. Collecting with a Glass Bottle outputs a `brew_bottle` / `splash_brew_bottle` / `lingering_brew_bottle` carrying the composed contents (dynamic "Brew of X" name, data-driven `custom_model_data` colour tint, effect tooltip), consuming one water level. Drinking applies the effects to the drinker; splash throws an instant area; lingering makes an `AreaEffectCloud` (reusing `ThrownBrewEntity`, extended to carry composed contents). The initial prototype effect roster from this milestone has since been source-corrected by the 2026-06-06 deltas; use the current Custom Brew row and QA note above for the live ingredient list, including Leather -> Jump Boost instead of the old Rabbit Foot placeholder. The three brew-bottle IDs removed last session are back as these real composable containers. `compileJava processResources` clean; JSON validated. Fresh QA: heat a water-filled Witch's Cauldron, drop e.g. Glowstone then Spider Eye, collect with a Glass Bottle, and confirm a "Strong Brew of Poison" that poisons on drink; test Gunpowder→splash and Dragon's Breath→lingering; verify the bottle colour tint renders. Deeper roster (block-altering brews, ritual brews, gas/liquid spread blocks) remains the existing named-brew items.

Current local delta (2026-06-02): Last placeholders completed — `LEGACY_PLACEHOLDER_ITEMS` is now empty. `wolf_head` and `hellhound_head` were promoted to real mounted-head blocks (ported from 1.7.10 `BlockWolfHead`, skullType 0=wolf / 1=hellhound): new `WolfHeadBlock` + `HellhoundHeadBlock` (thin subclass for its own codec/texture flag), `WolfHeadBlockEntity` (stores floor rotation), and BESR `WolfHeadRenderer` + `WolfHeadModel` (the vanilla-wolf-head geometry — head 6x6x4, two ears, snout, 64x32). Floor/wall placement, floor rotation, VoxelShapes, and inventory-flat-icon all mirror `AlluringSkull`. Wolf reuses the vanilla wolf texture; hellhound uses `textures/entity/hellhound.png` (copied from 1.7.10). Registered: blocks, one shared block-entity type, block items, BE renderer + model layer, blockstates (6 FACING variants), particle block models, self-drop loot tables, `block.witchery.*` lang names, creative-tab entries. The other four IDs were removed rather than faked: `brew_bottle`/`splash_brew_bottle`/`lingering_brew_bottle` are Resurrected generic NBT brew CONTAINERS the port replaces with individual `brew_of_*` items, and `trapped_plant` was an invented ID with no source item. `compileJava processResources` clean; all JSON validated. Fresh QA: place both heads on floor and walls, rotate, break (self-drop), and confirm the wolf/hellhound textures render in-world plus the flat icons in inventory.

Current local delta (2026-06-02): Legacy placeholder roster de-scarified and de-duplicated. The stale "107 placeholders" figure was counting the whole `ITEM_REVIEW.md` review roster (mostly already promoted), not the live registry. The live `LEGACY_PLACEHOLDER_ITEMS` set is now stated accurately: **six** genuinely inert IDs, enumerated one by one in `ITEM_REVIEW.md` and the legacy-placeholder bullet below — `hellhound_head`, `wolf_head` (decorative mounted-head blocks, need head block + BESR), `trapped_plant` (trap block), and `brew_bottle` / `splash_brew_bottle` / `lingering_brew_bottle` (Resurrected empty brew-container IDs, need the cauldron brew-into-bottle flow). Cleaned out the four `brew_of_soul_*` placeholders as duplicates of the real `hunger_demon_soul` / `anguish_demon_soul` / `fear_demon_soul` / `torment_demon_soul` ingredients ("Soul of X Demon"): removed their registry entries, creative-tab group line, lang strings, and orphaned item-model JSONs. `compileJava processResources` clean; lang JSON validated.

Current local delta (2026-06-02): Mystic Branch symbol magic implemented. The branch is no longer a creature-power stand-in; it is now the source air-glyph casting wand (`ItemMysticBranch` + the `infusion.infusions.symbols` system, 1.7.10-0.24.1). New `com.emoniph.witchery.symbol` package: `StrokeSet`, `SymbolEffect`, `SymbolEffectProjectile`, and `EffectRegistry` with the iconic non-knowledge spells at their exact source stroke sequences — Accio, Confundus, Episkey, Expelliarmus, Flipendo, Impedimenta, Incendio, Lumos, Nox, Stupefy. Hold use to channel (`ItemUseAnimation.BLOCK`, 36000-tick duration); the client (`client.SymbolDrawState`, driven from `WitcheryClientEvents.onClientTick`) tracks pitch/yaw flicks into stroke bytes (up=0/down=1/left=2/right=3, 7-degree threshold, max 15), and sends a C2S `SpellPreparedPayload` when a symbol is recognised. Releasing casts the prepared spell on the server: validates infusion presence (`hasValidInfusion`), spends `floor(2^(level-1)*baseCost)` infused energy, and runs the effect — projectile spells launch a new `SpellEffectEntity` (ThrowableProjectile, NoopRenderer + witch-particle trail) that resolves `onCollision`; instant spells (Episkey/Nox) run directly. Polish: action-bar symbol-name readout while drawing, recognition chime, evoker/illusioner cast sounds, impact particles, snare + red feedback on unknown-symbol / not-infused / no-energy, and a usage tooltip. Transient `preparedSpellId`/`preparedSpellLevel` added to `WitcheryPlayerData` (not in CODEC). Altar-top placement is preserved. Deliberate port simplifications vs source (documented in PORTING_NOTES): no Worship-potion level cap (drawn stroke length picks the level directly), no Beast-infusion-gated unforgivable curses (Crucio/Imperio/Avada Kedavra not registered yet), and no persistent spellbook/cooldown (only knowledge-free spells in this slice). `compileJava processResources` clean. Fresh QA: get infused, hold-use the branch, flick a symbol (e.g. Lumos = down,down,right), release, and confirm the cast plus energy spend; verify the action-bar name and fail messages.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Structure goto QA fixes. (1) `/witchery goto straw_man` could teleport to a located Straw Man that never placed any blocks: `StrawManPiece.hasDryLandFootprint` aborted the whole effigy whenever `WORLD_SURFACE > OCEAN_FLOOR` (true over any snow layer, grass, or flower) even on dry land that the structure-level `findGenerationPoint` had already approved. That redundant guard is removed; the explicit fluid checks plus the structure water/biome guards still keep it off water. (2) and (3) Coven/Stone Circle and Straw Man both generated one block too high (a raised dirt/grass plinth), because `getFirstOccupiedHeight` returns the air block above terrain here while both pieces anchored the floor at that Y. Both structures now anchor the piece at `groundY - 1`, so the floor/ground layer lands flush and the standing stones rest on the surface. Fresh QA should `/witchery goto straw_man` and confirm an actual effigy appears flush with terrain, and `/witchery goto coven_circle` (or `stone_circle`) and confirm the ring floor is flush rather than on a plinth.

### Sun Collector chain + VampiricPoppet fixes (2026-05-24)

- **SunCollectorBlock + SunCollectorBlockEntity** promoted from legacy placeholder: BaseEntityBlock with server-ticking charge loop. Right-click with Quartz Sphere inserts it; empty right-click retrieves; drops stored item on break. Charges during daytime (sky access, no rain, dayTime < 13000) at 1 tick/game-tick; after 6000 ticks in sunlight outputs Charged Quartz Sphere. The block now also mirrors source `power=0..15` presentation state for empty/inserted/charged display sync.
- **charged_quartz_sphere** new item (foil enchant glow). Item model/assets added.
- **Sun Grenade recipe** updated again after source/companion comparison: 1 Charged Quartz Sphere -> 1 Sun Grenade, and thrown grenades now return a Quartz Sphere when the sunlight releases. Full chain: Quartz Sphere -> Sun Collector (6000 daylight ticks) -> Charged Quartz Sphere -> Sun Grenade -> Quartz Sphere return.
- **Sun Collector recipe** restored to the Resurrected source shape: gold ingots, repeater, daylight detector, and iron ingots. The block now also uses the source compact footprint and original `3.5F` hardness plus old implied `17.5F` blast resistance.
- **VampiricPoppetItem** fully rewritten with two-taglock semantics (source = witch protected, victim = damage absorber). `PoppetEvents.onVampiricRedirect` was broken — `matchesBound` found poppets where TAG_UUID == damaged player, then looked up that same UUID and bailed because target==player. Fixed: now finds poppets where TAG_UUID == damaged player (source match), then redirects to TAG_VICTIM_UUID.
- **PlayerCompassItem** promoted: right-click player → attuned; right-click air → shows 8-point compass direction + block distance. Offline/other-dimension messages. Shapeless recipe: compass + taglock_kit.
- **WitchHunter poppet disruption** added to PoppetEvents: 30% chance on WitchHunterEntity hit to destroy a bound ProtectionPoppetItem from inventory or shelf.
- Placeholder count: 195 → 192.
- BUILD SUCCESSFUL.

Current local delta (2026-06-20): **Vanishing Witch Hut — slice 1 (additive scope `vanishing_witch_hut`).** A block hut that relocates when unobserved: buried `VanishingHutAnchorBlock` brain tracks who has been inside + a look-cone watch test, and on the inside→exit→unwatched trigger (`VanishingHutHelper.shouldRelocate`) `VanishingHutEvents` rebuilds it elsewhere — usually nearby Overworld, ~25% crossing to the Spirit World at mirror coords — leaving ash/debris + FX + a farewell line. Tracked live by `VanishingHutData`. The resident witch is tethered to the hut and carried (same witch) on each move; **salt-line counterplay is in** — a Witches' Salt line (`witchery:witches_salt`, smelt kelp / buy from a Coven Witch) across the doorway holds it. It now **generates naturally** in forests and swamps (`VanishingHutStructure`/`VanishingHutPiece`, structure `witchery:vanishing_hut` + `has_vanishing_hut` biome tag, random_spread spacing 44/sep 20), reusing the same build/relocate logic as the command path. QA: `/witchery setup|goto vanishing_hut`, `/witchery hut move|watch`. Code + relocation loop + worldgen done and unit-tested; the other "hold it" routes (red thread / iron nail / offering) remain later slices; in-game QA pending.

Current local delta (2026-06-20): **Village walls are now a ~40% spawn, not universal (config-tunable).** Automatic generation rolls a deterministic per-village chance (`VillageWallGenerator.shouldGenerateWalls`, seeded by world seed + village min corner) in `VillageWallEvents.queueVillage`; ~60% of villages now stay unwalled. The rate is exposed as `worldgen.villageWallSpawnPercent` (0-100, default 40). The roll is stable across reloads, not persisted on a "no", and does not affect the `/witchery walls` QA force-build. Existing walled villages are unchanged.
