Current local delta (2026-06-18): **NBT-backed structure generation.** The old procedural Hobgoblin Hut placement path now builds the designer-authored `hobgobhut.nbt` template, cleaned of snow and chest/barrel storage. It is limited to forest/swamp tags, rejects water/ocean/river and steep terrain, clears clutter instead of carving broad shelves, fills only support columns under the actual base, and spawns a resident Hobgoblin. A new Warped Coven Nether structure builds `warpedcoven.nbt` in warped forests, with snow/mirror removed, no blackstone under-fill, stable-floor and interior-clearance selection, late placement/volume clearing to remove stems/leaves inside, a resident Coven Witch, and a `witchery:chests/warped_coven` loot table. `/witchery goto warped_coven` searches the Nether target dimension, and `/witchery goto nether` plus `/witchery exit nether` provide safe Nether travel with a stored return point. Headless NBT tests pin template sizes, cleanup, biome tags, and structure JSON settings.

Current local delta (2026-06-14): **Custom Brew Ingredient Reference in the native Primer.** `Brews & Infusions -> Brew Reagents` is now generated from `Brews.ingredientReferences()` so the in-game reference follows the live custom-brew registry instead of a hand-maintained copy. The entry groups capacity, modifiers, reach/targets, dispersal, potion reagents, and action reagents on custom `witchery:item_reference` pages that render real reagent item icons next to each row in both the normal Primer and placed block book, and annotates level, altar-power, and explicit sneak-stir-only ingredients. This closes the interim discoverability gap while JEI remains blocked on a 1.21.11 build; focused brew tests and generated-book coverage pin representative registry metadata and page shape.

Current local delta (2026-06-14): **Dog Tongue ritualized custom-brew delivery parity.** Sneak-use `Tongue of Dog` on a heated, full Witch's Cauldron now releases the live cauldron brew through the old `BrewActionRitual` entry path instead of requiring a thrown bottle. The port charges the source 250 altar-power trigger cost in addition to the brew's ingredient power, keeps Tongue of Dog explicit so dropped cauldron/kettle offerings are preserved, rejects empty/drink-only/missing-trigger-target/non-placeable releases before consuming state, refunds altar power if final delivery fails late, and then consumes Dog Tongue/empties the cauldron only on success. Supported release shapes are the source instant block radius `3 + extent`, moving `brewgas`/`brewliquid` placement above the cauldron, and the triggered dropped-item curse entry point with one charge or two when a cat familiar is nearby. Focused tests lock the trigger cost, radius, coven-distance helper, item-curse charge math, and ritual delivery failure matrix. Still open: exact long-pulse ritual choreography, target modifiers/entity-target variants, and ritual-specific backfire oddities.

Current local delta (2026-06-13): **Placed Modonomicon, scrying/Waystone, Apothecary, and triggered-dispersal polish landed.** The placed Primer/guide-book block now stays tied to the live generated Modonomicon book data: entry index pages, gating, per-entry page order, back-to-entries button, text/button-only page-surface clicks, reverse/forward page-flip direction, hidden text during opening/flip animations, level-5 glow while attended, glow-off abandoned rest state, hidden ugly debug hitbox, and tighter text flow are all in the block renderer. Page item art now follows the same GUI `ItemStackRenderState` path as the normal Primer; sprite items remain flat sprites, while block/station items use the normal GUI isometric look and follow book rotation/tilt close to the paper. Crystal Ball scrying now uses the source-style held item routes: complete same-dimension Bound Waystones, Blooded Waystones, and filled Taglock Kits consume altar power where source does, drive a coordinate camera packet through either a live target marker or hidden `ScryingEyeEntity`, hide the held item while scrying, clamp living-target third-person camera to terrain, and restore the normal camera on release/finish/invalid/timeout. Waystone binding/copy/transposition outputs are on the distinct source IDs, and Ender Dew is now in the creative tab for QA. Apothecary villagers now bind to the building cauldron as their jobsite anchor, keep/repair the custom Apothecary name, look, and full levelled trade list, and old apothecaries recover the nearest cauldron when needed.

Current local delta (2026-06-13): **Full Disease brew + disease-block spread parity.** Rotten Flesh now restores the source `Brew of Disease`: level-4 capacity, 2000 altar power, three-minute `witchery:diseased`, strength modifier support, and source immunity gates for undead, demonic, werewolf, boss, golem, and named Witchery boss/minion targets. `Diseased` applies the capped source attack-damage penalty, ticks on the old 40-tick phase, and can leave a `witchery:disease` patch at the infected entity's feet on the source 1-in-3 spread chance. The new disease patch is no-drop, tinted, non-colliding, self-decays on the source 1-in-10 removal chance, and infects vulnerable living entities for 1-4 minutes on the source 1-in-3 contact chance. Locked by `BrewLogicTest`, `BrewActionMathTest`, and full `gradlew test`.

Current local delta (2026-06-13): **Custom Brew Cure/Ritual cleanup pass.** Normal Purified Milk now restores the 1.7.10 source disease exception: strength 2+ clears the registered `witchery:diseased` marker even though the source effect is incurable, and strength 3+ scans/removes Witchery disease blocks in a 9-block cube. Ritual Cure Debuffs backfire math is pinned for the future ritualized-brew path: harmful effects are eligible, reapply chance is 1% below modified strength, 25% at equal strength, 75% above strength, and readded effects come back one amplifier higher. Focused brew tests cover the thresholds, source marker, radius, and ritual math helpers. Still open: exact ritual pulse/backfire/target-modifier parity and non-Diseased `setIncurable()` cure hooks.

Current local delta (2026-06-13): **Source projectile transport parity for custom brews.** Supported fixed and composed thrown brews now use the old `EntityBrew` item-throw shape: eye-height minus 0.1, 0.16 block yaw backset, -20 pitch offset, 0.75 velocity, 1.0 inaccuracy, and 0.05 gravity. Hand throws, Brew Bag, Leonard's Urn, and Mystic Branch urn casts share the same source launch helper. Composed projectiles also save/load fixed and composed payload state, liquid brews render as liquid bottles in flight, splash delivery receives exact block-hit context, and gas/liquid placement follows source `Coord(mop, impactPosition, true)` semantics. Remaining custom-brew gaps are the wider dynamic action roster, exact ritual pulse/backfire/target-modifier parity, old spell-projectile route, and visual edge cases.

Current local delta (2026-06-13): **Gas Brew parity pass.** Iron Ore now contributes the source Gas Mask brew effect (`witchery:gas_mask`, level-2 capacity, 100 altar power, 90 seconds). Moving `brewgas` contact mirrors source protection semantics by skipping harmful potion effects only when the target already has Gas Mask; beneficial gas effects and non-potion actions still pass. Gas block entities now persist a per-brew max run cap derived from lifetime tiers, so Belladonna/Lapis/Hay extend moving gas from base 30 seconds through 60/150/300 seconds, and the invisible gas cloud now emits mostly brew-coloured dust with a lighter Witch-particle accent. `BrewLogicTest` and `BrewActionMathTest` lock the registry route, harmful-effect filter, and run-cap math. Remaining moving-brew gaps are wider dynamic action roster, exact ritual pulse/backfire/target-modifier parity, and visual edge cases.

Current local delta (2026-06-20): Triggered dispersal's **cursed-block family** is implemented, data-driven (no block replacement). A triggered (Zombie Head) splash that lands on a button, lever, pressure plate, wooden door, or ladder records a curse on that position in a per-level `CursedBlockData` (`SavedData`, persisted); the next activation/contact dumps the stored brew on whoever triggered it, and the record clears when the charges run out (re-cursing the same brew stacks a charge). Because the block is never swapped, it keeps its exact wood type/state, the curse is invisible, "revert" is automatic, and there is no registry/asset bloat. Activation hooks: `CursedBlockEvents` uses `PlayerInteractEvent.RightClickBlock` for buttons/levers/doors, a `LevelTickEvent.Post` rising-edge POWERED poll for pressure plates, and contact-entry polling for ladders. A parallel warning path is also live: say a line in public chat near a heated cauldron, add `Writable Book` to bind those words, add `Zombie Head`, bottle the Warning Brew, then throw it at supported blocks or loose items. The trigger shows the stored text as purple actionbar text with warning particles, and can be combined with normal triggered brew effects. `CursedBlocks.tryCurse`/`tryWarn` check the hit block and the block one step along the hit face (buttons/levers/plates/ladders can be pass-through or thin targets). The cursed cave well warns on its top ladder and seeds the triggered poison curse on a lower ladder. QA: `/witchery curse` plus a hand-made `Writable Book` warning brew. The ritual item-curse entry point also exists through Dog Tongue cauldron release; thrown triggered splashes remain the direct bottle route. `CursedBlockDataTest`/`CursedBlockWarningDataTest` + full suite green; in-world activation/redstone/ladder contact is manual-QA.

Current local delta (2026-06-12): Custom brew liquid polish and altar guide-book focus are live. Brew liquid now uses finite vanilla-like spread/decay, refuses to displace non-empty vanilla fluids, renders shallow tinted flowing states, keeps bottle/bucket/cauldron/liquid/particle colour in sync from `BrewContents.color()`, and custom splash brews play glass-break impact sound. A stored placed guide book / Modonomicon on one altar footprint block now grants a once-counted +1 recharge-scale focus bonus before Pentacle/Infinity multipliers, with immediate altar invalidation when the book is inserted or removed. Gas Mask/lifetime gas parity, Disease brew/block spread, and normal Purified Milk disease cleanup landed after this slice; remaining moving-brew work is the wider action roster and exact old ritualized pulse choreography.

Current local delta (2026-06-10): **Creaking familiar (Rite of the Creakening) — new additive feature + activation fix.** A `bind_creaking_familiar` rite (MEDIUM ritual ring, triggered by a dropped Creaking Heart with resin clump + pale oak sapling + Whiff of Magic + Tear of the Goddess + Mutandis Extremis offerings, plus a wild vanilla `Creaking` standing in the circle) binds the Creaking as a familiar (type id 5). Since `Creaking` is not a `TamableAnimal`, it uses a parallel implementation: per-entity `CREAKING_FAMILIAR` attachment (`CreakingFamiliarData`), `CreakingFamiliarHelper` (bind/restore/dismiss/follow/defend/sit + freeze-when-watched-by-non-owner + periodic resin drops + scare/sense ticks), and `CreakingFamiliarEvents`; `FamiliarHelper` was widened `TamableAnimal` → `LivingEntity` so both paths share callers. `/witchery familiar creaking`. **Fix:** the rite would not activate in-game because `RitualEvents.selectIngredientSource` chose the nearest dropped item of any type as the trigger, and the Creaking Heart is not one of the rite's own offerings (unlike `bind_familiar`), so a nearer offering was mis-selected and the rite silently failed; `selectIngredientSource` now explicitly prefers a dropped Creaking Heart on a MEDIUM ritual ring. Build + 134 unit tests green; dispatch mapping regression-locked in `RitualDefinitionTest`. In-game bind/follow/sit/dismiss feel remains manual-QA. (Note: this is an off-roadmap Pale Garden hook — `CURRENT_SCOPE_HANDOFF` lists Creaking/Pale Garden as "carefully scoped", not approved.)

Current local delta (2026-06-06, superseded by 2026-06-12/13 polish and 2026-06-17 gas-contact polish): Moving custom-brew gas/liquid spine restored for composed brews. Direct 1.7.10 `DispersalGas`, `DispersalLiquid`, `BlockBrewGas`, `BlockBrew`, and `TileEntityBrewFluid` comparison shows impact should place source IDs `witchery:brewgas` / `witchery:brewliquid`, store the brew NBT plus color/duration/expansion/thrower, tick at 5 ticks, spread outward, and apply contact effects at 0.25 power and 0.5 duration on the old 1-in-10 collision roll. The port now registers hidden/no-loot moving brew gas and visible moving brew liquid blocks with a shared `BrewFluidBlockEntity`; Bat Wool composed thrown brews place `brewgas`, Wormwood composed brews bottle as `Liquid Brew` and place `brewliquid`, and the source lifetime/extent helpers drive the first spread/decay spine. Gas contact has since been hardened so `brewgas` scans living entities from its scheduled gas tick on a deterministic every-other-5-tick pulse instead of relying on empty-shape collision callbacks; liquid keeps collision contact. JVM brew tests cover Wormwood liquid selection/bottling, replacement ordering, the removed Invisibility route, source fluid expansion/decay math, and deterministic gas contact cadence. Since this note, optimal-flow direction choice, slope/lower liquid rendering, brew buckets, the 1% adjacent block-action pulse, shared brew colour, splash glass sound, triggered cursed-block dispersal, Gas Mask protection, lifetime-scaled gas run caps, normal Purified Milk disease cleanup, and Disease brew/block spread have landed; remaining moving-brew gaps are wider dynamic action roster, exact old ritualized pulse choreography, and visual edge cases.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Snow Trail placement gates now match `PotionSnowTrail` more closely: entity footprints require air, source temperature below `1.6F`, and snow-layer survivability; Snow Golem/Snowball covering bursts use the old seven-block vertical search and only place over opaque-cube or ice-material support, with modern survivability retained as a safety check. The temperature path uses NeoForge-modified biome temperature plus a locally recreated vanilla-style `PerlinSimplexNoise` sample scaled through the old `y > 64`, `noise * 4`, `/ 30` source formula.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Coal / Extinguish, Stone / Dissipate, Dandelion / Grow Flowers, and Bone Meal / Fertilization are restored for the supported level-1 composed-brew action route. They are explicit-stir ingredients to preserve current dropped rite and cauldron offerings. Extinguish clears burning entities, damages Blazes when strengthened, respects the old Nether suppression until strength II+, and removes fire blocks in the source filled-circle column scan on block hits. Dissipate damages current source-tagged raised undead and removes tagged Witchery gas clouds; the old powered non-Witchery gaseous-fluid branch remains blocked until those block types are ported. Grow Flowers and Fertilization use source filled-circle radius `3 + extent + strength`, the old vertical three-block search, Dandelion plus nine old red-flower variants, and `strength + 1` bonemeal applications.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Snow Trail and Chilled block-hit composed-brew branches are restored for the supported instant/splash path. Direct 1.7.10 Snowball calls `PotionSnowTrail.createSnowCovering` at radius `2 + 2 * strength`, while Icy Needle checks the impact position and replaces connected water-material blocks through `BlockActionReplaceSphere` at the same radius. The port now passes explicit block-hit context from thrown composed brews, applies Snow Trail's source snow-covering burst only on real block hits, freezes connected vanilla water to Ice for Icy Needle / Chilled block hits, and leaves entity/gas delivery unchanged.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Slime Ball / Frog's Tongue Pull is restored for the normal supported composed-brew action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps Slime Ball to `witchery:brew.frogtongue`, 150 altar power, effect level 2, and old display name `Frog's Tongue`, while the cauldron book page names the behavior `Pull`. The port maps modern `minecraft:slime_ball` through level-2 capacity, pulls living targets and block-hit non-living entities with source `delta * 0.15` horizontal motion plus fixed `0.4` vertical motion, gives pulled living targets one second of Jump Boost II, and uses the source powered-targeting branch: base Pull goes to impact, strengthened Pull goes to the real caster.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Breath of the Goddess / Air Hike is restored for the normal supported composed-brew entity action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps old `itemBreathOfTheGoddess` to `witchery:brew.airhike`, 750 altar power, effect level 2, and old display name `Air Hike`. The port maps modern `witchery:breath_of_goddess` through level-2 capacity, clears fall distance, and launches affected living targets upward with the source `0.6 + 0.2 * strength` Y-motion formula. Existing Breath of the Goddess rite/recipe uses are unchanged.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Silver Deposits / Harm Werewolves is restored for the normal supported composed-brew entity action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps old `itemSilverDust` / `Silver Deposits` to `witchery:brew.harmwerewolves`, 1000 altar power, effect level 4, and display name `Harm Werewolves`. The port maps modern `witchery:silver_deposits` through level-4 capacity, applies the source `ceil((3 << cappedStrength) * splashPower)` damage table to Wolfman entities and actively transformed werewolf players, applies `ceil((1 << cappedStrength) * splashPower)` to other living targets, and uses a brew-scoped synthetic silver source for matching hits so existing Wolfman silver handling and transformed-player non-werewolf-caster silver bonus behavior can observe the old silver damage semantics. Were Villagers and unshifted werewolf players intentionally do not match the strong branch because the source uses `CreatureUtil.isWerewolf(target)` with `includeUnshifted=false`. The ritual rain/thunder branch from the old cauldron book remains blocked by missing exact ritualized pulse/target wiring.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Creeper Heart / Potion Longevity is restored for the normal supported composed-brew action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps Creeper Heart to `witchery:brew.durationboost`, 5000 altar power, effect level 6, and the old display name `Potion Longevity`. The port maps modern `witchery:creeper_heart` through level-6 capacity, extends active non-instant effects by up to their remaining duration or three minutes scaled by splash power, clears and reapplies active effects like source, and then adds Queasy for `3 * max(1, 4 - strength)` minutes. If used while already Queasy, it backfires with a one-in-three Hunger branch for 2 minutes or Nausea for 5 minutes.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Skeleton Skull / Steal Buffs is restored for the normal supported composed-brew action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps `Items.skull` metadata 0 to `witchery:brew.stealbuffs`, 100 altar power, effect level 6, and the old display name `Steal Buffs`. The port maps modern `minecraft:skeleton_skull` through level-6 capacity, scans nearby living entities within `(strength + 1) * 8` blocks of the affected target, and steals up to `ceil((strength + 1) * 2 * splashPower)` active positive curable effects whose amplifier is at or below brew strength. Existing matching effects on the target are only replaced when the donor effect is at least as long and at least as strong. Harm Werewolves is covered by the newer Silver Deposits delta above.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Ice / Demonbane is restored for the normal supported composed-brew direct damage action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps `Blocks.ice` to `witchery:brew.harmdemons`, 500 altar power, effect level 5, and the old display name `Demonbane`. The port maps modern `minecraft:ice` through level-5 capacity, applies the source `ceil((10 << cappedStrength) * splashPower)` damage table to source demonic targets and `ceil((3 << cappedStrength) * splashPower)` to non-demonic targets, keeps the normal strength cap at 1 unless Tropical Fish disables it up to 3, and uses the existing instant-splash action falloff. Demonic matching follows the old source set represented in the port: Demon, Ghast, Blaze, Magma Cube, Leonard, Lord of Torment, Flame Imp, Lilith, and Wither; Hellhounds are excluded because the source did not list them. Leonard uses the source special weakness route below 40% health with a 15-damage cap; the large Resizing 8-damage cap is tested as source math but has no live effect branch yet. Harm Werewolves is covered by the newer Silver Deposits delta above.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Garlic / Weaken Vampires is restored for the normal supported composed-brew action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps `SEEDS_GARLIC` to `witchery:brew.weakenvampires`, 500 altar power, effect level 4, and the old display name `Weaken Vampires`. The port maps modern `witchery:garlic` through level-4 capacity, drains vampire-player Blood Power by `50 + 20 * strength` with source `exact=false` semantics and creative-player bypass, applies Weakness for 90 seconds with source duration/splash scaling, and carries strength, duration, and Gold Nugget ambient/no-particles modifiers into action applications. Vampire matching uses the live Vampire entity and vampire player attachment. Harm Werewolves is covered by the newer Silver Deposits delta above.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Pumpkin / Undeadbane and Blue Orchid / Insectbane are restored for the normal supported composed-brew direct damage action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps Pumpkin to `witchery:brew.harmundead`, Blue Orchid (red-flower metadata 1) to `witchery:brew.harminsects`, 200 altar power, and effect level 2. The port maps modern `minecraft:pumpkin` and `minecraft:blue_orchid` through level-2 capacity, applies the source damage table of `ceil((10 << cappedStrength) * splashPower)` to matching undead/arthropod targets and `ceil((3 << cappedStrength) * splashPower)` to nonmatching targets, keeps the normal strength cap at 1 unless Tropical Fish disables it up to 3, and threads the same instant-splash falloff into action delivery. Undead matching includes vanilla undead tags plus the port Vampire entity/player attachment; arthropods use the modern vanilla `EntityTypeTags.ARTHROPOD`. Harm Werewolves is covered by the newer Silver Deposits delta above.

Current local delta (2026-06-06, updated 2026-06-13): Source Purified Milk / Cure Debuffs is restored for the normal supported composed-brew action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps Purified Milk to brew-name `witchery:brew.removedebuffs`, altar power 200, and effect level 2. The port maps `witchery:purified_milk` through level-2 capacity as an explicit-stir ingredient so dropped Purified Milk still feeds existing kettle recipes, then removes active harmful curable effects from the target when their amplifier is at or below the brew strength. The normal source disease exception is also restored: strength 2+ clears `witchery:diseased`, and strength 3+ removes nearby Witchery disease blocks in a 9-block cube. JVM tests cover capacity gating, altar cost, strength scaling, explicit-stir protection, positive/negative/curable gates, the disease exception, and the cleanup radius. Dog Tongue ritualized cauldron release now covers supported delivery; exact ritual pulse/backfire/target-modifier parity remains future work, and ritual Cure Debuffs backfire probabilities are pinned.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Reek of Misfortune / Cure Buffs is restored for the supported composed-brew action route. Direct 1.7.10 maps Reek of Misfortune to brew-name `witchery:brew.removebuffs`, altar power 250, and effect level 2. The port maps `witchery:reek_of_misfortune` through level-2 capacity as an explicit-stir ingredient so dropped Reek still feeds existing kettle recipes, then removes active positive curable effects from the target when their amplifier is at or below the brew strength. JVM tests cover capacity gating, altar cost, strength scaling, explicit-stir protection, and the positive/negative/curable source gates.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Grotesque / Fear is restored for the currently supported composed-brew entity-effect route. Direct 1.7.10 maps Infernal Blood to brew-name `witchery:brew.fear`, `Witchery.Potions.GROTESQUE`, 3 minutes, 500 altar power, and effect level 2; the old displayed effect name is `Grotesque`. The port registers `witchery:grotesque`, maps `witchery:infernal_blood` through level-2 capacity, and restores the source mob-only 4-block repulsion aura every five ticks, exempting demons, bosses, and golems. The old cosmetic demon-head overlay remains a renderer backlog item; the existing direct Brew of the Grotesque item behavior is unchanged.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Chilled / Cold is restored for the currently supported composed-brew entity-effect route. Direct 1.7.10 maps Icy Needle to brew-name `witchery:brew.cold`, `Witchery.Potions.CHILLED`, 3 minutes, 200 altar power, and effect level 2. The port registers `witchery:chilled` with the old display name `Chilled`, maps `witchery:icy_needle` through level-2 capacity, applies the source operation-2 movement slow, reduces fire damage with the old floor formula, deals the source 1 magic damage tick to Blazes or level-III+ Chilled targets on the old `25 >> amplifier` cadence, and restores the source block-hit connected-water freeze sphere.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Paralysis / Paralysed is restored for composed custom brews. Direct 1.7.10 maps Demon Heart to `Witchery.Potions.PARALYSED` / `witchery:brew.paralysis`, display name `Paralysed`, for 10 seconds, 750 altar power, and effect level 4. The port registers `witchery:paralysed`, maps `witchery:demon_heart` through the level-4 capacity route, applies the source operation-2 movement lock with player/boss/villager gates, resets Creeper fuse, pins vertical velocity to `-0.2`, removes high-amplifier paralysis when the target takes at least 1 damage, wires Swim Boost's player branch to stop while Paralysed, and applies the source 90-second `Queasy` after-effect when high-amplifier player Paralysed expires naturally. Vampire-directed villager AI remains documented as an open dependent-system blocker.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Sun Allergy / Undead's Curse is restored for composed custom brews. Direct 1.7.10 maps raw fish metadata 1 to `Witchery.Potions.SUN_ALLERGY` / `witchery:brew.allergysun`, display name `Undead's Curse`, for 60 seconds, 1000 altar power, and effect level 6; the port maps modern Salmon through that route and registers `witchery:sun_allergy`. Every 20 game ticks in source daytime, affected entities with brightness above `0.5`, sky access, no water, and a random roll below `brightness - 0.45` suffer the old branch: players take 1 fire damage, or 2 at amplifier 3+, while non-player helmeted entities damage the helmet by 0-1 durability and other non-player entities burn for 8 seconds.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Fortune / Luck is restored for composed custom brews. Direct 1.7.10 maps Clay Ball to `Witchery.Potions.FORTUNE` / `witchery:potion.fortune`, display name `Luck`, for 3 minutes, 1000 altar power, and effect level 6. The port registers `witchery:fortune`, maps modern Clay Ball through the level-6 capacity route, and restores the source harvest-drop hook through NeoForge `BlockDropsEvent`: while a player has Luck, non-silk, non-block-entity block drops that already exist are recomputed with current Fortune plus `+1`, or `+2` when the Luck amplifier is above 2.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Swim Boost is restored for the player movement half of the composed custom-brew route. Direct 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` maps raw fish metadata 0 to `Witchery.Potions.SWIMMING` / `witchery:brew.swimming` for 3 minutes, 0 altar power, and effect level 1; the port maps modern Cod through that route and registers `witchery:swimming` with the old display name `Swim Boost`. While the local player has Swim Boost, is in water, is not Paralysed, and is pressing forward, the client tick path multiplies horizontal water motion by the source player branch constant `1.15 + 0.03 * 3 = 1.24`. The source non-player paralyzed water branch remains documented until it has a safe modern movement delivery shape.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Snow Trail is restored as a composed custom-brew mob effect. Snowball now uses the level-1 capacity route to produce `witchery:snow_trail` for 3 minutes at 0 altar power. Affected entities lay source-style snow footprints every 10 ticks in biomes below temperature `1.6F`, Snow Golems carrying the effect have the old 1-in-20 overload burst, 3-power explosion, radius-8 snow covering, and discard behavior, and real splash block hits now trigger the source `2 + 2 * strength` snow-covering burst.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source effect-level capacity gating is restored for supported composed custom-brew potion effects and the existing Heart of Gold Animal Attraction / Repulsion action route. The resolver now mirrors the old `EffectLevelCounter` add/consume model: Mandrake Root opens level 1, Nether Wart opens level 2, Nether Wart + Tear of the Goddess opens level 4, supported potion/action ingredients consume their source level before applying, including Demon Heart's level-4 Paralysis route, and under-capacity stir attempts are refused instead of producing an active effect.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Ink Sac Blindness is restored in composed custom brews. Modern Ink Sac now produces vanilla Blindness for 15 seconds at 1000 altar power, matching the old `Dye.INK_SAC` / `witchery:brew.blindness` registry entry for the currently supported potion-effect path. The existing fixed Brew of Ink item behavior and ritual Ink Sac offering routes are unchanged.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Wolfsbane is restored as a real composed-brew potion marker. Wolfsbane Flower now produces `witchery:wolfsbane` for 60 seconds at 0 altar power, player werewolf suppression accepts that source marker while preserving the existing carried-flower shortcut, and source-converted Wolfmen now check Wolfsbane rather than the previous Poison placeholder before reverting to Were Villager off the full moon.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source vanilla-effect brew ingredients for Wither, Absorption, and Health Boost are restored in composed custom brews. Wither Skeleton Skull now produces vanilla Wither for 15 seconds at 200 altar power, Golden Apple produces Absorption for 30 seconds at 1000 altar power, and Enchanted Golden Apple produces Health Boost for 2 minutes at 1000 altar power, matching the old skull/golden-apple metadata routes.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Moonshine / Feel No Pain and Stout Belly are restored as real composed-brew potion effects. Wheat now produces `witchery:moonshine` for 90 seconds at 0 altar power, converting player damage from source mob/player/living attackers into source-shaped food loss while food is available and adding the amplified random Hunger side effect. Foul Fume now produces `witchery:stout_belly` for 90 seconds at 1000 altar power and clears Hunger only when amplified above level I, matching the old tick gate. Non-Diseased source `setIncurable()` cure hooks remain future work.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Ender Inhibition is restored as a real composed-brew potion effect. Ender Dew now produces `witchery:ender_inhibition` for 90 seconds at 200 altar power, and the effect cancels source-equivalent ender entity and ender pearl teleports while active.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Poison Weapons is restored as a real composed-brew potion effect. Red Mushroom now produces `witchery:poison_weapons` for 90 seconds at 200 altar power, and the effect gives melee attackers the old poison/wither-on-hit table while ignoring non-melee damage.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Reflect Projectiles and Attract Projectiles are restored as real composed-brew potion effects. Cobweb now produces `witchery:reflect_projectiles` for 90 seconds at 250 altar power, while Fermented Spider Eye + Cobweb produces `witchery:attract_projectiles` for 45 seconds, with the old projectile reflection/homing tick logic.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Repell Attacker is restored as a real composed-brew potion effect. Bramble now produces `witchery:repell_attacker` for 90 seconds at 250 altar power, and the effect shoves nearby non-projectile living attackers away from the protected entity with the old amplifier-scaled pushback formula.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Feather Fall is restored as a real composed-brew potion effect. Feather now produces `witchery:feather_fall` for 60 seconds at 100 altar power, with the old velocity/fall-distance clamp behavior; Phantom Membrane no longer counts as a Witchery custom-brew ingredient.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Jump is restored in composed custom brews. Leather now produces Jump Boost for 3 minutes at 200 altar power, matching 1.7.10 `WitcheryBrewRegistry` and Resurrected `jump.json`; Rabbit Foot is no longer treated as a Witchery custom-brew ingredient.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source Blaze brew roles are corrected. Blaze Rod is now the tier-2 strength modifier for composed brews, matching 1.7.10 and Resurrected `power_2.json`; Blaze Powder is no longer treated as that modifier and instead restores the old Damage Boost / Weakness potion-effect ingredient. Blaze Powder remains explicit-stir-only so dropped stacks still feed the Infernal Chalk cauldron upgrade route.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source composed-brew effect modifiers now include Gold Nugget and Tropical Fish for the supported potion-effect path. Gold Nugget maps to the old `noParticles`/ambient flag for 50 altar power and is explicit-stir-only so dropped Gold Nuggets still feed Golden Chalk cauldron offerings. Tropical Fish represents original fish metadata 2 / Resurrected `minecraft:fish,2`, costs 200 altar power, and disables per-effect strength ceilings without being confused with Pufferfish/fish metadata 3 Water Breathing. Brick/Nether Brick target toggles are live for the supported potion/action route: Nether Brick disables block targets, Brick disables entity targets, and both persist after effect/action reset; old liquid/block moving-brew routing remains separate backlog.

Current local delta (2026-06-06): Source composed-brew instant/splash extent and lifetime impact modifiers are restored. Direct 1.7.10 and Resurrected comparisons map Wood Ash -> extent tier 1 / 50 altar power, Cocoa Beans -> tier 2 / 100 power, and Wispy Cotton -> tier 3 / 150 power; Belladonna -> lifetime tier 1 / 50 power, Lapis Lazuli -> tier 2 / 100 power, and Hay Block -> tier 3 / 150 power. The composed-brew resolver now caps those tiers, counts their altar costs, feeds `DispersalInstant` radius `3 + extent` for supported splash potion-effect delivery, feeds moving `brewgas`/`brewliquid` decay with source lifetime ratios, and maps moving-fluid expansion to source `4 + extent` capped at 10. Exact optimal liquid flow, the 1% block-action pulse, brew buckets, renderer/color polish, Gas Mask protection, and lifetime-scaled moving gas caps have landed; source projectile transport core has landed; wider dynamic action roster remains moving-brew follow-up.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): **Guidebook progression system.** The native Modonomicon Witches' Primer is now a single growing book of **12 book-aligned categories** (Getting Started always open + 11 gated), 51 content entries plus 11 "What Comes Next" next-recipe entries. Players are **given the Primer on first join** (new `WitcheryAttachments.RECEIVED_PRIMER` flag) and can re-craft it cheaply (Book + black dye + feather). Each topic book, when crafted, **unlocks its Primer category** (hidden until then via INDEX-mode parent-screen condition filtering) and unlocks the **next** book's recipe through a 12-step advancement chain under `data/witchery/advancement/guide/`. The 4 previously book-less topics got new books (`book_poppets`, `book_spirit_world`, `book_creatures`, `book_transformations`). Debug: `/witchery guide unlock [all|<book>]` / `/witchery guide reset`. **Polish pass:** the brew reagents (Odour of Purity / Breath of the Goddess / Whiff of Magic) are **Witches' Oven** products (sapling→fume), so **reordered Collecting Fumes before Brews & Infusions** (Oven must precede the books that need its fumes) — fixing the real craft-order deadlock; chain audited end-to-end (every reagent obtainable from strictly-earlier knowledge or vanilla) and the 12-step advancement chain re-validated intact + acyclic. Added **baked station-recipe images**: `tools/recipe_image.py` composites the real Witches' Oven / Distillery GUI with item sprites at the menu's slot coordinates into Modonomicon `BookImagePageModel` textures (vanilla recipe pages only handle 3×3 crafting), wired into Collecting Fumes (5 oven brews) and Distilling (the 2-in/4-out run). The 4 new books have dedicated thematic textures (voodoo doll / cyan wisp / amber eye / blood-moon) on the shared purple guide-book base. **Upgrade:** replaced the baked recipe PNGs with a custom Modonomicon page `witchery:station_recipe` that renders **live ItemStacks** on the station GUI background, giving real **hover tooltips + stack counts** for every recipe item (oven/distillery/kettle/cauldron). Registered via `LoaderRegistry.registerPageLoader` (common setup) + `PageRendererRegistry.registerPageRenderer` (client setup). Compiles + `runData` + `build` green; in-game QA pending. Village Keep ladders and Apothecary goto landing corrected after live QA. Source `ComponentVillageKeep` uses old ladder meta `2` for tower shafts and both bridge spans; the fixed NORTH-oriented port now places all Keep ladders as `LadderBlock.FACING=NORTH`, making them attach to the generated south-side supports instead of rendering 180 degrees into air. `/witchery goto apothecary` now anchors to the transformed front of the source-shaped Apothecary at `bb.maxZ()+1` and prefers a safe village road/path landing near that front door before falling back to doorway-height safe landing.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Village Watch Tower upper ladder handoff now extends three blocks farther down for live traversal. The lower source shaft remains local `(4,1..12,4)`, while the offset upper span now covers `(3,10..15,5)` instead of `(3,13..15,5)` and uses its own support-facing state against the solid south wall. This keeps the current player-tested lower shaft facing while filling the missing upper-span blocks found in fresh QA.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Natural Rowan tree placement rarity is halved by request. The shared `witchery:rowan_tree` placed feature now uses `rarity_filter` chance `200` instead of `100`, reducing natural Rowan placement attempts from `1-in-100` to `1-in-200` while leaving sapling growth, configured tree shape, clearance checks, and wild-tree Belladonna decoration behavior unchanged.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Building-targeted village goto now reuses the stable generic village arrival prep for unseen villages. `/witchery goto apothecary` still filters the ticked safe-locate candidates to starts containing `WitcheryVillageElement[apothecary]` and still lands through the Apothecary doorway/floor anchor, but it no longer forces a building-only landing shortcut for fresh villages. Unprocessed target villages full-load the village footprint one chunk per tick, hold prep tickets, and run the same wall/gate generation path as `/witchery goto village_*`; only already-processed villages use the smaller known-village landing bubble.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Gatehouse lantern bracket states now connect back toward the generated gatehouse face for both wall and fence bracket materials, so the hanging lantern support no longer renders as an isolated post beside the tower. Village Watch Tower ladders keep the lower decompiled source shaft `(4,1..12,4)` and now use the extended upper handoff `(3,10..15,5)`, with per-span support-facing states so each run sticks onto a wall in-game.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Meadow villages now have explicit plains-family coverage. 1.21.11 vanilla data exposes Meadow village generation through `minecraft:has_structure/village_plains` (`Biomes.MEADOW`) and the existing `minecraft:village_plains` / `village/plains/houses` path, not a separate loaded `village_meadow` pool in this workspace. Witchery building injection and automatic walls therefore apply to Meadow villages through the plains path; `VillageBuildingMaterials` also recognizes `village_meadow`/`village/meadow` defensively as plains. `/witchery goto village_meadow` now performs a biome-filtered safe locate over plains-village candidates and accepts only Meadow centers.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Witchery village palettes are now resolved from the enclosing vanilla village `StructureStart` instead of per-piece terrain-biome samples. `VillageBuildingMaterials` has explicit village-family palettes for plains, desert, savanna, taiga, and snowy villages; Apothecary, Bookshop, village Witch Hut, Watch Tower, Keep, and the generated wall/gate palette all share that resolved family. This fixes live QA where a plains village Watch Tower generated with savanna terracotta/acacia after sampling nearby savanna terrain. Terrain-biome sampling remains only as a fallback when no village start can be resolved.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Ent Twig is now visible in the Witchery creative tab's ingredients/alchemy section for QA and recipe setup. The right-click Debug Inspector keeps its state/altar/circle inspection behavior but now renders as the Ent Twig art with its existing forced foil glint, making it easy to identify as the glowing QA twig.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Village Apothecary roof bands now use the shared biome roof-solid palette instead of the foundation block, so taiga/snowy Apothecaries use spruce roof solids with their spruce stair rows. The modern village-building one-per-type cap is also deterministic from the `StructureStart`: only the canonical planned origin for each Witchery building type may place, and `/witchery goto apothecary` anchors to that same canonical candidate. This intentionally tightens the port beyond 1.7.10 `WorldHandlerVillageApothecary`, which could request up to two Apothecaries in larger source villages.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Savanna village walls and gatehouses now use the requested terracotta-forward palette. Wall bodies, wall-walk floors, gate pillars, and gate arches use Orange Terracotta; merlons, over-gate rails/lantern brackets, and rampart lips use Red Sandstone wall/stair pieces because vanilla has no terracotta shaped variants. Savanna roads/path grading still use Dirt Path plus Dirt support, and desert/badlands/plains/taiga/snowy wall palettes are unchanged.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): `/witchery setup stations` QA hut gable rows are narrowed after screenshot QA showed plank infill cutting through the pitched stair roof. The bottom gable row still closes the wall under the overhang, but the two higher rows now fill only the air under the roof line, keeping the Rowan stair surfaces and Rowan-log ridge visible.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Village Watch Tower ladders now use the original two-run shape again with modern support-safe facings. The lower source shaft is local `(4,1..12,4)`, and the offset upper handoff has been extended from the old `(3,13..15,5)` span to `(3,10..15,5)` after live traversal QA found three lower blocks missing. The temporary straight-shaft extension and cobble support post are removed.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Village Watch Tower roof cap and corner stair visuals corrected. Source `ComponentVillageWatchTower` places the peak slab as old wooden-slab metadata `0`, so the modern Watch Tower now uses the biome bottom slab instead of a top slab at local `4,22,4`. The source stair rows overlap at inner roof corners; because modern stairs store corner joins in `StairsShape` instead of deriving them at render time, the two inner roof rings now explicitly mark those eight corner stairs as outer L-shapes while leaving the source footprint, stair facings, ladders, chest, guards, and biome materials intact.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): `/witchery setup stations` QA hut roof stairs are rotated 180 degrees from the first pitched-roof pass. The command still builds the same custom 11x11 Rowan tester hut, foundation, stations, boosted altar, stocked chest, traders, garden, ridge, gables, and overhangs; only the left/right slope stair facings are swapped so every roof stair presents the opposite orientation requested by live visual QA.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): QA plant/trinket follow-up after the Dream Catcher source pass. Blood Rose self-sampling with a Taglock Kit is now allowed as a requested port exception with its own Blood Rose message, while Boline-only Blood Rose drops and other stored-sample own-sample refusals remain intact. Leaping Lily items now use the vanilla water-surface placement route. Critter Snare release now clears caught state around the spawn path to stop repeat-release cloning, and vanilla Rabbits are accepted for the requested desert hare/bunny capture extension. Alluring Skull expectations are documented as source pathing-only: undead are path-navigated toward the lit skull, but existing player attack targets are not cleared or overridden.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Dream Catcher host/weave handling now follows the old hidden-block pattern more closely. The standalone `dream_catcher` host item remains registered for internal/book/icon compatibility but is no longer shown in the Witchery creative tab; the five Dream Weaver items are the normal placeable objects. A Dream Weaver places the wall-hung host carrying that weave, occupied catchers refuse a second weave instead of swapping/duplicating, and break/support loss returns only the stored weave. The placed model now renders frame/decorations separately from five weave-specific inner net overlays using the original `DreamWeave` texture offsets.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): `/witchery goto apothecary` now exists as a building-targeted QA command. It searches all five vanilla village structure families through the existing ticked safe locate queue and accepts only candidate starts whose pool pieces include `WitcheryVillageElement[apothecary]`. Follow-up QA showed the building-only landing shortcut could still freeze on unseen villages, so fresh building-targeted goto now uses the same full village arrival prep and wall/gate generation path as generic village gotos. Live landing QA then corrected the transformed front anchor and made the final resolver prefer the village road/path in front of the Apothecary door.

Tester QA result (2026-06-05): Apothecary loot/build/room checks are confirmed acceptable, and the Apothecary chest contents are OK; named villager/trades were not tested. `/witchery goto apothecary` no longer uses the unstable unseen-village building-only shortcut, and the latest landing patch should place the player on the path in front of the transformed Apothecary door rather than behind/roof. Rowan Door redstone retest is confirmed OK. Garlic Garland pass-through, break/drop, vampire pushback, and survival vampire break prevention are confirmed; art stretching/hitbox width were patched for retest.

Tester QA result (2026-06-05): bloodied Wickerman ignition is confirmed in-game. Lighting a valid bloody straw man / Wickerman spawned the Horned Huntsman, and follow-up combat QA says the Huntsman feels good. Remaining Wicker/Huntsman QA is narrowed to break/blast feel, exact structure offset details, Demonic Blood compatibility, axis preservation if not directly observed, and exact Huntsman pose/scale/arrow/wolf/drop details.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Apothecary chest loot is now gameplay-tuned instead of source-exact, per tester direction, while the custom story-book overlay is preserved. The `witchery:chests/apothecary` table now rolls 3-6 focused ingredient/utility stacks built around bottles, food, clay, small currency, Witchery herbs/components, Mutandis/Chalk/Taglock support, and rare Water Diviner, Voodoo Poppet, Fire Protection Poppet, Skeleton Skull, and Diamond pulls. The previous random enchanted-book entry is removed; `witchery:village_story_books` remains registered as the unchanged 18% global modifier for Apothecary and Bookshop chests.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Rowan Door redstone bypass fixed. Source `BlockWitchDoor.func_150014_a` ignores redstone-open requests for Rowan doors, while still allowing the redstone-off close call. The modern Rowan Door now places closed beside power, records neighbor power without setting `OPEN=true`, and closes on power removal only when already open. Manual key/keyring behavior, UUID storage, lower-half loot, toughness, models, and recipes are unchanged.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Witchery planks toughness parity is restored. Source `BlockWitchWood` covers rowan/alder/hawthorn metadata variants, calls `setHardness(2.0F)`, and has no explicit resistance override, so the split modern plank registrations now use `2.0F` hardness plus old implied `10.0F` blast resistance while preserving recipes, tags, loot, models, fuel/flammability expectations, altar and Brew of Sprouting use, and Ice World block-family conversion behavior.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Witchery leaves toughness parity is restored. Source `BlockWitchLeaves` covers rowan/alder/hawthorn metadata variants, calls `setHardness(0.2F)`, and has no explicit resistance override, so the split modern leaves registrations now use `0.2F` hardness plus old implied `1.0F` blast resistance while preserving no-occlusion rendering, random ticks/decay, drops, sapling/berry outcomes, creative placement, altar and Brew of Sprouting use, and particles.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Witchery log toughness parity is restored. Source `BlockWitchLog` covers rowan/alder/hawthorn metadata variants, calls `setHardness(2.0F)`, and has no explicit resistance override, so the split modern log registrations now use `2.0F` hardness plus old implied `10.0F` blast resistance while preserving rotated-pillar placement, drops, fuel/flammability, leaf decay, altar and Brew of Sprouting use, setup-hut use, and Ent break-spawn behavior.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Rowan and Alder Door toughness parity is restored. Source `DOOR_ROWAN` and `DOOR_ALDER` are both `BlockWitchDoor` registrations that call `setHardness(5.0F)` and `setHardness(3.0F)` with no explicit resistance overrides, so the modern Rowan/Alder Door registrations now use `5.0F/25.0F` and `3.0F/15.0F` hardness/resistance while preserving Rowan lock/key flow, Alder redstone behavior, loot, recipes, models, and textures. Hawthorn Door remains untouched because it follows the later Resurrected `BloodTrap` path.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Pit Dirt / Pit Grass toughness parity is restored. Source `BlockPitDirt` calls `setHardness(0.5F)` and source `BlockPitGrass` calls `setHardness(0.6F)` with no explicit resistance overrides, so the modern trap blocks now use `0.5F/2.5F` and `0.6F/3.0F` hardness/resistance while preserving pass-through camouflage, loot, creative placement, names, state guards, and Tormented Twine cauldron creation.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Blood-stained Wool toughness parity is restored. Source `BlockBloodedWool` calls `setHardness(0.8F)` and has no explicit resistance override, so shared modern `bloodedwool` / `blood_stained_wool` properties now use `0.8F` hardness plus old implied `4.0F` blast resistance while preserving Bone Needle creation, compatibility smelting, loot, block art, and vampire clothing mechanics.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Statue of Hobgoblin Patron toughness parity is restored. Source `STATUE_OF_WORSHIP` uses `BlockStatueOfWorship`, which sets `3.5F` hardness and `20.0F` blast resistance; the modern `statue_hobgoblin_patron` registration now carries that pair while preserving the current practical worship-count, feedback, anger chance, and Hobgoblin retaliation behavior.

Current local delta (2026-06-05): Garlic Garland wall render and targeting are restored closer to source. Source `BlockGarlicGarland` calls `setHardness(0.2F)`, has no explicit resistance override, no collision box, and is rendered by `RenderGarlicGarland` as a wall hanging rather than crossed bush art. The modern registration keeps `0.2F` hardness plus old implied `1.0F` blast resistance, facing, no-collision/click-through behavior, vampire repulsion/counterplay, and recipe. Follow-up QA confirmed pass-through and exposed an inverted placement-side mapping, stretched flat art, and too-narrow target outline; placement now stores the opposite horizontal player direction, the wall plane uses the full 16x16 face to preserve the 32x32 texture aspect, and the interaction shape covers the full block-face width for retest.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Wicker Bundle toughness parity is restored. Source `BlockWickerBundle` covers both plain and bloodied metadata variants, calls `setHardness(0.5F)`, and has no explicit resistance override, so the split modern registrations now use `0.5F` hardness plus old implied `2.5F` blast resistance while preserving rotated pillars, flammability, blooding, crafting, Wickerman validation/ignition, and Horned Huntsman summon behavior. Tester QA confirms Infernal Blood stains five nearby plain bundles and a bloodied Wickerman ignites/spawns the Horned Huntsman.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Wolf Altar toughness parity is restored. Source `WOLF_ALTAR` is `BlockStatueWerewolf`, which sets `2.5F` hardness and `1000.0F` blast resistance; the port now registers that full pair without changing the current practical Moon Charm exchange, werewolf mastery boon, active-transformation hooks, or Wolftrap full-moon bait adjacency checks.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Sun Collector toughness parity is restored. Source `BlockDaylightCollector` calls `setHardness(3.5F)`, implying old blast resistance `17.5F`; the port now registers that full pair without changing recipe, footprint, low light, Quartz Sphere storage/rendering, daylight charging, Charged Quartz Sphere output, Sun Grenade handoff, or old cross-plane art.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Coffin blast resistance parity is restored. Source `BlockCoffin` calls `setHardness(1.0F)`, implying old blast resistance `5.0F`; the port now registers that full pair without changing placement gates, support checks, lid obstruction, sleep/vampire behavior, rendering, footprint, or the flat item icon.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Critter Snare default toughness parity is restored. Source `BlockCritterSnare` sets sound/bounds but never calls `setHardness`, and `BlockBaseBush` does not set hardness or blast resistance, so the port now registers Critter Snare with the old default `0.0F` profile while preserving caught-state storage, support-loss drops, catch/release behavior, Polynesia stock-bat handling, and sprig creation routes.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Wolf Head and Hellhound Head default toughness parity is restored. Source `BlockWolfHead` covers both skull types and never calls `setHardness`, and `BlockBaseContainer` does not set hardness or blast resistance, so the split modern mounted-head registrations now use the old default `0.0F` profile while preserving placement, rotation, self-drops, icons, and BESR rendering.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Grassper default toughness parity is restored. Source `BlockGrassper` never calls `setHardness`, and its `BlockBaseContainer` parent does not set hardness or blast resistance, so the port now registers Grassper with the old default `0.0F` profile while preserving its stash, comparator, removal-drop, facing, and held-item render behavior.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Demon Heart toughness parity is restored. Original `BlockDemonHeart` calls `setHardness(1.0F)`, implying old blast resistance `5.0F`; the port now registers that pair without changing the custom animated model, low light, facing, ambience, heartbeat pulse, or Blight prototype's separate heart blocks.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Chalice, Crystal Ball, and Dream Catcher toughness parity is restored. Source constructors only call `setHardness(3.0F)`, `setHardness(2.0F)`, and `setHardness(1.0F)`, so the old Block setter also implied blast resistance `15.0F`, `10.0F`, and `5.0F`. The port now registers those exact pairs without changing filled Chalice state, Crystal Ball prediction/scrying routes, or Dream Catcher wall/no-collision/weave behavior.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Broom damage lifecycle is closer to source. A 1.7.10 `EntityBroom.attackEntityFrom` comparison showed hits should accumulate `damage * 10` into a decaying `damageTaken` counter and only break the broom above `40.0F` or on creative-player damage. The port now keeps small-hit brooms placed instead of immediately ejecting/dropping/discarding them, while preserving the matching normal/enchanted broom drop, custom name, and source `BrushColor` data on real breaks.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Candelabra toughness parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockCandelabra` constructor comparison showed the source calls `setHardness(2.0F)`, which also implies `10.0F` blast resistance through the old Block setter. The port now registers the Candelabra at `2.0f` hardness and `10.0f` blast resistance, without changing light output, support-required placement/drop behavior, five-point flame/smoke ambience, footprint, item renderer assets, or altar contribution behavior.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Poppet Shelf toughness parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockPoppetShelf` constructor comparison showed the source calls `setHardness(2.5F)`, which also implies `12.5F` blast resistance through the old Block setter. The port now registers the Poppet Shelf at `2.5f` hardness and `12.5f` blast resistance, without changing storage, bound-poppet chunk loading, comparator behavior, facing, footprint, renderer assets, or client slot sync.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Brazier toughness parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockBrazier` constructor comparison showed the source calls `setHardness(3.5F)`, which also implies `17.5F` blast resistance through the old Block setter. The port now registers the Brazier at `3.5f` hardness and `17.5f` blast resistance, without changing ingredient storage, ignition, redstone/comparator behavior, player-break drops, water-bottle reset, footprint, renderer assets, or sided automation.

The port now registers both normal and filtered Fume Funnels at `3.5f` hardness and `17.5f` blast resistance, without changing linked-oven opening, filter upgrades, side/top byproduct behavior, renderer assets, recipes, or sided oven automation.
