Current local delta (2026-06-04): Altar toughness parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockAltar` constructor comparison showed the source calls `setHardness(2.0F)`, which also implies `10.0F` blast resistance through the old Block setter. The port now registers the Altar at `2.0f` hardness and `10.0f` blast resistance, without changing strict 2x3 validation, altar power/contributor logic, renderer/model assets, or altar-top placed-item behavior.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Kettle toughness parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockKettle` constructor comparison showed the source calls `setHardness(2.0F)`, which also implies `10.0F` blast resistance through the old Block setter. The port now registers the Kettle at `2.0f` hardness and `10.0f` blast resistance, without changing its restored side/ceiling support, original model renderer, bucket flow, recipe handling, or sided automation.

Current local delta (2026-06-04): Brazier source interaction parity is restored for break drops and water-bottle resets. A 1.7.10 `BlockBrazier.breakBlock` comparison showed idle Braziers drop their stored ingredient slots, while burning Braziers drop one `Wood Ash`. The port already covered idle player-break ingredient drops; burning player-breaks now drop `witchery:wood_ash` instead of losing the active burn state and ingredients with no ash. A direct `BlockBrazier.func_149727_a` comparison also showed plain water bottles reset non-empty Braziers and return Glass Bottles, so vanilla water-potion stacks now clear loaded Braziers before the generic ingredient path can accept them.

Current local delta (2026-06-02): Alluring Skull unbreakable hardness parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockAlluringSkull` comparison showed the source calls the old unbreakable setter and only reapplies `1000.0F` blast resistance afterward. The port now registers the Alluring Skull with unbreakable hardness instead of the previous `1.0F`, while preserving the live low light, floor/wall placement, compact footprint, undead allure pathing behavior, renderer, and Necromantic Stone toggle/removal route. Follow-up source review confirmed the old allure code only path-navigates undead toward the lit skull and does not clear or override an existing survival-player attack target.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Poppet Shelf hardness parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockPoppetShelf` comparison showed the shelf has `2.5F` hardness. The port registered the Poppet Shelf with that source hardness instead of `2.0F`; the 2026-06-04 toughness pass added the old implied `12.5F` blast resistance without changing restored storage, chunk-loading, comparator, facing, footprint, or renderer behavior.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Leech Chest feline lid-blocker parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockLeechChest` comparison showed the blocked-open helper only scans sitting `EntityOcelot` instances above the chest, not every sitting tameable. The port now maps that source feline blocker to modern Cats plus Witchery Cat Familiar and Spectral Familiar entities, so sitting wolves and non-feline familiars no longer prevent opening.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Leech Chest toughness parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockLeechChest` comparison showed the block has `25.0F` hardness and `1000.0F` blast resistance. The port now registers the Leech Chest with that source toughness instead of ordinary chest strength, without changing its restored storage, taglock, redstone, drop-data, or Mutating Sprig behavior.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Coffin lid obstruction parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockCoffin` comparison showed sneak-use refuses to toggle the lid when either half has a solid block immediately above it. The port now checks for a sturdy underside above both the head and foot halves before syncing `OPEN`, so blocked Coffins silently leave the lid state unchanged like source.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Coffin placement gate and toughness parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `ItemCoffin` / `BlockCoffin` comparison showed the item only places from a top-face use, requires air in both foot/head spaces, requires solid support under both halves, and the block uses `1.0F` hardness with old implied `5.0F` blast resistance. The port now refuses invalid side-face, blocked-space, unsupported-foot, and unsupported-head placements before creating either half, and the Coffin registration is back on source toughness.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Mutating Sprig creature-route feedback and footprint parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `ItemMutator.onItemUseFirst` comparison showed the Critter Snare, Flying Monkey, Owl, Toad, and Parasytic Louse routes all use a center `ParticleEffect.SLIME` plus big-slime sound instead of generic smoke/grass-place feedback; the consumed creatures also get their source slime-wrapped death-sound cues, Flying Monkey clears the four cardinal cocoa blocks, and Parasytic Louse clears the four cardinal Vine positions. The port now restores those source feedback and clearing paths, and the shared sprig slime helper uses the old width-derived particle density/randomized pitch instead of treating packet range as a particle count.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Mutating Sprig plant-route feedback parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `ItemMutator.onItemUseFirst` comparison showed the Cactus-to-Wild-Bramble, Reed-to-Void-Bramble, and Wheat-to-Wormwood routes each use source `ParticleEffect.SLIME` plus the big-slime sound at the clicked center, while the vertical bramble routes also send silent slime particles at each converted cactus/reed block. The port now plays that source-shaped feedback for those three plant routes instead of the generic smoke/grass-place finish cue, while preserving their existing source footprints and one-durability sprig use.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Mutating Sprig chest-family feedback parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `ItemMutator.onItemUseFirst` comparison showed the Chest-to-Grasspers, Trapped-Chest-to-Leech-Chest, and Leech-Chest-to-Blood-Roses routes each use source `ParticleEffect.SLIME` plus the big-slime sound at the center. The port now plays that source-shaped slime feedback for those three chest-family routes instead of the generic smoke/grass-place finish cue, while preserving their existing source footprints and one-durability sprig use.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Mutating Sprig Grass/Mycelium feedback parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `ItemMutator.onItemUseFirst` comparison showed the basic Grass Block <-> Mycelium flips use the same old instant-spell plus big-slime feedback family, with 8 particles at the block's `y + 1.0` position. The port now gives those simple block conversions source-shaped feedback instead of the generic smoke/grass-place cue while preserving the one-durability sprig use.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Mutating Sprig underwater clay footprint parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `ItemMutator.onItemUseFirst` / `ItemGeneral.setBlockToClay` comparison showed the source route is one-way Dirt-to-Clay, starts only from Dirt with actual water above, tries the center plus four cardinal cells, and only converts each cell if that cell is also Dirt with water above. The port now removes the non-source clay-to-dirt and ice-above branches and restores the five-cell conversion footprint with the old slime/instant-spell feedback on converted cells.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Mutating Sprig Leech Chest vine consumption parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `ItemMutator.isMutatableTrapChest` comparison showed the source route removes the empty Trapped Chest plus all four adjacent Vine blocks before setting the center Leech Chest. The port now clears those Vines during the mutation instead of leaving them hanging around the newly created Leech Chest.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Mutating Sprig Critter Snare footprint parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `ItemMutator.isMutatableWeb` comparison showed the source route uses four planted cardinal Alder Saplings around a cobweb over water plus a nearby Zombie, kills the Zombie with the old zombie-death/slime feedback, clears the center cobweb, and places four empty Critter Snares in the cardinal positions. The port now follows that footprint instead of accepting dropped Alder Sapling items and replacing the center cobweb with a single snare.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Altar object and Critter Snare support-loss drop parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockCandelabra` / `BlockChalice` / `BlockCrystalBall` comparison showed support loss drops the old item form before clearing the block, and `BlockCritterSnare` inherits the same invalid-support drop path from old `BlockBush`. The port now avoids the modern shape-update-to-air shortcut for those blocks, letting their drop-aware neighbor-change hooks preserve Candelabra, empty/filled Chalice, Crystal Ball, and empty/caught Critter Snare item state.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Critter Snare Polynesia bat-stock parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockCritterSnare` / `ItemPolynesiaCharm` comparison showed caught bats preserve the source `WitcheryShopStock` merchant-inventory marker as metadata `9` and restore an empty stock tag when released. The port now keeps that `Caught=9` state in block/item custom data, masks it back to bat for rendering/sounds/release, and leaves full Polynesia animal-merchant trading as the remaining larger item task.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Ruby Slippers home-dimension target parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `ItemWitchesClothes.trySayTheresNoPlaceLikeHome` comparison showed the no-Waystone home route only considers the current dimension's bed first, then the Overworld bed/spawn fallback. The port now only accepts modern respawn data when it belongs to the player's current dimension or the Overworld, instead of letting an unrelated third-dimension respawn target hijack the chant.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Ruby Slippers bound-Waystone consumption parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `ItemWitchesClothes.trySayTheresNoPlaceLikeHome` comparison showed the bound-Waystone branch kills the dropped `EntityItem` outright after energy succeeds, then uses the already-read Waystone stack for the normal teleport or rare misfire branch. The port now discards the offered item entity instead of shrinking one item and preserving a remainder.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Ruby Slippers world-spawn fallback parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `ItemWitchesClothes.trySayTheresNoPlaceLikeHome` comparison showed the no-bed home route falls back to the Overworld's actual world spawn rather than a fixed coordinate. The port now uses the modern Overworld level-data spawn position before its safe-position search instead of the temporary hardcoded `(0,64,0)` fallback.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Ruby Slippers home cooldown ordering parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `ItemWitchesClothes.trySayTheresNoPlaceLikeHome` comparison showed the bed/spawn route writes `WITCLastRubySlipperTime` after resolving the home target but before the final infusion-energy spend/teleport attempt. The port now follows that source ordering, so insufficient infusion energy on a valid home-route attempt can still start the persisted 30-minute cooldown; the bound-Waystone branch remains source-shaped by starting its one-minute cooldown only after energy succeeds and the offered Waystone is consumed.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Baba's Hat source damage gate parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `GenericEvents` comparison showed Baba's Hat only blinks on entity or projectile damage, does not run while the player is actively transformed as wolf/wolfman, and lets powered draining bolts bypass the protection. The port now removes the previous magic-only/bypasses-armor trigger, skips transformed werewolf players through the current transformation flag, and skips powered draining `BoltEntity` hits.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Shared Void Bramble teleport helper parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockVoidBramble`, `ItemWitchesClothes`, `GenericEvents`, and `ItemGeneral` comparison showed Void Bramble collisions, Ruby Slippers' rare bound-Waystone misfire, and Baba's Hat's infused hit-cancel blink all use `BlockVoidBramble.teleportRandomly`, with portal/enderman feedback before and after successful teleports. The port now exposes that shared one-roll vertical scan helper, routes all three live callers through it, restores the source-shaped teleport feedback, removes Ruby Slippers' previous non-source fallback from a failed misfire scan to the bound Waystone, and removes Baba's Hat's bespoke heightmap/retry blink search.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Void Bramble teleport scan parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockVoidBramble.teleportRandomly` comparison showed the source uses a single random X/Z roll within 500 blocks, then scans down/up from the bramble Y for a solid non-bedrock base with three air blocks above, capped at +64/Y250, before teleporting. The port now uses that one-roll vertical scan instead of the previous heightmap plus 16-attempt `randomTeleport` approximation.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Void Bramble portal ambience parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockVoidBramble` comparison showed a one-in-two client tick portal particle with downward velocity around the block's upper body. The port now emits that ambient `PORTAL` effect while preserving the existing owner-removal, ritual-nulling, no-collision, light, unbreakable, and teleport behavior.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Wild Bramble support-loss parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockBramble` comparison showed brambles require non-air support below and drop themselves from the neighbor-change validity check when that support is lost. The port now destroys unsupported Wild Bramble with drops on neighbor updates while keeping no-shears spread limited to player harvest.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Wild Bramble break-spread replacement parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockBramble` comparison showed the wild meta spreads only from no-shears break into the eight neighboring columns and only replaces air, full Snow Block, or the old `tallgrass` block with support below. The port now limits modern targets to air, Snow Block, Short Grass, and Fern instead of broad `canBeReplaced()` while preserving the source scan order and random rolls.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Ember Moss and Glint Weed spread substrate parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockEmberMoss` / `BlockGlintWeed` comparison showed both plants spread over dirt, grass, mycelium, sand, and farmland. The port now uses that source list instead of the previous stone/soul-sand mistranslation while preserving the existing source cadence, density cap, support checks, particles, light, fire touch, and Ember Moss shears-only drop behavior.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Blood Rose support/outline parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockBloodRose` comparison showed no source support-survival check and a smaller client selection outline than the constructor bounds. The port now lets Blood Roses survive without support below and uses the old 0.3625-0.6375 X/Z, 0-0.5375 Y outline while preserving empty collision, no normal drops, and Boline-only harvesting.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Dream Catcher footprint/collision parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockDreamCatcher` comparison showed a thin wall-mounted selection box but a `null` collision AABB. The port now uses the source thin wall outline and no collision instead of the previous broad colliding slab.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Blood Rose Boline harvest parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockBloodRose` + `ItemBoline` comparison showed ordinary Blood Rose block drops are empty, while Boline owns the special break path that yields the rose item. The port now keeps the block's own drops empty and creates the item only from the Boline block-break event.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Leaping Lily pulse gate parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockLeapingLily` comparison showed the old block only applies its Speed I and Jump Boost V pulses when the living entity does not already have the matching effect. The port now keeps that source non-refreshing gate instead of refreshing both effects every tick while the entity remains on the lily.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Grassper stored-item removal parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `BlockGrassper` comparison showed stored contents drop from the normal block-removal hook, not only direct player breaking. The port now drops the held one-slot item through the modern removal/explosion hooks, covering replacement, explosion, command removal, and normal break without duplicating the right-click retrieval path.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Wolfman/Were Villager lifecycle tick parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `EntityVillagerWere` + `EntityWolfman` comparison showed Were Villagers check full-moon-night conversion at tick `% 100 == 3`, and Wolfmen clear vanilla Weakness at tick `% 40 == 4`. The port now matches both timings while preserving the current full-moon-night and Wolfsbane-placeholder gates.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Infectious Wolfman villager-bite parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `GenericEvents` + `Shapeshift` comparison showed infectious `EntityWolfman` bites convert Villagers into `EntityVillagerWere` once the incoming damage would drop them below 25% health, without waiting for the victim to die. The port now restores that low-health damage hook for `WolfmanEntity` and the existing `WereVillagerEntity`.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Hobgoblin out-of-world damage immunity parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `GenericEvents` / Resurrected `CommonEvents` comparison showed old `EntityGoblin` cancels `DamageSource.outOfWorld` in the non-player hurt branch. The port now applies that source hook to `HobgoblinEntity` in `HobgoblinEvents`, and intentionally does not include `GoblinGulgEntity` or `GoblinMogEntity` because the old boss classes do not extend `EntityGoblin`.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Player target-screening parity in the Witchery target hook is restored. A 1.7.10 `Infusion` / Resurrected `CommonEvents` comparison showed player target changes first honor vanilla-style invisibility by clearing the target unless the invisible player is still the nearest player within 16 blocks, then clear player targets from blinded attackers before checking Witch Robes, Zombie Respect, or Necromancer Robes. The port now restores that order in `WitcheryPlayerEvents.onLivingChangeTarget`.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Vampire Zombie Respect targeting parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `Infusion` / Resurrected `CommonEvents` comparison showed the old target hook makes `EntityZombie` drop a player target only when that player has vampire level `>= 10`; the broader undead-family target clearing remains the Necromancer Robes branch. The port now mirrors that order in the modern target-change hook: Zombies ignore level-10 vampire players, while Necromancer Robes still protect non-level-10 vampires and ordinary players from all undead targets.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Town zombie/villager protection parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `Config` + `GenericEvents` + `Infusion` comparison showed `TownZombieAttackReductionMode` defaults to `1`, clamps to `0..2`, makes Zombie attacks on Villagers deal exactly `0.5F` damage at mode `1+`, and clears Zombie Villager targets at mode `2`. The port now exposes the same `general.TownZombieAttackReductionMode` common config key, applies the 0.5-damage rule through the villager incoming-damage hook, and clears zombie villager targets through the modern target-change hook.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Village protector villager-damage parity is restored. A 1.7.10 `GenericEvents` comparison showed ordinary Villagers should not take damage from Witchery Village Guards or Witch Hunters; the port now cancels incoming Villager damage whose responsible attacker is `VillageGuardEntity` or `WitchHunterEntity`, covering melee and projectile ownership through the modern damage source.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Village Guard blood/conversion now follows the old vampire interaction slice: guards carry a persisted 500-point `BloodLevel`, vampire bites can drain guards with the source two-thirds guard rule and low-blood hurt feedback, mesmerise accepts guards, and a vampire-blood Full Glass Goblet can convert a transfixed, fully drained guard near a Coffin into the current Vampire mob. The old guard's passive village blood-regeneration/village-side-effect loop remains later guard-AI polish because the port does not yet model the source `villageObj` guard tick path.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Glass Goblet villager conversion now follows the old ordinary-villager handoff: a vampire-blood Full Glass Goblet used on a transfixed, fully drained Villager near a Coffin now spawns the current Vampire mob through normal conversion spawn initialization, removes the Villager, empties the goblet, plays the red-dust drink cue, and advances a level-9 caster to level 10 when the goblet contains their own blood and their cap allows it.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Glass Goblet filling now matches the old source gate: only vampire level 9+ players can fill an empty goblet, the cost is 125 Blood Power instead of the current human-blood store, creative-mode vampires bypass only the cost like source `decreaseBloodPower`, and failures use the old not-enough-blood, not-high-enough, and nothing-happens messages with snare feedback. Successful fills now use red dust plus drink feedback before returning the source-facing Full Glass Goblet stack.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Bone Needle Blood-stained Wool conversion now matches the old source gate: only vampire level 4+ can convert placed White Wool, the cost is 125 Blood Power instead of human blood level, creative-mode vampires bypass only the cost like source `decreaseBloodPower`, and failed attempts return to snare-only feedback. The previous `witchery:blood_stained_wool` port ID remains registered and smeltable as a compatibility block, but `witchery:bloodedwool` stays the source-facing creative/acquisition route.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): `witchery:bloodedwool` now restores the exact source Blood-stained Wool block/item ID from 1.7.10. Bone Needle conversion places `bloodedwool`, the creative tab exposes that source-facing item, block/item models reuse the old `bloodedwool` art, self loot returns `bloodedwool`, and the primary smelting recipe now uses it for Woven Cruor. The previous `witchery:blood_stained_wool` port ID remains registered and smeltable as a compatibility block, but it is no longer the source-facing creative/acquisition route.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): `witchery:vampire_book` now restores the source-facing Observations of an Immortal ID. It shares the live Observations readable-book/level-cap-unlock behavior, uses the original `vbook` art and old tooltip, appears in creative as base and level-9 stacks like source metadata 0/9, accepts Torn Page upgrades on either the source ID or the modern `immortals_observations_book` compatibility ID, and now owns the source-shaped crafting recipe plus guaranteed Bookshop book roll. Torn Page art now uses the old `ingredient.vbookPage` sprite too. Remaining polish is exact old markup-screen/page-position UI, not the core progression callback.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): `witchery:cauldron_book` now restores the source-facing Cauldron Book guide ID as a one-stack Witches' Primer-opening item with the original `bookcauldron` art, old Witches' Brews name/tip text, guide creative placement, and source-shaped Book + Black Dye + Feather + Dirt recipe. The earlier `witchery:witches_brews_book` compatibility item remains live for saved stacks/Bookshop loot, but the craftable source route now outputs `cauldron_book`.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Sun Grenade now restores the old sustained light-release slice from 1.7.10 `EntityGrenade` plus `BlockLight`. The port registers hidden non-item `witchery:light`, the projectile places/moves that full-light block only through air while flying or impacted, burns nearby undead during the active release, cleans the light on removal/unload, and finishes after the source 200-tick projectile lifetime with undead fire, nearby living-target Nausea, and Quartz Sphere return. The existing Duplication Grenade reflection branch remains on the shared projectile path.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Circle magic playtest corrections are documented in `docs/archive/status/RITUAL_QA_PROGRESS.md`. Confirmed behavior now includes golden-center activation, source-scale ring detection, upgraded altar range lookup, active ritual inspector output, Charging with dropped or Grassper-held support items, Sanctity/Imprisonment support handling, the small Protection barrier blocking projectiles while letting players pass, and portable Protection blocking players with its larger short-lived barrier. Source-backed fixes from the same audit moved portable Protection back to the small ring, kept incomplete medium Charging/Protection branches from blocking smaller valid rites, restored Banish Demon support sacrifices, restored the translucent source barrier render path, and source-shaped the Protection barrier geometry after medium player-blocking QA exposed a pass-through gap.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:fanciful_thinking_charm` and `witchery:pentacle` now leave the placeholder roster. The exact charm recipe outputs the Resurrected ID and Nightmare armor-strip protection accepts it, while the exact Pentacle ID is craftable, places on altars, and restores the source x2 altar recharge boost. The existing `charm_of_disrupted_dreams` and `koboldite_pentacle` compatibility IDs remain registered and accepted where relevant.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Living-village gatehouses now hang two lanterns from small outside wall brackets on the exterior tower faces. The lantern placement is gated with wall guards, so abandoned/zombie villages still get unlit gatehouses with no living-village decoration.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Village gatehouse reference height now samples only the immediate 3-wide outside strip plus immediate 3-wide inside strip, ignores water/building columns, and rounds the local average. This keeps the inner path block at the gate threshold from dropping one block too low while leaving the 15-block inward terrain-only grading to smooth terrain after the threshold is established.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:infinity_egg` now restores source `BlockInfinityEgg` as a Dragon Egg-shaped falling block that does not teleport on click. It appears in the Witchery creative tab, uses the old `dragon_egg.png` texture, drops itself, contributes 1000 altar power as a nearby capped nature source, and multiplies altar power/recharge by 10 when placed on a connected altar top.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:shaded_glass` now restores source `BlockShadedGlass` as one modern colored block with a redstone `powered` state instead of the old hidden active block ID. The block has 16 colored creative/recipe stacks, old off/on texture families, source-like no normal drops, powered opacity 15 light blocking, and stained-glass + Redstone recipes yielding 8 per color.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:pit_dirt` and `witchery:pit_grass` now have the old Tormented Twine heated Witch's Cauldron recipe route in practical source form. With a heated, filled Witch's Cauldron and a valid nearby altar holding 4000 power, Dirt + Mandrake Root plus Tormented Twine yields four Pit Dirt, while Dirt + Wheat Seeds + Dandelion plus Tormented Twine yields four Pit Grass.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Direct Brew of Frost / `brew_of_ice` block use now restores a practical single-click slice of the old Ice World block-family conversion. It converts wooden doors, fences, fence gates, stairs, slabs, pressure plates, wooden stockades, terrain, logs, stone, planks, and leaves into the already-live Witchery ice/snow family while preserving supported orientation, open/hinge, stair/slab/fence/gate, stockade, and waterlogged state.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Direct Brew of Frost / `brew_of_ice` block use restored the old Ice World stockade conversion in first practical form: using it on a wooden `witchery:stockade` converts that block into `witchery:ice_stockade`, preserving shared stockade connection and waterlogged state.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Gatehouse inner path grading is restored after the inside wall-walk ledge pass. Gatehouses now clear stale high ledge masonry in the first inward path columns and place the new inside ledge only after the terrain-only inward gate approach has graded, so the ledge no longer stops the inner terrain adjuster.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Gatehouses now add a one-block village-facing inside wall-walk ledge at rampart height, extended one block past each side tower, so guards can move from adjacent wall runs onto the inside of the gatehouse and across the over-gate perch.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Abandoned/zombie villages still receive adaptive walls and gatehouses, but now suppress every Witchery Village Guard spawn. The generator detects vanilla zombie village starts from `/zombie/` jigsaw pool element paths and skips both wall-walk spacing guards and gatehouse center-perch guards.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Gatehouses now clear old wall/rampart blocks through the full locked 7x3 footprint up to `WALL_HEIGHT + 10`, six blocks higher than the previous clearance, before rebuilding the gate opening, side towers, and over-gate guard perch. Gatehouse vegetation cleanup uses the same taller cell scrub.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): WolfmanEntity now keeps the old 1.7.10 `ModelWolfman` geometry paired with a dedicated old-source `wolfman_entity.png` UV texture, while player werewolf rendering keeps the Resurrected werewolf texture/model path. WereVillagerEntity now renders through the vanilla villager model/base texture like source `EntityVillagerWere` instead of the earlier Witch Hunter placeholder model.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): generated gate paths and the path floor through gatehouse openings now fill downward with terrain-style support instead of wall brick. Non-desert villages use dirt, desert villages use sand, and badlands use red sand; gatehouse pillars and wall foundations still use their masonry materials.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): rampart lips and inner wall-walk ledges now require a matching parallel neighboring wall column at compatible height, so isolated one-block rampart nubs are skipped. Corner walk bridges now also require both adjoining wall runs to be rampart-compatible.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Coven Circle structure selection now rejects river/ocean biome footprints, and `CovenCirclePiece` aborts before writing blocks if the 11x11 footprint has fluid or ice at the floor/surface band. Stone Circles should now skip frozen-water and shoreline sites instead of generating with the floor at or below water level.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): wall guard spacing now uses 150 usable wall-walk blocks, and rampart L-corners now add a diagonal inside walk bridge with cleared headroom so guards can path through turns instead of getting stuck at corners.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): inward gate path grading now reaches 15 blocks from the gate instead of 10, but the inward pass is terrain-only. Before sampling or grading a column inside the village, it checks the surface/headroom column and stops if it sees non-terrain building blocks, protecting houses/farms/furnishings while still smoothing dirt, grass, sand, gravel, raw stone, snow/ice, water/air, roads, and soft vegetation.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Watch Tower roof stairs now use the source old-meta direction mapping directly for all four local roof faces. North/south faces map to `NORTH`/`SOUTH`, while the local west/east rows map to `EAST`/`WEST`, correcting the 180-degree wrong slope seen in fresh QA while leaving ladder, chest, guard, and biome-material work intact.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): village walls now increase guard coverage by lowering usable wall-walk spacing to 80 and adding one guard on each new over-gate center rampart. Gatehouses clear the full 7x3 footprint upward before rebuilding so old curtain/rampart blocks do not remain over openings, and the new gate rampart has rail blocks only on the outside-facing front. Corner wall columns no longer use a taller special cap or skip rampart details, so exposed lips and inner walk ledges continue through inner/outer turns on suitable flat runs. Apothecary wall signs now face the generated front wall support instead of floating over the door.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): the latest goto-prep freeze ended inside a Bookshop placement slice after `Placing bookshop ...` and before the `Finished placing bookshop` breadcrumb. Bookshop display frames now avoid worldgen-time `ItemFrame.survives()` collision/entity lookup, use direct active-slice support/air checks with per-world frame-position claims, and emit config-gated phase logs for shell, chest, frames, foundation, and resident spawning. Village wall/gate/path cleanup now also removes red/brown mushrooms, giant mushroom caps, and mushroom stems as clearable natural vegetation.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): village wall ramparts now opt out on rough wall steps instead of trying to decorate columns whose neighboring wall bases differ by more than one block. Corner columns keep the existing simple tower cap but no longer get diagonal rampart lumps, and guard spawning now uses returned inner wall-walk/rampart ledge positions instead of the old center-column top. The prior biome-aware exposed-face stair caps and inner wall-walk ledges remain on flatter non-corner runs.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): village walls now borrow the decompiled Witchery Walls addon's top-course visual language without changing the stable queue/gate pipeline. Curtain columns add exposed-face block lips plus biome-aware stair caps (`STONE_BRICK_STAIRS`, `SANDSTONE_STAIRS`, or `RED_SANDSTONE_STAIRS` for savanna) and inner wall-walk ledges while keeping the existing processed-village, chunk-prep, gatehouse, tree-cleanup, and terrain-safety logic intact.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): the exact Resurrected infused brew IDs are now live. `witchery:infused_soaring_brew` shares the current Soaring brew direct-use class, `witchery:infused_grave_brew` shares the current Grave brew shell, both appear in brews/infusions with source names, and the Infuse Brew rites now output those exact IDs while `brew_of_soaring` and `brew_of_grave` remain compatibility stacks.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): the remaining direct-use Resurrected brew aliases with existing live behavior are now registered. `ice_brew`, `frogs_tongue_brew`, `cursed_leaping_brew`, `hitchcock_brew`, `infection_brew`, `flowing_spirit_brew`, `hollow_tears_brew`, `wasting_brew`, `bats_brew`, `revealing_brew`, `substitution_brew`, `grotesque_brew`, `depths_brew`, `sleeping_brew`, and the five `solidifying_*_brew` IDs share their canonical direct-use brew classes, appear in brews/infusions, and no longer carry placeholder names. Flowing Spirit, Grotesque, and Depths alias checks were added where current systems inspect those brew families.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): the exact Resurrected Flame Imp contract IDs are now registered. `witchery:living_flame_contract`, `fiery_tolerance_contract`, `evaporation_contract`, `fiery_touch_contract`, and `melting_touch_contract` share the live Demonic Contract item shell, use the old display names, appear beside the compatibility `contract_*` IDs, and are now the current contract recipe outputs.
Previous local delta (2026-06-21): Flame Imp contracts now carry source-style signed target data and ability behavior. Ownership contracts must be signed by the player before taming, spell contracts bind to a target, and a pleased unpowered Flame Imp can spend them for Living Flame, Fiery Tolerance, Evaporation, Fiery Touch, or Melting Touch.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): the exact Resurrected Witchcraft guidebook IDs are no longer inert placeholders. `witchery:distilling_book`, `circle_magic_book`, and `herbology_book` now share the live Witches' Primer-open behavior, source/Resurrected display names, guide creative placement, Resurrected shaped recipes, and a bookshop chest loot roll.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): the exact Resurrected Hunter armor IDs are no longer inert placeholders. `witchery:hunter_hat`, `hunter_coat`, all `silvered_hunter_*` pieces, and all `dawn_hunter_*` pieces now share the live Hunter clothing item class, upgrade recipes, full-set checks, silvered/garlicked counterplay, poppet blocking, curse protection, and nullifying-bolt empowerment with the older compatibility IDs.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:gulgs_grudle` is no longer an inert Resurrected placeholder. Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:witches_robes` is no longer an inert Resurrected placeholder. Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:icy_slippers` is no longer an inert Resurrected placeholder. Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:bound_taglock_kit` is no longer an inert Resurrected placeholder. Filled Taglock Kit collection now outputs the bound ID, and all current taglock consumers accept the real bound item as a TaglockKitItem.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:crossbow_pistol` is no longer an inert Resurrected alias placeholder. It now reuses the live source-style Hand Bow item class, appears beside `witchery:hand_bow`, uses the source/Resurrected Crossbow Pistol recipe, and shares the same idle/pulling/loaded item model property path.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:staff_of_duplication` is no longer an inert placeholder. It now has a first practical source-style `ItemDuplicationStaff` path: first block use stores a marker, second block use captures the exclusive cuboid between the two corners into item custom data, later uses place the stored pattern one block above the clicked block while cycling 0/90/180/270-degree rotations, and sneak-use resets to unrotated placement or clears stored data when used in air. It keeps source epic rarity and the existing staff art. Fresh QA should capture a small mixed-block shape, place it at every rotation, confirm air cells clear existing blocks, and confirm oversized selections are refused.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Brew alias family checks now cover the newly live Sprouting, Love, Ink, and Raising aliases in systems that previously looked only for canonical IDs. Nature's Power, Forest, Raise Column, Blindness, and the Love-based cure rites now accept `sprouting_brew`, `ink_brew`, and `love_brew` wherever the matching `brew_of_*` item was accepted, while Kettle robe extraction treats `raising_brew` as Brew of Raising for the Witches' Robe/Necromancer Robes bonus split.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:sprouting_brew`, `witchery:erosion_brew`, `witchery:raising_brew`, and `witchery:love_brew` are no longer inert Resurrected alias placeholders. Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:vines_brew`, `witchery:webs_brew`, `witchery:thorns_brew`, and `witchery:ink_brew` are no longer inert Resurrected alias placeholders. Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:odd_porkchop` and `witchery:cooked_odd_porkchop` are no longer inert Resurrected alias placeholders. They now use the same source food values as the canonical odd porkchop pair, appear beside `odd_porkchop_raw` / `odd_porkchop_cooked` in ingredients/alchemy, keep their alias models, and lose the placeholder suffix.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:endless_water_brew` is no longer an inert Resurrected alias placeholder. It now uses the same source-style reusable water utility behavior as `witchery:brew_of_endless_water`, appears beside it in the brews/infusions creative section, keeps the existing alias model, shows the real Endless Water Brew name, and reports the shared 100-use tooltip instead of a placeholder suffix.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Dropped blank Waystones now restore the old passive Otherwhere-circle binding path. After the source-style two-second delay, a blank Waystone item entity on or near a small Otherwhere ring converts up to eight blanks into location-bound `witchery:bound_waystone` items when no target is nearby, or spends 4000 nearby altar power to create one target-bound `witchery:blooded_waystone` when a living target is within about two blocks; success clears the chalk ring and preserves any leftover blank Waystones.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Blooded Waystone binding now restores source recipe 89's alternate no-power support path. The small-circle entity-binding route accepts Ender Dew + Slimeball + Snowball with 500 altar power, or Ender Dew + Slimeball + Icy Needle + Charged Attuned Stone with no power draw, and both routes summon `witchery:blooded_waystone` bound to the nearby living target.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:blooded_waystone` is no longer an inert placeholder. It now represents the old hidden `waystoneCreatureBound` entity-bound Waystone: the small-circle binding route can blood-bind a blank Waystone to a nearby living target using Ender Dew, Slimeball, Snowball, and 500 altar power; the item stores taglock-style player/creature data, shows the bound target, can be used through Crystal Ball taglock scrying, and works in Transposition to Waystone by sending circle entities to the live bound target.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:bound_waystone` is no longer an inert placeholder. The old source distinct bound-Waystone identity now has a real item registration using the existing bound texture/model, bind/copy rites summon that ID, bound-Waystone consumers accept both the new ID and old saved bound `witchery:waystone` stacks, and blank-waystone offerings remain restricted to unbound `witchery:waystone`.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:bolt_stake`, `witchery:bolt_anti_magic`, `witchery:bolt_holy`, and `witchery:bolt_splitting` are no longer inert placeholders. They now bridge the old/source Hand Bow bolt IDs into the live bolt family: the Hand Bow can find and consume them as stake, nullifying/anti-magic, holy/bone, and splitting bolts, while cycling and projectile pickup still normalize back to the canonical port IDs.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:subdued_village_spirit` is no longer an inert placeholder. The old nearest-village waypoint flight remains future Spirit AI polish.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:infernal_blood` is no longer an inert placeholder. Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:nullified_leather` is no longer an inert placeholder. It is now a real ingredient with the source shaped recipe: eight Leather around one Null Catalyst produces three Nullified Leather for Hunter outfit crafting.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:sheep_liver` and `witchery:cooked_sheep_liver` are no longer inert placeholders. Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:odd_porkchop_raw` and `witchery:odd_porkchop_cooked` are no longer inert placeholders. Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:universal_antidote` is no longer an inert placeholder. Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Hunter-family armor now uses a dedicated source-style item class: plain, silvered, and Dawn variants have uncommon rarity, leather/cloth armor values with iron durability, old tooltip lines, and the source full-set drawback that disables bound poppet magic while worn.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Full Hunter-family armor sets now restore source `ItemHunterClothes` protection rolls: any complete plain, silvered, or Dawn outfit has a 25% chance to cancel magic damage before poppet protection and a 90% chance to dampen curse rites through the ritual curse helper.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Silvered hunter armor now restores the missing source counter-wear behavior from `ItemHunterClothes`: silvered Hunter and Dawn pieces reduce incoming werewolf-attacker damage and hurt werewolf players who try wearing them once per second, in addition to the already-live werewolf attacker recoil.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:witch_hunter_dawn_*` now carries the first source garlicked hunter counterplay slice from `ItemHunterClothes`: Dawn pieces count as silvered for werewolf attacker recoil, reduce incoming vampire-attacker damage, and burn vampire wearers with the old cactus-style once-per-second punishment.
Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:brew_bag` is now a real one-stack item with the source Impregnated Leather + Golden Thread recipe, eight custom-data inventory slots for current throwable splash/gas brews, selected-slot cycling, other-hand brew insertion, stored-brew throwing, survival Glass Bottle return, and tooltip visibility for selected/stored slots. Previous local delta (2026-06-01; tightened 2026-06-02): `witchery:ruby_slippers` now restores the rare source waystone misfire from `ItemWitchesClothes`: after the nearby bound-Waystone route pays energy, consumes the offered waystone, and starts the one-minute cooldown, a 1% roll invokes the shared Void Bramble one-roll scan within 500 blocks instead of the bound-waystone jump. Previous local delta (2026-06-01; tightened 2026-06-02): `witchery:ruby_slippers` now restores the main source chat-activation split from `ItemWitchesClothes`: nearby dropped bound Waystones within three blocks take priority, consume 40/80 infusion energy and discard the offered dropped entity like source, and use their own one-minute cooldown; the bed/spawn fallback keeps its separate 30-minute cooldown, starts that cooldown after selecting the home target but before the final energy spend like source, only accepts current-dimension or Overworld respawn targets, falls back to the Overworld level-data spawn when no bed target exists, and now spends 80/120 infusion energy. Previous local delta (2026-06-01): `witchery:ice_slippers` now restores the source walking conversion from `Infusion.handleIcySlippersEffect`: every tick, the four half-step cells beneath the wearer freeze Water into Ice and harden Lava into Obsidian, with the old 10% durability loss on lava conversion. The item now also has the source-style tooltip.
Previous local delta (2026-06-21): The source footwear reward trio now restores the old worn visual path. `witchery:ice_slippers` / `witchery:icy_slippers`, `witchery:ruby_slippers`, and `witchery:seeping_shoes` use a shared source-scale `ModelWitchesClothes(0.45F, false)` feet-slot model with the old item tint colors baked over `witchclothes.png`; Seeping Shoes also expose the source tooltip.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Observations/Torn Page drops now follow the old boss-display rule for the currently ported Witchery boss roster. Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village Bookshops now restore the old rear display row from `ComponentVillageBookShop`. Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village Apothecaries now spawn an actual apothecary villager again instead of a Coven Witch stand-in. Previous local delta (2026-05-31): `/witchery setup rite` now places a player-facing ritual QA pad beside the existing `/witchery setup stations` hut helper. It clears/builds a 31x11 pad five blocks ahead, gives it a three-block stone-brick foundation, and places four complete small/medium/large ring sets: white glyph, golden ritual, otherwhere, and infernal. The command consumes public ring positions from `RitualCircleDetector`, keeping QA placement aligned with the detector that Arthana activation uses.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village Keeps are now confirmed to appear after the weight-32 pass, and their bridge ladders now match the old keep's ladder metadata. `ComponentVillageKeep` uses the same ladder meta `2` for the tower shaft and both bridge ladder runs; `VillageKeepPiece` now uses the same support-facing state for those bridge ladders instead of sideways east/west facings that can self-prune during generation. Fresh QA should verify both keep bridge ladders and tower ladders are climbable all the way up and down.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village Apothecary now follows the old `ComponentVillageApothecary` shell much more closely. The port replaces the flat slab-roof/machine-shop version with the source 9x9x6 shape: cobble/sandstone-family floor and stepped roof bands, plank walls, oak-style stair roof rows, old window positions, real front door/sign/step, sparse source furnishings, old chest and resident local positions, and a corrected 9-block advertised village-pool height. The resident follow-up restored a librarian-skinned `Apothecary` Villager with the source trade list.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Mystic Branch creature powers now map the decompiled creature infusion sacrifice families instead of the earlier five temporary mappings. The port now grants Spider, Creeper, Bat, Squid, Ghast, Blaze, Pigman, Zombie, Skeleton, Jump, Speed, Enderman, and Heal powers from their old mob families, awards 10 charges for most powers and source-like 1/2 heal charges, and adds practical active effects for the missing powers: web/snare, self-blast, target blindness, ghast/blaze fireballs, strength/slowness tradeoffs, and a skeleton arrow shot.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Latest exploration freeze stopped inside procedural village building replay, not wall generation: wall debug skipped unloaded referenced starts safely, then worldgen logged an already claimed Apothecary placement slice and stopped. Procedural village buildings now sample biome materials from the active generation-box intersection during chunk replay, avoid direct sign/front-step writes outside the active generation box, log `Finished placing ...` after each slice, and catch placement exceptions with a `[VillageBuildings] Failed to place ...` error instead of letting one broken slice crash worldgen.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): `witchery:brew_of_endless_water` is now a real source-style utility item instead of an inert placeholder. It is a glowing 100-use item that fills empty or partial cauldrons to full, places water sources on clicked sides, damages on successful use, plays water feedback, and shows remaining uses in its tooltip.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Coven Circle/Stone Circle and Straw Man structure selection now samples a one-block padded dry-land footprint, rejects sites with more than two blocks of height variance, and anchors the piece to the lowest accepted surface instead of a center-only height. This should stop the visible one-block raised dirt/grass plinth while still avoiding water and rough terrain.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village Watch Towers first kept the straight supported center ladder shaft while restoring the old three-block upper handoff ladder beside the deck support column; later traversal QA replaced that with the lower source shaft plus extended upper handoff described above. The watchtower loot chest is lowered three blocks from local `y=13` to local `y=10`, placing it on the mid platform instead of high on the upper deck.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): The 21:34 taiga village goto hard-stalled while chunk `[70,216]` was replaying an already claimed apothecary whose resident spawn position lived in a neighboring chunk. Procedural village residents in the Apothecary, Bookshop, Witch Hut, and Hobgoblin Hut now only spawn when their resident position is inside the active chunk generation box, matching the existing Watch Tower/Keep/Coven Circle guard and witch guards and avoiding cross-chunk entity adds during goto prep/worldgen.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village Bookshop front stairs now match the old north-facing stair metadata instead of pointing 180 degrees away from the entrance, and the bookshop no longer uses a special one-block-lowered jigsaw baseline, raising the whole building one block relative to the village path. Savanna Witchery village buildings now use orange terracotta foundations and acacia entrance stairs instead of cobblestone/cobblestone stairs through the shared village material palette.
Latest local delta (2026-05-31): Village Keeps now have a much stronger house-pool selection weight (`32`, up from `8`) because fresh QA still had not seen one. The one-per-village cap remains, and config-gated `[VillageBuildings:Debug]` logs now mark Keep jigsaw offers, claims, replays, duplicate skips, and final placement so the next test can distinguish "not selected" from "selected but rejected/hidden".
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village Watch Tower roof stairs now face outward under the fixed NORTH-oriented piece transform. The local north/south roof stair states are intentionally swapped to account for local-Z mirroring, and the new center ladder faces the support column so it survives and remains climbable.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village Watch Towers briefly used a straight supported center ladder shaft from entry to the upper deck instead of the old split/offset upper handoff that made the top ladders disappear or become unusable; later QA superseded that temporary route with the lower source shaft plus extended upper handoff described above. The top torch moved off the shaft exit, and guard spawns moved onto solid deck cells around the ladder hole.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): The compact village Witch Hut's front stair now uses the owning village's matching stair palette and faces back toward the door instead of away from it. The visible hut floor/support skirt now uses the village floor material instead of cobblestone, so plains/savanna/taiga/snowy huts no longer show a cobble step/foundation while desert huts remain sandstone-family.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village goto prep now holds every one-chunk-per-tick prepared destination chunk with a temporary radius ticket until wall generation, landing selection, or failure completes. The latest soft-fail logs had `182/182 chunk(s) ready` but then `0 gate candidate(s)` on every retry because early prepared road chunks had already unloaded before `VillageWallGenerator.generate` scanned them. Goto prep now verifies the prepared footprint is still resident, releases tickets on success/failure/cancel/logout, and no longer spams the final ready count every tick.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village goto no longer treats `0 gate candidate(s)` as successful wall generation. The generator now reports whether it actually placed walls, goto-prep and wall jobs retry briefly while village road chunks settle, processed-wall data is written only after a real generation, and the teleport landing searches the loaded village for a safe road/surface position instead of using the raw locate X/Z. This targets the latest report where goto arrived underground/without visible walls and `/witchery walls` required `walls reset` before rebuilding.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): `witchery:cursed_snow_pressure_plate` is now registered as the hidden source cursed snow wrapper around the normal Snow Pressure Plate. It has no item/creative-tab entry, renders with the normal snow pressure-plate models, behaves as an all-entity pressure plate, carries the same display name as the normal snow plate, and drops `witchery:snow_pressure_plate`; the remaining source gap is the old cursed block-entity/brew payload conversion path.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Witchery common config now includes top-level `debug = true` by default. While enabled, the village wall/goto system logs `[VillageWalls:Debug]` INFO breadcrumbs for queue sizes, loaded-only reference lookups, safe-locate candidate probes, synchronous goto full-chunk prep before/after points, forced ticket requests, automatic footprint readiness, and wall-generator entry. This is intentionally noisy enough for freeze forensics and can be disabled by setting `debug = false`.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Fresh-world exploration could still freeze after two automatic villages queued near spawn because passive village reference lookup/refresh could synchronously load a referenced start chunk even though automatic wall jobs no longer requested wall tickets. Automatic discovery now only resolves village starts from chunks already resident in memory, safe village goto carries the probed `StructureStart` forward instead of doing a second reference lookup, active wall jobs round-robin through a bounded two-step queue, and pending automatic starts scan only a small bounded set while queueing at most one ready natural-load generation per tick.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): The 19:07 taiga/snowy village goto froze after safe locate found a village 5344 blocks away, prepared only one arrival chunk, teleported, and then stopped at `1/168` naturally loaded wall chunks with the player in empty terrain. Village goto now queues a pre-teleport arrival prep job instead: for unprocessed villages it full-loads the target wall footprint one chunk per tick, generates walls/gates once ready, and only then teleports; for already processed villages it still prepares a small 5x5 landing bubble before crossing. This avoids forced wall tickets while also avoiding the one-chunk void arrival path.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): `witchery:pit_dirt` and `witchery:pit_grass` became real no-collision camouflage trap blocks based on source `BlockPitDirt` and `BlockPitGrass`. They copy the vanilla dirt/grass visuals, keep source hardness/sound feel, drop themselves, appear in the Witchery creative tab, and use the old source display names "Dirt" and "Grass"; their Tormented Twine Witch's Cauldron recipes were restored in the latest local delta, leaving final in-game Torment trap QA.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): `witchery:stockade` is now a real first-pass wooden Stockade block/item instead of a missing source block. It uses the existing source-shaped Stockade collision/damage/connection behavior, oak-log block/item models, self loot, Witchery creative-tab wiring, and the old shaped recipe pattern with `Exhale of the Horned One` plus logs for nine stockades; remaining source parity is the old per-wood metadata variants and brew freezing conversion into Ice Stockades.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): `/witchery goto stone_circle` now aliases the registered `witchery:coven_circle` structure. The old source and lang call this generated feature a stone circle in player-facing contexts, so QA can now use either the modern registry name or the old-world name without changing saved-world structure IDs.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Coven Circle/Stone Circle generation now restores the three source cobweb details from `ComponentCoven` at local `(3,1,0)`, `(10,2,7)`, and `(0,3,7)`. The earlier port had replaced two of those source cobwebs with mossy marker stones and missed the third, so fresh circles should now match the old standing-stone detail layout more closely.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village walls, gatehouses, and graded gate paths now clear connected natural tree logs/leaves when their placement corridor touches a tree. The cleanup is bounded around the wall/path cell and targets only tree-tagged blocks, so taiga gates should no longer leave sliced spruce trunks or floating canopies while village wood structures remain protected.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Witchery village buildings are capped to one of each type per village. The procedural village element keys claims by the owning village `StructureStart` when structure references are available, falls back to a nearby village cluster only if needed during placement, and skips later duplicate apothecary, witch hut, bookshop, watch tower, or keep origins while still allowing repeated chunk passes for the same selected piece.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Witchery village building entrances now line up with vanilla paths. The procedural pool element accounts for StructurePiece NORTH flipping local `z=0` to the generated south side, advertises `building_entrance` on the actual front face, and uses the vanilla y=0 child jigsaw baseline so Witchery buildings sit one block above the road. The Witch Hut also places its front stair outside the door even when the stair is outside the piece box.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Hobgoblin Hut resident spawn Y now accounts for the source-shaped slab floor. The hut still uses the decompiled 7x6x7 schematic and the same interior X/Z spawn point, but the hobgoblin is placed at `minY + 1.5` so its feet rest on the bottom-slab floor instead of clipping into it.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village goto no longer blocks teleport on forced wall tickets. The 15:48 taiga log safe-located the second village and queued `132 wall chunk ticket(s)`, then froze before the first forced-ticket progress line. The first fix removed forced wall tickets from village goto; the later 19:07 void-arrival fix supersedes the one-chunk immediate teleport path with pre-teleport chunk prep and wall generation. Manual `/witchery walls` keeps the explicit one-at-a-time forced-ticket path for nearby loaded villages.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Chalk glyph marks now restore the old metadata-style visual spread. Source `ItemChalk` used `rand.nextInt(12)` when placing or reusing ritual/otherwhere/infernal glyphs, while `BlockCircleGlyph` selected one of 12 icons per chalk family. The port now stores a `glyph=0..11` state on chalk-circle blocks, maps every ritual/otherwhere/infernal state to the existing `circleglyph1/2/3.1-12` textures, and rerolls same-family chalk marks instead of deleting them.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Witchery village buildings should be noticeably less rare in vanilla villages. All five vanilla house pools now use apothecary `16`, witch hut `12`, bookshop `16`, watch tower `8`, and keep `8` weights. The village witch hut now matches the old compact `4x6x5` `ComponentVillageWitchHut` footprint with a real door-side jigsaw at local `x=1`, and the Hobgoblin Hut now uses the decompiled `7x6x7` source schematic with one generated resident instead of the old port's small oak hut. Keep/watchtower weights were raised from the very low earlier pass so keeps should be easier to encounter, though fresh cross-biome village QA is still required.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): The latest chained goto freeze happened before walls again: logs stop at `[Goto] Searching for minecraft:village_plains ...]`, with no destination prep or wall job. Vanilla village `/witchery goto` now avoids the synchronous `findNearestMapStructure` call and queues a safe locate job instead, probing one candidate village chunk per server tick before handing the result to the existing wall pre-generation and delayed teleport path. Manual `/witchery walls` likewise avoids vanilla locate and targets only a nearby generated village start. This should trade the hard integrated-server lock for a slower visible search plus either successful delayed teleport or clean no-match feedback.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): `witchery:ice_stockade` is now a real source-shaped block/item instead of an inert placeholder. It restores the old `BlockStockade(true)` slice with low fence-like collision, translucent vanilla-ice presentation, waterlogging, 25.0 hardness / 20.0 resistance, light opacity 1, cactus contact damage, connections to itself/Iron Bars/Ice Gates, blockstate/model/loot/lang wiring, and Witchery creative-tab placement. No direct crafting recipe was added because source creates the ice variant by freezing wooden stockades.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): `/witchery goto village_savanna` could stall on the fourth chained village goto while the explicit wall job waited at `15/16 requested ready`. Explicit village goto/walls jobs now sort forced wall chunks nearest-first, cap very large explicit forced footprints at 144 chunks, request one chunk ticket at a time, and release/skip a forced chunk after 10 seconds without readiness progress. Gate height sampling and inward path grading were first bounded to avoid flattening paths through nearby houses; the later terrain-only pass now allows 15 inward terrain blocks while still stopping at building blocks.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): `witchery:ice_pressure_plate` now matches source `BlockPerpetualIcePressurePlate(Material.ice)` more closely: the plate only emits redstone for living entities wearing `witchery:ice_slippers`, uses the source 2.0 hardness / 5.0 resistance profile, and explicitly survives on `witchery:ice_fence`. The shared snow/ice pressure-plate class also keeps snow plates as normal all-entity plates while preserving the old ice-fence support rule.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): `/witchery goto village_plains` froze after the wall job logged `Requested all 195 wall chunk ticket(s)` and before any readiness/generation log. The batched job was spacing ticket requests across ticks, but it did not wait for the previous requested batch to load before requesting the next one. Explicit `/witchery goto village_*` and `/witchery walls` jobs now use a true readiness gate: request one forced chunk ticket, wait until that requested chunk is present in `getChunkNow`, then request the next ticket. Progress-based timeout handling releases/skips a stuck requested chunk.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): `witchery:straw_man` no longer forces a raised pale sand pad in badlands or hilly dry terrain. Structure selection now rejects dry footprints with more than two blocks of height variance and anchors the piece at the lower footprint height instead of the center column, while the piece reuses the live local solid surface block for non-desert ground/support columns and only forces vanilla sand in actual desert biomes. Support fill is limited to the old cross-shaped footprint instead of filling the whole 6x5 rectangle. Badlands still get dead-bush decoration, but the base should blend into red sand/terracotta-style terrain rather than standing on a white sand platform.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): `witchery:snow_pressure_plate`, `witchery:snow_stairs`, and `witchery:snow_slab` are now real blocks/items instead of inert placeholders. They follow the old snow-family source: 0.2 hardness, snow sound/material feel, all-entity pressure-plate sensitivity, source snow-block/snow-layer recipes, block-backed models using the vanilla snow texture, normal stair/slab/pressure-plate loot with double slabs returning two singles, vanilla stair/slab tags, and Witchery creative-tab placement. Remaining ice/snow family work is cursed snow pressure plate and future brew/rite generation hooks.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): `witchery:ice_stairs` and `witchery:ice_slab` are now real blocks/items instead of inert placeholders. They follow the old Perpetual Ice stair/slab source: ice friction, hardness 2.0, blast resistance 5.0, light opacity 3, translucent block-backed models using the vanilla ice texture, normal stair/slab loot with double slabs returning two singles, vanilla stair/slab tags, and Witchery creative-tab placement. Later passes also promoted the snow variants and ice stockade; remaining ice/snow family work is cursed snow pressure plate and future brew/rite generation hooks.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Blood Crucible is no longer missing as a block/system stub. The port already had the block, block entity, renderer, vampire fill path, and level-10 ultimate-selection interaction; this pass adds the source-shaped stone-brick recipe, corrects stone sound/material and the old hardness/resistance split, and tightens the interaction to source behavior where creative bypasses fullness only and the Farm ultimate catalyst is a written book. Remaining vampirism work is broader Blood Power and late-level gate parity, not the basic crucible block.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Vanilla village `/witchery goto` now uses a smaller safe locate radius than general Witchery structure goto. The 12:57-13:00 run proved automatic walls, savanna goto, and snowy goto could all complete, then froze at `[Goto] Searching for minecraft:village_plains...]` before chunk preparation or wall pre-generation. That path is vanilla `findNearestMapStructure` scanning too far for a biome-specific village. Village gotos now search 8 structure rings instead of the general 32, trading occasional quick "not found nearby" failures for no server-thread lockup; stale automatic pending wall entries also skip if a goto/manual job already marked the village processed.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Automatic village wall discovery no longer force-loads wall chunks. The 12:48 fresh-world log showed an automatic spawn-village job starting a 210-ticket padded-footprint request while the integrated server had only generated a few spawn chunks. Automatic queue jobs now wait until the padded footprint is naturally loaded by normal player/view-distance chunk loading, then generate without forced tickets. `/witchery goto village_*` and `/witchery walls` remain intentional pre-generation paths, but the latest path now limits them to nearest-first one-at-a-time forced tickets with stuck-chunk skip handling.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village wall jobs still wait for the full padded village footprint naturally for automatic generation, while explicit forced-chunk tickets are requested across ticks instead of one large 168-210 ticket spike. The 12:32-12:35 logs showed `/witchery goto village_plains` forcing an already processed plains bounding box and stacking a second wall, then a savanna goto freezing right after a 210-ticket request. `/witchery goto village_*` now respects processed villages and, after the 19:07 follow-up, prepares a small landing bubble before teleport when walls already exist; `/witchery walls` also avoids rerunning a processed village unless `/witchery walls reset` is used first. Automatic/goto walls should keep road-aware gate detection without the one-tick chunk-load avalanche or duplicate wall stacks.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village wall generation now runs through a ticketed wall job instead of synchronously force-loading chunks in one tick, and automatic jobs wait for the padded village wall footprint rather than only the perimeter. The 11:31 automatic wall logs showed `0 gate candidate(s)` because gate detection scans existing road/path chunks; goto/walls explicit jobs use temporary nearest-first forced tickets, automatic jobs wait for naturally loaded chunks, and pending village teleports complete only after the wall job finishes. The 11:13 plains-style 56-chunk synchronous force-load stall should stay gone, while automatic/goto walls should no longer miss gates just because interior road chunks were unavailable.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Straw Man generation now requires the full 6x5 footprint to be dry land. The placement check rejects any footprint column where water sits above the solid floor and also rejects ocean/river biomes before adding the structure piece; `StrawManPiece.postProcess` repeats a live fluid/heightmap footprint check before writing blocks so late water mismatches abort instead of placing the effigy underwater.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village wall gate detection now requires roads to hit a three-wide flat wall face head-on before a gatehouse can be selected. The center wall cell plus one block on each side along the wall must be wall, with village interior behind the approach and empty/outside space beyond, so nearby paths no longer create sideways gates on wall segments or corners.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Broom bristles now render like source `ModelBroom`: the base model draws the handle, and a dedicated bristle layer redraws the bristle strands with the old wool dye table from synced entity `BrushColor`, defaulting missing color to brown (`12`). The prior lifecycle slice already round-tripped custom names and `BrushColor`, dye interaction, sneak-pickup, and damage drops.
Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Glint Weed ambience now matches 1.7.10 `BlockGlintWeed` again: `animateTick` emits `FLAME` with zero velocity. The previous `END_ROD` particle was a stale correction; the current block keeps its already-restored source light level and density-capped legacy spread support.
Earlier local delta (2026-05-31): Somnian Cotton natural spread now matches source `BlockCotton` more closely: 1-in-6 random tick chance, Spirit World only, Flowing Spirit beside the block below the source cotton, and dirt/grass spread support only. It no longer spreads freely across the Spirit World or around Flowing Spirit outside that dimension.
Earlier local delta (2026-05-31): Ritual altar-power costs now honor `WitcheryConfig.RITUAL_POWER_MULTIPLIER` through shared helpers. Startup/one-shot rites scale the configured cost once before altar drain, and active staged rites scale upkeep debt before whole power is consumed; the Climate Change biome-changing flag was already enforced before queueing/support-item consumption.
Earlier local delta (2026-05-31): Village goto now avoids the suspected pre-teleport stall path seen in the latest plains-village freeze: it logs before locate, uses a bounded vanilla-style locate search (`skipReferenced=false`, radius 32), and preloads only the arrival chunk instead of a 5x5 full-chunk area. Fresh village QA still needs to retest the village variants after a client restart.
Earlier same-day local delta (2026-05-31): Coven Circle support cleanup. Non-desert `witchery:coven_circle` generation keeps the grass surface and standing-stone material palette, but its terrain support is now stone-backed instead of dirt-backed so uneven terrain cannot show a one-block dirt skirt under the stone circle. Fresh plains/savanna QA is still needed.
Earlier same-day delta (2026-05-31): Hand Bow anti-magic power-drain pass. Nullifying bolts fired while the shooter wears a full hunter/silvered/dawn hunter set now carry a powered-draining flag, drain the shooter's current infusion-energy pool on launch using the old max-energy reduction rule, and drain half of a player target's max infusion energy on hit after the existing effect strip. The previous client model predicate pass also remains live: uncharged idle uses `crossbow_pistol`, active use uses `crossbow_pulling_0/1/2`, and loaded idle uses `charged_crossbow`. Remaining bolt work is in-game projectile/visual QA.
Previous same-day village delta (2026-05-31): Witchery village buildings now expose a real jigsaw connection through `WitcheryVillageElement`, so procedural apothecaries, bookshops, witch huts, watch towers, and keeps can be selected by vanilla village house pools. Bookshop is live as a 10x8x9 source-inspired shop with a book loot chest, shelves, lectern, and villager shopkeeper, and the village house-pool weights were raised from the first very sparse Witchery-building pass. Adaptive village walls now process without an artificial delay after discovery, originally kept savanna out of the desert sandstone/sand palette (superseded 2026-06-05 by the orange-terracotta/red-sandstone savanna wall palette while retaining Dirt Path roads), fill generated gate paths with terrain-style support instead of wall brick, avoid wall placement under water, skip occupied living-entity wall columns, and use reduced perimeter-only force-loading. Coven Circle now keeps sandstone to desert/badlands, restores the hidden mineshaft-loot dispenser plus two vanilla Witch spawners under the altar, checks center/corner water before placement, uses a grass surface with stone-backed support in non-desert biomes so uneven terrain no longer exposes a one-block dirt skirt, and uses the source 90% resident Coven Witch spawn chance. Straw Man worldgen is live as `witchery:straw_man` from old `ComponentWickerMan`, with a 6x8x5 hay effigy, biome plants, hidden Zombie/Zombie/Skeleton spawner, and `/witchery goto straw_man`. Remaining value is cross-biome fresh-village/Coven Circle/Straw Man QA and savanna hang retest, not another broad wall rewrite.

Previous: Earmuffs wearable parity. `witchery:earmuffs` now keeps the old leather-level head-slot protection without drawing the vanilla leather helmet, uses the original wiki/source-style flat icon, renders a compact earmuff model on the player head, keeps Mandrake nausea and Banshee scream damage protection, and now mutes all client world sounds while worn instead of only the scream placeholders.
Source priority used: latest `PORTING_NOTES.md` over older `FEATURE_MATRIX.md`/`PORTING_PLAN.md` snapshots.

Latest item-tab note: the live Witchery creative tab is now category-sorted for QA usability, preserving all previous explicit entries while adding the Silver Vat to the machine section; legacy placeholders remain visible together near the end as inert TODO markers.

Latest machine note: Fume Filter can now be used directly on a placed unfiltered Fume Funnel to upgrade it into the filtered block in-world, preserving facing and consuming one filter outside creative, while the shaped filtered-funnel recipe remains available.

Latest presentation note: Sun Collector now renders from the old daylightcollector cross-plane block art in-world, shows the stored sphere through a block-entity renderer, uses source `power` state as a render fallback for inserted/charged spheres, keeps a flat generated inventory sprite instead of the old broken cube/item-atlas path, and uses source `3.5F` hardness plus old implied `17.5F` blast resistance.

Latest plant note: Leaping Lily now uses the old vanilla lily-pad texture/display model with Witchery block/item tinting, replacing the custom texture path that rendered as a full square in-world. Its source crafting output is also restored to five lilies and now requires a Swiftness Potion rather than any potion.

Latest getting-started QA note: the full early survival route from breaking grass/ferns for Witchery herb seeds, planting/harvesting the Anointing Paste herbs, making and using Anointing Paste, collecting Rowan saplings/berries from Rowan leaves, and producing Mutandis is tester-confirmed working.

---

### Local delta history rotated from NEXT_TASKS.md (2026-05-28 .. 2026-06-02)

Current local delta (2026-06-02): Iron-material block strength/sound parity. A 1.7.10 `BlockSilverVat` / `BlockFumeFunnel` comparison (both `Material.iron`) showed two drifted properties. Silver Vat uses `setHardness(8.0F)`, which in 1.7.10 also auto-raises blast resistance to `8*5=40`, but the port registered it at `strength(3.0f)` (hardness and resistance 3.0); it now registers `witchery:silver_vat` at `strength(8.0f, 40.0f)` so survival mining time and blast resistance match source while its metal sound, smelting-collection block entity, and footprint stay unchanged. Fume Funnel and Filtered Fume Funnel are iron-material with a metal step/break sound in source, but the port used `STONE` sound/map color; both now use `METAL` sound and map color while keeping `3.5` hardness. (Statue Goddess `2.5/1000`, Glow Globe `0/14`, Candelabra `2.0/15`, Demon Heart `1.0/3`, and Chalice `3.0` were re-verified as already matching; the Daylight/Sun Collector's iron material was left as the port's intentional glass-quartz re-theme.) Fresh QA should confirm a Silver Vat breaks noticeably slower than before and resists blasts, and that both fume funnels sound metallic when stepped on/broken.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Alluring Skull unbreakable hardness parity.
