A 1.7.10 `BlockAlluringSkull` comparison showed the constructor calls the old unbreakable setter, then applies `1000.0F` blast resistance without restoring normal hardness. The port now registers `witchery:alluring_skull` with unbreakable hardness while keeping its existing low light, compact floor/wall footprint, undead allure tick, and Necromantic Stone toggle/pickup route unchanged. Fresh QA should confirm ordinary survival mining does not break the skull, Necromantic Stone still toggles it on and then removes/drops it on the next use, and creative/admin removal still behaves sanely.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Poppet Shelf hardness parity. A 1.7.10 `BlockPoppetShelf` comparison showed the shelf uses `2.5F` hardness, not the port's previous `2.0F` wood-strength value. The port now registers `witchery:poppet_shelf` with source hardness while leaving its restored 9-slot poppet-only storage, chunk-loading, comparator output, facing, low footprint, and renderer paths unchanged. Fresh QA should compare survival break feel against ordinary wood storage blocks and confirm contents/drop/render behavior still works.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Leech Chest feline lid-blocker parity. A 1.7.10 `BlockLeechChest` comparison showed the lid obstruction helper scans only `EntityOcelot` instances in the block above the chest and returns true when one is sitting; it does not let every sitting tameable block the lid. The port now keeps solid-block obstruction but narrows the entity blocker to modern Cats plus the Witchery Cat Familiar and Spectral Familiar feline equivalents, so sitting wolves and non-feline familiars no longer stop the chest opening. Fresh QA should place a solid block above the Leech Chest, then test a sitting cat/Cat Familiar/Spectral Familiar and a sitting wolf or other non-feline tameable over the lid to confirm only the source-style feline blockers prevent opening.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Leech Chest toughness parity. A 1.7.10 `BlockLeechChest` comparison showed the block uses `25.0F` hardness and `1000.0F` blast resistance, not ordinary chest toughness. The port now registers `witchery:leech_chest` with that source toughness while leaving its restored 27-slot storage, taglock queue, blocked-open rules, redstone/comparator behavior, block item drop data, and Mutating Sprig route unchanged. Fresh QA should confirm a Leech Chest breaks much more slowly than a normal chest and resists ordinary blast cleanup while still dropping its item/data on player break.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Coffin lid obstruction parity. A 1.7.10 `BlockCoffin` comparison showed sneak-use refuses to toggle the lid when either the head half or foot half has a solid block immediately above it (`isSideSolid(..., DOWN)`), and it does so silently before flipping the open flag. The port now checks both halves for a sturdy underside above before syncing the `OPEN` state, so blocked Coffins no longer open or close through overhead solid blocks. Fresh QA should place an openable Coffin with clear air, then put a solid block above the head half and the foot half separately and confirm sneak-use leaves the lid state unchanged.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Coffin placement gate and hardness parity. A 1.7.10 `ItemCoffin` / `BlockCoffin` comparison showed Coffins only place from a top-face use, require both foot and head spaces to be air, require solid support under both halves, and use `1.0F` hardness. The port now refuses side-face, blocked-space, unsupported-foot, and unsupported-head placements before creating either half, and the registered Coffin strength is back to source hardness. Fresh QA should place Coffins on normal full supports, then retry side-face placement, non-air head/foot spaces, and missing support under either half to confirm no partial Coffin appears.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Mutating Sprig creature-route feedback and footprint parity. A 1.7.10 `ItemMutator.onItemUseFirst` comparison showed the Critter Snare, Flying Monkey, Owl, Toad, and Parasytic Louse routes all finish with a center `ParticleEffect.SLIME` plus big-slime sound instead of generic smoke/grass-place feedback. The old creature-sacrifice checks also play slime particles with Zombie/Wolf/Ocelot/Creeper/Ghast death sounds before discarding the consumed animal, Flying Monkey clears the four cardinal cocoa blocks, and Parasytic Louse clears the four cardinal Vine positions. The port now restores those feedback and clearing paths, and the sprig slime helper now uses the old packet's width-derived particle density/randomized slime sound pitch instead of treating the range argument as a particle count. Fresh QA should rerun all five creature routes and confirm their sacrifice sounds/particles, center slime cue, source footprint clears, and lack of generic smoke.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Mutating Sprig plant-route feedback parity. A 1.7.10 `ItemMutator.onItemUseFirst` comparison showed the Cactus-to-Wild-Bramble, Reed-to-Void-Bramble, and Wheat-to-Wormwood routes each play `ParticleEffect.SLIME` with the big-slime sound at the clicked center instead of the port's generic smoke/grass-place finish cue; the two vertical bramble routes also send silent slime particles at every converted cactus/reed block in the source top-down column loop. The port now gives those three plant routes their source slime feedback while leaving their already-restored block footprints and one-durability sprig use unchanged. Fresh QA should run all three mutations and confirm the center slime cue appears, the bramble columns get per-block silent slime particles, and the generic smoke puff does not appear.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Mutating Sprig chest-family feedback parity. A 1.7.10 `ItemMutator.onItemUseFirst` comparison showed the Chest-to-Grasspers, Trapped-Chest-to-Leech-Chest, and Leech-Chest-to-Blood-Roses routes each play `ParticleEffect.SLIME` with the big-slime sound at the ritual center instead of the port's generic smoke/grass-place finish cue. The port now gives those three chest-family routes their source slime feedback while leaving their already-restored block footprints and one-durability sprig use unchanged. Fresh QA should run all three chest-family mutations and confirm the slime feedback appears at the center without the generic smoke puff.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Mutating Sprig Grass/Mycelium feedback parity. A 1.7.10 `ItemMutator.onItemUseFirst` comparison showed the basic Grass Block <-> Mycelium flips play the old instant-spell particle plus big-slime sound at the mutated block instead of the port's generic smoke/grass-place finish cue. The port now lets those simple block conversions own the same source feedback path used by the clay route, with 8 instant-spell particles at the old `y + 1.0` position and one durability loss for the use. Fresh QA should flip Grass Block to Mycelium and back with a Mutating Sprig and confirm the slime/instant-spell cue appears without the old generic smoke puff.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Mutating Sprig underwater clay footprint parity. A 1.7.10 `ItemMutator.onItemUseFirst` / `ItemGeneral.setBlockToClay` comparison showed the old dirt route only starts when the clicked block is Dirt with actual water above it, then tries the center plus four cardinal cells; each cell only becomes Clay if that cell is also Dirt with water above, and each conversion plays the slime/instant-spell cue. The port now removes the non-source clay-to-dirt and ice-above branches and restores that one-way five-cell footprint. Fresh QA should use the sprig on water-covered Dirt with water-covered cardinal Dirt neighbors and confirm the converted cells become Clay, then confirm Clay under water, Dirt under Ice, and non-Dirt cardinal neighbors do not convert.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Mutating Sprig Leech Chest vine consumption parity. A 1.7.10 `ItemMutator.isMutatableTrapChest` comparison showed the old trapped-chest route clears the empty Trapped Chest and all four adjacent Vine blocks before placing the Leech Chest. The port already validated the same empty single Trapped Chest, four adjacent Vines, and water below those Vines, but it left the Vines behind after the mutation. The route now clears the source five-block footprint before placing `witchery:leech_chest`. Fresh QA should mutate an empty Trapped Chest with four adjacent Vines over water and confirm the four Vines disappear while the Leech Chest appears at the center.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Mutating Sprig Critter Snare footprint parity. A 1.7.10 `ItemMutator.isMutatableWeb` comparison showed the old route checks a cobweb over water, four cardinal planted Alder Saplings, and a nearby Zombie, then kills the Zombie with the zombie-death/slime cue, clears only the center cobweb, and replaces the four Alder Saplings with empty Critter Snares. The port no longer accepts dropped Alder Sapling items or produces one center snare for this route; it now preserves the source four-snare cardinal footprint and leaves the center clear. Fresh QA should build the cobweb-over-water plus four planted Alder Saplings setup, trap a Zombie in the old nearby volume, use the Mutating Sprig, and verify four empty `witchery:critter_snare` blocks appear around the cleared center with the Zombie consumed.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Altar object and Critter Snare support-loss drop parity. A 1.7.10 `BlockCandelabra` / `BlockChalice` / `BlockCrystalBall` comparison showed each block drops its own item from the old neighbor-change validity check before clearing when its solid support disappears; `BlockCritterSnare` inherits the same drop-on-invalid-support behavior from the old bush path while preserving caught metadata. The port no longer lets their modern `updateShape` path replace them with air first, so Candelabras, empty/filled Chalices, Crystal Balls, and empty/caught Critter Snares now reach the existing drop-aware support-loss hook. Fresh QA should place each on a removable support, break or replace the support, and verify the correct item drops, including a filled Chalice staying filled and a caught Critter Snare keeping its caught custom data.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Critter Snare Polynesia bat-stock bridge. A 1.7.10 `BlockCritterSnare` / `ItemPolynesiaCharm` comparison showed bats caught by Critter Snares preserve whether they already had a `WitcheryShopStock` merchant inventory by storing metadata `9`, then restore an empty stock inventory when released. The port now adds the source `WitcheryShopStock` helpers to `PolynesiaCharmItem`, stores such bats as `Caught=9`, renders/sounds/releases them as bats through the source-style low-bit mask, preserves that custom data in picked/dropped snares, and keeps plain bat-ball crafting strict to normal `Caught=1` bat snares. Fresh QA should tag a bat with `WitcheryShopStock`, catch/release it through a Critter Snare, and verify the released bat retains an empty stock tag while ordinary bat snares still craft the Concentrated Bat Ball.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Ruby Slippers home-dimension target parity. A 1.7.10 `ItemWitchesClothes.trySayTheresNoPlaceLikeHome` comparison showed the no-Waystone home route checks the player's bed in the current dimension first, then checks the Overworld bed/spawn fallback; it does not jump to an unrelated stored bed in a third dimension. The port now only accepts modern respawn data when it belongs to the player's current dimension or the Overworld, otherwise it falls back to the Overworld spawn route. Fresh QA should set a respawn in a non-Overworld dimension, chant from another non-matching dimension, and verify Ruby Slippers use the Overworld fallback instead of that unrelated respawn target.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Ruby Slippers bound-Waystone consumption parity. A 1.7.10 `ItemWitchesClothes.trySayTheresNoPlaceLikeHome` comparison showed the bound-Waystone branch calls `EntityItem.setDead()` after the infusion-energy spend succeeds, then uses the already-read Waystone stack for the normal teleport or rare Void Bramble misfire. The port now discards the offered dropped Waystone entity outright instead of shrinking one item and leaving a remainder, matching the source route and the old one-stack bound-Waystone subitem. Fresh QA should drop a bound Waystone near an infused Ruby Slippers wearer, chant the phrase, and confirm the dropped entity is fully consumed before the teleport or rare misfire branch runs.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Ruby Slippers world-spawn fallback parity. A 1.7.10 `ItemWitchesClothes.trySayTheresNoPlaceLikeHome` comparison showed the no-bed home route falls back to the Overworld's actual world spawn from `world.getSpawnPoint()`, then resolves a safe bed-style spawn around that coordinate. The port no longer uses the temporary hardcoded `(0,64,0)` fallback; it now starts from the modern Overworld level-data spawn position before applying the existing safe-position search. Fresh QA should remove the player's bed, move or use a world with a non-origin spawn, say "there's no place like home" with enough infusion energy, and verify the slippers target that world spawn instead of origin.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Ruby Slippers home cooldown ordering parity. A 1.7.10 `ItemWitchesClothes.trySayTheresNoPlaceLikeHome` comparison showed the bed/spawn route records `WITCLastRubySlipperTime` after resolving the home target but before the final infusion-energy spend and teleport attempt. The port now mirrors that source ordering: an insufficient-infusion home-route attempt still starts the persisted 30-minute home cooldown once a target has been selected, while the bound-Waystone route keeps its source ordering of starting the one-minute cooldown only after energy succeeds and the offered Waystone is consumed. Fresh QA should attempt the home chant with too little infusion energy, confirm the old no-charge failure appears, then repeat and verify the cooldown message persists.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Baba's Hat source damage gate parity. A 1.7.10 `GenericEvents` comparison showed the infused Baba's Hat blink only runs for entity or projectile damage, skips the protection branch while the player is actively transformed as a wolf/wolfman, and also skips powered draining `BoltDamageSource` hits. The port now mirrors those gates: magic-only/bypasses-armor damage no longer triggers Baba's Hat by itself, transformed werewolf players do not get the hat blink, and powered draining `BoltEntity` hits bypass the blink. Fresh QA should equip Baba's Hat with infusion energy and verify mob melee and arrows can still trigger the 25% blink, plain magic damage does not, powered draining nullifying bolts do not, and transformed werewolf state suppresses it.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Shared Void Bramble teleport helper parity. A 1.7.10 `BlockVoidBramble`, `ItemWitchesClothes`, `GenericEvents`, and `ItemGeneral` comparison showed the random blink helper is reused by Void Bramble collisions, Ruby Slippers' 1% bound-Waystone misfire, and Baba's Hat's infused hit-cancel blink. The port now exposes that shared one-roll vertical scan helper, restores the old 20-particle portal/enderman feedback before and after successful same-dimension teleports, routes Ruby Slippers' misfire through it without the previous non-source fallback to the bound Waystone, and routes Baba's Hat through it instead of a heightmap/16-attempt safe-position search. Fresh QA should retest Void Bramble touch teleports, temporarily force Ruby Slippers' misfire roll, and trigger Baba's Hat outside Torment to confirm the shared one-column scan feel, feedback, energy/cooldown behavior, and hit cancel.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Void Bramble teleport scan parity. A 1.7.10 `BlockVoidBramble.teleportRandomly` comparison showed the source chooses one random X/Z target within 500 blocks, scans downward from the bramble Y to find a solid base, scans upward at most 64 blocks and no higher than Y 250 until it finds a non-bedrock base with three air blocks above, and teleports once if that scan succeeds. The port previously used a heightmap lookup with up to 16 modern `randomTeleport` attempts. It now follows the old one-roll vertical scan while preserving owner column removal, ritual nulling, light level, no collision, unbreakable profile, and the restored portal ambience. Fresh QA should walk living entities through Void Bramble near uneven terrain, caves, bedrock roofs, and cramped cover to confirm the old scan feel and that failed scans can simply leave the entity in place until the next touch tick.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Void Bramble portal ambience parity. A 1.7.10 `BlockVoidBramble` comparison showed the source block emits a falling portal particle on roughly every other client tick at a random point in its upper body. The port now restores that `PORTAL` ambience while leaving owner column removal, ritual nulling, light level, no collision, unbreakable profile, and teleport mechanics unchanged. Fresh QA should place Void Bramble in a lit area, confirm faint portal particles drift downward around it, and recheck owner/creative column removal plus ritual-null behavior.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Wild Bramble support-loss parity. A 1.7.10 `BlockBramble` comparison showed both bramble metas require a non-air block below, and `onNeighborBlockChange` drops the bramble item then clears the block when that support is lost. The port already had the survival predicate but did not run the source drop-on-neighbor-change path. It now destroys unsupported Wild Bramble with drops on neighbor updates, without invoking the player-only no-shears break-spread path. Fresh QA should place Wild Bramble on a removable support, break or replace the support, and confirm the bramble item drops; then break a supported Wild Bramble by hand to confirm no-shears spread still only happens on player harvest.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Wild Bramble break-spread replacement parity. A 1.7.10 `BlockBramble` comparison showed Wild Bramble's no-shears break spread only tries the eight neighboring columns, scans y-1..y+1, and may replace air, full Snow Block, or the old `tallgrass` block while requiring non-air support below. The port was using broad modern `canBeReplaced()`, which could consume unrelated replaceable plants. It now limits break spread to air, Snow Block, Short Grass, and Fern while keeping the source eight-neighbor order, vertical scan, 50% placement roll, 2/3 early-stop roll, no-collision shape, damage, and shears suppression. Fresh QA should break Wild Bramble with and without shears beside air, Snow Blocks, short grass/ferns, flowers, crops, and other replaceable blocks, confirming only the source target set is consumed.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Ember Moss and Glint Weed spread substrate parity. A 1.7.10 `BlockEmberMoss` / `BlockGlintWeed` comparison showed both plants spread over dirt, grass, mycelium, sand, and farmland; the previous port translation had incorrectly used stone and soul sand for the last two legacy block fields. The port now uses the source substrate list for both spreading plants while leaving their spread cadence, density cap, support rules, particles, fire touch, light, and shears-only Ember Moss drop behavior unchanged. Fresh QA should place each plant above all five allowed substrates and confirm random spread can advance there, then confirm stone and soul sand no longer act as spread bases.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Blood Rose support/outline parity. A 1.7.10 `BlockBloodRose` comparison showed the rose is a `BlockBaseContainer` plant-like block without a source support-survival check, and its client selection outline is smaller than the constructor bounds: 0.3625-0.6375 X/Z and 0-0.5375 Y. The port now removes the modern support check and uses that smaller source outline while keeping empty collision, no normal drops, and the Boline-owned harvest path. Fresh QA should place or generate Blood Roses, remove the block below one and confirm it remains, verify the selection box sits tightly around the flower, and retest sample storage plus Boline harvest.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Dream Catcher source footprint/collision parity. A 1.7.10 `BlockDreamCatcher` comparison showed the wall-hung block has a thin selection outline, about half-block wide and one old-pixel thick, while its collision AABB is `null`. The port now uses that thinner wall outline and an empty collision shape instead of a broader colliding slab. Fresh QA should place Dream Catchers on all four wall faces, confirm the selection box hugs the hanging model, confirm players/mobs do not collide with it, and then recheck support break plus stored-weave drop behavior.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Blood Rose Boline harvest parity. A 1.7.10 `ItemBoline` + `BlockBloodRose` comparison showed Blood Roses do not drop from normal block loot, while Boline handles a special break path that removes the block and drops one Blood Rose item. The port now keeps the rose's own drops empty and restores the Boline-owned break-event drop instead of relying on a conditional block loot table. Fresh QA should break a Blood Rose bare-handed and with ordinary tools to confirm no item drops, then break one with a Boline and confirm one Blood Rose item appears while stored taglock behavior still works.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Leaping Lily pulse gate parity. A 1.7.10 `BlockLeapingLily` comparison showed the block only adds its 10-tick Speed I and Jump Boost V effects when the living entity does not already have the matching effect. The port now preserves that non-refreshing source pulse instead of reapplying both effects every tick while an entity remains on the lily. Fresh QA should step onto a Leaping Lily, confirm the short boost still applies, then stand on it and confirm the effect timer is not continuously refreshed every tick.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Grassper stored-item removal parity. A 1.7.10 `BlockGrassper` comparison showed the block drops its stored one-slot inventory from the normal block-removal hook, not only when a player breaks it directly. The port now drops and clears held items from the modern removal/explosion hooks, so replacement, normal break, and explosions release the stored component once while still preserving the existing right-click retrieval path. Fresh QA should load a Grassper, break it by hand, replace it with another block, and destroy one by explosion or command, confirming the held item drops once and comparator output clears.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Wolfman/Were Villager lifecycle tick parity. A 1.7.10 `EntityVillagerWere` + `EntityWolfman` comparison showed Were Villagers check their full-moon-night transformation on tick `% 100 == 3`, and Wolfmen periodically clear vanilla Weakness on tick `% 40 == 4`. The port now matches both adjacent source timings while preserving the existing full-moon-night and Wolfsbane-placeholder gates. Fresh QA should spawn a Were Villager on a full-moon night and confirm it converts promptly, then apply Weakness to a Wolfman and confirm the effect is stripped within about two seconds.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Infectious Wolfman villager-bite parity. A 1.7.10 `GenericEvents` + `Shapeshift` comparison showed infectious `EntityWolfman` bites convert Villagers into `EntityVillagerWere` once the incoming damage would drop them below 25% health, without needing the victim to die. The port now runs that source low-health damage hook for `WolfmanEntity`, spawns the existing `WereVillagerEntity`, preserves the infectious flag, cancels the old damage on conversion, and uses the source conversion level event. Fresh QA should spawn an infectious Wolfman, damage a Villager below the threshold, and confirm the Villager becomes a Were Villager immediately instead of waiting for a full-moon death roll.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Hobgoblin out-of-world damage immunity parity. A 1.7.10 `GenericEvents` / Resurrected `CommonEvents` comparison showed old `EntityGoblin` cancels `DamageSource.outOfWorld` in the non-player hurt branch. The port now restores that hook for `HobgoblinEntity` through `HobgoblinEvents`, while leaving `GoblinGulgEntity` and `GoblinMogEntity` out because the old boss classes do not extend `EntityGoblin`. Fresh QA should use a controlled out-of-world damage source against a Hobgoblin and confirm it survives, then confirm ordinary damage still affects it.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Player target-screening parity in the Witchery target hook. A 1.7.10 `Infusion` / Resurrected `CommonEvents` comparison showed player target changes first honor vanilla-style invisibility by clearing the target unless the invisible player is still the nearest player within 16 blocks, then clear player targets from blinded attackers before checking Witch Robes, Zombie Respect, or Necromancer Robes. The port now restores that order in `WitcheryPlayerEvents.onLivingChangeTarget`. Fresh QA should have a mob target an invisible player with and without another visible player nearby, then apply Blindness to a hostile mob and confirm it drops its player target before clothing/vampire checks matter.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Vampire Zombie Respect targeting parity. A 1.7.10 `Infusion` / Resurrected `CommonEvents` comparison showed the old target hook makes `EntityZombie` drop a player target only when that player has vampire level `>= 10`; the broader undead-family target clearing remains the Necromancer Robes branch. The port now mirrors that order in the modern target-change hook: Zombies ignore level-10 vampire players, while Necromancer Robes still protect non-level-10 vampires and ordinary players from all undead targets. Fresh QA should set a player to vampire level 9 and 10, spawn Zombies and other undead, and confirm only Zombies drop the level-10 vampire target unless Necromancer Robes are worn.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Town zombie/villager protection parity. A 1.7.10 `Config` + `GenericEvents` + `Infusion` comparison showed `TownZombieAttackReductionMode` defaults to `1`, clamps to `0..2`, makes zombie attacks on Villagers deal exactly `0.5F` damage at mode `1+`, and clears Zombie Villager targets at mode `2`. The port now exposes the same `general.TownZombieAttackReductionMode` common config key, applies the 0.5-damage rule through the villager incoming-damage hook, and clears zombie villager targets through the existing modern target-change hook. Fresh QA should confirm default config mode 1 lets a Zombie target a Villager but only deal `0.5` damage, mode 0 restores normal Zombie damage, and mode 2 prevents Zombies from keeping Villagers targeted.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Village protector villager-damage parity. A 1.7.10 `GenericEvents` comparison showed ordinary Villagers should not take damage from Witchery Village Guards or Witch Hunters. The port now has a `VillageSocialEvents` incoming-damage hook that cancels Villager damage when the responsible attacker is `VillageGuardEntity` or `WitchHunterEntity`, matching the old social-protection rule while leaving other villager threats alone. Fresh QA should spawn a guard and hunter beside a Villager, force melee and projectile hits, and confirm the Villager takes no damage while Zombies, vampires, werewolves, and player attacks still affect Villagers normally.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Village Guard vampire blood/conversion parity. A 1.7.10 `EntityVillageGuard`, `GenericEvents`, and `ItemGlassGoblet` comparison showed guards carry a persisted 500-point `BloodLevel`, vampire bites drain guard blood with the source two-thirds guard rule, mesmerise accepts guards, and a vampire-blood Full Glass Goblet can convert a transfixed, fully drained guard near a Coffin into an `EntityVampire`. The port now stores guard BloodLevel, routes vampire bites/mesmerise through Village Guards, lets clothing drink/mesmerise hooks apply, and extends the full-goblet conversion gate to Villagers or Village Guards. Fresh QA should mesmerise and drain a guard to 0, place a Coffin within four blocks, use the owner's vampire-blood goblet, and confirm the guard is replaced by a Vampire mob, the goblet empties, and a level-9 owner can advance to level 10. The source guard's passive village blood regeneration/village side effects remain later guard-AI polish because the port does not yet model the old `villageObj` guard tick path.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Glass Goblet villager-to-vampire conversion parity. A 1.7.10 `ItemGlassGoblet` comparison showed a vampire-blood Full Glass Goblet should convert a transfixed, fully drained villager near a Coffin into an `EntityVampire`, empty the goblet, play the red-dust drink cue, and advance a level-9 caster to level 10 when the goblet contains their own blood and their cap allows it. The port now completes that ordinary villager conversion by spawning the current source-backed Vampire mob through normal conversion spawn initialization, replacing the held full goblet with an empty goblet, and syncing the owner level-up.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Glass Goblet Blood Power fill parity. A 1.7.10 `ItemGlassGoblet` comparison showed empty goblets only fill for vampire level 9+ players and spend 125 Blood Power through `decreaseBloodPower(125, true)`; failures use the old not-enough-blood, not-high-enough, and nothing-happens messages with snare feedback, and success uses red dust plus drink feedback. The port now uses the level-9 gate, Blood Power cost, creative cost bypass, source-style messages, red dust particles, and the source-facing Full Glass Goblet output while keeping the existing induction, chicken-blood, and villager validation paths. Fresh QA should test non-vampire failure, level-8 vampire failure, level-9 insufficient Blood Power failure, level-9 success spending 125 Blood Power, and creative-mode cost bypass.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Bone Needle Blood-stained Wool gate parity. A 1.7.10 `ItemGeneral` comparison showed the old Bone Needle route only converts White Wool for vampire level 4+ players and spends 125 Blood Power through `decreaseBloodPower(125, true)`, with only snare feedback on failure. The port now uses that level gate and Blood Power cost, preserves the source creative-mode cost bypass, keeps placing source `witchery:bloodedwool`, and updates the Primer/status docs to stop describing the first-pass human-blood route. Fresh QA should test level 3 failure, level 4 failure with too little Blood Power, level 4 success with 125+ Blood Power, and creative-mode vampire cost bypass.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Blood-stained Wool source-ID restoration. A 1.7.10 `BlockBloodedWool`, block registry, language, Bone Needle, and smelting comparison showed the old block/item path is `witchery:bloodedwool` with `bloodedwool` art and the display name `Blood-stained Wool`. The port now registers `witchery:bloodedwool`, exposes it in the Witchery creative tab, uses the old block art for block/item models, makes Bone Needle conversion place it, gives it self loot, and moves the primary Woven Cruor smelting recipe to it. The previous `witchery:blood_stained_wool` port ID remains registered and smeltable for compatibility. Fresh QA should use Bone Needle on White Wool as a vampire with 125 Blood Power, confirm the placed/picked block is `bloodedwool`, smelt it into Woven Cruor, and verify old `blood_stained_wool` stacks still smelt.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Vampire Book source-ID restoration. A 1.7.10 `VAMPIRE_BOOK` / `ItemMarkupBook` / recipe / Bookshop comparison showed the old `witchery:vampirebook` is Observations of an Immortal with `vbook` art, creative variants at levels 0 and 9, a Nether Wart + Nether Star + Book + Garlic Seeds recipe, Torn Page upgrades through levels 1-9, and a read callback that raises the vampire level cap to book level + 1. The port now exposes `witchery:vampire_book` as the source-facing Observations item, uses the old `vbook.png` art and source tooltip, shows base and max-level stacks in the creative tab, maps the old Garlic Seeds slot to the current `witchery:garlic` crop item, moves the shaped recipe and Bookshop guaranteed book roll from the modern `immortals_observations_book` compatibility ID to `vampire_book`, lets Torn Page upgrades work on either book ID, and keeps the existing read callback / page-count / level-cap unlock behavior. Torn Page art is also back on the old `ingredient.vbookPage` sprite. Fresh QA should craft `vampire_book`, add Torn Pages through level 9, right-click as a vampire to confirm cap unlocks, and inspect Bookshop loot.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Cauldron Book source-ID restoration. A 1.7.10 `WitcheryItems` / `WitcheryRecipes` check showed the cauldron guide is the plain `ItemMarkupBook(7)` item named `witchery:cauldronbook`, textured as `bookcauldron`, with the two-row Book + Black Dye + Feather + Dirt recipe. The port now exposes `witchery:cauldron_book` as a one-stack Witches' Primer-opening guidebook, adds it to the guide creative section, uses the original `bookcauldron.png` art and old display name/tip text, and moves the source-shaped crafting output from the earlier `witches_brews_book` compatibility item to the source-facing ID. `witchery:witches_brews_book` remains a compatibility guidebook/Bookshop loot item, but the craftable source route now outputs `cauldron_book`. Fresh QA should craft `cauldron_book`, confirm the source art/name and guide-tab placement, and right-click it with Patchouli present/absent.

Previous local delta (2026-06-02): Sun Grenade source-release parity. A 1.7.10 `EntityGrenade` / `BlockLight` comparison showed the Sun Grenade should not be only an instant impact blast: while active it carries a hidden full-light `witchery:light` block through air/impact, burns nearby undead during the release, then finishes after the old 200-tick projectile lifetime with a final undead fire/Nausea burst and Quartz Sphere return. The port now registers the hidden non-item light block, saves/cleans the placed light position on the grenade entity, preserves the Duplication Grenade reflection branch, and keeps the temporary light from replacing real blocks or fluids. Fresh QA should throw a Sun Grenade in a dark area, confirm the temporary light follows/holds around impact then disappears, confirm Zombies/Skeletons/Vampires ignite during and after release, confirm nearby living targets get Nausea on the final burst, and confirm the Quartz Sphere returns.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Circle magic playtest corrections. Source-scale circle detection, golden-center activation, upgraded altar range lookup, active ritual inspector output, Charging with dropped/Grassper-held support items, Sanctity/Imprisonment support handling, and confirmed small plus portable Protection barriers are documented in `docs/archive/status/RITUAL_QA_PROGRESS.md`. This pass also corrected portable Protection back to the source small-ring Obsidian + Charged Attuned Stone route, stopped incomplete medium Charging/Protection branches from blocking valid smaller rites, restored Banish Demon support sacrifices (Blaze Powder + blank Waystone, plus power or Charged Attuned Stone for portable), restored the faint translucent barrier render path, source-shaped Protection barrier geometry after medium player-blocking QA exposed a gap, and kept the user-approved green protection ring pulse. Fresh QA should retest medium Protection player blocking, then test both Banish Demon branches next.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Custom-brew flower reagent promotion. The twelve dandelion/poppy/shrub derivative items now register as normal simple alchemy ingredients instead of legacy placeholders. They keep the existing recipes, item models, textures, and clean display names, appear with ingredients/alchemy in the creative tab, and remain available as brew-effect inputs such as `dandelion_of_thorns`, `poppy_of_ink`, and `shrub_of_webs`. Fresh QA should confirm all twelve are absent from the placeholder group, visible with ingredients, and still craft and feed their existing brew recipes.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Blank Demonic Contract source-alias promotion. The old 1.7 `contract` subitem represented by `witchery:contract_ownership` is now a live `DemonicContractItem` alias instead of a placeholder. It appears beside `witchery:demonic_contract`, Flame Imps accept either blank contract for the current 25-level tame path, and the five Imp-magic contract recipes now use a `witchery:demonic_contracts` item tag so either blank ID can be upgraded. Fresh QA should confirm `contract_ownership` is absent from placeholders, appears with contracts, tames an untamed Flame Imp, and crafts the five contract upgrades.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Torment contract alias promotion. The original 1.7 `contract_torment` item and Resurrected's exact `torment_contract` ID now register as live Torment contract items instead of placeholder/asset-only entries. They use the source contract art, show the old standing-stone warning tooltip, appear with dreams/demons/contracts, and large-circle Lord of Torment activation accepts them beside the existing `torment_scroll`. Fresh QA should drop each Torment contract on the correct large circle and confirm the ritual path still works, then confirm neither contract appears in the placeholder group.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Legacy source Witchcraft book alias promotion. The original 1.7 `book_oven`, `book_distilling`, `book_circle_magic`, `book_infusions`, `book_herbology`, `book_wands`, and `book_burning` generic-item subitems are now real one-stack Witches' Primer-opening compatibility guidebooks instead of inert placeholders. They appear in the guide creative group, keep the source display names and old `ingredient.book_*` art, and have source-shaped adapted recipes that output the legacy IDs. Fresh QA should craft/use each old-ID book and confirm the placeholder group no longer contains any Witchcraft guidebooks.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Advanced Witchcraft book promotion. Resurrected's `collecting_fumes_book`, `brews_and_infusions_book`, `symbology_book`, `conjuration_and_fetishes_book`, and `witches_brews_book` exact IDs now leave the placeholder roster. They are one-stack Witches' Primer-opening guidebooks, appear in the guide creative group, use their Resurrected names/art, and can roll from Bookshop chests beside the already-promoted Distilling/Circle Magic/Herbology books. The Witches' Brews shaped recipe later moved to the source-facing `cauldron_book` ID. Fresh QA should craft the recipe-backed guidebooks, confirm they open the Primer or Patchouli-missing feedback, and inspect Bookshop loot.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Demon Soul ingredient alias promotion. The old 1.7 `brewSoulHunger`, `brewSoulAnguish`, `brewSoulFear`, and `brewSoulTorment` ingredient subitems and Resurrected's exact `hunger_demon_soul`, `anguish_demon_soul`, `fear_demon_soul`, and `torment_demon_soul` IDs all share the same four "Soul of ... Demon" names. The port now registers those exact IDs as normal simple ingredient items, exposes them with dreams/demons/contracts, keeps the source soul art, and removes their placeholder suffix/group entries. Fresh QA should confirm all four Demon Soul items appear beside Soul of Torment and no longer appear in the placeholder group; actual Soul Brew effect mechanics remain in the brew backlog.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Deployed Hand of Death exact-ID promotion. Resurrected uses a real `witchery:deployed_deaths_hand` item while the Death armor set scythe mode is active, then swaps back to `witchery:deaths_hand`. The port now registers that exact deployed ID with the live Hand of Death behavior, uses it for right-click deployment, lets combat treat either the exact item or older saved `WITCScytheDeployed` stacks as deployed, reverts deployed stacks when the full Death set is missing, and removes the placeholder suffix/group entry. Fresh QA should equip the full Death set, right-click Hand of Death, confirm the stack/model swaps to the scythe item, attack with the AOE, right-click again to return, and confirm old custom-data deployed stacks still work.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Heart of Gold ingredient promotion. Resurrected registers `witchery:golden_heart` as a real ingredient item named `Heart of Gold`, and its discovered data use is the future `animal_attraction` brew effect ingredient. The port now promotes `golden_heart` out of `LEGACY_PLACEHOLDER_ITEMS`, exposes it in the ingredients/alchemy creative group, and removes the placeholder suffix while keeping the existing item art. Fresh QA should confirm Heart of Gold appears with ingredients, no longer appears in the placeholder group, and still has its model.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Silver Dust placeholder cleanup. A 1.7.10 plus Resurrected scan showed `SILVER_DUST` is only an internal constant name for the real `witchery:silver_deposits` item, while `witchery:silver_dust` is a recipe id for external `dustSilver` conversion rather than a registered Witchery item. The fake `silver_dust` placeholder item, creative-tab entry, lang key, and unused item/model/texture assets are now removed; the live Silver Deposits item and deposit-based local recipes remain unchanged. Fresh QA should confirm the placeholder group no longer contains Silver Dust and that Silver Vat collection plus Silver Deposits crafting routes still work.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Charged Crossbow placeholder cleanup. A Resurrected/source scan showed `witchery:charged_crossbow` is not a real registered item; it is the charged model used by `witchery:crossbow_pistol` and the port's Hand Bow loaded-state model predicates. The fake placeholder item is now removed from `LEGACY_PLACEHOLDER_ITEMS`, the placeholder creative-tab group, and lang, while the model asset stays in place for the live bow/crossbow visuals. Fresh QA should load a bolt into `crossbow_pistol`/`hand_bow` and confirm the charged visual still appears, with no separate Charged Crossbow item in the creative placeholder group.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Medallion of Blood and Beast promotion. Resurrected's `witchery:creative_medallion` is no longer an inert placeholder: it now shares the live one-stack, epic Bat/Wolf creative progression token behavior, appears beside `witchery:creative_bat_wolf_token` in the vampire/werewolf creative section, uses the Resurrected display name, and has its own tooltip. Normal held use cycles werewolf mastery/state through 0-3, sneak-use cycles vampire level/state through 0-10, and both paths sync player data with feedback. Fresh QA should use both token IDs, confirm messages/state changes match, and verify the medallion no longer appears in the placeholder tab group.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Resurrected charm and pentacle exact-ID promotion. `witchery:fanciful_thinking_charm` and `witchery:pentacle` now leave the inert placeholder roster. The Fanciful Thinking charm uses the Resurrected recipe output, appears beside the existing Charm of Disrupted Dreams, and now protects players from Nightmare armor stripping through the same restored check. The exact Pentacle ID uses the live altar-placed item behavior, has the old Koboldite Nugget/Ingot/Diamond recipe output, appears with tools, and restores the original x2 altar recharge multiplier when placed on top of a valid altar. `witchery:koboldite_pentacle` and `witchery:charm_of_disrupted_dreams` remain registered compatibility stacks. Fresh QA should craft both exact IDs, place the Pentacle on an altar and verify recharge doubles, then carry the Fanciful Thinking charm while a Nightmare hits the player.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Living-village gatehouse lanterns. Gatehouses now hang two lanterns from small outside wall brackets on the exterior tower faces, using the same living-village branch that spawns wall guards. Abandoned/zombie villages still receive walls and gatehouses but skip both guards and these lanterns, preserving the dead-village look. Fresh QA should inspect plains/savanna/desert/taiga gates at night and confirm the lanterns do not block the opening or appear on abandoned villages.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Gate threshold height sampling fix. Village gatehouses now choose their reference floor height only from the immediate 3-wide path strip outside the gate plus the immediate 3-wide strip inside the gate. The sampler ignores water/building columns, rounds the six-cell average instead of flooring it, and no longer lets the 15-block inward road-grading run drag the gate threshold down through slopes or nearby houses. Fresh QA should inspect hilly plains/taiga/savanna gates and confirm the path block just inside the gate lands level with the opening instead of one block too low.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Infinity Egg altar boost restoration. `witchery:infinity_egg` now restores the old source `BlockInfinityEgg` slice as a Dragon Egg-shaped falling block using the original `dragon_egg.png` texture, 3.0 hardness, 15.0 resistance, faint light, no teleport-on-click behavior, creative-tab/block-item/assets/loot wiring, and the player-facing Infinity Egg name. Altar scans now count a nearby Infinity Egg as a separate 1000-power nature source with cap 1, and an Infinity Egg placed on any connected altar top multiplies altar max power and recharge scale by 10 like source. Fresh QA should place one near an altar, place one on top of a valid 2x3 altar, inspect `/right-click altar` power/recharge readout, and confirm right/left-clicking the egg does not teleport it.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Shaded Glass redstone block restoration. `witchery:shaded_glass` now restores the old source `BlockShadedGlass` behavior in a modern single-blockstate form: sixteen colored stacks preserve their color through `minecraft:block_state`, unpowered glass uses the old `shadedglassoff*` textures, redstone-powered glass switches to the old active `shadedglass*` textures and blocks light at opacity 15, normal breaking drops nothing like the source, and every stained-glass color crafts eight Shaded Glass around Redstone. Fresh QA should craft several colors, place/pick-block them, power/unpower them, and verify the powered state darkens/blocks skylight without needing the old hidden active block ID.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Tormented Twine pit trap cauldron route. `witchery:pit_dirt` and `witchery:pit_grass` now have the old source-style heated Witch's Cauldron trigger path: fill and heat a Witch's Cauldron, keep a valid altar with 4000 power nearby, drop Dirt + Mandrake Root for four Pit Dirt or Dirt + Wheat Seeds + Dandelion for four Pit Grass, then use Tormented Twine on the cauldron. The working consumes one fill level, the trigger twine, the offered items, and altar power, and drops the four trap blocks from the cauldron bowl. Fresh QA should verify both recipes, insufficient-power messaging, and that the blocks still behave as no-collision camouflage after placement.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Practical Ice World block-family conversion. Direct Brew of Frost / `brew_of_ice` block use now has a first single-click slice of the old Ice World brew action for the already-live block families: wooden doors become `witchery:ice_door`, fences/gates become `witchery:ice_fence` / `witchery:ice_gate`, stairs/slabs become `witchery:ice_stairs` / `witchery:ice_slab`, pressure plates become `witchery:ice_pressure_plate`, wooden stockades become `witchery:ice_stockade`, dirt/grass/mycelium/sand become Snow Blocks, cobblestone/stone bricks/logs become Packed Ice, and stone/bricks/sandstone/planks/leaves become `witchery:perpetual_ice`. Direction/open/hinge, stair/slab/fence/gate, stockade, and waterlogged properties are preserved where the target supports them. This remains a bounded direct-use substitute, not the full old dynamic radius/custom-brew Ice World action yet.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Stockade ice-brew conversion. Direct Brew of Frost / `brew_of_ice` block use restored the source Ice World stockade conversion in first practical form: using it on a wooden `witchery:stockade` replaces that block with `witchery:ice_stockade`, preserving the shared connection/waterlogged properties, playing ice feedback, returning the empty bottle, and consuming the brew only on a successful conversion.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Gatehouse inner path regression fix. The inside wall-walk ledge is now placed after gate path grading, and stale ledge masonry in the first inward path columns is cleared before grading, so the terrain-only inward gate adjuster no longer mistakes the new high ledge for a building column and stops immediately. Fresh QA should confirm the outside and inside gate approaches both grade terrain again.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Gatehouse inside wall-walk ledge. Gatehouses now add a one-block village-facing rampart ledge at wall-walk height, extended one block past each side tower, so guards can move from the adjacent inner wall-walk onto the inside of the gatehouse and across the over-gate perch instead of being blocked by the side towers. Fresh wall QA should inspect both gatehouse ends and confirm guards can path from wall runs onto the gatehouse interior ledge.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Future ritual backlog note. `docs/roadmap/FUTURE_FEATURES.md` now tracks the user-requested future **Ritual of Tracing**: a Spirit World investigative rite that reveals fading traces of all player footsteps as spectral footprints or glyph trails, with retention limits and multiplayer privacy/config toggles called out before implementation. This is a future expansion note, not current parity scope.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Abandoned village wall guards. Vanilla abandoned/zombie villages are now detected from `/zombie/` jigsaw pool elements, and adaptive walls still generate but skip every Witchery wall guard spawn, including both interval wall-walk guards and the over-gate perch guard. Fresh QA should locate an abandoned village, confirm walls/gatehouses still build, and confirm no Village Guard entities are added on the dead village walls.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Gatehouse overhead footprint cleanup. Gatehouses now clear old wall/rampart blocks through the full locked 7x3 footprint up to `WALL_HEIGHT + 10`, six blocks higher than the previous clearance, before rebuilding the opening and side towers. The same taller scrub is used for touched natural vegetation at gatehouse cells. Fresh QA should inspect tall ramparts, corners, and gatehouse overlaps and confirm no old wall blocks remain above the opening, center perch, or side towers.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Wolfman and Were Villager renderer UV parity. The standalone Wolfman entity still uses the old 1.7.10 `ModelWolfman`, so its renderer now uses a dedicated `wolfman_entity.png` copied from the 1.7.10 `textures/entities/wolfman.png` UV layout instead of the Resurrected werewolf/player texture. Were Villagers now render with the vanilla villager model and base villager texture like source `EntityVillagerWere` did through `RenderVillager`, rather than using the Witch Hunter biped and generated `were_villager.png`. Fresh QA should spawn `/witchery spawn wolfman` and `/witchery spawn were_villager`, confirm no transparent model holes, and separately recheck transformed-player wolf form because it intentionally still uses the Resurrected werewolf texture/model path.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Natural gate path support fill. Generated gate paths and the three-lane gate opening no longer fill underneath with wall stone bricks. Path support now uses terrain-style filler: dirt for normal/savanna/taiga/snowy villages, sand for desert, and red sand for badlands. Fresh QA should inspect raised or cliffside gate paths and confirm no stone-brick filler shows under path blocks while gatehouse pillars still keep their masonry supports.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Paired wall rampart details. Fresh QA showed isolated one-block rampart details still looking odd. Rampart lips and inner wall-walk ledges now require a matching parallel neighboring wall column at compatible height, and corner walk bridges require both adjoining wall runs to be rampart-compatible. Fresh QA should inspect short wall stubs, jagged corners, and hilly edges and confirm no single-block rampart nubs remain.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Coven Circle above-water floor guard. Fresh QA found a Stone/Coven Circle whose floor generated at or below a frozen water surface. Coven Circle structure selection now rejects river/ocean biome footprints, and the live piece placement aborts if any 11x11 floor column contains fluid or ice at the floor/surface band. Fresh QA should retry snowy/taiga shoreline and frozen-water sites and confirm circles either place fully on land with the floor above water, or skip generation cleanly.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Wall corner walkability and 150 guard spacing. Fresh QA showed guards still could not path through rampart corners. Wall guard spacing now uses 150 usable wall-walk blocks, and L-corners add a flat inside diagonal bridge with cleared headroom wherever two wall runs meet around an interior turn. Fresh QA should inspect inner and outer wall turns and confirm guards can walk through corners without getting trapped while the rampart lip still reads cleanly.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Terrain-only 15-block inward gate grading. Gate approach grading inside villages now reaches up to 15 blocks inward from each gate, but only while the next column is terrain/road/air/liquid/soft vegetation. The same guard is used when sampling gate reference height, so planks, cobble, fences, doors, chests, sandstone house blocks, logs, and other building blocks stop the inward path before it rewrites or samples through a structure. Outward grading keeps the existing vegetation cleanup behavior. Fresh QA should inspect hilly gates near houses/farms and confirm the path smooths terrain farther than before without cutting through buildings.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Watch Tower roof stair facing correction. Fresh QA showed every Watch Tower roof stair was still 180 degrees wrong after the earlier ladder/roof pass. The port now maps the original old stair metas directly for the four local roof faces: north `3` -> `NORTH`, south `2` -> `SOUTH`, west `0` -> `EAST`, and east `1` -> `WEST`. Fresh QA should generate plains/desert/savanna/taiga/snowy Watch Towers and confirm all four pyramidal roof sides slope outward while the fixed center ladder, upper handoff ladder, lowered chest, and guard deck remain intact.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Village gate/rampart guard polish. User QA showed too few wall guards, mismatched inner/outer rampart corners, old wall blocks surviving inside gatehouses, and an Apothecary sign floating over the front door. Wall guard spacing now counts every 80 usable wall-walk/rampart positions instead of 200, gatehouses spawn a guard on a new center perch over each gate opening, corner columns use the same height and rampart detail pass as straight runs so lips/inner ledges continue cleanly through turns, and gatehouses first clear their full 7x3 footprint upward before rebuilding the opening and side towers. The Apothecary wall sign now faces the generated front wall support. Fresh QA should inspect plains/desert/savanna/taiga gates for cleared old wall blocks, a walkable over-gate guard perch with only outside-facing rail blocks, several guards on medium/large villages, and attached Apothecary signs.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Bookshop replay stall and mushroom wall cleanup. The latest freeze log stopped after `Placing bookshop ...` with no matching `Finished placing bookshop`, during goto-prep chunk generation. `BookshopPiece` now avoids `ItemFrame.survives()` collision/entity lookups during worldgen replay, uses direct slice-local support/air checks plus a per-world frame-position claim, and emits config-gated Bookshop phase breadcrumbs for shell/chest/frame/foundation/resident work. Village walls/gatehouses/gate paths now also treat red/brown mushrooms, giant mushroom caps, and mushroom stems as clearable natural vegetation so walls can overwrite and remove them the same way they already handled touched logs/leaves. Fresh QA should retry the taiga/savanna goto chain, confirm every Bookshop placement logs all phase breadcrumbs and `Finished placing bookshop`, confirm display frames still appear once, and confirm walls/paths remove small and giant mushrooms they touch.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Village rampart slope/corner/guard polish. Fresh visual QA showed the new rampart pass could punch holes on sharp wall height steps, made corners too busy, and still spawned guards from the old center-column top. Ramparts now skip any non-corner wall column whose neighboring wall base rises or drops by more than one block, corners keep the simpler tower cap without diagonal rampart lumps, and guards spawn only from valid returned inner wall-walk/rampart ledge positions. Fresh QA should generate plains/desert/savanna/taiga/snowy walls on hilly villages and confirm steep steps stay plain/solid, corners read cleanly, flatter runs keep stair-capped ramparts, and guards stand on walkable ledges.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Village wall rampart visual polish. A local check against `decompiled/witcherywalls-1.12.2-1.1.0/com/witcherywalls/worldgen/VillageWallGenerator.java` showed the addon thickens exposed wall-top faces with extra block mass and stair caps, switching to sandstone stairs for desert walls. The port now keeps the stable automatic/goto/manual wall pipeline intact but adds a biome-aware stair material, exposed-face stair-capped rampart lips, and village-facing wall-walk ledges to flatter placed wall columns. Fresh QA should generate plains/desert/savanna/taiga/snowy walls and inspect the top silhouette, stair directions, corner towers, and desert sandstone variants.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Infused brew exact-ID promotion. Witchery Resurrected rite data outputs `witchery:infused_soaring_brew` and `witchery:infused_grave_brew`, while the port's working infusion rites still produced the older `brew_of_soaring` and `brew_of_grave` IDs and left the exact IDs as placeholders. The exact Resurrected IDs now register as live items, appear beside the other brews/infusions, use the source display names, and are the current Infuse Brew rite outputs; the older `brew_of_soaring` and `brew_of_grave` IDs remain registered as compatibility stacks. Fresh QA should run both Infuse Brew rites, confirm the exact IDs/names have no placeholder suffix, drink the Soaring alias, and verify the old IDs still exist.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Resurrected direct-brew alias expansion. The remaining direct-use Resurrected brew aliases with already-live canonical behavior now leave the inert placeholder roster: `ice_brew`, `frogs_tongue_brew`, `cursed_leaping_brew`, `hitchcock_brew`, `infection_brew`, `flowing_spirit_brew`, `hollow_tears_brew`, `wasting_brew`, `bats_brew`, `revealing_brew`, `substitution_brew`, `grotesque_brew`, `depths_brew`, `sleeping_brew`, and the five `solidifying_*_brew` aliases. They register with the same direct-use classes as their `brew_of_*` counterparts, appear beside the canonical brews in brews/infusions, keep their alias bottle models, and no longer show placeholder suffixes. Current Spirit World transfer accepts the Flowing Spirit alias, and the Grotesque/Depths curse rites accept either canonical or alias offerings. Fresh QA should `/give` each promoted alias, use it on the same direct target as the canonical brew, and verify the listed rites consume aliases correctly.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Flame Imp contract exact-ID promotion. Witchery Resurrected exposes the five Flame Imp ability contracts as `witchery:living_flame_contract`, `fiery_tolerance_contract`, `evaporation_contract`, `fiery_touch_contract`, and `melting_touch_contract`, while the port only had the older `contract_*` compatibility IDs registered. The port now registers the exact Resurrected IDs as live `DemonicContractItem` stacks, shows them beside the existing contracts, gives them the source display names, and moves the current contract crafting recipes to the exact IDs while preserving the older IDs as valid compatibility items. Fresh QA should craft each contract, confirm the exact ID/name has no missing translation or placeholder suffix, and verify the older `contract_*` stacks still exist for saved-world compatibility.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Witchcraft guidebook exact-ID promotion. Witchery Resurrected exposes `witchery:distilling_book`, `circle_magic_book`, and `herbology_book` as obtainable guidebooks with old Witchcraft titles, but the port still kept those exact IDs as inert placeholders. The port now registers all three as one-stack Witches' Primer-opening guidebooks, shows them beside the Primer in the guide section, gives them the source/Resurrected names, restores the Resurrected shaped crafting recipes using the port's live Belladonna, Breath of the Goddess, Whiff of Magic, Poppy, and Dandelion IDs, and adds one random Witchcraft guidebook roll to bookshop loot. Fresh QA should craft each exact ID, verify no placeholder suffix, use each with Patchouli installed and absent, and open a fresh Bookshop chest to confirm one of the three guidebooks can appear.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Hunter armor exact-ID promotion. Witchery Resurrected exposes the Hunter set as `witchery:hunter_hat`, `hunter_coat`, `hunter_leggings`, `hunter_boots`, plus `silvered_hunter_*` and `dawn_hunter_*` upgrade families, while the port still kept several of those IDs as placeholders behind older compatibility names. The port now registers the exact hat/coat, silvered, and Dawn IDs as live `HunterClothingItem` armor, shows them with the armor gear, moves the Hunter/silvered/Dawn recipe outputs to the exact IDs, keeps compatibility IDs valid through shared upgrade tags, and lets full-set, silvered, garlicked, poppet, curse, and draining-bolt checks recognize either ID family. Fresh QA should craft the base Hunter hat/coat/trousers/boots, upgrade with both silver routes, upgrade to Dawn pieces, verify no placeholder suffixes, then test full-set poppet blocking, nullifying-bolt empowerment, werewolf/vampire counterplay, and old compatibility stacks.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Gulg's Gurdle exact-ID promotion. Witchery Resurrected exposes the boss reward as the misspelled-but-live `witchery:gulgs_grudle` ID with Gulg's Gurdle display text, while the port's mechanics lived only on `witchery:gulgs_girdle` and the exact ID was inert. The port now promotes `witchery:gulgs_grudle` as the same live `GoblinClothesItem` legs-slot reward, shows it beside the compatibility Girdle and Mog's Quiver, makes Gulg drop the exact ID, and routes empty-hand launch hits plus nearby quiver/girdle Resistance pairing through a shared Gulg's Girdle check so existing `gulgs_girdle` stacks remain valid. Fresh QA should kill Gulg until the reward drops, verify the item ID/name is `witchery:gulgs_grudle` / Gulg's Gurdle, then test bare-fist launch hits and the Mog's Quiver pairing buff with both girdle IDs.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Witches Robes exact-ID promotion. Original 1.7 exposes the robe as a witches robe item and Witchery Resurrected uses the exact `witchery:witches_robes` registry ID, recipe, and tooltip. The port now promotes `witchery:witches_robes` as the same live chest-slot Witches Robe behavior as `witchery:witches_robe`, moves the source shaped robe recipe to the exact ID, gives both robe IDs the old tooltip, and routes creeper avoidance, Kettle second-brew bonus, Bark Belt clothing capacity, and Impregnated Leather repair through one shared robe check. Fresh QA should craft `witchery:witches_robes`, verify no placeholder suffix, equip both robe IDs to test creeper target dropping and Kettle bonus eligibility, and repair both with four Impregnated Leather.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Icy Slippers exact-ID promotion. Original 1.7 registers the slippers as `iceslippers`, while Witchery Resurrected exposes the recipe and item as `witchery:icy_slippers`; the port had only `witchery:ice_slippers` live while the exact ID stayed inert. The port now promotes `witchery:icy_slippers` as the same live `IcySlippersItem`, places both slipper IDs with armor gear, moves the source shaped recipe to the exact ID, and routes walking freeze, Perpetual Ice pressure plates, Bark Belt clothing capacity, and Impregnated Leather repair through one shared slipper check so existing `ice_slippers` stacks remain valid. Fresh QA should craft `witchery:icy_slippers`, verify the name has no placeholder suffix, then test water/lava walking conversion, ice pressure plate triggering, Bark Belt capacity counting, and Impregnated Leather repair with both slipper IDs.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Bound Taglock Kit exact-ID promotion. Original 1.7 used one Taglock Kit item with a bound damage state, while Witchery Resurrected split filled kits into `witchery:bound_taglock_kit`. The port now promotes `witchery:bound_taglock_kit` out of the placeholder roster as a real one-stack `TaglockKitItem`, and every normal collection path (direct creature/player sampling, beds, Blood Roses, Leech Chests, and Hawthorn Doors) now produces the bound ID when filling a kit. Poppet binding, Player Compass binding, fetish known-player editing, Treefyd ignore commands, Crystal Ball scrying, and taglock-targeted rites now accept any real TaglockKitItem rather than only the blank `taglock_kit` registry ID. Fresh QA should collect a player and mob sample, confirm the resulting item ID/name is `witchery:bound_taglock_kit` with no placeholder suffix, then reuse it through Player Compass, poppet binding, Crystal Ball, and a taglock curse/transposition rite.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Crossbow Pistol exact-ID promotion. Original 1.7 exposes the Hand Bow as the `CROSSBOW_PISTOL` item field and Witchery Resurrected uses the `witchery:crossbow_pistol` registry ID and recipe. The port now promotes `witchery:crossbow_pistol` out of the placeholder roster as the same live `HandBowItem` behavior as `witchery:hand_bow`, gives it the source/Resurrected shaped recipe, shows it beside the Hand Bow in the weapon group, and points its item definition at the same loaded/pulling/charged range-dispatch model path. Fresh QA should craft `witchery:crossbow_pistol`, crouch-load and cycle bolts, fire Wooden/Nullifying/Bone/Splitting/Silver shots, and confirm the idle/pulling/loaded models match the existing Hand Bow path.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Biome Book and Biome Note first practical port. Source `ItemBook` / `ItemBiomeNote` comparison showed the old Book of Biomes copies its selected biome page to a Biome Note with paper, and that note feeds Shifting Seasons. The port now promotes `witchery:book_biomes`, `witchery:extended_biomes_book`, the Resurrected `witchery:biomes_book` alias, and `witchery:biome_note` out of placeholders. The extended/alias book stores a selected biome page in item custom data, right-click cycles pages, `witchery:biome_note_copy` crafts book + Paper into a named Biome Note while returning the book, and Rite of Shifting Seasons now honors exact Biome Notes before falling back to the older practical item foci. Fresh QA should craft the two-book chain, cycle the extended book, craft notes for several pages, verify the book remains in the grid, then run Shifting Seasons with a note and confirm the target biome matches the note.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Duplication Grenade first practical port. The port promotes `witchery:duplication_grenade` out of the placeholder roster as the source `ItemSunGrenade(1)` mirror decoy item: it stacks to 16, has uncommon rarity, throws through the existing Sun Grenade projectile path, consumes on survival use, returns a Quartz Sphere on impact, and spawns a 10-second owner-skinned Reflection decoy that does not drop copied equipment or clear Mirror World rooms. The item also carries optional owner UUID/name custom data for the later mirror-charging path. Fresh QA should throw it near hostile mobs, verify the decoy copies the thrower's skin/name, expires after about 10 seconds, does not hurt/target the thrower, tries to pull nearby mob attention, and leaves a Quartz Sphere at the impact.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Staff of Duplication first practical port. The port promotes `witchery:staff_of_duplication` out of the inert placeholder roster as the source `ItemDuplicationStaff` utility: first block use marks one corner, second block use captures the exclusive cuboid between corners into item custom data, stored patterns place one block above the clicked block, normal placement cycles 90-degree rotations, sneak placement resets to unrotated, and sneak-use in air clears stored marker/schematic data. Fresh QA should capture a small structure containing oriented stairs/logs/doors plus air, place all rotations, confirm air clears old blocks, and confirm selections above the guarded 32-block/8192-cell limit refuse cleanly.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Brew alias ritual acceptance. The port now has shared family checks for Brew of Sprouting, Love, Ink, and Raising. Nature's Power activation, Rite of the Forest, Raise Column, Blindness, and the Love-based cure rites accept the matching Resurrected alias offerings (`sprouting_brew`, `ink_brew`, `love_brew`) as well as the canonical `brew_of_*` items. Kettle extraction also treats `raising_brew` as Brew of Raising for the Witches' Robe versus Necromancer Robes bonus split. Fresh QA should repeat those rites with alias brew items, then confirm the same offerings are consumed and the same effects fire.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): More direct brew alias promotion. Resurrected exposes `witchery:sprouting_brew`, `witchery:erosion_brew`, `witchery:raising_brew`, and `witchery:love_brew` beside the current source-backed `brew_of_sprouting`, `brew_of_erosion`, `brew_of_raising`, and `brew_of_love` IDs. Those alias IDs now leave the inert placeholder roster, use the same direct-use item classes as the canonical brews, appear beside them in brews/infusions, and keep their existing alias bottle models without placeholder suffixes. Fresh QA should `/give` each alias, use them on the same direct block/entity targets as the canonical brews, and confirm behavior and bottle return match.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Direct brew alias promotion. Resurrected exposes `witchery:vines_brew`, `witchery:webs_brew`, `witchery:thorns_brew`, and `witchery:ink_brew` beside the current source-backed `brew_of_vines`, `brew_of_webs`, `brew_of_thorns`, and `brew_of_ink` IDs. Those alias IDs now leave the inert placeholder roster, use the same direct-use item classes as the canonical brews, appear beside them in brews/infusions, and keep their existing alias bottle models without placeholder suffixes. Fresh QA should `/give` each alias, use them on the same block/entity targets as the canonical brews, and confirm Vines/Webs/Thorns/Ink behavior and bottle return match.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Odd Porkchop alias promotion. Resurrected exposes `witchery:odd_porkchop` / `witchery:cooked_odd_porkchop` beside the current source-backed `odd_porkchop_raw` / `odd_porkchop_cooked` IDs. Those alias IDs now leave the inert placeholder roster, use the same source food values as the canonical pair (raw 3 hunger / 0.3 saturation, cooked 8 hunger / 0.8 saturation), appear beside the canonical items in ingredients/alchemy, and keep their existing alias models without placeholder suffixes. Fresh QA should give/eat all four IDs and confirm the two raw IDs feel identical and the two cooked IDs feel identical.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Endless Water Brew alias promotion. Resurrected exposes `witchery:endless_water_brew` as an alias-style ID beside the original/source `witchery:brew_of_endless_water`, but the port still had the alias in the inert placeholder roster. The alias now registers as the same real `BrewOfEndlessWaterItem`, appears beside Brew of Endless Water in the brews/infusions creative group, keeps its existing alias bottle art, has the plain `Endless Water Brew` name, and uses the same 100-use cauldron-filling/water-source placement behavior and tooltip. Fresh QA should use both IDs on empty/partial cauldrons and clicked-side replaceable spaces, then confirm both lose durability only on successful survival use.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Dropped Waystone Otherwhere auto-binding. Original `EntityItemWaystone` gives dropped blank Waystones a source-style passive binding pulse after they sit for about two seconds on a small Otherwhere circle. The port now mirrors that item-entity path: with no nearby living target, a dropped blank Waystone stack converts up to eight blanks into separate location-bound `witchery:bound_waystone` items bound to the circle center; with a living target within about two blocks, it consumes 4000 nearby altar power and creates one target-bound `witchery:blooded_waystone`, leaving any extra blanks behind. Successful conversion clears the chalk ring; missing power smoke/snare feedback leaves the dropped item and circle intact. Fresh QA should test no-target stacks, player targets, creature targets, no-power failure, ring clearing, and leftover blank stacks.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Blooded Waystone alternate binding route. Source recipe 89 binds the same entity-bound `waystoneCreatureBound` item with Ender Dew, Slimeball, Icy Needle, and Charged Attuned Stone, without the 500-power snowball sacrifice used by recipe 88. The live Blooded Waystone rite now accepts both source support sets: Ender Dew + Slimeball + Snowball with 500 altar power, or Ender Dew + Slimeball + Icy Needle + Charged Attuned Stone with no power draw. Fresh QA should run both support routes with a player and a creature target, verify both produce `witchery:blooded_waystone`, and confirm the charged-stone route consumes the charged stone rather than altar power.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Blooded Waystone entity-bound source-ID promotion. Original 1.7 keeps a hidden `waystoneCreatureBound` sub-item for entity-bound Waystones, separate from the location-bound Waystone used by bind/copy rites. `witchery:blooded_waystone` is now a real hidden `WaystoneItem` instead of a placeholder: the small ritual circle can blood-bind a blank Waystone to a nearby living target using Ender Dew, Slimeball, Snowball, and 500 altar power; the item stores taglock-style player/creature data, shows the bound target, can be used on Crystal Balls through the Taglock scry path, and works with Transposition to Waystone by moving circle entities to the live bound target. Fresh QA should bind player and creature targets, verify the output ID and tooltip, then test Crystal Ball use and Transposition to Blooded Waystone. Remaining old dropped-Waystone parity is the bound/Blooded Waystone transposition item path.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Bound Waystone source-ID promotion. Original 1.7 uses a distinct bound-Waystone sub-item for the bind/copy rites and for later Waystone sacrifices, but keeps it out of the creative sub-item list until a rite binds it. `witchery:bound_waystone` is now a real `WaystoneItem` registration instead of a placeholder, uses the existing bound waystone art without a placeholder suffix, and is the output of both the bind rite and the copy rite. Bound consumers now accept any complete `WaystoneItem` binding so old saved bound `witchery:waystone` stacks still work with transposition, Ruby Slippers, Flying Monkeys, Crystal Balls, and optional rite relocation, while blank-waystone offerings remain restricted to unbound `witchery:waystone`. Fresh QA should bind a new Waystone, copy it with a blank Waystone, use the new bound ID in transposition/Crystal Ball/Ruby Slippers/Flying Monkey routes, and confirm old saved bound blank-waystone stacks still work.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Source bolt ID alias promotion. Original 1.7 names the Hand Bow bolt family as `boltStake`, `boltAntiMagic`, `boltHoly`, and `boltSplitting`; the port's functional bolt mechanics lived only on the modern `wooden_bolt`, `nullifying_bolt`, `bone_bolt`, and `splitting_bolt` IDs while the source/Resurrected IDs stayed inert placeholders. `witchery:bolt_stake`, `witchery:bolt_anti_magic`, `witchery:bolt_holy`, and `witchery:bolt_splitting` are now real items, appear with the weapon/bolt set, and the Hand Bow can detect and consume either the canonical port IDs or the source alias IDs for the matching loaded bolt type. Cycling still returns canonical bolts, and projectile pickup still returns canonical bolts, so the old IDs are a compatibility input bridge rather than duplicate recipe outputs. Fresh QA should `/give` each alias bolt, crouch-load/cycle them through the Hand Bow, fire them, and verify stake/nullifying/holy/splitting behavior matches the canonical bolt family.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Subdued Village Spirit promotion. Original 1.7 and Resurrected both expose a village-targeted subdued-spirit item beside the normal `subdued_spirit`, and source `EntitySpirit` persists `SpiritType` so village spirits drop the village variant again. `witchery:subdued_village_spirit` is now promoted out of placeholders as a real `SubduedSpiritItem`, releases Spirits marked with the source `SpiritType=1` village identity, saves/loads that identity, drops the village item from marked Spirits, appears beside the normal Subdued Spirit in the ingredients/alchemy creative section, and transfers back from the Spirit World with other spirit loot. Fresh QA should release both subdued-spirit variants, save/reload a marked Spirit, kill each variant, and confirm the normal/village item identity round-trips. True old nearest-village waypoint flight remains a future Spirit AI polish item.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Infernal Blood source-ID promotion. Source `itemInfernalBlood` is the Horned Huntsman drop, the bloodied Wicker Bundle ingredient, the Ruby Slippers ingredient, and a Summon Imp offering; it also right-clicks Wicker Bundles into the bloodied variant in a five-block cluster. `witchery:infernal_blood` is now promoted out of placeholders with the same wicker-use behavior as the existing Resurrected-style `demonic_blood` bridge, Horned Huntsmen drop the source ID again, bloodied wicker crafting uses the source ID again, and Summon Imp accepts either Infernal Blood or the existing Demonic Blood compatibility item. Fresh QA should kill a Horned Huntsman, craft/use Bloodied Wicker Bundles, craft Ruby Slippers, and run Summon Imp with Infernal Blood.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Nullified Leather ingredient promotion. Source `WitcheryRecipes` crafts three Nullified Leather from eight vanilla Leather surrounding one Null Catalyst, and the Hunter outfit recipes already consume that item as their source material. `witchery:nullified_leather` is now promoted out of the placeholder roster as a real ingredient, appears with ingredients/alchemy, uses the plain source display name, and has the old shaped 3-output recipe.
