Fresh QA should craft it from Leather + Null Catalyst and then craft each Hunter outfit piece from the produced leather.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Sheep Liver food/drop promotion. Original 1.7 used `itemMuttonRaw` / `itemMuttonCooked` as Witchery's pre-vanilla mutton pair, and Resurrected maps that pair to `witchery:sheep_liver` / `witchery:cooked_sheep_liver` with 2 hunger / 0.2 saturation and 6 hunger / 0.8 saturation. The port now promotes those IDs out of placeholders, adds the raw-to-cooked smelting route, lets Sheep Liver stand in for source mutton in Raw Meaty Stew, and restores the old transformed-werewolf sheep kill drop chance. Fresh QA should kill adult sheep while shifted as a werewolf, eat/smelt both liver variants, and craft Raw Meaty Stew using Sheep Liver.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Odd Porkchop food promotion. Source `ItemGeneral.Edible` registers raw Odd Porkchop at 3 hunger / 0.3 saturation and cooked Odd Porkchop at 8 hunger / 0.8 saturation, with Mutandis conversions around rotten flesh/porkchop and a furnace route to the cooked variant. `witchery:odd_porkchop_raw` and `witchery:odd_porkchop_cooked` are now promoted out of the placeholder roster with those food values, ingredients creative-tab placement, the safe source Mutandis conversions, and smelting; the later alias pass also maps `witchery:odd_porkchop` / `witchery:cooked_odd_porkchop` to the same food values. Fresh QA should craft rotten flesh + Mutandis, cook the raw odd porkchop, eat all four IDs, and confirm Mutandis converts raw/cooked odd porkchops back to vanilla porkchops.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Universal Antidote item promotion. Source `ItemVanillaPotion.potionAntidote` is a four-stack drinkable potion that removes Poison and Wither, returns a Glass Bottle, and crafts two outputs from a Null Catalyst plus two Poison potions. `witchery:universal_antidote` is now promoted out of the placeholder roster with that drink/use behavior, source output count, Poison-potion shapeless recipe, real display name, and ingredients creative-tab placement. Fresh QA should poison/wither a player, drink the antidote in survival and creative, and confirm only Poison/Wither are removed while the bottle return follows normal potion flow.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Hunter clothing item class parity. Source `ItemHunterClothes` uses cloth/leather armor values with iron durability, uncommon rarity, source tooltip lines, and a drawback where full Hunter outfits cannot use bound poppets. The port now registers plain, silvered, and Dawn hunter gear through a dedicated `HunterClothingItem` with leather armor attributes, iron-piece durability, uncommon rarity, and source tooltip lines; `PoppetEvents` and direct Voodoo Poppet actions now ignore/block poppet magic while a complete Hunter-family outfit is worn. Fresh QA should inspect durability/tooltips/rarity for all Hunter variants, confirm silvered/Dawn no longer grant full iron armor values, and confirm bound protective/voodoo poppets do not activate while a full Hunter outfit is equipped.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Hunter full-set magic/curse protection. Source `ItemHunterClothes.isFullSetWorn(entity, false)` treats any complete Hunter-family outfit as eligible, including plain, silvered, and garlicked pieces, then rolls 25% magic protection and 90% curse protection. `SilverEvents` now restores the 25% full-set magic damage cancel before poppet protection can consume a poppet, and `RitualExecutor` now routes curse dampening through the same slot-specific full-set helper so plain, silvered, and Dawn hunter outfits all qualify. Fresh QA should wear each full Hunter-family set, take repeated magic damage, and run curse rites until the dampening message appears; mixed incomplete sets should not protect.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Silvered hunter armor counterplay. Source `ItemHunterClothes(true, false)` hurts werewolf wearers once per second and gives stronger protection when a werewolf attacks the wearer. `SilverEvents` now adds that missing slice for silvered Hunter armor and the Dawn family that counts as silvered: werewolf attackers still take the existing 1.0 recoil, their incoming hit damage is reduced, and werewolf players who equip silvered/Dawn hunter armor take the old cactus-style self-damage pulse. Fresh QA should attack a silvered/Dawn wearer as a werewolf, then equip those pieces as a werewolf and confirm the self-damage pulse.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Witch Hunter Dawn garlicked counterplay. Source `ItemHunterClothes(true, true)` showed garlicked hunter pieces are also silvered, hurt vampire wearers once per second, and strengthen protection against vampire attackers. The current port uses `witch_hunter_dawn_*` as the silvered/garlicked endgame hunter family, so `SilverEvents` now counts Dawn armor as silvered for werewolf attacker back-damage, reduces incoming vampire-attacker damage while any Dawn piece is worn, and applies the source cactus tick to vampire players who try wearing Dawn gear. Fresh QA should attack a Dawn wearer as a vampire and werewolf, then equip Dawn armor as a vampire and confirm the self-damage pulse.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Brew Bag first practical storage. Source `ItemBrewBag` comparison showed an eight-slot, one-stack bag crafted from Impregnated Leather around Golden Thread, storing brews in item NBT and launching a selected brew from the bag. `witchery:brew_bag` now uses a real `BrewBagItem` instead of a simple item: the source recipe is restored, the bag stores up to eight stacks of current throwable splash/gas brews in custom data, shift-use cycles the selected slot, use with a throwable brew in the other hand stores one, and ordinary use throws the selected stored brew while returning a Glass Bottle in survival. Fresh QA should craft the bag, store several splash/gas brews, cycle slots, throw from the bag, and verify stored counts/tooltips survive relog.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Ruby Slippers persistent cooldown parity. Source `ItemWitchesClothes.trySayTheresNoPlaceLikeHome` stores real-time home and bound-Waystone cooldown timestamps in the player's Witchery NBT as `WITCLastRubySlipperTime` and `WITCLastRubySlipperWayTime`. `RubySlippersItem` now uses the same tag names in player persistent data instead of process-local static maps, so the 30-minute home cooldown and one-minute bound-Waystone cooldown survive player entity recreation and server restarts with the world save. Fresh QA should use both routes, relog/restart, and confirm the cooldown message still reports time remaining.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01; tightened 2026-06-02): Ruby Slippers rare misfire parity. The 1.7.10 waystone branch has a 1% chance to send the wearer through `BlockVoidBramble.teleportRandomly` instead of the bound Waystone. `RubySlippersItem` now mirrors that source surprise after paying energy, consuming the offered bound Waystone, and starting the one-minute waystone cooldown: on the rare roll it invokes the shared Void Bramble one-roll scan within 500 blocks and does not fall back to the bound-Waystone jump if that scan finds no landing, matching the old helper call. Fresh QA should temporarily raise `WAYSTONE_RANDOM_MISFIRE_CHANCE` in a scratch build or repeat bound-waystone tests until the rare route fires, then confirm ordinary waystone jumps still work on non-misfire rolls.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01; tightened 2026-06-02): Ruby Slippers waystone/energy route. A 1.7.10 `ItemWitchesClothes.trySayTheresNoPlaceLikeHome` comparison showed the current chat hook was only doing the bed/spawn fallback, with no nearby bound-waystone branch and no infusion-energy cost. `RubySlippersItem` now first looks for a dropped bound `witchery:waystone` within three blocks, uses a separate one-minute waystone cooldown, spends 40/80 infusion energy for same/cross-dimension waystone jumps, discards the offered dropped Waystone entity like source, and teleports near the bound position. The bed/spawn fallback now uses its own 30-minute cooldown, starts that persisted cooldown after selecting a home target but before the final infusion-energy spend like source, spends 80/120 infusion energy, only accepts current-dimension or Overworld respawn data, falls back to the Overworld level-data spawn instead of a fixed origin coordinate when no bed target exists, uses the fixed translation keys, and the item exposes source-style tooltip lines for the waystone and bed routes. Fresh QA should infuse a player, drop a bound Waystone near them, say "there's no place like home", confirm the waystone is consumed and energy/cooldown apply, then retest the no-waystone bed route including no-bed, off-dimension respawn, and insufficient-energy attempts.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Icy Slippers walking freeze parity. A 1.7.10 `Infusion.handleIcySlippersEffect` comparison showed the boots should act every tick, not only on the restored ice pressure plate: the four half-step cells directly under the wearer turn water into Ice and lava into Obsidian, with a 10% durability hit only when lava is converted. `witchery:ice_slippers` now uses a dedicated item class with the old tooltip lines, and `WitcheryPlayerEvents` applies the source four-cell water/lava conversion before the slower 20-tick player-effect pass. Fresh QA should walk across shallow water and lava in Ice Slippers, confirm nearby cells convert while ordinary boots do nothing, and confirm lava conversion can damage the boots.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01; tightened 2026-06-02): Baba's Hat reward behavior. A 1.7.10 `ItemWitchesClothes` / `GenericEvents` comparison showed Baba Yaga's guaranteed hat drop should be epic Witchery clothing, show the old tooltip, keep its existing kettle extra-brew roll path, and give infused wearers a 25% chance to spend 5 infusion energy to blink away instead of taking eligible entity/projectile damage outside Torment, unless actively transformed or hit by a powered draining bolt. `witchery:babas_hat` now uses a dedicated item class with source tooltip/epic rarity, and `BabasHatEvents` now spends energy, invokes the shared Void Bramble one-roll scan at the old six-block range, and cancels the hit like the source branch instead of doing its own heightmap/retry safe-position search. Bark Belt charge capacity also treats Baba's Hat, Necromancer Robes, Ice Slippers, Ruby Slippers, and Seeping Shoes as Witchery clothing again. Fresh QA should equip the hat with infusion energy, take repeated mob/projectile hits outside Torment, confirm occasional hit-cancel teleports and energy spend, confirm no teleport in Torment, and recheck kettle extraction bonus text/behavior.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Leonard's Urn brew-focus promotion. A 1.7.10 `ItemLeonardsUrn` and Leonard symbol comparison showed the boss drop is a glowing epic urn that stores one to four splash brews and lets Leonard magic cast a stored brew without consuming it. The port now registers `witchery:leonards_urn` as a real one-stack epic item with permanent glint, source-style tooltip, custom-data `Inventory.Items` storage, focus selection, thrown-brew insertion from the offhand, tooltip listing of stored foci, and practical right-click release of the selected stored splash/gas brew without consuming the saved focus. Fresh QA should kill Leonard, put a Foulness/Foaming/Poison splash or gas brew in the other hand, store it in the urn, sneak-use to cycle focus, and use the urn repeatedly to confirm the stored brew is launched and retained. Remaining parity is the old GUI and exact infusion-symbol cost integration.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Gulg/Mog reward gear mechanics. A 1.7.10 `ItemGoblinClothes(1/2)` comparison showed the boss drops should be wearable epic goblin armor, not inert simple items. `witchery:mogs_quiver` is now a leather chest-slot quiver with permanent glint, source tooltip, limitless bow ammo while worn, a custom fast arrow shot that cancels vanilla arrow use, creative-only arrow pickup, bow durability/stat handling, and the old airborne-target punishment through `WITCMogged` arrows. `witchery:gulgs_girdle` is now a leather legs-slot girdle with permanent glint, source tooltip, empty-hand hits forced to 5 damage, and a vertical launch on hit. If nearby players wear the paired quiver/girdle within eight blocks, the wearer receives Resistance II for 200 ticks every source-style 100-tick pulse. Fresh QA should equip both rewards on two players, test the pairing resistance, fire a bow with no arrows, hit airborne targets, and punch with an empty hand while wearing the girdle.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Twisting Band promotion. A 1.7.10 `ItemGoblinClothes(0)` / `KOBOLDITE_HELM` comparison showed the remaining `witchery:twisting_band` placeholder is the old Koboldite Helm reward: a rare iron-armor, diamond-durability head item that disorientates observers and is crafted from Koboldite Ingots plus a Charged Attuned Stone. The port now registers it as a real head-slot item with tooltip, source-shaped recipe, creative placement beside the other goblin rewards, and a first practical observer-disorientation tick that blinds players looking directly at the wearer, flips their facing, and nauseates non-player observers while carved pumpkins block the effect. Fresh QA should craft/equip it and have a second player or mob look directly at the wearer within 16 blocks.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Devil's Tongue Charm promotion. A 1.7.10 `ItemPolynesiaCharm(true)` comparison showed `witchery:devils_tongue_charm` should be the demon-facing Polynesia Charm variant, not an inert placeholder. The port now registers it as a real uncommon, one-stack, 50-durability charm, keeps the source-shaped Polynesia Charm and Devil's Tongue Charm recipes, gives it its own tooltip, and makes Demons ignore players holding it in either hand except for the old 5% failure chance on targeting, melee, and fireball attacks. Fresh QA should craft both charms, hold Devil's Tongue in main/offhand near Demons, and confirm attacks only rarely slip through.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Witch Hand active-infusion shell promotion. A 1.7.10 `ItemWitchHand` comparison showed `witchery:witch_hand` should be the uncommon, one-stack infusion-active hand and should drop from witches/coven witches at the old source rare-drop chance, while the Resurrected `witchery:witches_hand` alias was still an inert placeholder. The port now registers both IDs as real `WitchHandItem`s, restores the 400-tick use shell, adds first-pass active hooks for Light, Overworld, Otherwhere, and Infernal infusions, bridges left-click target actions, stores/uses Otherwhere recall points, spends current infusion energy, and drops the canonical hand from vanilla Witches or Coven Witches killed by a player with the old 33%/50% Arthana chance. Fresh QA should test both item IDs with `/witchery infusion light|overworld|otherwhere|infernal`, verify left-click and held/release actions consume infusion energy, confirm Otherwhere recall/target teleporting, and kill witches/coven witches with/without Arthana to check drops.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Creative Bat/Wolf Token promotion. A 1.7.10 `ItemWolfToken` comparison showed the current `witchery:creative_bat_wolf_token` registry ID was still an inert placeholder even though vampire and werewolf QA state already exists. The port now registers it as a real one-stack, epic, 20-tick bow-use QA token: normal use cycles the current port's werewolf mastery/werewolf state through 0-3, sneak-use cycles vampire level through 0-10, and each successful cycle syncs player data with feedback. Fresh QA should use it in creative, confirm normal use toggles werewolf/mastery state, confirm sneak-use cycles vampire level and wraps to 0, and verify `/witchery status` matches the messages.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Horn of the Hunt summoning item promotion. A 1.7.10 `ItemHornOfTheHunt` / Resurrected `ItemHuntsHorn` comparison showed `witchery:horn_of_the_hunt` and the `witchery:hunts_horn` alias were still inert placeholders even though the Horned Huntsman boss path is live. The port now registers both IDs as real one-use, one-stack, 40-tick bow-use horn items with the restored tooltip, creative-tab placement for the source ID, safe 2-8 block nearby Huntsman spawning, source-like 150-tick invulnerability/max-quarter health entrance, horn and Wither-spawn feedback, item break on successful survival use, and the source-only explosive entrance flag for horn summons. Fresh QA should use `horn_of_the_hunt` and `hunts_horn` in survival and creative, confirm the horn vanishes only on successful survival summon, confirm the boss appears near but not inside the player, and watch the delayed explosive entrance when invulnerability ends.

Previous local delta (2026-06-01): Moon Charm werewolf-control promotion. A 1.7.10 `ItemMoonCharm` / Resurrected comparison showed `witchery:moon_charm` was still an inert placeholder even though the port already tracked werewolf transformation state. The port now registers Moon Charm as a real rare, single-stack, 49-durability item repairable with Gold Nuggets. Holding use for the old 60-tick bow-style action toggles a werewolf player between human and transformed wolf-form state, damages the charm, shows source-style smoke/explosion feedback, and refuses while wolfsbane is carried. Carrying a Moon Charm prevents automatic full-moon transform/restoration, wolf-form buffs/render/howl now require active transformation instead of only the curse flag, and Wolf Altar right-click with three Gold Nuggets creates a Moon Charm for werewolves. Fresh QA should become a werewolf, use `/witchery wolfmastery` at a few levels, hold-use the charm to transform and restore, carry/remove it across full-moon and non-full-moon periods, test wolfsbane suppression, and trade three nuggets at a Wolf Altar.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Cane Sword item promotion. A 1.7.10 `ItemCaneSword` / Resurrected comparison showed `witchery:cane_sword` and `witchery:drawn_cane_sword` were still inert placeholder IDs even though the assets existed. The port now registers sheathed/drawn Cane Sword item variants with source-style rarity, durability, enchantability, attack modifiers, sneak-use draw/sheathe transform preserving stack custom data, an internal `WITCBloodReserve` reservoir, source-shaped Woven Cruor + Diamond Sword + Gold Ingot recipe, and vampire-only reservoir extraction into blood power while the sword is sheathed. Drawn Cane Sword kills against villagers or players fill the reservoir up to 250. Fresh QA should craft the sword, sneak-use to draw/sheathe, confirm the drawn/sheathed models swap, kill a villager/player as a vampire with the drawn sword, then sheathe and use it to move reservoir blood into vampire blood power.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Meaty Stew food promotion. A 1.7.10 `ItemStew` / `WitcheryRecipes` comparison showed the raw and cooked stew pair was still only an inert Resurrected alias. `witchery:raw_meaty_stew` and `witchery:meaty_stew` are now real food items using the source hunger/saturation values (raw 1/0.1, cooked 12/1.0), return a Bowl to the player inventory/drop on eating, use the original `stewraw` / `stew` item sprites, and restore the old garlic + raw meat + potato + carrot + bowl + brown mushroom shapeless recipes plus furnace cooking to cooked stew. Fresh QA should craft raw stew with Beef, Porkchop, Chicken, Cod, and Mutton, smelt it, eat both variants, and confirm bowl return.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Brew of Combustion fuel-item promotion. A Resurrected source comparison (`ItemCombustionBrew` / `CombustionBrewEffect`) showed `witchery:combustion_brew` should be a glowing, stackable furnace fuel with four custom-data `Level` burn tiers: 2400, 5000, 10000, and 50000 ticks. The port now promotes that exact registry ID out of the placeholder roster, exposes it in the brews creative section, gives it the source-style `Combustion` tooltip, supports the burn tiers through `Level`, and adds a small coal-based kettle recipe for the base tier. Fresh QA should brew/extract it from a Kettle, burn it in a vanilla furnace and Witches' Oven, and use `/give` with `Level` 1-3 custom data to verify higher burn times.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Observations/Torn Page boss drop completion. A 1.7.10 `GenericEvents.onLivingDeath` comparison confirmed that any boss-display entity should be a guaranteed Torn Page target while the killer carries an incomplete Observations of an Immortal. `WitcheryMobDropEvents` now includes the newly ported Witchery bosses in that always-drop family: Baba Yaga, Leonard, Gulg, Mog, Lilith, and the Horned Huntsman, alongside Death, Lord of Torment, Reflection, and vanilla bosses. Fresh QA should kill each boss while carrying an incomplete book and confirm one Torn Page drops once.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village Bookshop rear display-frame restoration. A direct 1.7.10 `ComponentVillageBookShop` comparison showed the old shop added four item frames with random bookshop loot-family stacks along the rear wall after creating the chest. `BookshopPiece` now restores that row by keeping the rear display strip solid and spawning four inward-facing frames with Books, Torn Pages, or Observations of an Immortal when the active generation slice contains both the frame and support cells. Fresh QA should generate bookshops and confirm the rear wall has four readable display frames without duplicate frames after chunk replay.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village Apothecary villager/profession restoration. `ApothecaryPiece` no longer uses a Coven Witch stand-in; it now spawns a vanilla Villager at the old source resident position, marks it as a Witchery apothecary, names it `Apothecary`, forces librarian profession data for the requested librarian skin, and replaces its offers with the 1.7.10 apothecary trade list: Tongue of Dog, Bat Wool, Spectral Dust, Garlic, Artichoke, Poppy, Slime Ball, and Clay Ball for Emeralds. Fresh QA should generate apothecaries in several village biomes and confirm the resident uses the librarian skin, opens as `Apothecary`, and sells only the restored apothecary trades without turning into an ordinary librarian.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): `/witchery setup rite` QA command. The setup command tree now has a `rite` sibling beside `stations`, placing a player-facing 31x11 stone-brick/smooth-stone pad five blocks ahead. It builds four complete small/medium/large ring sets for white glyph, golden ritual, otherwhere, and infernal chalk, with visible center floor markers and a three-block foundation like the QA hut. The command uses the ritual detector's own ring-position helper so future detector changes can be reused by QA placement. Fresh QA should run `/witchery setup rite`, right-click each golden center circle, and confirm the right circle variant is detected for small, medium, and large rites.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village Keep ladder facing correction. Fresh QA confirmed Keeps now appear, but some ladders looked wrong and could be removed, blocking traversal. Direct comparison with `ComponentVillageKeep` showed the source uses the same ladder metadata (`2`) for the tower and both bridge ladder runs. `VillageKeepPiece` now places the two bridge ladders with the same support-facing state as the surviving tower ladder instead of sideways east/west facings, so they should no longer self-prune. Fresh QA should climb both keep towers and bridge shafts from bottom to top and back down in plains/desert/savanna/taiga/snowy keeps.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village Apothecary source-shape correction. Fresh visual QA suggested the apothecary looked wrong, and direct comparison with `ComponentVillageApothecary` confirmed the port had drifted into a flat slab-roof shop with invented Witchery machine blocks. `ApothecaryPiece` now uses the old 9x9x6 shell: cobble/sandstone-family foundation and stepped roof bands, plank walls, oak-style stair roof rows with overhang, source window layout, real front door/sign/step, sparse old interior furnishings, source local chest/spawn positions, and the village pool element now advertises the correct 9-block height. The follow-up pass restored the dedicated apothecary villager as a librarian-skinned vanilla Villager with the source trade list. Fresh QA should generate plains/desert/savanna/taiga/snowy apothecaries and check roof silhouette, front door/sign/step, windows, chest access, resident, trades, and biome palette.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Mystic Branch creature-power roster pass. A decompiled check showed the old creature infusion registry used Spider/Cave Spider, Creeper, Bat/Owl, Squid, Ghast, Blaze, Pig Zombie, Zombie, Skeleton, Slime/Magma Cube/Toad, Silverfish/Ocelot/Wolf/Horse, Enderman, and passive animal/Villager heal sacrifices instead of the port's five temporary Zombie/Spider/Creeper/Bat/Enderman mappings. `CreaturePowerType` now includes practical active effects for Spider, Creeper, Squid, Ghast, Blaze, Pigman, Zombie, and Skeleton powers, while `WitcheryPlayerEvents` maps the old mob families and grants source-like charges: 10 for most powers, 1 heal charge from passive animals, and 2 heal charges from Villagers/Mooshrooms. Fresh QA should sacrifice each family near a powered altar, right-click Mystic Branch, and confirm the absorbed power/charge count and active behavior.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village building replay worldgen-slice guard. Latest exploration freeze stopped after `[VillageBuildings:Debug] Replaying already claimed apothecary...` / `Placing apothecary...`, while wall debug lines showed unloaded referenced start chunks were being skipped safely. `VillageBuildingMaterials` now samples the biome from the current generation-box intersection instead of the full building center when a procedural village building replays across neighboring chunks, and direct apothecary sign / witch-hut front-step writes are skipped unless the target block is inside the active generation box. `WitcheryVillageElement` also logs `Finished placing ...` after each postProcess slice and catches placement exceptions so the next stall can be pinned to a specific slice. Fresh QA should explore through several newly generating villages and confirm apothecary/bookshop/witch hut/watch tower/keep replays log both `Placing` and `Finished placing` without freezing.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Brew of Endless Water utility promotion. Decompiled `ItemBrewEndlessWater` was still represented by the inert `witchery:brew_of_endless_water` placeholder. The port now registers a real glowing, 100-use utility item that fills empty/partial cauldrons to full or places a water source on the clicked side, damages like the old reusable brew item, plays water feedback, and reports remaining uses in its tooltip. Fresh QA should verify cauldron filling, side-click water placement, creative no-damage behavior, and that no glass bottle is returned.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Coven Circle/Straw Man terrain anchoring. Fresh QA showed a Stone/Coven Circle sitting on a visible one-block dirt plinth, and Straw Man uses the same style of flattened surface support. `CovenCircleStructure` and `StrawManStructure` now sample a one-block padded dry-land footprint, reject rough sites with more than two blocks of height variance, and anchor to the lowest accepted surface instead of a center-only height. Fresh QA should confirm Coven Circles and Straw Men no longer sit on an automatic raised dirt/grass layer.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village Watch Tower upper ladder/chest polish. Fresh QA found the tower still missing the final three ladder blocks needed to descend completely, and the chest sat three blocks too high. `VillageWatchTowerPiece` now keeps the straight supported center shaft, restores the old three-block upper handoff ladder beside the deck support column, and lowers the loot chest from local `y=13` to local `y=10` on the mid platform. Fresh village QA should confirm Watch Towers can be climbed up and descended all the way down, and that the chest is on the mid platform.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village Keep visibility/verification pass. Fresh QA still has not seen a Keep after several villages, so the old weight-8 pass was too timid for a 16x26x16 jigsaw building. All five vanilla village house pools now weight Keep at `32` while preserving the one-Keep-per-village cap. `WitcheryVillageElement` also emits config-gated `[VillageBuildings:Debug]` breadcrumbs for Keep jigsaw offers, building claims, duplicate skips, and final placement. Fresh QA should generate or goto several plains/desert/savanna/taiga/snowy villages, confirm at least one Keep appears, and check logs for `Claimed village_keep` / `Placing village_keep` if none are visible.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village Watch Tower roof stair direction fix. Fresh QA called out Watch Tower stair direction after the ladder repair. `VillageWatchTowerPiece` is fixed at `setOrientation(Direction.NORTH)`, which flips local Z without rotating block states, so the local north/south roof stairs now intentionally use swapped world facings and the center ladder now faces the support block behind it. Fresh village QA should confirm Watch Tower roof stairs face outward on all four sides and the center ladder remains climbable to the upper deck.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village Watch Tower ladder access fix. Fresh QA found top ladders missing/unusable, leaving players unable to climb up and guards unable to descend. `VillageWatchTowerPiece` now replaces the old split/offset ladder handoff with one straight supported center shaft from entry to upper deck, moves the top torch off the shaft exit, and spawns the three guards on solid deck cells around the ladder hole instead of on the shaft. Fresh village QA should confirm Watch Towers have a continuous climbable ladder to the top, the top exit is open, and guards can move down into the tower.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village Witch Hut front stair/base polish. Fresh QA showed the compact village Witch Hut stair facing 180 degrees away from the door and still using a cobblestone stair plus cobblestone visible foundation. `WitchHutPiece` now builds the visible floor/support skirt from the owning village palette floor material and places the outside stair as the matching roof/wood/sandstone stair facing back toward the door. Fresh village QA should confirm plains/savanna/taiga/snowy huts no longer show a cobblestone step/foundation, desert huts stay sandstone-family, and the front stair climbs into the road-facing door.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village goto prepared-chunk residency fix. Latest logs showed `/witchery goto village_*` soft-failing with "could not prepare that village safely" after `Preparing village arrival ... 182/182 chunk(s) ready`, then every goto-prep wall attempt saw `0 gate candidate(s)`. Debug breadcrumbs showed the located village start chunk was no longer loaded by the time the generator retried, so one-chunk-per-tick prep had generated chunks but not held them resident for the road scanner. Village goto prep now adds a temporary radius ticket for each prepared chunk, verifies the full footprint is still resident before wall generation, keeps those tickets through gate retries and landing selection, releases them on success/failure/cancel/player logout, and no longer logs the final ready count every tick. Fresh QA should chain plains/desert/savanna/taiga/snowy village gotos and confirm `0 gate candidate(s)` no longer repeats after a full prep unless the village truly has no usable road exits.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Remaining Witchery village building material pass. Desert QA called out Watch Towers and Keeps still using cobble/stone/oak instead of sand-type village blocks. `VillageBuildingMaterials` now also exposes roof solids, slabs, parapets, and axis-aware trim, and the Apothecary, Watch Tower, and Keep all use the same biome palette as Bookshop/Witch Hut. Desert/badlands village buildings now use sandstone-family structural, stair, slab, roof, and trim blocks; savanna, taiga, snowy, and plains villages keep matching wood palettes. Fresh village QA should specifically inspect desert Watch Towers/Keeps/Apothecaries plus the already-fixed Bookshop/Witch Hut for sandstone-family materials.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Bookshop/witch-hut village structure polish. Fresh village QA showed the Bookshop roof floating/oversized and the whole shop sitting one block too high from the path, plus generated glass panes not visually connecting to the walls and the Bookshop/Witch Hut using the same wood palette across all village biomes. `BookshopPiece` now follows the old `ComponentVillageBookShop` compact rear-shop footprint and stair roof instead of the newer full-box gable, while its entrance jigsaw sits one block higher than the floor so the structure itself lowers to path height. `VillageBuildingMaterials` now adapts Bookshop and village Witch Hut materials to desert/badlands sandstone, savanna acacia, taiga/snowy spruce, and plains oak/cobble palettes. Both structures now place explicit connected glass-pane states instead of default pole panes; the village Witch Hut also uses the biome door/stair and keeps its road-facing raised entrance.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village goto gate-readiness and landing fix. Latest logs showed discovered villages generating walls correctly, but `/witchery goto village_*` generated immediately after full-chunk prep with `0 gate candidate(s)`, marked the village processed, then teleported; manual `/witchery walls` refused until `walls reset`, and a later forced command found normal gate candidates. `VillageWallGenerator.generate` now returns a generation result and refuses to place/mark walls when no gate candidates are visible, so early roadless reads no longer create a false processed state. Goto-prep and wall jobs retry after short delays while chunks settle, then mark processed only after a real wall/gate generation. Village goto also lands on a loaded village road/safe surface with two blocks of headroom instead of blindly using the locate X/Z height.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Cursed Snow Pressure Plate first-pass block shell. Source `BlockPressurePlateBase(SNOW_PRESSURE_PLATE, "snow", Sensitivity.everything)` was still missing after the snow-family promotion. The port now registers hidden `witchery:cursed_snow_pressure_plate` as a snow-textured all-entity pressure plate with no block item or creative-tab entry, the same player-facing name as the normal Snow Pressure Plate, and loot that drops `witchery:snow_pressure_plate`. Remaining parity is the old cursed block-entity/brew payload path that converts a normal snow plate into the cursed plate during triggered dispersal.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Config-gated village wall diagnostics. `config/witchery-common.toml` now gets a top-level `debug = true` flag by default, and the village wall/goto system emits `[VillageWalls:Debug]` INFO diagnostics only while that flag is enabled. The debug logs record queue sizes every 40 ticks while active, loaded-only village reference lookups, safe-locate candidate probes, before/after synchronous goto chunk prep, forced wall-ticket requests, automatic footprint readiness, and wall-generation entry points. If another freeze happens, the last debug line should identify whether the server stopped inside structure probing, full chunk prep, forced ticketing, or the generator itself. Set `debug = false` later to quiet the extra QA logs.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Passive multi-village wall queue hardening. The 19:17 fresh-world freeze stopped after two automatic village wall starts queued near spawn and logged natural chunk waits, with no Java exception. Automatic village discovery still had a hidden synchronous start-chunk lookup through structure references, and the active wall-job queue was head-of-line only. Automatic reference lookup/refresh now only inspects already loaded chunks, safe village goto passes the located `StructureStart` directly into arrival prep instead of rediscovering it, active wall jobs rotate through a bounded two-step budget, and pending automatic villages scan/check only a small bounded set per tick while queueing at most one ready generation job. Fresh QA should start new worlds near multiple villages, walk for 30-60 seconds, and confirm the log can show several `Waiting for naturally loaded wall chunks` entries without the integrated server freezing.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village goto void-arrival freeze fix. The 19:07 log shows the latest taiga/snowy goto did not freeze in locate or wall generation: it found the village, prepared one arrival chunk, teleported, then stopped at `Waiting for naturally loaded wall chunks ... 1/168 ready` while the player was in ungenerated terrain. Village goto now queues a pre-teleport prep job that loads the target village footprint one full chunk per tick, generates walls/gates for unprocessed villages, then teleports only after the destination is ready. Already processed villages still prepare a small 5x5 landing bubble before teleport.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Pit Trap block promotion. Source `BlockPitDirt` and `BlockPitGrass` were still missing from the modern port, even though Torment/Pit parity docs called them out. `witchery:pit_dirt` and `witchery:pit_grass` now register as real no-collision camouflage blocks with vanilla dirt/grass visuals, source hardness/sound feel, self loot, creative-tab placement, and old display names "Dirt" and "Grass". Remaining source parity is the old Tormented Twine Witch's Cauldron recipe route plus in-game trap/Torment QA.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Wood Stockade first-pass promotion. Source `BlockStockade(false)` was still fully missing while `BlockStockade(true)`/Ice Stockade was already live. `witchery:stockade` now registers as a real block item using the shared Stockade collision/damage/connection behavior, oak-log models, self loot, creative-tab placement, and the old shaped recipe pattern (`logs` around `Exhale of the Horned One`) for nine stockades. Remaining stockade parity is the old per-wood metadata variants and brew/freezing conversion from wood stockade to Ice Stockade.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Stone Circle naming alias. A source/lang check showed Witchery refers to the generated `ComponentCoven` feature as a stone circle in player-facing contexts. The structure registry stays `witchery:coven_circle` for saved-world compatibility, but `/witchery goto stone_circle` now locates that same structure. Fresh QA can use either `/witchery goto coven_circle` or `/witchery goto stone_circle` when checking plains/savanna/desert/badlands circles.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Coven Circle cobweb detail parity. A fresh decompiled check of `ComponentCoven` showed the source Stone Circle has three cobweb detail blocks at local `(3,1,0)`, `(10,2,7)`, and `(0,3,7)`. The port had turned two of them into mossy marker stones and missed the third. `CovenCirclePiece` now places the cobwebs at the source coordinates while keeping the existing altar, hidden dispenser, spawners, biome materials, water rejection, and stone-backed support behavior. Fresh QA should locate/generate coven circles in plains/savanna/desert/badlands and confirm the old web details appear without disturbing the ring layout.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village wall/gate path tree cleanup. Taiga QA showed gate approaches and walls slicing through trees and leaving cut trunks/canopies around the path. `VillageWallGenerator` now detects when a wall column, gatehouse cell, or graded gate path cell touches nearby `LOGS`/`LEAVES`, then removes the connected natural tree material within a bounded corridor. This keeps cleanup local to the generated wall/path and avoids clearing planks/fences/building wood. Fresh QA should retest taiga and snowy/forest villages, especially gate paths, and confirm no half-cut spruce trunks or floating leaf crowns remain where walls or paths pass through trees.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Witchery village building duplicate cap. Fresh QA found four village Witch Huts in one village after the house-pool weight increase. `WitcheryVillageElement` now keeps a per-village claim set keyed by the owning village `StructureStart` when available, with a nearby-cluster fallback only when structure references are unavailable during placement, and allows at most one apothecary, one witch hut, one bookshop, one watch tower, and one keep per village. Repeated chunk passes for the same selected piece are still allowed, but later duplicate origins in the same village skip their block placement and log `[VillageBuildings] Skipping duplicate ...`. Fresh QA should generate several villages and confirm each Witchery building type appears no more than once per village while nearby separate villages can still receive their own copies.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Witchery village building entrance alignment fix. Village Witch Huts were reported with their door away from the path and one block too low. The procedural village element now advertises its `building_entrance` jigsaw on the actual generated front side of the NORTH-oriented StructurePiece and uses vanilla's y=0 child entrance baseline, raising Witchery village buildings one block relative to roads. The compact Witch Hut also places its front stair directly outside the generated front even when that stair is just outside the piece bounding box. Fresh QA should generate villages and confirm Witch Huts attach door/stair-first to paths and sit one block above the road like vanilla houses.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Hobgoblin Hut resident floor fix. Fresh QA found the hut hobgoblin spawned partly inside the source-shaped goblin hut floor. The source-compatible interior spawn tile is a bottom slab in the modern rebuild, so the resident now spawns at `minY + 1.5` on top of that slab instead of `minY + 1` inside its collision. Fresh QA should locate/generate `witchery:hobgoblin_hut` and confirm the hobgoblin stands on the interior floor near the cauldron without clipping.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village goto no-force wall follow-up. The latest chained goto froze after safe locate found a taiga village and queued `132 wall chunk ticket(s)`, but before the first forced-ticket progress log. That isolated that stall to `updateChunkForced` for distant wall chunks, not locate, wall generation, or teleport. The first fix moved village goto to natural wall loading; the later 19:07 void-arrival fix supersedes the immediate one-chunk teleport with pre-teleport chunk prep and wall generation. Manual `/witchery walls` keeps the explicit one-at-a-time forced-ticket path. Fresh QA should chain plains/savanna/desert/taiga/snowy gotos and verify each successful find logs `Queued village arrival prep`, `Preparing village arrival`, wall generation before teleport for fresh villages, and no `Queued wall job (goto) ... ticket(s)`.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Chalk glyph visual parity follow-up. White/ritual chalk was reported to draw the same visible symbol every use. Source `ItemChalk` places or rerolls glyph metadata with `world.rand.nextInt(12)`, and `BlockCircleGlyph` renders one of 12 texture variants for each chalk family. The port now gives chalk-circle blocks a `glyph=0..11` state, maps ritual/otherwhere/infernal blockstates to the existing old `circleglyph1/2/3.1-12` textures, and makes same-chalk use reroll the glyph art instead of deleting the mark. Fresh QA should draw several white, otherwhere, and infernal marks, then click existing same-family marks again and confirm the visible symbol changes while ritual detection still works.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village structure density and source-shape follow-up. Fresh comparison with 1.7.10 showed the old village witch hut is a compact `4x6x5` `ComponentVillageWitchHut` with its door at local `x=1,z=0`, and the old goblin/hobgoblin hut uses a `7x6x7` source schematic with one resident. All five vanilla village house pools now give Witchery buildings higher selection weights: apothecary `16`, witch hut `12`, bookshop `16`, watch tower `8`, and Keep later rose to `32` after it still went unseen in QA. `WitcheryVillageElement` now reports the source-sized witch hut and building-specific door jigsaw X positions, the village witch hut is rebuilt to the old compact model, and the hobgoblin hut is rebuilt from the decompiled 7x6x7 schematic. Fresh QA should generate plains/desert/savanna/taiga/snowy villages and verify apothecaries/bookshops/witch huts are common, keeps appear more often than before, the witch hut attaches by its real front door, and the hobgoblin hut resembles the old source shape.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village goto locate freeze follow-up. The 15:02 run completed one natural village plus several `/witchery goto village_*` teleports with walls, then froze after `[Goto] Searching for minecraft:village_plains ...]` and before any `Prepared`, `Queued wall job`, or `Wall generation` log. That isolated the remaining stall to vanilla `findNearestMapStructure` again. Village gotos now bypass the synchronous vanilla locate call entirely: they queue a safe village locate job that probes one candidate village chunk per server tick; the later 19:07 follow-up adds pre-teleport chunk prep and wall generation before crossing. Manual `/witchery walls` also no longer runs vanilla locate; it uses a nearby already generated village start and fails cleanly if none is loaded. Fresh QA should retest chained plains/savanna/desert/taiga/snowy gotos and watch for `Queueing safe village search`, `Safe village locate still probing`, `Queued village arrival prep`, and either a delayed successful prep/teleport or a clean no-match message instead of a hard freeze.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Ice Stockade promoted from the legacy placeholder roster. Source `BlockStockade(true)` is now represented by a real `witchery:ice_stockade` block/item with low fence-like collision, translucent vanilla-ice presentation, waterlogging, 25.0 hardness / 20.0 resistance, light opacity 1, source-style cactus contact damage, and connections to itself, vanilla Iron Bars, and the Witchery Ice Gate. It has blockstate/model/loot/lang/creative-tab wiring and no direct recipe yet because the old source created ice stockades through brew conversion from wooden stockades. Fresh QA should place it beside Iron Bars and Ice Gates, confirm contact damage, and verify the item is no longer shown as a placeholder.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village goto savanna stall and gate grading follow-up. The 14:32 savanna goto stopped while a wall pre-generation job waited at `15/16 requested ready`, before generation or teleport. Explicit `/witchery goto village_*` and `/witchery walls` jobs now sort forced wall chunks nearest-first, cap very large explicit forced footprints at 144 chunks, request one chunk ticket at a time, and release/skip a forced chunk that makes no readiness progress for 10 seconds. Gate height sampling and inward gate path grading were first bounded to keep gate adaptation from flattening paths through houses; the current terrain-only pass allows up to 15 inward blocks but stops at building blocks. Fresh QA should retest chained plains/savanna/desert/taiga/snowy village gotos and watch for one-at-a-time requested-batch logs, no client freeze, and gate paths smoothing terrain without entering buildings.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Perpetual Ice pressure plate trigger parity. Source `BlockPerpetualIcePressurePlate` gives ice and snow plates different behavior: snow is a normal all-entity pressure plate, while ice only triggers when a living entity steps on it wearing Icy Slippers. The port now uses a shared `PerpetualIcePressurePlateBlock`: `ice_pressure_plate` checks `EquipmentSlot.FEET` for `witchery:ice_slippers`, restores the old 2.0 hardness / 5.0 resistance, and explicitly survives on `witchery:ice_fence`; `snow_pressure_plate` keeps normal all-entity behavior plus the same old ice-fence support rule. Fresh QA should verify ordinary mobs do not trigger ice plates, Icy Slippers do, and snow plates still trigger for normal entities.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village goto wall batch readiness fix. The latest plains goto froze after locate completed and after the wall job logged `Requested all 195 wall chunk ticket(s)`, with no later readiness or generation log. The wall job was batching ticket requests but not waiting for each requested batch to load before issuing the next one. Explicit `/witchery goto village_*` and `/witchery walls` jobs now request forced chunk tickets one at a time, wait for the requested chunk to be present in `getChunkNow`, then request the next one; progress-based timeout handling releases/skips a stuck requested chunk instead of flooding worldgen. Fresh QA should retest chained village gotos and manual walls on large plains/savanna/desert villages, watching for "Waiting for requested wall chunk batch" logs instead of one immediate "requested all" spike.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Straw Man terrain blend and height cleanup. The reported badlands Straw Man showed a raised pale sand pad around the hay figure. `witchery:straw_man` placement now rejects overly uneven dry footprints, anchors the piece at the lower accepted footprint height instead of the center column, keeps forced vanilla sand to actual desert biomes, reuses each footprint column's live local solid surface block for non-desert ground/support, and fills support only under the old cross-shaped footprint instead of the whole 6x5 rectangle. Badlands still use dead-bush decoration, but the base should blend into red sand/terracotta-style terrain rather than standing on a white sand platform.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Snow pressure plate, stairs, and slab promoted from the legacy placeholder roster. Source `BlockPerpetualIcePressurePlate(Material.snow)`, `BlockSnowStairs`, and `BlockSnowSlab` are bounded snow-family block slices: 0.2 hardness, snow sound/material feel, normal all-entity pressure-plate sensitivity, snow-block/snow-layer source recipes, and slab double-state drops returning two singles. The port now registers `witchery:snow_pressure_plate`, `witchery:snow_stairs`, and `witchery:snow_slab` as real blocks/items with block-backed vanilla-snow models, loot, recipes, vanilla stair/slab tags, Witchery creative-tab placement, and non-placeholder lang. Remaining Perpetual Ice/Snow family work is cursed snow pressure plate and any future brew/rite placement wiring.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Perpetual Ice stairs and slab promoted from the legacy placeholder roster. Source `BlockPerpertualIceStairs` and `BlockPerpetualIceSlab` were bounded block slices: ice friction, hardness 2.0, blast resistance 5.0, light opacity 3, translucent/no-occlusion rendering, and normal stair/slab item drops with double slabs returning two singles. The port now registers `witchery:ice_stairs` and `witchery:ice_slab` as real blocks/items, block-backed models using the vanilla ice texture, vanilla stair/slab tags, loot tables, Witchery creative-tab placement, and non-placeholder lang. Later passes also promoted the snow variants and ice stockade; remaining Perpetual Ice/Snow family work is cursed snow pressure plate and future brew/rite wiring that should place the ice variants.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Perpetual Ice base block promoted from the legacy placeholder roster. Source `BlockPerpetualIce` is a bounded block slice: ice friction, hardness 2.0, blast resistance 5.0, light opacity 3, solid collision/support, and an empty random-tick method so it never melts naturally. The port now registers `witchery:perpetual_ice` as a real `PerpetualIceBlock` with a block item, vanilla ice break/loot behavior, a block-backed model using the vanilla ice texture, Witchery creative-tab placement, and non-placeholder lang. Remaining Perpetual Ice/Snow family work is the old ice stairs/slabs/stockade and snow pressure/stair/slab pieces plus any future brew/rite wiring that should place the block.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Blood Crucible source-parity reconciliation. A fresh 1.7.10 check showed the port already had the block, block entity, renderer, vampire fill path, and level-10 ultimate selection path, but the status docs still listed the block as missing. This pass restores the source-shaped stone-brick recipe, corrects the block registration to old stone sound/material feel with hardness 2.5 and blast resistance 1000, lets creative bypass only the full-crucible check, and narrows the Farm ultimate catalyst back to a written book. Broader Blood Power/late vampire progression gates remain separate work.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village goto locate radius bounded for vanilla villages. The 12:57-13:00 QA run showed automatic village walls generating correctly, savanna and snowy gotos completing with walls and gates, then the next `/witchery goto village_plains` froze after the `[Goto] Searching...` log and before any chunk preparation or wall job. That isolates the remaining freeze to vanilla `findNearestMapStructure`, not wall generation. Vanilla village gotos now use a smaller safe locate radius (`8` structure rings instead of the general `32`), while non-village Witchery gotos keep the wider range. Stale automatic pending wall entries also skip immediately once another path has already marked the same village processed. Fresh QA should retest chained `/witchery goto village_plains/savanna/desert/taiga/snowy`; if a biome-specific village is not found within the safe radius, the command should fail quickly instead of freezing the server thread.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Automatic village wall jobs no longer force-load chunks during world entry. The 12:48 fresh-world log showed spawn loading only a few chunks before an automatic wall queue began a 210-ticket padded-footprint job, which could freeze the integrated server while the initial view-distance load was still settling. Automatic chunk-load discovery now waits until the full padded wall footprint is already naturally loaded by normal player/view-distance chunk loading, then generates without forced tickets. `/witchery goto village_*` and `/witchery walls` still pre-generate intentionally, but now use nearest-first one-at-a-time forced tickets with stuck-chunk skip handling. Fresh QA should retest new-world spawn villages, walking into new villages, manual `/witchery walls`, and `/witchery goto village_plains/savanna/desert/taiga/snowy`, watching for no forced-ticket log on automatic world-entry jobs, no stacked duplicate walls, no `0 gate candidate(s)` on ordinary road villages, and no hang after large ticket counts.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village wall batched chunk-loading and duplicate-goto fix. The 12:32-12:35 session showed the neighboring plains village auto-generating walls successfully, then `/witchery goto village_plains` forcing the exact same processed bounding box again and stacking a second wall. The later savanna goto froze immediately after requesting 210 padded-footprint wall chunk tickets in one tick. `/witchery goto village_*` now respects the processed-village set and, after the 19:07 follow-up, prepares a small landing bubble before teleport when walls already exist, while `/witchery walls` no longer force-reruns unless `/witchery walls reset` is used first. Wall jobs still keep road-aware gate detection, but forced-chunk tickets are now requested nearest-first one at a time before the generator runs and releases them. Fresh QA should retest walking into new villages, manual `/witchery walls`, and `/witchery goto village_plains/savanna/desert/taiga/snowy` from a clean client restart, watching for no stacked duplicate walls, no `0 gate candidate(s)` on ordinary road villages, and no hang after large ticket counts.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village wall chunk-readiness follow-up. The 11:31 automatic wall logs showed multiple fresh villages generating with `0 gate candidate(s)` because the queued job waited only for perimeter chunks while gate detection scans the village road/path footprint. Automatic wall jobs now wait for the padded village wall footprint to be naturally loaded, while explicit goto/walls jobs use temporary nearest-first forced-chunk tickets, generate walls, then release the tickets; `/witchery goto village_*` still holds teleport completion until that job finishes. This keeps the no-synchronous-force-load path from the 11:13 plains fix while making automatic/goto walls see road chunks before gate selection. Fresh QA should retest walking into new villages, manual `/witchery walls`, and `/witchery goto village_plains/savanna/desert/taiga/snowy` from a clean client restart, watching for both missing gates and long generation ticks.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Straw Man land-only placement. `witchery:straw_man` no longer relies on just center/corner water checks; generation now scans the full 6x5 footprint and rejects any column with water above the solid floor, plus ocean/river biomes, before adding the structure piece. A second guard in `StrawManPiece.postProcess` now checks the actual worldgen fluid/heightmap state across the footprint before writing any hay, ground, plants, or spawner blocks, so late terrain/fluid mismatches should abort cleanly. Fresh QA should verify `/witchery goto straw_man` and natural `/locate structure witchery:straw_man` targets in plains/desert/savanna/badlands do not place the effigy underwater or in river/ocean terrain.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Village wall gate-face polish. Gate detection now only accepts a road exit when the road reaches a three-wide flat wall face head-on: the center wall cell plus one block on each side along the wall must all be wall, with village interior on the road side and outside/empty space beyond the wall. This rejects sideways hits on nearby wall segments/corners that made gatehouses face the wrong part of the path. Fresh village QA should retest plains/savanna/desert/taiga/snowy gate placement, especially curved or shoreline roads.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Broom bristle tint parity. Source `ModelBroom` renders the handle normally, then renders all bristle strands under the source wool dye table, defaulting missing `BrushColor` to brown (`12`). The port now hides bristles in the base broom model and re-renders them through a dedicated bristle layer tinted from the synced entity `BrushColor`, so dyed placed brooms should visibly recolor their tail while keeping the handle on the normal texture path. Remaining broom value is in-game flight/break/pickup/visual QA rather than another data/render wiring pass.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Broom item/entity lifecycle parity slice. Source comparison with `EntityBroom` and `ItemGeneral.placeBroom` showed placed brooms should keep custom item names, carry a `BrushColor`, accept dye while placed, persist those fields, and return the broom item when broken instead of being permanently invulnerable. The port now round-trips custom names and `BrushColor` through `BroomItem` custom data, lets dye items set the placed broom's brush color, saves/loads that color on the entity, returns the named/colored normal or enchanted broom on sneak-pickup, and drops the matching broom item when damaged.

Previous local delta (2026-05-31): Glint Weed source particle parity. A fresh 1.7.10 `BlockGlintWeed` check showed `animateTick` used `ParticleEffect.FLAME` with zero velocity, so the port's stale `END_ROD` detour has been reverted to `ParticleTypes.FLAME`. The existing source-shaped light/spread behavior stays intact: full light, 1-in-6 random spread, a five-plant density cap, dirt/grass/stone/sand/soul-sand spread substrate, and solid support either below or above the target.

Earlier local delta (2026-05-31): Somnian Cotton spread parity. Source comparison with `BlockCotton` showed natural spread should roll 1-in-6, run only in the Dream/Spirit dimension, require Flowing Spirit beside the block below the source cotton, and place new cotton only over dirt/grass. The port now uses that gate instead of allowing Spirit World cotton to spread without Flowing Spirit or overworld cotton to spread around Flowing Spirit.

Earlier local delta (2026-05-31): Ritual power config routing. `WitcheryConfig.RITUAL_POWER_MULTIPLIER` now applies through shared ritual altar-power helpers: one-shot/startup ritual costs are scaled once before altar drain, while active staged rites scale their per-tick upkeep debt before whole power is consumed. `ALLOW_BIOME_CHANGING` was already enforced before the Climate Change rite queued or consumed its support items.

Earlier local delta (2026-05-31): Village goto freeze follow-up. Latest logs for the reported plains-village freeze had no exception and never reached the `[Goto] Prepared...` line, so the suspected stall was pre-teleport locate/preload work rather than the wall generator. `/witchery goto` now logs before locate, uses a bounded vanilla-style locate search (`skipReferenced=false`, radius 32), and preloads only the arrival chunk instead of a 5x5 full-chunk area before teleport. Fresh village QA should retest `village_plains`, `village_savanna`, `village_taiga`, and `village_snowy` after a client restart.

Earlier same-day local delta (2026-05-31): Coven Circle support cleanup. Non-desert `witchery:coven_circle` generation keeps the grass surface and standing-stone material palette, but its terrain support is now stone-backed instead of dirt-backed so uneven terrain cannot show a one-block dirt skirt under the stone circle. Fresh plains/savanna QA is still needed.

Earlier same-day delta (2026-05-31): Hand Bow anti-magic power-drain pass. Nullifying bolts fired while the shooter wears a full hunter/silvered/dawn hunter set now carry a powered-draining flag like the old powered anti-magic bolt path. The shot drains the shooter's current infusion-energy pool on launch with the source max-energy reduction rule, and a powered Nullifying Bolt hit drains half of a player target's max infusion energy after the existing potion/effect strip; non-player targets still receive the current port's effect strip only because the old external magic-power hook has no modern local pool yet. The previous client model predicate pass remains live too: uncharged idle uses `crossbow_pistol`, active use uses the three pull-frame models via `witchery:hand_bow_pulling` / `witchery:hand_bow_pull`, and a loaded-but-not-currently-drawing bow uses `charged_crossbow` through `witchery:hand_bow_charged`. Remaining bolt work is in-game projectile/visual QA.

Previous same-day village delta (2026-05-31): village structure/wall pass. `WitcheryVillageElement` now supplies a real `minecraft:building_entrance` jigsaw connection, so procedural apothecaries, bookshops, village witch huts, watch towers, and keeps can attach to vanilla village house pools. Bookshop is live as a 10x8x9 source-inspired shop with the bookshop loot chest, shelves, lectern, sign, and villager shopkeeper, and the house-pool weights were raised from the first very sparse Witchery-building pass. Adaptive village walls are live around vanilla villages with stone-brick-family walls, water-safe placement, terrain-robust gatehouses, desert-only sandstone path material, savanna dirt/stone materials, terrain-style gate/path support fill, graded gate approaches, occupied living-entity wall-column skips, one-ring perimeter chunk force-loading, no artificial wall-generation delay, and `/witchery goto` cooldown/flavor feedback. Coven Circle is also live as `witchery:coven_circle`; this heartbeat tightened its biome material predicate to match old `ComponentCoven`, so sandstone/sand variants are limited to actual desert/badlands instead of every hot biome, restored the hidden mineshaft-loot dispenser plus two vanilla Witch spawners under the altar, checks center/corner water before placement, uses a grass surface with stone-backed support in non-desert biomes so uneven terrain no longer exposes a one-block dirt skirt, and uses the source 90% resident Coven Witch spawn chance. Straw Man worldgen is now live as `witchery:straw_man` from old `ComponentWickerMan`, with a hay effigy, biome plants, hidden Zombie/Zombie/Skeleton spawner, village exclusion, water rejection, and `/witchery goto straw_man`. Next action is fresh-village QA across plains/desert/savanna/taiga/snowy, with special attention to goto-freeze retests, bookshop placement, structure distribution, wall timing, gate filler, and path materials, plus fresh Coven Circle and Straw Man checks in plains/desert/savanna/badlands.

Last reconciled: 2026-05-25 (parity scan merged; Sun Collector/Charged Quartz Sphere/Sun Grenade chain implemented plus source recipe/shape/hardness and Quartz Sphere return/tagged vampire hits; Silver Sword held-damage plus tagged-vampire silver/hunter hooks restored; Wooden Stake recipe and sleeping-vampire staking restored; Wooden/Silver/Bone/Nullifying/Splitting Bolt source crafting restored; Glass Goblet recipe, first practical vampire blood fill/drink induction, and chicken-blood ritual capture restored; Observations of an Immortal + Torn Page promoted with source recipe, custom update recipe, page-progress tooltip, vanilla readable book screen, source-family player-kill Torn Page drops including the source `isNonBoss=false` branch for current bosses/specials, and Bookshop loot seed; Warm Blood and Lilith's Blood consumable semantics restored in practical form; source-style persisted villager blood draining restored for vampire bites; Fume Filter + Filtered Fume Funnel source recipes/chance restored; Silver Vat stored Silver Deposits byproduct plus source deposit recipe routes/local silver-ingot bridge restored; Kettle bottle automation and source bonus rolls restored; Witches' Oven source sided inventory and sapling fume table restored; Refilling Chest source refill/loot decision reconciled; Poppet Shelf special item pose corrected to the source low half-block baseline; Mirror front/back model UVs corrected for placed and item views; Leech Chest/Refilling Chest GUI item heights normalized to the old neutral item baseline; source-style taglock-to-poppet binding recipe and mob-bound Voodoo/Vampiric resolution restored; VampiricPoppetItem two-taglock fix; Poppet Shelf Compass source recipe, practical loaded-shelf locate, and old six-frame distance icon model restored; Player Compass filled-player-taglock binding route restored; Crystal Ball filled-Taglock-Kit scrying and weighted old prediction reading roster restored in practical form; PlayerCompassItem; WitchHunter poppet disruption; villager witchery goal added; garlic grass-seed parity corrected; Garlic Garland facing/click-through/repulsion and vampire break cancel restored)
Previous local delta (2026-05-29l): Goblin boss chain implemented. **`GoblinGulgEntity`** (melee boss, 400 HP, armor 8): damage resistance scales with distance to nearest `GoblinMogEntity` (immune ≤3 blocks, 20% at ≤6, 50% at ≤9, 80% at ≤16, cap 15 beyond); melee attack power inversely scales (stronger near Mog); invulnerability phase (invulTimer synced, heals 20 HP/10 ticks); teleports toward target every 200 ticks when stuck; passive regen 2 HP/20 ticks; purple boss bar; drops koboldite nuggets + enchanted iron armor + 50% Gulg's Girdle. **`GoblinMogEntity`** (ranged boss, 400 HP): same partner-distance immunity mechanic checking for nearest `GoblinGulgEntity`; `RangedBowAttackGoal` fires arrows faster when target is airborne; passive regen 1 HP/20 ticks; randomly self-equips bow; teleports every 300 ticks; drops koboldite nuggets + enchanted iron armor + 50% Mog's Quiver. Both use `HobgoblinModel` (same goblin proportions) with original 1.7.10 textures (gulg.png, mog.png). **`gulgs_girdle`** and **`mogs_quiver`** promoted from placeholder to registered items. Spawn commands, spawn eggs, creative tab, lang added. BUILD SUCCESSFUL.
Previous delta (2026-05-29k): `VampireEntity` ported (source parity: EntityVampire 1.7.10). Stats: 40 HP, 0.4 speed, 5.0 attack, 20 follow range. UNDEAD type (registered in `minecraft:undead` entity tag). AI: `FloatGoal`, `RestrictSunGoal`, `FleeSunGoal` (flees daylight), melee, door-opening, wander, target non-vampire players + villagers. Daytime: burns in sunlight every 20 ticks, clears target, teleports to coffinPos if >16 blocks away. Nighttime: drains blood from villagers (10% chance per hit → heal 4 HP, add 4 to damageDone); when damageDone ≥ 20 returns to coffin and fills nearest `BloodCrucibleBlockEntity` within 6 blocks. Spawn equipment: vampire_boots always; male (coat/chain-coat + trousers/skirted) or female (corset/chain-coat-ladies + skirted/trousers), chosen randomly in `finalizeSpawn`. No drops (source parity). `VampireRenderer` reuses `VillageGuardModel` (identical UV layout: texOffs(28,46) head) with original `vampire.png` texture (copied from 1.7.10 JAR). `HumanoidArmorLayer` renders vampire clothing automatically. Spawn command, spawn egg, creative tab, lang added. Fixed `WolfmanModel.LAYER_LOCATION` conflict with `WerewolfModel` (both were `witchery:wolfman/main` → wolfman entity renamed to `witchery:wolfman_entity/main`). BUILD SUCCESSFUL.
Previous delta (2026-05-29j): `BabaYagaEntity` ported — 500 HP boss witch with purple boss bar. Source parity from EntityBabaYaga 1.7.10: throws witchery brew approximations (1/3: Slowness/Blindness/Wither via splash potion) or vanilla potions (2/3: distance-based Slowness/Poison via ThrownSplashPotion); caps incoming damage at 15; reduces magic/BYPASSES_ARMOR damage to 15%; reflects 25% physical damage back to non-wooden-weapon player attackers; spawns CaveSpider when hit by undead or untamed pets; teleports toward target (~2%/tick, >100 tick cooldown); applies Blindness V to falling/sprinting targets (5%/tick); ownership system (neutral to owner, despawns after 600 ticks, drops witch ingredients every 100 ticks when near owner). Drops: witch ingredients (2-4 stacks) + enchanted book + Baba's Hat (guaranteed). `BabaYagaModel` uses villager proportions (8×10×8 head, 4×8×4 arms, 4×10×4 legs) with 3-tier stacked witch hat (brim at texOffs(0,98), tiers at 0,76 / 0,87 / 0,95), 128×128 texture matching existing babayaga.png. Spawn command, spawn egg, creative tab, and lang added. BUILD SUCCESSFUL.
Previous delta (2026-05-29i): Silver system implemented. **`BoltEntity extends AbstractArrow`**: crossbow bolt projectile carrying `boltType` (0=wooden, 4=silver); silver type detected by `SilverEvents.isSilverSource()`; fires from `HandBowItem`. **`HandBowItem`**: full bow-mechanic USE/releaseUsing — charged shot support started here and later expanded to Nullifying/Bone/Splitting bolt behavior on 2026-05-31. **`SilverEvents`** (new centralized handler): `onSilverDamage` adds +5 bonus damage to vampires/werewolves from silver sources (melee: item tag `witchery:silver_weapons`; projectile: `BoltEntity.isSilver()`); `onSilveredArmorHit` deals 1.0 back-damage to werewolves attacking wearers of silvered hunter armor (source parity: ItemHunterClothes.getProperties). **`WitcheryItemTags.SILVER_WEAPONS`**: tag key + JSON (silver_sword, silver_bolt). **`WolfmanEntity`**: silver detection delegated to `SilverEvents.isSilverSource()` — now correctly catches bolt hits as well as melee. `WerewolfEvents` duplicate silver handler removed. BUILD SUCCESSFUL.
Previous delta (2026-05-29h, renderer corrected 2026-06-01): `WolfmanEntity` and `WereVillagerEntity` ported and registered. `WolfmanEntity` (Monster, 40 HP, 0.4 speed, 5.0 attack, 80 follow range): targets players, villagers, witch hunters; silver weapons deal 1.5× damage (cap 15), all other damage capped at 1.0 per hit; every 100 ticks on non-full-moon nights converts back to `WereVillagerEntity` (carrying formerProfession + infectious flag); drops silver deposits/rotten flesh/bone; uses zombie sounds as placeholder (wolf variant system changed in 1.21, no simple static WOLF_AMBIENT etc.). `WereVillagerEntity` (PathfinderMob, 20 HP): wanders peacefully, panics from threats, transforms to `WolfmanEntity` on full-moon night every 100 ticks. `WolfmanModel` is ported from ModelWolfman 1.7.10 (64x64, double-jointed hind legs, tail, ears+snout, attack animation) and now uses a dedicated old-source UV texture; `WolfmanRenderer` has a custom `WolfmanRenderState` carrying attackTime. `WereVillagerRenderer` now follows source by using the vanilla villager model/base texture instead of the earlier Witch Hunter biped placeholder. `WerewolfEvents.spawnWereVillagerPlaceholder` replaced with real `WereVillagerEntity` spawn. Spawn commands, spawn eggs, creative tab, and lang added. Parity matrix updated. BUILD SUCCESSFUL.
Previous delta (2026-05-29g): Village Guard AI + flavor text polished. `HurtByTargetGoal(this, VillageGuardEntity.class)` added so nearby guards mutually alert each other when one is attacked. Quote system added: on target acquired the guard shouts a threat-specific war cry (vampire / werewolf / witch / demon / generic monster pools), on sword draw ("Too close for arrows — blades out!"), and on taking a hit (1-in-3 chance). All shouts broadcast as action-bar messages (gold, 20-block radius, 10 s cooldown). `EgassyEntity` converted to same system: 15 fart quotes and 4 injury reactions now use translatable lang keys and display on the action bar in yellow. Fixed lang JSON missing comma that caused all new keys to silently fail (raw keys were showing instead of text). BUILD SUCCESSFUL.
Previous delta (2026-05-29f): `VillageGuardEntity` design locked. Helmet rendering fixed: armor head model now uses 8×8×8 (matching `ZombieVillagerModel.createBaseArmorMesh`) rather than 8×10×8. The 8-unit height stops at y=−2, leaving the face area (y=−2 to y=0) uncovered — exactly how vanilla handles villager-proportion heads. Grey face-wrapping artefact eliminated. Equipment updated to leather-by-default with 1-in-5 iron upgrade per piece and 1-in-20 chainmail chest chance. Two texture variants corrected: guard2 was the Resurrected `plain.png` designed for standard biped UV; replaced with a dark-armour variant generated from guard1 at the correct texOffs(28,46) UV layout. Combat (bow at range, sword close) confirmed working. BUILD SUCCESSFUL.
Previous delta (2026-05-29e): `VillageGuardEntity` ported and registered. 25 HP melee Monster with iron sword and 4 armour points. Attacks: vanilla undead (`EntityTypeTags.UNDEAD`), raiders/illagers (`EntityTypeTags.RAIDERS`), Witchery vampires (entity tag), `CovenWitchEntity`, vanilla `Witch`, `DemonEntity`, and vampire/werewolf players. Neutral to normal players. Two appearance variants (type 0 = silver iron guard, type 1 = blue-grey captain); textures generated (`village_guard1.png`, `village_guard2.png`, 64×32). Sounds: villager hurt/death. Registered in entities, client model layer, renderer, spawn command `/witchery spawn village_guard`, spawn egg (`village_guard_spawn_egg`) added to creative tab. BUILD SUCCESSFUL.
Previous delta (2026-05-29d): `BloodCrucibleBlock` visual correctness pass. Three bugs fixed: (1) **Front wall Z offset** — `PartPose.offset(-4f, 19f, -5f)` corrected to `(-4f, 19f, -4f)`; the old value placed the front wall 1/16 block outside the AABB (world Z 0.1875–0.25 instead of 0.25–0.3125), leaving a visible gap at the front corners of the bowl. (2) **Blood UV texture region** — regenerated `blood_crucible.png` (32×64) so rows 44–63 are solidly bright crimson; the old texture had dark/transitional material at rows 44–50 where the blood top-face samples `texOffs(0,44)`, making blood render near-black. (3) **Blood tint colour** — `BLOOD_COLOR` changed from `colorFromFloat(1.0F, 0.55F, 0.05F, 0.05F)` (dark red multiplier → near-black result) to `colorFromFloat(0.88F, 1.0F, 1.0F, 1.0F)` (white tint with slight transparency — lets the crimson texture show through as-is). BUILD SUCCESSFUL.
Previous delta (2026-05-29c): `GlintWeedBlock` has since been rechecked against 1.7.10 `BlockGlintWeed`: source `animateTick` uses `ParticleEffect.FLAME` with zero velocity, full light `0.9375F`, 1-in-6 density-capped spread, dirt/grass/stone/sand/soul-sand spread substrate, and solid support either below or above the target. The newer port restores the flame particle; the earlier `END_ROD` correction note was stale. `EmberMossBlock` and `SpanishMossBlock` confirmed complete: ember moss fires walkers and spreads, spanish moss grows at double vine speed with high flammability.
Previous delta (2026-05-29b): `LeonardEntity` movement fixed — removed `setNoGravity(true)` which was breaking ground pathfinding (WaterAvoidingRandomStrollGoal and MeleeAttackGoal both stalled because the path planner could not find floor waypoints for a no-gravity Monster). Leonard now walks on the ground like Banshee/Spectre. Visual shade feel preserved: `LeonardRenderer.scale()` applies a whole-body sine hover bob (±0.06 blocks, 0.05 rad/tick) instead of the broken per-part `body.y` offset that was leaving head/arms behind. `LeonardModel.setupAnim` per-part oscillation removed.
Previous delta (2026-05-29a): `LeonardEntity` (Shade of Leonard, 500 HP boss) and `LostSoulEntity` (immunity-phase mob, 3 types) ported and registered. Leonard is neutral until attacked; spawns 6 Lost Souls (2 GREEN/RED/BLACK) at 300 HP threshold; immune to all damage while Lost Souls are alive within 20 blocks; applies Wither II on melee hit (Mortal Coil approximation). GREEN souls take all damage, RED only fire/explosion, BLACK only `BYPASSES_ARMOR` magic damage. Leonard drops Leonard's Urn (promoted from placeholder). Model: GhostModel-layout shadowy humanoid (`LeonardModel`); dedicated `leonard.png` (64×32, dark purple-black shade with green eyes, generated); Lost Souls use orb model (`LostSoulModel`, `lost_soul.png`) with green/red/black ARGB tinting per type. Cauldron summon ritual: Witches' Hat + Refined Evil + Mandrake Root + Tear of the Goddess + Nether Wart + 10 000 altar power → spawns Leonard above cauldron. Both spawnable via `/witchery spawn leonard` and `/witchery spawn lost_soul`. Primer entry added. `ExperimentsScreen` auto-confirmed via `ScreenEvent.Opening` to stop the vanilla experimental-features gate on every world creation. `HobgoblinHutPiece.postProcess` deadlock fixed: `getCurrentDifficultyAt` called on raw `ServerLevel` during worldgen now replaced with `new DifficultyInstance(level.getDifficulty(), 0L, 0L, 0.0F)`. BUILD SUCCESSFUL.
Previous delta (2026-05-28d): Elle + Lilith entities ported and registered. `ElleEntity` (PathfinderMob, 20 HP) wanders and transforms into `LilithEntity` after 20 ticks near lava; spawns from the chicken-blood goblet ritual via `GlassGobletItem`. `LilithEntity` is a 200 HP boss (Monster + RangedAttackMob): caps incoming damage at 12/hit, blocks lethal hits and transitions to friendly mode instead of dying; fires `LargeFireball` at range; friendly Lilith offers three quests — full goblet → vampire induction, garlic → cure vampire, brown mushroom at level 6 → advance to level 7. Auto-level hooks added for levels 4, 5, 9 (auto on blood-power fill). `ImmortalsObservationsBook` updates vampire level cap when read by a vampire.
